
---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 1

## Topic: Introducing Yourself

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **নিজের সম্পর্কে 1–2 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলতে পারা**।

---

## 1. প্রথমে এই 20টি Question শিখো

### Basic Questions

1. **What is your name?**
   তোমার নাম কী?

2. **Where are you from?**
   তুমি কোথা থেকে এসেছ?

3. **Where do you live?**
   তুমি কোথায় থাকো?

4. **What do you do?**
   তুমি কী করো?

5. **Are you a student?**
   তুমি কি ছাত্র?

6. **What did you study?**
   তুমি কী পড়াশোনা করেছ?

7. **What is your educational background?**
   তোমার শিক্ষাগত যোগ্যতা কী?

8. **What are your hobbies?**
   তোমার শখ কী?

9. **What do you do in your free time?**
   অবসর সময়ে তুমি কী করো?

10. **What are you interested in?**
    তুমি কোন বিষয়ে আগ্রহী?

---

## 2. একটু বেশি Speaking Question

11. **Why did you choose your subject?**
    তুমি কেন তোমার বিষয়টি বেছে নিয়েছিলে?

12. **What are your strengths?**
    তোমার শক্তিশালী দিকগুলো কী?

13. **What is one of your weaknesses?**
    তোমার একটি দুর্বলতা কী?

14. **What skills do you have?**
    তোমার কী কী দক্ষতা আছে?

15. **What are you learning now?**
    তুমি এখন কী শিখছ?

16. **What is your career goal?**
    তোমার career goal কী?

17. **Why do you want to improve your English?**
    তুমি কেন তোমার English improve করতে চাও?

18. **What do you want to achieve in the future?**
    ভবিষ্যতে তুমি কী অর্জন করতে চাও?

19. **What kind of person are you?**
    তুমি কেমন ধরনের মানুষ?

20. **How would you describe yourself?**
    তুমি নিজেকে কীভাবে বর্ণনা করবে?

---

# 3. Question → Answer শেখা

### Q1: What is your name?

**Simple Answer:**

> My name is Atiar.

আরও natural:

> My name is Atiar. It's nice to meet you.

---

### Q2: Where are you from?

> I am from Bangladesh.

আরও ভালো:

> I am from Bangladesh, and I currently live in Dhaka.

---

### Q3: What do you do?

এখানে **What do you do?** মানে সাধারণত **তুমি কী কাজ/পেশা করো?**

Example:

> I am a software developer.

অথবা:

> I am a CSE graduate, and I am currently looking for a job as a backend or full-stack developer.

---

### Q4: What did you study?

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering.

আরও natural:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. I am particularly interested in software development.

---

### Q5: What are your hobbies?

> My hobbies are reading books, learning new things, and programming.

আরও natural:

> In my free time, I enjoy reading, programming, and learning new technologies.

---

# 4. ⭐ Follow-up Question Technique

এটাই আজকের সবচেয়ে important part।

একটা answer দেওয়ার পর **সেখান থেকে নতুন question তৈরি করবে।**

### Main Question:

> What do you do?

### Answer:

> I am a software developer.

এখন follow-up:

> Why did you choose software development?

Answer:

> I chose software development because I enjoy solving problems and building useful applications.

আবার follow-up:

> What technologies do you use?

Answer:

> I mainly work with Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and databases such as PostgreSQL and MySQL.

আবার:

> Which technology do you like most?

Answer:

> I particularly enjoy working with Django and Django REST Framework because I like backend development and API design.

👉 এভাবে **একটা question থেকে 4–5টা question** তৈরি করতে পারবে।

---

# 5. 🧠 Answer করার Formula

প্রতিটি answer-এর জন্য এই formula ব্যবহার করো:

### **Answer + Reason + Example**

Example:

**Q: Why do you like programming?**

> I like programming **because I enjoy solving problems**.
> For example, I enjoy building web applications and APIs.

আরও বড় answer চাইলে:

**Answer + Reason + Example + Future**

> I like programming because I enjoy solving problems. For example, I like building web applications and APIs. I also enjoy learning new technologies. In the future, I want to become a skilled full-stack developer.

---

# 6. Important Vocabulary

| Word       | Meaning       | Example                                  |
| ---------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| Background | পটভূমি        | My educational background is CSE.        |
| Graduate   | স্নাতক        | I am a CSE graduate.                     |
| Hobby      | শখ            | Reading is my hobby.                     |
| Interested | আগ্রহী        | I am interested in programming.          |
| Skill      | দক্ষতা        | Communication is an important skill.     |
| Strength   | শক্তিশালী দিক | Problem-solving is my strength.          |
| Weakness   | দুর্বলতা      | Public speaking is my weakness.          |
| Goal       | লক্ষ্য        | My goal is to become a developer.        |
| Career     | পেশাগত জীবন   | I want to build my career in technology. |
| Improve    | উন্নত করা     | I want to improve my English.            |

---

# 7. 🔥 Full Self-Introduction

এখন সবগুলো একসাথে:

> Hello, my name is Atiar. I am from Bangladesh, and I currently live in Dhaka. I studied Computer Science and Engineering. I am interested in software development, especially backend and full-stack development.
>
> I enjoy programming because I like solving problems and building useful applications. In my free time, I like learning new technologies, practicing English, and working on programming projects.
>
> My goal is to become a skilled software developer and build a successful career in the technology industry. I also want to improve my English because good communication skills are important for my career and professional growth.

**এটা মুখস্থ করবে না।** নিজের information দিয়ে নিজের মতো করে বলবে।

---

# 8. 🎤 Speaking Challenge

এখন বই/স্ক্রিন না দেখে এই **10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলো**:

1. What is your name?
2. Where are you from?
3. Where do you live?
4. What do you do?
5. What did you study?
6. Why did you choose your subject?
7. What are your hobbies?
8. What skills do you have?
9. What are you learning now?
10. What is your career goal?

### Rule:

প্রথমবার → **1 sentence**

দ্বিতীয়বার → **2–3 sentences**

তৃতীয়বার → **4–5 sentences**

এতে তোমার brain ধীরে ধীরে English-এ **longer answer তৈরি করতে শিখবে।**

---

# 📝 Homework

আজ নিজের সম্পর্কে **20টি question-এর answer** মুখে practice করবে।

তারপর **1 মিনিটের একটি self-introduction** record করবে।

### Target:

**Day 1:** 30 seconds
**Day 2:** 1 minute
**Day 3:** 1.5 minutes

একই topic বারবার বলবে, কিন্তু **প্রতিবার নতুন sentence ব্যবহার করার চেষ্টা করবে।**

---

## ⭐ Lesson 1-এর মূল শিক্ষা

**Speaking-এর জন্য শুধু answer শেখা নয়:**

> **Question → Answer → Follow-up Question → Longer Answer**

এই pattern-টাই পুরো course জুড়ে ব্যবহার করব।

**পরবর্তী: Lesson 2 — Family & Relationships**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 2

## Topic: Family & Relationships

আজকের লক্ষ্য: **নিজের family সম্পর্কে 2–3 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলতে পারা** এবং family-related conversation চালিয়ে যাওয়া।

---

# 1. 🎯 আজকের Speaking Targets

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Family সম্পর্কে question করা
* Family সম্পর্কে answer দেওয়া
* **Who / What / How many / How often / Why** দিয়ে question তৈরি করা
* Short answer → Long answer করা
* Follow-up question করা
* Family member-দের describe করা
* Relationship নিয়ে নিজের opinion দেওয়া

---

# 2. 🟢 Basic Questions — 20 Questions

### Family Information

1. **How many people are there in your family?**
   তোমার পরিবারে কতজন মানুষ আছে?

2. **Who are the members of your family?**
   তোমার পরিবারের সদস্য কারা?

3. **Who do you live with?**
   তুমি কার সাথে থাকো?

4. **Do you have any brothers or sisters?**
   তোমার কি কোনো ভাই-বোন আছে?

5. **How many brothers do you have?**
   তোমার কয়জন ভাই আছে?

6. **How many sisters do you have?**
   তোমার কয়জন বোন আছে?

7. **Are you the oldest child in your family?**
   তুমি কি পরিবারের সবচেয়ে বড় সন্তান?

8. **Are you the youngest child?**
   তুমি কি সবচেয়ে ছোট সন্তান?

9. **Who is the oldest person in your family?**
   তোমার পরিবারের সবচেয়ে বয়স্ক ব্যক্তি কে?

10. **Who is the youngest person in your family?**
    তোমার পরিবারের সবচেয়ে ছোট ব্যক্তি কে?

---

# 3. 👨‍👩‍👧 Family Members সম্পর্কে Questions

11. **What does your father do?**
    তোমার বাবা কী করেন?

12. **What does your mother do?**
    তোমার মা কী করেন?

13. **What is your brother like?**
    তোমার ভাই কেমন?

14. **What is your sister like?**
    তোমার বোন কেমন?

15. **Who are you closest to in your family?**
    পরিবারের কার সাথে তোমার সবচেয়ে বেশি ঘনিষ্ঠতা?

16. **Who do you usually talk to in your family?**
    পরিবারের কার সাথে তুমি সাধারণত কথা বলো?

17. **Who helps you when you have a problem?**
    তোমার সমস্যা হলে কে তোমাকে সাহায্য করে?

18. **Who gives you the best advice?**
    কে তোমাকে সবচেয়ে ভালো পরামর্শ দেয়?

19. **Who understands you the most?**
    কে তোমাকে সবচেয়ে বেশি বোঝে?

20. **Who do you spend the most time with?**
    তুমি কার সাথে সবচেয়ে বেশি সময় কাটাও?

---

# 4. ⭐ Short Answer → Long Answer

এটা খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

### Question:

> How many people are there in your family?

### ❌ Beginner Answer

> There are five people in my family.

সঠিক, কিন্তু খুব ছোট।

### 🟢 Better Answer

> There are five people in my family. They are my father, mother, two siblings, and me.

### 🔥 Advanced Answer

> There are five people in my family, including my parents, my two siblings, and me. We are quite close to each other, and we usually spend time together whenever we get the opportunity.

---

# 5. 👨 Father সম্পর্কে কথা বলা

### Q:

> What does your father do?

### Answer Pattern:

> My father is a ______. He works in ______. He is a hardworking person, and I really respect him.

Example:

> My father is a businessman. He runs his own business. He is a hardworking and responsible person, and I really respect him.

### Follow-up Questions

> What kind of person is your father?

> How often do you talk to your father?

> What have you learned from your father?

> What do you like about your father?

---

# 6. 👩 Mother সম্পর্কে কথা বলা

### Q:

> What is your mother like?

**Answer:**

> My mother is a kind and caring person. She always supports me and encourages me to do my best.

### Follow-up:

> What do you like about your mother?

> What have you learned from your mother?

> How does your mother support you?

---

# 7. 👦 Brother / 👧 Sister

### Q:

> Do you have any brothers or sisters?

### Answer:

> Yes, I have one brother and one sister.

তারপর answer expand:

> Yes, I have one brother and one sister. My brother is older than me, while my sister is younger than me. We have a good relationship and usually help each other.

---

# 8. 🧠 খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ Grammar

## Have / Has

### তুমি বলবে:

> I have one brother.

> I have two sisters.

> I have a small family.

### অন্য ব্যক্তি:

> He has one brother.

> She has two sisters.

> My father has a small business.

---

# 9. Relationship Describe করার Vocabulary

| English       | বাংলা         |
| ------------- | ------------- |
| Close         | ঘনিষ্ঠ        |
| Caring        | যত্নশীল       |
| Kind          | দয়ালু        |
| Helpful       | সাহায্যকারী   |
| Supportive    | সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ |
| Friendly      | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ |
| Honest        | সৎ            |
| Responsible   | দায়িত্বশীল   |
| Hardworking   | পরিশ্রমী      |
| Understanding | বোঝে এমন      |
| Respectful    | শ্রদ্ধাশীল    |
| Loving        | স্নেহশীল      |
| Strict        | কঠোর          |
| Funny         | মজার          |
| Patient       | ধৈর্যশীল      |

---

# 10. 🔥 Relationship Questions

এখন একটু বেশি speaking practice।

### Q1

**Who are you closest to in your family?**

> I am closest to my mother because she understands me very well. I can talk to her about my problems and personal matters.

### Q2

**Do you spend enough time with your family?**

> I try to spend enough time with my family, but sometimes I become busy with my studies, work, and other responsibilities.

### Q3

**How often do you visit your relatives?**

> I usually visit my relatives during holidays and special occasions. Sometimes we also meet at family events.

### Q4

**Do you think family is important?**

> Yes, I think family is extremely important because family members support us emotionally and help us during difficult times.

---

# 11. ⭐ Opinion Question

### Question:

> Why is family important?

Answer structure:

**Opinion → Reason → Example → Conclusion**

### Example:

> I think family is very important because family members support us during both good and difficult times. They encourage us when we face problems and celebrate our achievements with us. For example, when I have a difficult situation, I can talk to my family and get their advice. That's why I believe family plays an important role in our lives.

---

# 12. 🔥 Follow-up Question Chain

এটা আজকের সবচেয়ে important exercise।

### Q1:

> Do you have any brothers or sisters?

**Answer:**

> Yes, I have one brother.

### Q2:

> How old is your brother?

**Answer:**

> He is 25 years old.

### Q3:

> What does he do?

**Answer:**

> He works at a private company.

### Q4:

> What is he like?

**Answer:**

> He is friendly, hardworking, and helpful.

### Q5:

> What do you usually do together?

**Answer:**

> We usually talk, watch movies, and sometimes go outside together.

### Q6:

> Are you close to him?

**Answer:**

> Yes, we are quite close. I can share many things with him.

👉 দেখো, **একটা question থেকে 6টা question** তৈরি হলো।

এটাই তোমার speaking fluency তৈরির মূল technique।

---

# 13. 🎤 2-Minute Speaking Practice

এখন এই topic-এর উপর uninterrupted কথা বলার চেষ্টা করো:

### Topic:

**"My Family"**

এই order follow করবে:

**1. Family size**

↓

**2. Family members**

↓

**3. Parents**

↓

**4. Brothers/sisters**

↓

**5. Relationship**

↓

**6. What you do together**

↓

**7. Why your family is important**

### Example structure:

> There are ___ people in my family.
>
> They are ___.
>
> My father is ___.
>
> My mother is ___.
>
> I have ___ brothers and ___ sisters.
>
> I am particularly close to ___.
>
> We usually ___.
>
> My family is important to me because ___.

**নিজের information দিয়ে blanks পূরণ করবে।**

---

# 14. 🗣️ Conversation Practice

### Person A:

> How big is your family?

### Person B:

> There are five people in my family.

### A:

> Who are they?

### B:

> My parents, my two siblings, and me.

### A:

> Are you close to your family?

### B:

> Yes, we are very close.

### A:

> Who are you closest to?

### B:

> I am closest to my mother.

### A:

> Why?

### B:

> Because she understands me very well and always supports me.

### A:

> What do you usually do together?

### B:

> We usually have meals together and talk about our daily activities.

---

# 15. 🚀 Challenge — 10 Questions

এখন **কোনো answer দেখে না**, নিজে English-এ বলবে:

1. How many people are there in your family?
2. Who do you live with?
3. Do you have any brothers or sisters?
4. What does your father do?
5. What does your mother do?
6. Who are you closest to?
7. What is your family like?
8. How often do you spend time together?
9. What do you usually do with your family?
10. Why is your family important to you?

### Rule:

প্রতিটি answer:

**Beginner:** 1 sentence
**Intermediate:** 2–3 sentences
**Advanced:** 4–5 sentences

---

# 📝 Lesson 2 Homework

আজ ৩টি কাজ করবে:

### Task 1

**20 Questions-এর answer মুখে বলবে।**

### Task 2

**"My Family" topic-এ 2 মিনিট কথা বলবে।**

### Task 3

নিজে **5টা নতুন Family question** তৈরি করবে।

Example:

> What does your family usually do on weekends?

> How do you celebrate special occasions with your family?

---

## ⭐ Lesson 2-এর Golden Rule

Family সম্পর্কে শুধু তথ্য বলবে না।

**Information → Description → Relationship → Reason → Example**

যেমন:

> I have one brother.
> He is older than me.
> We are very close.
> He always gives me good advice.
> Whenever I have a problem, I usually discuss it with him.

এভাবে **একটি simple question-এর answer-কে 5 sentence পর্যন্ত expand করার অভ্যাস** করো। এটাই speaking fluency তৈরির সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ skill।

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 3

## Topic: Daily Routine

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য হলো **তোমার প্রতিদিনের কাজগুলো English-এ naturally বলা** এবং একটি question থেকে follow-up question তৈরি করা।

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের daily routine বলতে
* **Present Simple** ব্যবহার করতে
* Time-related question-এর উত্তর দিতে
* সকাল থেকে রাত পর্যন্ত sequence করে বলতে
* 2–3 মিনিট continuous speaking করতে

---

# 1. 🧠 প্রথমে Daily Routine-এর 20 Questions

### 🌅 Morning

1. **What time do you usually wake up?**
   তুমি সাধারণত কয়টায় ঘুম থেকে ওঠো?

2. **What do you do after waking up?**
   ঘুম থেকে ওঠার পর কী করো?

3. **Do you exercise in the morning?**
   তুমি কি সকালে ব্যায়াম করো?

4. **What do you usually have for breakfast?**
   তুমি সাধারণত breakfast-এ কী খাও?

5. **What time do you start studying or working?**
   তুমি কয়টায় পড়াশোনা বা কাজ শুরু করো?

---

### ☀️ Afternoon

6. **What do you usually do in the afternoon?**
   দুপুরে সাধারণত কী করো?

7. **Where do you usually have lunch?**
   সাধারণত কোথায় lunch করো?

8. **What time do you have lunch?**
   কয়টায় lunch করো?

9. **Do you take a break in the afternoon?**
   দুপুরে কি break নাও?

10. **What do you do after lunch?**
    lunch-এর পর কী করো?

---

### 🌆 Evening

11. **What do you usually do in the evening?**
    সন্ধ্যায় সাধারণত কী করো?

12. **Do you go outside in the evening?**
    সন্ধ্যায় কি বাইরে যাও?

13. **Do you study or work in the evening?**
    সন্ধ্যায় কি পড়াশোনা বা কাজ করো?

14. **How much time do you spend on your phone?**
    ফোনে কত সময় কাটাও?

15. **What do you usually do in your free time?**
    অবসর সময়ে কী করো?

---

### 🌙 Night

16. **What time do you have dinner?**
    কয়টায় dinner করো?

17. **What do you do after dinner?**
    dinner-এর পর কী করো?

18. **Do you watch TV or use your phone at night?**
    রাতে TV দেখো নাকি ফোন ব্যবহার করো?

19. **What time do you go to bed?**
    কয়টায় ঘুমাতে যাও?

20. **What do you usually do before going to bed?**
    ঘুমানোর আগে সাধারণত কী করো?

---

# 2. ⭐ খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ Grammar — Present Simple

Daily routine বলতে সাধারণত **Present Simple Tense** ব্যবহার করি।

### I / You / We / They

> I wake up at 7 AM.

> I have breakfast at 8 AM.

> I study English every day.

> I go to bed at 11 PM.

### He / She

এখানে verb-এর সাথে সাধারণত **s/es** যুক্ত হয়।

> He wakes up at 7 AM.

> She studies English every day.

> He goes to work at 9 AM.

---

# 3. ❌ Common Mistakes

### ভুল:

> I wakes up at 7.

### সঠিক:

> I wake up at 7.

---

### ভুল:

> I am wake up at 7.

### সঠিক:

> I wake up at 7.

---

### ভুল:

> I am go to university.

### সঠিক:

> I go to university.

---

### ভুল:

> I am study English every day.

### সঠিক:

> I study English every day.

---

# 4. ⏰ Time Expressions

Daily routine-এর জন্য এগুলো খুব useful:

| English          | বাংলা          |
| ---------------- | -------------- |
| Every day        | প্রতিদিন       |
| Every morning    | প্রতিদিন সকালে |
| Usually          | সাধারণত        |
| Normally         | সাধারণভাবে     |
| Sometimes        | মাঝে মাঝে      |
| Often            | প্রায়ই        |
| Rarely           | কদাচিৎ         |
| Never            | কখনো না        |
| In the morning   | সকালে          |
| In the afternoon | দুপুরে         |
| In the evening   | সন্ধ্যায়      |
| At night         | রাতে           |
| Before           | আগে            |
| After            | পরে            |
| Then             | তারপর          |
| Later            | পরে            |
| Finally          | শেষ পর্যন্ত    |

---

# 5. 🔥 Daily Routine Answer কীভাবে বড় করবে?

### Question:

> What do you do in the morning?

### ❌ Very Short:

> I study.

### 🟢 Better:

> I usually study in the morning. I study for about two hours.

### 🔥 Advanced:

> I usually spend my morning studying and working on my projects. I try to study for about two hours because I feel more focused in the morning.

---

# 6. 🧩 Sequence Words ব্যবহার করো

একটার পর একটা কাজ বলতে এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

**First → Then → After that → Next → Later → Finally**

### Example:

> First, I wake up at 7 AM.
> Then, I brush my teeth and wash my face.
> After that, I have breakfast.
> Next, I start studying.
> Later, I take a short break.
> Finally, I finish my work and relax.

এতে তোমার speaking অনেক বেশি **organized** শোনাবে।

---

# 7. 🎯 Morning Routine Practice

### Q: What do you do after waking up?

একটা natural answer:

> I usually wake up at around 7 AM. After waking up, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I drink some water and have breakfast. After that, I start studying or working on my projects.

### Follow-up Questions:

> What time do you wake up?

> Do you exercise in the morning?

> What do you eat for breakfast?

> Do you study in the morning?

> Why do you prefer studying in the morning?

---

# 8. ☀️ Afternoon Routine

### Q:

> What do you usually do in the afternoon?

### Answer:

> In the afternoon, I usually have lunch and take a short break. After that, I continue my studies or work on my projects. Sometimes I use my phone or check social media during my break.

### Follow-up:

> What time do you have lunch?

> What do you eat for lunch?

> How long is your break?

> What do you do after lunch?

---

# 9. 🌆 Evening Routine

### Q:

> What do you usually do in the evening?

### Answer:

> In the evening, I usually finish my work or studies. Sometimes I go outside for a walk. I also spend some time with my family and friends.

আরও বড়:

> In the evening, I usually finish my important tasks and take some time to relax. Sometimes I go outside for a walk or spend time with my family. If I have unfinished work, I complete it before dinner.

---

# 10. 🌙 Night Routine

### Q:

> What do you do before going to bed?

### Answer:

> Before going to bed, I usually check my phone, review what I learned during the day, and plan my tasks for the next day. Then I go to bed around 11 PM.

---

# 11. 🔥 Question Chain

এটা খুব ভালোভাবে practice করবে।

### Q1:

**What time do you wake up?**

> I usually wake up at 7 AM.

### Q2:

**What do you do after waking up?**

> I brush my teeth and wash my face.

### Q3:

**What do you do after that?**

> I have breakfast.

### Q4:

**What do you do after breakfast?**

> I start studying.

### Q5:

**What do you study?**

> I usually study English and programming.

### Q6:

**Why do you study English?**

> I study English because I want to improve my communication skills.

### Q7:

**How do you practice English?**

> I practice English by speaking, listening, reading, and learning new vocabulary.

👉 এখানে দেখো:

**Routine → Activity → Activity → Subject → Reason → Method**

এটাই conversation চালানোর technique।

---

# 12. 🧠 Useful Daily Routine Vocabulary

| Word/Phrase      | Meaning                |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| Wake up          | ঘুম থেকে ওঠা           |
| Get up           | বিছানা থেকে ওঠা        |
| Brush my teeth   | দাঁত ব্রাশ করা         |
| Wash my face     | মুখ ধোয়া              |
| Take a shower    | গোসল করা               |
| Get dressed      | পোশাক পরা              |
| Have breakfast   | নাস্তা করা             |
| Go to work       | কাজে যাওয়া            |
| Go to university | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ে যাওয়া |
| Study            | পড়াশোনা করা           |
| Work             | কাজ করা                |
| Take a break     | বিরতি নেওয়া           |
| Have lunch       | দুপুরের খাবার খাওয়া   |
| Relax            | বিশ্রাম নেওয়া         |
| Go for a walk    | হাঁটতে যাওয়া          |
| Have dinner      | রাতের খাবার খাওয়া     |
| Use my phone     | ফোন ব্যবহার করা        |
| Watch TV         | TV দেখা                |
| Read a book      | বই পড়া                |
| Go to bed        | ঘুমাতে যাওয়া          |

---

# 13. ⚠️ Important Difference

### Wake up

ঘুম ভাঙা:

> I wake up at 7 AM.

### Get up

বিছানা থেকে ওঠা:

> I get up at 7:05 AM.

তাই বলতে পারো:

> I wake up at 7 AM, but I get up five minutes later.

---

# 14. ⭐ Full Daily Routine

এখন একটি complete example:

> I usually wake up at around 7 AM. First, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I have breakfast. After breakfast, I start studying or working on my projects.
>
> In the afternoon, I have lunch and take a short break. After that, I continue my work. Sometimes I use my phone or check social media during my break.
>
> In the evening, I usually finish my important tasks. Sometimes I go outside for a walk or spend some time with my family. After dinner, I usually relax, use my phone, or watch something.
>
> Before going to bed, I review what I learned during the day and make a plan for the next day. Finally, I go to bed at around 11 PM.

**এটা মুখস্থ করবে না।** নিজের real routine দিয়ে বলবে।

---

# 15. 🎤 2-Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Daily Routine"**

এই structure follow করবে:

**Morning**

→ Wake up
→ Breakfast
→ Study/Work

**Afternoon**

→ Lunch
→ Break
→ Study/Work

**Evening**

→ Work/Study
→ Family/Friends
→ Free time

**Night**

→ Dinner
→ Phone/TV/Reading
→ Planning
→ Sleep

Target:

### প্রথমবার

30–45 seconds

### দ্বিতীয়বার

1–2 minutes

### তৃতীয়বার

2–3 minutes

**থেমে গেলেও Bengali-তে switch করবে না।** সহজ English ব্যবহার করে somehow কথা চালিয়ে যাবে।

---

# 16. 📝 Homework

### Task 1

উপরের 20টি question-এর answer **মুখে বলবে**।

### Task 2

**"My Daily Routine"** নিয়ে 2 মিনিট record করবে।

### Task 3

নিজে 5টি নতুন question তৈরি করবে।

Example:

> How much time do you spend studying every day?

> What part of your daily routine do you enjoy most?

> What part of your routine do you want to change?

> Do you follow the same routine every day?

> How does your routine change on weekends?

---

# ⭐ Lesson 3 Golden Rule

Daily routine বলতে শুধু:

> I wake up. I eat. I study. I sleep.

এভাবে বলবে না।

বরং:

**Time + Activity + Frequency + Reason**

উদাহরণ:

> I usually study English in the morning for about one hour because I can concentrate better at that time.

এই ধরনের sentence তৈরি করতে পারলে **Beginner speaking থেকে Intermediate speaking-এর দিকে এগোতে শুরু করবে।**

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 4

## Topic: My Home & My Room

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **তোমার বাড়ি, room, surroundings এবং সেখানে কী করো—এসব নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Today's Targets

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* বাড়ি সম্পর্কে প্রশ্ন করা
* নিজের room describe করা
* **There is / There are** ব্যবহার করা
* Location বলতে **in, on, under, beside, near, between** ব্যবহার করা
* Adjective দিয়ে কোনো জিনিস/জায়গা describe করা
* Answer থেকে follow-up question তৈরি করা

---

# 1. 🏠 20 Important Questions

### Basic Questions

1. **Where do you live?**
   তুমি কোথায় থাকো?

2. **Do you live in a house or an apartment?**
   তুমি বাড়ি নাকি apartment-এ থাকো?

3. **How many rooms are there in your home?**
   তোমার বাড়িতে কয়টি room আছে?

4. **Who do you live with?**
   তুমি কার সাথে থাকো?

5. **What is your favorite room?**
   তোমার favorite room কোনটি?

6. **What does your room look like?**
   তোমার room দেখতে কেমন?

7. **Is your room big or small?**
   তোমার room বড় নাকি ছোট?

8. **What furniture do you have in your room?**
   তোমার room-এ কী কী furniture আছে?

9. **Do you have a study table?**
   তোমার কি study table আছে?

10. **Where do you study?**
    তুমি কোথায় পড়াশোনা করো?

---

### Description Questions

11. **What is on your study table?**
    তোমার study table-এর ওপর কী আছে?

12. **Where is your bed?**
    তোমার bed কোথায়?

13. **Is there a window in your room?**
    তোমার room-এ কি জানালা আছে?

14. **What can you see from your window?**
    জানালা দিয়ে তুমি কী দেখতে পাও?

15. **Is your home near your university or workplace?**
    তোমার বাড়ি কি university বা workplace-এর কাছে?

16. **What do you usually do in your room?**
    তুমি সাধারণত room-এ কী করো?

17. **How often do you clean your room?**
    তুমি কত ঘন ঘন room পরিষ্কার করো?

18. **What do you like about your home?**
    তোমার বাড়ির কোন বিষয়টি তোমার ভালো লাগে?

19. **What would you like to change about your room?**
    তোমার room-এ কী পরিবর্তন করতে চাও?

20. **Would you like to live somewhere else in the future?**
    ভবিষ্যতে কি অন্য কোথাও থাকতে চাও?

---

# 2. ⭐ আজকের সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ Grammar

## There is / There are

কোনো জায়গায় কিছু **আছে** বলতে ব্যবহার করি।

### Singular → There is

> There is a bed in my room.

> There is a table near the window.

> There is a computer on my desk.

### Plural → There are

> There are two chairs in my room.

> There are many books on my table.

> There are three windows in our house.

---

# 3. ❌ Common Mistakes

### ভুল:

> There have a bed in my room.

### সঠিক:

> There is a bed in my room.

---

### ভুল:

> There is two chairs.

### সঠিক:

> There are two chairs.

---

### ভুল:

> There are a computer.

### সঠিক:

> There is a computer.

---

# 4. 📍 Location Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা     | Example                                 |
| ----------- | --------- | --------------------------------------- |
| In          | ভিতরে     | The books are in the bag.               |
| On          | উপরে      | The phone is on the table.              |
| Under       | নিচে      | The shoes are under the bed.            |
| Beside      | পাশে      | The table is beside the bed.            |
| Next to     | একদম পাশে | The chair is next to the desk.          |
| Near        | কাছে      | My house is near the market.            |
| Behind      | পেছনে     | The garden is behind the house.         |
| In front of | সামনে     | The car is in front of the house.       |
| Between     | মাঝখানে   | The chair is between the table and bed. |
| Opposite    | বিপরীতে   | The shop is opposite my house.          |

---

# 5. 🪑 Furniture Vocabulary

| Word       | Meaning     |
| ---------- | ----------- |
| Bed        | বিছানা      |
| Chair      | চেয়ার      |
| Table      | টেবিল       |
| Desk       | পড়ার টেবিল |
| Sofa       | সোফা        |
| Wardrobe   | আলমারি      |
| Bookshelf  | বইয়ের তাক  |
| Mirror     | আয়না       |
| Curtain    | পর্দা       |
| Carpet     | কার্পেট     |
| Fan        | পাখা        |
| Light      | বাতি        |
| Computer   | কম্পিউটার   |
| Laptop     | ল্যাপটপ     |
| Television | টেলিভিশন    |
| Drawer     | ড্রয়ার     |

---

# 6. 🎨 Room Describe করার Adjectives

### Size

* Big — বড়
* Small — ছোট
* Spacious — প্রশস্ত
* Narrow — সরু

### Appearance

* Clean — পরিষ্কার
* Dirty — নোংরা
* Tidy — গোছানো
* Messy — অগোছালো
* Comfortable — আরামদায়ক
* Bright — উজ্জ্বল
* Dark — অন্ধকার
* Quiet — শান্ত
* Noisy — কোলাহলপূর্ণ

---

# 7. 🟢 Simple Answer

### Q:

**What does your room look like?**

### Answer:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed, a desk, and a wardrobe in my room.

---

# 8. 🟡 Intermediate Answer

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed near the wall and a study table beside the window. I have a computer, some books, and a few other things on my desk. I usually study and work on my projects in this room.

---

# 9. 🔥 Advanced Answer

> My room is relatively small, but it is comfortable and well organized. There is a bed near the wall, and my study table is beside the window. I keep my laptop, books, and other study materials on the desk. I spend a significant amount of time in my room because I usually study, practice English, and work on my programming projects there.

---

# 10. 🧠 Answer Expansion Technique

### Question:

> Do you have a study table?

### Level 1:

> Yes, I do.

### Level 2:

> Yes, I have a study table in my room.

### Level 3:

> Yes, I have a study table beside the window. I usually use it for studying and working on my laptop.

### Level 4:

> Yes, I have a study table beside the window. I usually use it for studying, practicing English, and working on programming projects. I prefer studying there because the place is quiet and I can concentrate better.

👉 **একটি answer-কে 1 sentence → 2 sentences → 4 sentences** করার practice করবে।

---

# 11. 🔥 Follow-up Question Chain

### Q1:

**Where do you study?**

> I usually study in my room.

### Q2:

**Why do you study there?**

> Because it is quiet and comfortable.

### Q3:

**What do you have in your room?**

> I have a bed, a desk, a chair, a wardrobe, and a computer.

### Q4:

**What do you usually do there?**

> I usually study and work on my projects.

### Q5:

**How long do you spend there every day?**

> I spend around five or six hours there every day.

### Q6:

**Do you like your room?**

> Yes, I do. I feel comfortable and focused when I am there.

---

# 12. 🏠 Talking About Your Home

### Q:

**Tell me about your home.**

### Answer:

> I live in a comfortable home with my family. There are several rooms in our house, including bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and a bathroom. Our living room is where we usually sit and talk together. I like my home because it is comfortable and peaceful.

---

# 13. 🏙️ Talking About Your Neighborhood

এবার শুধু house না—**surrounding area** নিয়ে কথা বলবে।

### Questions:

1. Is your neighborhood quiet?
2. Is there a market near your home?
3. Are there any schools nearby?
4. Is there a park near your home?
5. How far is the nearest bus stop?
6. What do you like about your neighborhood?
7. Is your neighborhood crowded?
8. Do you know your neighbors?
9. Is it safe?
10. What would you like to change?

### Example:

> My neighborhood is quite busy, but it is convenient. There are several shops and restaurants near my home. There is also a market within walking distance. The main road is usually crowded, especially in the evening.

---

# 14. 🎤 2-Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Home and My Room"**

এই structure follow করো:

### 1. Location

> I live in...

### 2. Type

> I live in a house/apartment...

### 3. Family

> I live with...

### 4. Rooms

> There are...

### 5. Your room

> My room is...

### 6. Furniture

> There is/There are...

### 7. Activities

> I usually...

### 8. Opinion

> I like my room because...

### 9. Future

> In the future, I would like to...

---

# 15. 🗣️ Real Conversation

**A:** Where do you live?

**B:** I live in Dhaka.

**A:** Do you live in a house or an apartment?

**B:** I live in an apartment with my family.

**A:** How many rooms are there?

**B:** There are three bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and two bathrooms.

**A:** What is your favorite room?

**B:** My favorite room is my bedroom.

**A:** Why do you like it?

**B:** Because it's quiet and comfortable. I can study and work there without much distraction.

**A:** What do you have in your room?

**B:** I have a bed, a study table, a chair, a wardrobe, and a computer.

**A:** What do you usually do there?

**B:** I usually study English, practice programming, and work on my projects.

---

# 16. 🔥 Challenge Questions

এগুলো একটু difficult:

### 1.

**If you could redesign your room, what would you change?**

### 2.

**What would your dream home look like?**

### 3.

**Would you prefer to live in a big city or a quiet village? Why?**

### 4.

**What are the advantages of living in an apartment?**

### 5.

**What makes a house feel like a home?**

### Example:

> If I could redesign my room, I would make it more spacious and organized. I would add a larger study desk, a comfortable chair, better lighting, and more bookshelves. I would also create a small space for relaxation.

# 📝 Lesson 4 Homework

### Task 1

এই 20 questions-এর answer মুখে বলো।

### Task 2

**"My Home and My Room"** নিয়ে **2–3 মিনিট** কথা বলো।

### Task 3

নিজের room-এর দিকে তাকিয়ে **There is / There are** দিয়ে 10টি sentence বলো।

Example:

> There is a bed in my room.

> There is a laptop on my desk.

> There are some books on the table.

> There are two chairs in my room.

### Task 4

নিজে 5টি নতুন question তৈরি করো।

## ⭐ Lesson 4 Golden Rule

আজ থেকে কোনো place describe করার সময় এই formula ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Location + Size + Things + Position + Activity + Opinion**

Example:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a desk beside the window, and there are some books on it. I usually study and work on my computer there. I like my room because it is quiet and helps me concentrate.


# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 4

## Topic: My Home & My Room

এই Lesson-এ আমরা **question making + answering + follow-up conversation** বেশি practice করব। লক্ষ্য হলো কোনো place সম্পর্কে **সহজ থেকে বিস্তারিতভাবে English-এ বলা**।

---

## 🎯 Lesson 4 Goals

আজ তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের বাড়ি describe করতে
* নিজের room describe করতে
* **There is / There are** ব্যবহার করতে
* **in, on, under, beside, near, behind** ব্যবহার করতে
* কোনো জায়গা নিয়ে opinion দিতে
* একটি question থেকে ৫–৬টি follow-up question তৈরি করতে
* 2–3 মিনিট continuous speaking করতে

---

# Part 1 — 🏠 Basic Questions

প্রথমে এই 20টি question **জোরে জোরে পড়বে**।

### 1.

**Where do you live?**
তুমি কোথায় থাকো?

### 2.

**Do you live in a house or an apartment?**
তুমি বাড়িতে নাকি apartment-এ থাকো?

### 3.

**Who do you live with?**
তুমি কার সাথে থাকো?

### 4.

**How many rooms are there in your home?**
তোমার বাড়িতে কয়টি room আছে?

### 5.

**What is your favorite room?**
তোমার প্রিয় room কোনটি?

### 6.

**What does your room look like?**
তোমার room দেখতে কেমন?

### 7.

**Is your room big or small?**
তোমার room বড় নাকি ছোট?

### 8.

**Is your room clean and tidy?**
তোমার room কি পরিষ্কার ও গোছানো?

### 9.

**What furniture do you have in your room?**
তোমার room-এ কী কী furniture আছে?

### 10.

**Do you have a study table?**
তোমার কি study table আছে?

### 11.

**Where is your bed?**
তোমার bed কোথায়?

### 12.

**What is on your study table?**
তোমার study table-এর ওপর কী আছে?

### 13.

**Is there a window in your room?**
তোমার room-এ কি জানালা আছে?

### 14.

**What can you see through the window?**
জানালা দিয়ে তুমি কী দেখতে পাও?

### 15.

**Where do you usually study?**
তুমি সাধারণত কোথায় পড়াশোনা করো?

### 16.

**What do you usually do in your room?**
তুমি সাধারণত room-এ কী করো?

### 17.

**How often do you clean your room?**
তুমি কত ঘন ঘন room পরিষ্কার করো?

### 18.

**What do you like about your home?**
তোমার বাড়ির কোন বিষয়টি তোমার ভালো লাগে?

### 19.

**What would you like to change about your room?**
তোমার room-এ কী পরিবর্তন করতে চাও?

### 20.

**What would your dream home look like?**
তোমার স্বপ্নের বাড়ি কেমন হবে?

---

# Part 2 — ⭐ There is / There are

আজকের সবচেয়ে important grammar।

## একটি জিনিস → There is

> There is a bed in my room.

> There is a computer on my desk.

> There is a window beside my bed.

## একাধিক জিনিস → There are

> There are two chairs in my room.

> There are some books on my desk.

> There are three bedrooms in my house.

### মনে রাখবে:

**One → There is**

**More than one → There are**

---

# Part 3 — 📍 Position Vocabulary

কোনো object কোথায় আছে বলতে এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে।

| Word        | Meaning | Example                                |
| ----------- | ------- | -------------------------------------- |
| in          | ভিতরে   | The books are in the bag.              |
| on          | উপরে    | The phone is on the table.             |
| under       | নিচে    | The shoes are under the bed.           |
| beside      | পাশে    | The desk is beside the window.         |
| next to     | পাশে    | The chair is next to the desk.         |
| near        | কাছে    | My house is near the market.           |
| behind      | পেছনে   | The garden is behind the house.        |
| in front of | সামনে   | The car is in front of the house.      |
| between     | মাঝখানে | The chair is between the desk and bed. |
| opposite    | বিপরীতে | The shop is opposite my house.         |

---

# Part 4 — 🪑 Room Vocabulary

### Furniture

* Bed — বিছানা
* Desk — পড়ার টেবিল
* Chair — চেয়ার
* Sofa — সোফা
* Wardrobe — আলমারি
* Bookshelf — বইয়ের তাক
* Drawer — ড্রয়ার
* Mirror — আয়না
* Table — টেবিল

### Other things

* Laptop
* Computer
* Books
* Fan
* Light
* Curtain
* Clock
* Bag
* Clothes
* Shoes

# Part 5 — 🎨 Room Describe করার Words

### Size

**small**

> My room is small.

**large**

> My room is large.

**spacious**

> My room is spacious.

### Condition

**clean**

> My room is clean.

**tidy**

> My room is tidy.

**messy**

> My room is messy.

### Feeling

**comfortable**

> My room is comfortable.

**quiet**

> My room is quiet.

**peaceful**

> My room is peaceful.

---

# Part 6 — 🟢 Answer Expansion

এটা খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

### Question:

**What does your room look like?**

### Level 1:

> My room is small.

### Level 2:

> My room is small but comfortable.

### Level 3:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed and a study table in my room.

### Level 4:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed near the wall and a study table beside the window. I also have a computer and some books on my desk.

### Level 5:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed near the wall and a study table beside the window. I keep my laptop, books, and other study materials on my desk. I usually study English and work on programming projects in my room because it is quiet and helps me concentrate.

👉 **Speaking-এর জন্য এই expansion technique খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।**

---

# Part 7 — 🔥 Follow-up Question Chain

একটি question থেকে conversation কীভাবে বড় হয় দেখো।

### Q1

**Where do you study?**

> I usually study in my room.

### Q2

**Why do you study there?**

> Because it is quiet and comfortable.

### Q3

**What do you have in your room?**

> I have a bed, a study table, a chair, a wardrobe, and a computer.

### Q4

**What do you usually do there?**

> I usually study English and work on my programming projects.

### Q5

**How much time do you spend there?**

> I spend about five or six hours there every day.

### Q6

**Do you like your room?**

> Yes, I do. I like it because it is quiet and comfortable.

এভাবে:

**Question → Answer → Follow-up → Answer → Follow-up**

এটাই real conversation-এর foundation।

---

# Part 8 — 🏠 Talk About Your Home

### Question:

**Tell me about your home.**

### Simple Answer:

> I live in a small house with my family. There are several rooms in our house. We have bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and bathrooms.

### Better Answer:

> I live in a comfortable house with my family. There are several rooms in our house, including bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and bathrooms. We usually spend time together in the living room. I like my home because it is peaceful and comfortable.

---

# Part 9 — 🏙️ Talk About Your Neighborhood

এবার শুধু বাড়ি নয়, বাড়ির surroundings নিয়েও কথা বলবে।

### Questions:

1. Is your neighborhood quiet?
2. Is there a market near your home?
3. Are there any restaurants nearby?
4. Is there a park near your home?
5. Is your neighborhood crowded?
6. Do you know your neighbors?
7. Is your area safe?
8. How far is the nearest bus stop?
9. What do you like about your neighborhood?
10. What would you like to change?

### Example:

> My neighborhood is quite busy, but it is convenient. There are several shops, restaurants, and markets near my home. There is also a bus stop nearby. The area can be crowded in the evening, but overall I like living there.

---

এখন আমরা নিজেরাই question তৈরি করব।

Topic:

**My Room**

### What

> What is in your room?

### Where

> Where is your bed?

### Why

> Why do you like your room?

> How often do you clean your room?

### How many

> How many books do you have?

### How much

> How much time do you spend in your room?

> What kind of furniture do you have?

### What would

> What would you change about your room?

### Would

> Would you like to have a bigger room?

### What do you think

> What do you think makes a room comfortable?

এগুলোতে 4–6 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**What makes a house feel like a home?**

### 2.

**Would you prefer a big house or a small comfortable house? Why?**

**Would you rather live in a city or a village? Why?**

**What would your ideal home look like?**

**If you had unlimited money, what would you change about your home?**

# Part 12 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking

Topic:

## **My Home and My Room**

এই order-এ বলবে:

**1. Location**

> I live in...

↓

**2. Type of home**

> I live in a house/apartment...

↓

**3. Family**

> I live with...

↓

**4. Rooms**

> There are...

↓

**5. Your room**

> My room is...

↓

**6. Furniture**

> There is... / There are...

↓

**7. Activities**

> I usually...

↓

**8. Opinion**

> I like my room because...

↓

**9. Future**

> In the future, I would like to...

---

# Part 13 — 🔥 Full Example

> I live in Dhaka with my family. We live in a comfortable apartment. There are several rooms in our apartment, including bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and bathrooms.
>
> My favorite place is my room. It is not very large, but it is comfortable and peaceful. There is a bed near the wall, and there is a study table beside the window. I keep my laptop, books, and other study materials on my desk.
>
> I usually spend several hours in my room every day. I study English, practice programming, and work on my projects there. I like my room because it is quiet and helps me concentrate.
>
> In the future, I would like to have a larger room with a bigger study desk, a comfortable chair, good lighting, and a small bookshelf.

**⚠️ এটি মুখস্থ করবে না।** নিজের real information দিয়ে বলবে।

> There is a bed in my room.

> There is a laptop on my desk.

# ⭐ Lesson 4 Golden Formula

কোনো **place** নিয়ে কথা বলার সময়:

> **Location → Size → Things → Position → Activity → Opinion → Future**

উদাহরণ:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a desk beside the window, and there are some books on it. I usually study and work on my computer there. I like my room because it is quiet. In the future, I would like to make it more spacious and organized.

### 🔥 আজকের Speaking Challenge

**2 মিনিটের জন্য "My Home and My Room" বলো।**

**লক্ষ্য:** Grammar perfect হওয়া নয়—**না থেমে English-এ কথা চালিয়ে যাওয়া।**

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 5

## Topic: Food & Eating Habits 🍚

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **খাবার, পছন্দ-অপছন্দ, cooking, eating habits এবং restaurant conversation** নিয়ে English-এ কথা বলা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* খাবার নিয়ে 20+ প্রশ্ন করা
* নিজের favorite food সম্পর্কে বলা
* **like / love / prefer / dislike** ব্যবহার করা
* খাবারের taste describe করা
* **How often / How much / How many / Why** দিয়ে question তৈরি করা
* Restaurant-এর basic conversation করা
* Food topic-এ 2–3 মিনিট কথা বলা

---

# Part 1 — 🍽️ 20 Basic Questions

### Favorite Food

1. **What is your favorite food?**
   তোমার প্রিয় খাবার কী?

2. **What is your favorite fruit?**
   তোমার প্রিয় ফল কী?

3. **What is your favorite drink?**
   তোমার প্রিয় পানীয় কী?

4. **What food do you like the most?**
   কোন খাবার তুমি সবচেয়ে বেশি পছন্দ করো?

5. **What food do you dislike?**
   কোন খাবার তুমি পছন্দ করো না?

---

### Eating Habits

6. **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**
   সাধারণত breakfast-এ কী খাও?

7. **What do you usually eat for lunch?**
   সাধারণত lunch-এ কী খাও?

8. **What do you usually eat for dinner?**
   সাধারণত dinner-এ কী খাও?

9. **How many meals do you have every day?**
   প্রতিদিন কয়বার খাবার খাও?

10. **How often do you eat outside?**
    কত ঘন ঘন বাইরে খাও?

---

### Cooking

11. **Can you cook?**
    তুমি কি রান্না করতে পারো?

12. **What can you cook?**
    তুমি কী রান্না করতে পারো?

13. **Who usually cooks in your family?**
    তোমাদের পরিবারে সাধারণত কে রান্না করেন?

14. **Do you enjoy cooking?**
    তুমি কি রান্না করতে উপভোগ করো?

15. **What would you like to learn to cook?**
    ভবিষ্যতে কী রান্না শিখতে চাও?

---

### Opinion

16. **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**
    বাড়ির খাবার নাকি restaurant-এর খাবার বেশি পছন্দ?

17. **Do you think healthy food is important?**
    তুমি কি মনে করো healthy food গুরুত্বপূর্ণ?

18. **Why do people like fast food?**
    মানুষ fast food কেন পছন্দ করে?

19. **What is a healthy diet?**
    Healthy diet কী?

20. **What food would you recommend to a foreigner visiting Bangladesh?**
    বাংলাদেশে আসা একজন বিদেশিকে কোন খাবার recommend করবে?

---

# Part 2 — ⭐ Like / Love / Prefer / Dislike

খাবার নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় এগুলো খুব বেশি ব্যবহার করবে।

### Like

> I like biryani.

### Love

> I love biryani.

**Love = Like-এর চেয়ে stronger**

### Prefer

দুইটা জিনিসের মধ্যে কোনটা বেশি পছন্দ বোঝাতে:

> I prefer homemade food to fast food.

### Dislike

> I dislike very spicy food.

### Don't like

> I don't like bitter food.

---

# Part 3 — 🔥 Answer Expansion

### Q:

**What is your favorite food?**

### Beginner:

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite food is biryani. I really enjoy its taste and smell.

### Advanced:

> My favorite food is biryani. I really enjoy it because it is flavorful, aromatic, and filling. I especially like having it with salad and a cold drink. I usually eat it on special occasions or when I go to a good restaurant.

---

# Part 4 — 🍛 Food Taste Vocabulary

| English    | বাংলা        |
| ---------- | ------------ |
| Sweet      | মিষ্টি       |
| Sour       | টক           |
| Salty      | লবণাক্ত      |
| Spicy      | ঝাল          |
| Bitter     | তিতা         |
| Delicious  | সুস্বাদু     |
| Tasty      | সুস্বাদু     |
| Fresh      | তাজা         |
| Hot        | গরম          |
| Cold       | ঠান্ডা       |
| Crispy     | মচমচে        |
| Soft       | নরম          |
| Juicy      | রসালো        |
| Healthy    | স্বাস্থ্যকর  |
| Unhealthy  | অস্বাস্থ্যকর |
| Nutritious | পুষ্টিকর     |
| Flavorful  | স্বাদযুক্ত   |
| Aromatic   | সুগন্ধযুক্ত  |

---

# Part 5 — 🧠 Useful Food Sentences

### Taste

> It tastes delicious.

> It is very spicy.

> It is a little salty.

> It tastes sweet and sour.

### Preference

> I really like it.

> I don't like it very much.

> I prefer rice to bread.

> I prefer homemade food to fast food.

### Frequency

> I eat it every day.

> I eat it occasionally.

> I rarely eat fast food.

> I usually eat outside on weekends.

---

# Part 6 — 🍳 Cooking Conversation

### Q:

**Can you cook?**

### Short:

> Yes, I can cook.

### Better:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes. I can prepare rice, eggs, noodles, and some basic Bengali dishes. I am not an expert cook, but I enjoy cooking when I have enough time.

---

### Follow-up Questions

> What can you cook?

> Who taught you to cook?

> How often do you cook?

> What is your favorite dish to cook?

> What dish do you want to learn?

---

# Part 7 — 🏠 Homemade Food vs Restaurant Food

### Question:

**Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**

### Answer:

> I prefer homemade food because it is usually healthier and cleaner. I also like the taste of homemade food. However, I enjoy eating at restaurants occasionally, especially when I go out with friends.

### Useful Structure:

**Preference → Reason → Example → Opposite side**

> I prefer ___ because ___. For example, ___. However, ___.

---

# Part 8 — 🍔 Fast Food

### Question:

**How often do you eat fast food?**

### Answer:

> I don't eat fast food very often. I usually eat it once or twice a month. I enjoy fast food occasionally, but I don't want to eat it regularly because it may not be healthy.

### Follow-up:

> What is your favorite fast food?

> Why do you like it?

> Do you think fast food is healthy?

> What happens if people eat too much fast food?

---

# Part 9 — 🔥 Question Chain

এখন একটা question থেকে conversation তৈরি করি।

### Q1

**What is your favorite food?**

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Q2

**Why do you like it?**

> Because it is delicious and flavorful.

### Q3

**How often do you eat it?**

> I usually eat it once or twice a month.

### Q4

**Where do you usually eat it?**

> I usually eat it at home or at a restaurant.

### Q5

**Who do you usually eat it with?**

> I usually eat it with my family or friends.

### Q6

**Can you cook it?**

> No, I can't cook it very well yet.

### Q7

**Would you like to learn?**

> Yes, I would. I would like to learn how to cook it properly.

👉 দেখো, একটি question থেকে **7-question conversation** তৈরি হলো।

---

# Part 10 — 🍽️ Restaurant Conversation

এবার real-life speaking practice।

### Customer:

> Excuse me, can I see the menu, please?

### Waiter:

> Sure. Here you are.

### Customer:

> What do you recommend?

### Waiter:

> I recommend our special biryani.

### Customer:

> Is it spicy?

### Waiter:

> Yes, it is moderately spicy.

### Customer:

> Okay. I'll have one biryani, please.

### Waiter:

> Would you like anything to drink?

### Customer:

> Yes, I'd like a bottle of water.

---

# Part 11 — ⭐ Restaurant Useful Expressions

### Ordering

> I'd like to order...

> Can I have...?

> I'll have...

> Could I get...?

### Asking

> What do you recommend?

> Is this spicy?

> Is this vegetarian?

> How much does this cost?

> What ingredients are in this dish?

### Problem

> Excuse me, I didn't order this.

> Could you please change this?

> This food is too spicy.

> Could I have some more water?

### Paying

> Could I have the bill, please?

> Can I pay by card?

> How much is the total?

---

# Part 12 — 🧠 Important Grammar

## Countable vs Uncountable

### Countable

যেগুলো গোনা যায়:

> an apple
> two apples
> three eggs
> five bananas

### Uncountable

সাধারণত গোনা যায় না:

> rice
> water
> milk
> sugar
> salt

তাই:

❌ two waters

সাধারণভাবে:

> two bottles of water

---

# Part 13 — How Much / How Many

### How many → Countable

> How many eggs do you eat?

> How many apples do you have?

### How much → Uncountable

> How much water do you drink?

> How much rice do you eat?

---

# Part 14 — 🔥 Advanced Food Questions

এগুলোতে 4–6 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**Why do people enjoy eating food from restaurants?**

### 2.

**Do you think traditional food is better than fast food? Why?**

### 3.

**What can people do to develop healthy eating habits?**

### 4.

**Why do some people eat unhealthy food even though they know it is harmful?**

### 5.

**If you could introduce one Bangladeshi dish to the world, what would it be and why?**

---

# Part 15 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Food Habits"**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Favorite food

> My favorite food is...

### 2. Why

> I like it because...

### 3. Daily meals

> For breakfast...

> For lunch...

> For dinner...

### 4. Eating outside

> I usually eat outside...

### 5. Cooking

> I can/can't cook...

### 6. Healthy food

> I think healthy food is important because...

### 7. Future

> I want to improve my eating habits by...

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 Full Example

> My favorite food is biryani because I really enjoy its rich flavor and aroma. I usually eat it once or twice a month, especially on special occasions or when I go to a restaurant with my family or friends.
>
> For breakfast, I usually eat something light. For lunch, I usually eat rice with vegetables, fish, meat, or other dishes. For dinner, I prefer something relatively light.
>
> I prefer homemade food because it is usually healthier and more hygienic. However, I also enjoy eating at restaurants occasionally. I don't eat fast food very often because I know that eating too much unhealthy food can be bad for our health.
>
> I can cook a few simple dishes, but I would like to learn how to cook more dishes in the future. I also want to develop healthier eating habits.

**⚠️ এটা মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের real food habits দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

**"My Food Habits"** নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট কথা বলবে।

যেমন:

> There is some rice in the kitchen.

> I usually eat breakfast at 8 AM.

নিজে 5টি নতুন food-related question তৈরি করবে।

→ How often do you eat it?

→ Where do you usually eat it?

# ⭐ Lesson 5 Golden Formula

Food topic-এ answer দেওয়ার সময়:

> **Food → Taste → Reason → Frequency → Place → Person → Opinion**

Example:

> I really like biryani. It is flavorful and aromatic. I like it because I enjoy spicy food. I usually eat it once or twice a month. I often eat it with my family or friends. Although I enjoy it, I don't think it should be eaten too frequently.
