# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 6

## Topic: Hobbies & Free Time 🎮📚🎵

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **তোমার hobbies, interests এবং free time activities নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলা** এবং একটা answer থেকে natural follow-up conversation তৈরি করা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Hobby সম্পর্কে 20টি practical question
* **like / enjoy / love / prefer / interested in** ব্যবহার
* কোনো hobby কেন পছন্দ করো তা explain করা
* **How often / How long / When / Where / Who** দিয়ে question তৈরি করা
* Free time সম্পর্কে কথা বলা
* Hobby নিয়ে opinion দেওয়া
* 2–3 মিনিট continuous speaking করা

---

# Part 1 — 🎯 20 Questions About Hobbies

### Basic Questions

1. **What are your hobbies?**
   তোমার শখ কী কী?

2. **What do you like to do in your free time?**
   অবসর সময়ে কী করতে পছন্দ করো?

3. **What is your favorite hobby?**
   তোমার প্রিয় hobby কী?

4. **Why do you like this hobby?**
   তুমি এই hobby কেন পছন্দ করো?

5. **When did you start this hobby?**
   তুমি কবে থেকে এই hobby শুরু করেছ?

6. **How often do you do it?**
   তুমি কত ঘন ঘন এটি করো?

7. **How much time do you spend on it?**
   এটাতে কত সময় দাও?

8. **Where do you usually do it?**
   সাধারণত কোথায় এটি করো?

9. **Do you enjoy doing it alone or with others?**
   একা নাকি অন্যদের সাথে করতে বেশি ভালো লাগে?

10. **Who introduced you to this hobby?**
    কে তোমাকে এই hobby-এর সাথে পরিচয় করিয়ে দিয়েছিল?

---

### More Speaking Questions

11. **What hobby would you like to learn?**
    কোন hobby শিখতে চাও?

12. **Do you have any indoor hobbies?**
    তোমার কি indoor hobby আছে?

13. **Do you have any outdoor hobbies?**
    তোমার কি outdoor hobby আছে?

14. **Do you prefer reading books or watching movies?**
    বই পড়া নাকি movie দেখা বেশি পছন্দ?

15. **Do you like listening to music?**
    তুমি কি গান শুনতে পছন্দ করো?

16. **Do you enjoy playing games?**
    তুমি কি game খেলতে উপভোগ করো?

17. **Do you like traveling?**
    তুমি কি ভ্রমণ করতে পছন্দ করো?

18. **What do you usually do on weekends?**
    Weekend-এ সাধারণত কী করো?

19. **Has your hobby changed over time?**
    সময়ের সাথে তোমার hobby কি পরিবর্তিত হয়েছে?

20. **Do you think hobbies are important? Why?**
    তুমি কি মনে করো hobbies গুরুত্বপূর্ণ? কেন?

---

# Part 2 — ⭐ Like / Enjoy / Love

Hobby নিয়ে কথা বলার সময় এই structures খুব important।

### Like + noun

> I like books.

> I like movies.

### Like + verb-ing

> I like reading books.

> I like watching movies.

> I like playing games.

---

### Enjoy + verb-ing

**Enjoy-এর পরে সাধারণত verb + ing হবে।**

> I enjoy reading.

> I enjoy traveling.

> I enjoy learning new things.

❌ I enjoy to read.

✅ I enjoy reading.

---

### Love + verb-ing

> I love listening to music.

> I love playing football.

---

# Part 3 — 🎮 Common Hobbies Vocabulary

| Hobby              | Example                         |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------- |
| Reading            | I enjoy reading books.          |
| Watching movies    | I like watching movies.         |
| Listening to music | I love listening to music.      |
| Playing games      | I enjoy playing games.          |
| Traveling          | I like traveling.               |
| Photography        | I am interested in photography. |
| Cooking            | I enjoy cooking.                |
| Gardening          | I like gardening.               |
| Drawing            | I enjoy drawing.                |
| Writing            | I like writing.                 |
| Swimming           | I enjoy swimming.               |
| Cycling            | I like cycling.                 |
| Playing sports     | I enjoy playing sports.         |
| Programming        | I enjoy programming.            |
| Learning languages | I like learning languages.      |

---

# Part 4 — 🔥 Answer Expansion

### Q:

**What are your hobbies?**

### Beginner:

> My hobbies are reading and programming.

### Intermediate:

> My hobbies are reading, programming, and learning new things. I usually do these activities in my free time.

### Advanced:

> I have several hobbies, including reading, programming, and learning new technologies. I especially enjoy programming because I like solving problems and building useful things. I also spend some of my free time reading because it helps me learn new ideas.

---

# Part 5 — 🧠 Why Do You Like Your Hobby?

### Question:

**Why do you like programming?**

### Formula:

**Hobby → Reason → Example → Benefit**

### Answer:

> I like programming because I enjoy solving problems. For example, I like building websites and APIs. It also helps me improve my logical thinking and technical skills.

---

# Part 6 — ⏰ How Often?

### Question:

**How often do you read books?**

Useful answers:

> I read every day.

> I usually read three or four times a week.

> I read occasionally.

> I rarely read books.

> I don't read very often.

---

# Part 7 — ⏱️ How Long?

### Question:

**How long do you spend on your hobbies?**

### Answer:

> I usually spend about one or two hours on my hobbies every day.

আরও natural:

> It depends on how busy I am. On weekdays, I usually spend about one hour on my hobbies, but I have more free time on weekends.

---

# Part 8 — ⭐ Hobby vs Interest

এদের difference বুঝে ব্যবহার করো।

### Hobby

যে activity তুমি regular basis-এ করো:

> Reading is one of my hobbies.

### Interest

যে বিষয়ের প্রতি তোমার আগ্রহ আছে:

> I am interested in artificial intelligence.

তুমি বলতে পারো:

> Programming is one of my hobbies, and I am also interested in artificial intelligence.

---

# Part 9 — 🔥 Follow-up Question Chain

### Q1:

**What is your favorite hobby?**

> My favorite hobby is programming.

### Q2:

**Why do you like programming?**

> Because I enjoy solving problems.

### Q3:

**When did you start programming?**

> I started programming when I was a university student.

### Q4:

**What do you usually build?**

> I usually build web applications and APIs.

### Q5:

**What technologies do you use?**

> I mainly use Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and databases.

### Q6:

**How much time do you spend programming?**

> It depends on my schedule, but I usually spend a few hours practicing or working on projects.

### Q7:

**Do you want to make programming your career?**

> Yes. I want to build my career as a software developer.

👉 এটাই **conversation expansion**।

---

# Part 10 — 📚 Reading as a Hobby

### Question:

**Do you like reading books?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I enjoy reading books. I especially like books about technology, personal development, and career development. Reading helps me learn new things and improve my knowledge.

### Follow-up:

> What kind of books do you read?

> Who is your favorite author?

> How often do you read?

> Where do you usually read?

> Do you prefer physical books or e-books?

---

# Part 11 — 🎬 Movies

### Questions:

1. Do you like watching movies?
2. What kind of movies do you like?
3. What is your favorite movie?
4. Who is your favorite actor?
5. How often do you watch movies?
6. Do you watch movies alone or with friends?
7. Do you prefer movies or TV series?
8. What was the last movie you watched?

### Example:

> I enjoy watching movies in my free time. I especially like action, science-fiction, and comedy movies. I usually watch movies on weekends when I have more free time.

---

# Part 12 — 🎵 Music

### Questions:

1. Do you like listening to music?
2. What kind of music do you like?
3. Who is your favorite singer?
4. When do you usually listen to music?
5. Does music help you relax?
6. Do you listen to music while studying?

### Example:

> Yes, I enjoy listening to music. I usually listen to music when I am relaxing or traveling. Music helps me feel relaxed and improves my mood.

---

# Part 13 — ✈️ Traveling

### Question:

**Do you like traveling?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling. I like visiting new places and experiencing different environments. Traveling also gives me an opportunity to learn about different cultures and people.

### Follow-up:

> Where have you traveled?

> Where would you like to go?

> Who do you like traveling with?

> What is your favorite place?

> Why do you like traveling?

---

# Part 14 — ⭐ Opinion Question

### Q:

**Do you think hobbies are important?**

Use:

**Opinion → Reason 1 → Reason 2 → Example → Conclusion**

### Answer:

> Yes, I think hobbies are very important. They help us relax and reduce stress. They also give us an opportunity to learn new skills and develop our interests. For example, reading can improve our knowledge, while sports can improve our physical health. So, I believe everyone should have at least one or two hobbies.

---

# Part 15 — 🧠 Useful Expressions

### Giving a reason

> I like it because...

> The main reason is that...

> One reason I enjoy it is...

### Talking about frequency

> I usually...

> I often...

> I sometimes...

> I rarely...

> I occasionally...

### Giving an example

> For example...

> For instance...

> Such as...

### Giving an opinion

> I think...

> In my opinion...

> From my point of view...

> I believe...

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন 4–6 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**Why do people need hobbies?**

### 2.

**Can hobbies become careers?**

### 3.

**What is the difference between a hobby and a passion?**

### 4.

**Do people have enough free time nowadays?**

### 5.

**Are digital hobbies better than traditional hobbies?**

### 6.

**What hobby would you choose if you had unlimited free time?**

---

# Part 17 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Hobbies and Free Time"**

এই structure ব্যবহার করবে:

### 1. Introduction

> I have several hobbies...

### 2. Main hobby

> My favorite hobby is...

### 3. Reason

> I like it because...

### 4. Frequency

> I usually do it...

### 5. Time

> I spend about...

### 6. Other hobbies

> Apart from that, I also...

### 7. Benefits

> It helps me...

### 8. Future

> In the future, I would like to learn...

---

# Part 18 — 🔥 Full Example

> I have several hobbies, but my favorite ones are programming, reading, and learning new things. I especially enjoy programming because I like solving problems and creating useful applications. I usually spend a few hours practicing programming or working on my projects when I have free time.
>
> I also enjoy reading books, especially books about technology and personal development. Reading helps me learn new ideas and improve my knowledge. Apart from these activities, I like listening to music and watching movies when I want to relax.
>
> I think hobbies are important because they help us reduce stress and make our free time more meaningful. In the future, I would also like to learn photography and improve my communication skills.

**⚠️ মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের hobby দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# 📝 Lesson 6 Homework

### Task 1

20টি question-এর answer **মুখে বলবে**।

### Task 2

**"My Hobbies and Free Time"** নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট record করবে।

### Task 3

নিজের hobby নিয়ে **10টি sentence** বলবে।

### Task 4

একটি hobby নিয়ে এই 6টি question-এর answer দেবে:

1. What is your hobby?
2. Why do you like it?
3. When did you start it?
4. How often do you do it?
5. How much time do you spend on it?
6. How does it help you?

---

# ⭐ Lesson 6 Golden Formula

Hobby সম্পর্কে কথা বলার সময়:

> **Hobby → Why → When started → How often → How long → Benefit → Future**

Example:

> Programming is one of my favorite hobbies. I like it because I enjoy solving problems. I started programming at university. I practice it almost every day for a few hours. It helps me improve my technical and problem-solving skills. In the future, I want to turn this interest into a successful career.

### 🎯 Today's Target

একটা simple question:

> **What is your hobby?**

এর answer যেন শুধু **“My hobby is reading.”** না হয়।

বরং **4–6 sentence naturally বলতে পারো**—এটাই Lesson 6-এর success.

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 7

## Topic: Friends & Friendship 🤝

আজকের লক্ষ্য হলো **বন্ধু, friendship, best friend, social interaction এবং বন্ধুত্বের qualities** নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট English-এ কথা বলতে পারা।

---

# 🎯 Today's Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Friend সম্পর্কে 20টি practical question
* একজন বন্ধুকে describe করা
* **Who / What / Why / How / How often** দিয়ে question তৈরি করা
* Personality describe করা
* Friendship নিয়ে opinion দেওয়া
* **because / but / although / however** দিয়ে answer বড় করা
* Follow-up question করে conversation চালানো

---

# Part 1 — 🤝 20 Questions About Friends

### Basic Questions

1. **Do you have many friends?**
   তোমার কি অনেক বন্ধু আছে?

2. **Who is your best friend?**
   তোমার সবচেয়ে ভালো বন্ধু কে?

3. **How did you meet your best friend?**
   তোমার best friend-এর সাথে কীভাবে পরিচয় হয়েছিল?

4. **How long have you known your best friend?**
   কতদিন ধরে তোমার best friend-কে চেনো?

5. **Where did you meet?**
   কোথায় তোমাদের দেখা/পরিচয় হয়েছিল?

6. **What is your best friend like?**
   তোমার best friend কেমন?

7. **What do you like about your best friend?**
   তোমার best friend-এর কোন বিষয়টি তোমার ভালো লাগে?

8. **What do you usually do together?**
   তোমরা সাধারণত একসাথে কী করো?

9. **How often do you meet your friends?**
   কত ঘন ঘন বন্ধুদের সাথে দেখা করো?

10. **Do you talk to your friends every day?**
    প্রতিদিন কি বন্ধুদের সাথে কথা বলো?

---

### Deeper Questions

11. **What qualities do you look for in a friend?**
    বন্ধুর মধ্যে কী কী গুণ খোঁজো?

12. **What makes someone a good friend?**
    কী একজন মানুষকে ভালো বন্ধু বানায়?

13. **Do you prefer having many friends or a few close friends?**
    অনেক বন্ধু নাকি কয়েকজন ঘনিষ্ঠ বন্ধু বেশি পছন্দ?

14. **Have you ever had a misunderstanding with a friend?**
    কখনো কি বন্ধুর সাথে ভুল বোঝাবুঝি হয়েছে?

15. **How do you solve problems with your friends?**
    বন্ধুদের সাথে সমস্যা হলে কীভাবে সমাধান করো?

16. **Do you trust your friends?**
    তুমি কি তোমার বন্ধুদের বিশ্বাস করো?

17. **How do your friends help you?**
    তোমার বন্ধুরা কীভাবে তোমাকে সাহায্য করে?

18. **How do you help your friends?**
    তুমি কীভাবে তোমার বন্ধুদের সাহায্য করো?

19. **What activities do you enjoy doing with your friends?**
    বন্ধুদের সাথে কী করতে ভালো লাগে?

20. **Why is friendship important?**
    Friendship কেন গুরুত্বপূর্ণ?

---

# Part 2 — 🧠 Friend Describe করার Vocabulary

একজন বন্ধুর personality describe করতে এই words ব্যবহার করবে।

| Word        | বাংলা            |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| Friendly    | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ    |
| Kind        | দয়ালু           |
| Honest      | সৎ               |
| Helpful     | সাহায্যকারী      |
| Funny       | মজার             |
| Responsible | দায়িত্বশীল      |
| Caring      | যত্নশীল          |
| Loyal       | বিশ্বস্ত         |
| Patient     | ধৈর্যশীল         |
| Generous    | উদার             |
| Hardworking | পরিশ্রমী         |
| Intelligent | বুদ্ধিমান        |
| Calm        | শান্ত            |
| Confident   | আত্মবিশ্বাসী     |
| Supportive  | সহযোগিতাপূর্ণ    |
| Reliable    | নির্ভরযোগ্য      |
| Talkative   | বেশি কথা বলে এমন |
| Quiet       | চুপচাপ           |
| Humble      | বিনয়ী           |
| Positive    | ইতিবাচক          |

---

# Part 3 — ⭐ "What is your friend like?"

এখানে একটা গুরুত্বপূর্ণ difference আছে।

### ❌ What does your friend like?

এর অর্থ:

> তোমার বন্ধু কী পছন্দ করে?

### ✅ What is your friend like?

এর অর্থ:

> তোমার বন্ধু কেমন মানুষ?

Example:

> My best friend is friendly, honest, and helpful. He is also very hardworking and reliable.

---

# Part 4 — 🔥 Answer Expansion

### Q:

**Who is your best friend?**

### Beginner:

> My best friend is Rahim.

### Intermediate:

> My best friend is Rahim. We studied together at university, and we have known each other for several years.

### Advanced:

> My best friend is Rahim. We met at university and gradually became close friends. He is a friendly, honest, and hardworking person. I especially appreciate him because he is always supportive when I face difficulties.

---

# Part 5 — 🧩 How to Describe a Friend

এই formula follow করবে:

> **Who → Where met → How long → Personality → Why like → Activities**

Example:

> My best friend is Rahim. We met at university several years ago. He is friendly, honest, and hardworking. I like him because he is very supportive and always gives me good advice. We usually talk about our studies, careers, and future plans.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Follow-up Question Chain

### Q1:

**Who is your best friend?**

> My best friend is Rahim.

### Q2:

**How did you meet him?**

> We met at university.

### Q3:

**How long have you known him?**

> I have known him for about five years.

### Q4:

**What is he like?**

> He is friendly, honest, and helpful.

### Q5:

**Why do you like him?**

> Because he is supportive and reliable.

### Q6:

**What do you usually do together?**

> We usually talk, eat together, and sometimes go outside.

### Q7:

**How often do you meet?**

> We usually meet once or twice a week.

### Q8:

**Do you trust him?**

> Yes, I trust him because he has always been honest with me.

👉 একটি topic থেকে **8-question conversation** তৈরি হয়ে গেল।

---

# Part 7 — ⏰ Important Grammar: "Have Known"

### Question:

> How long have you known your best friend?

এখানে **Present Perfect** ব্যবহার হয়েছে।

### Structure:

**have/has + past participle**

> I have known him for five years.

> I have known her since 2021.

### For

সময়ের duration:

> for five years

> for two months

> for a long time

### Since

কখন থেকে:

> since 2021

> since university

> since childhood

### Difference:

> I have known him **for five years.**

= পাঁচ বছর ধরে চিনি।

> I have known him **since 2021.**

= 2021 সাল থেকে চিনি।

---

# Part 8 — 👥 Many Friends vs Close Friends

### Question:

**Do you prefer having many friends or a few close friends?**

### Answer:

> I prefer having a few close friends rather than many friends. I think close friends understand us better and support us during difficult times. I don't think the number of friends is as important as the quality of the friendship.

### Useful phrase:

> **Quality is more important than quantity.**

অর্থ:

> সংখ্যার চেয়ে মান বেশি গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

---

# Part 9 — 💬 Friendship Problems

### Question:

**Have you ever had a misunderstanding with a friend?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have. Sometimes misunderstandings happen because of poor communication. When I have a problem with a friend, I prefer talking about it calmly instead of arguing. I think honest communication can solve most friendship problems.

### Useful words:

* misunderstanding — ভুল বোঝাবুঝি
* argument — তর্ক
* communication — যোগাযোগ
* apologize — ক্ষমা চাওয়া
* forgive — ক্ষমা করা
* trust — বিশ্বাস
* respect — সম্মান

---

# Part 10 — ⭐ Giving Opinion

### Question:

**What makes someone a good friend?**

Answer structure:

**Opinion → Qualities → Example → Conclusion**

### Example:

> I think a good friend should be honest, supportive, and trustworthy. A good friend should be able to listen to us and give us advice when necessary. For example, when we face a difficult situation, a good friend should support us instead of judging us. In my opinion, trust and respect are the most important parts of a strong friendship.

---

# Part 11 — 🔥 Useful Connectors

আজ থেকে answer-এ connector ব্যবহার করা শুরু করবে।

### Because

> I like him because he is honest.

### But

> He is quiet, but he is very friendly.

### Although

> Although we are busy, we try to stay in touch.

### However

> We don't meet often. However, we talk online regularly.

### Also

> He is intelligent. He is also very hardworking.

### That's why

> He always supports me. That's why I trust him.

---

# Part 12 — 📱 Staying in Touch

আজকের modern conversation-এর জন্য খুব useful।

### Questions:

> How do you keep in touch with your friends?

> Do you talk to your friends online?

> How often do you call them?

### Answer:

> We usually keep in touch through phone calls and messaging apps. We don't always have time to meet in person, so we often communicate online. However, we try to meet whenever we have free time.

---

# Part 13 — 🎯 Friendship Activities

Useful expressions:

> hang out with friends
> spend time together
> go out together
> watch movies
> play games
> have dinner
> study together
> work on projects
> discuss our plans
> go for a walk
> travel together

### Example:

> I usually hang out with my friends on weekends. We sometimes have dinner together, watch movies, or simply sit somewhere and talk.

---

# Part 14 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন একটু challenging।

### 1.

**What is more important in friendship: trust or honesty? Why?**

### 2.

**Can people be good friends even if they rarely meet?**

### 3.

**How has social media changed friendship?**

### 4.

**What should you do if your friend makes a serious mistake?**

### 5.

**Can a friend become like a family member? Why?**

### 6.

**What is the difference between a friend and a best friend?**

---

# Part 15 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Best Friend"**

এই structure ব্যবহার করবে:

### 1. Introduction

> My best friend's name is...

### 2. Meeting

> We met...

### 3. Time

> I have known him/her for...

### 4. Personality

> He/She is...

### 5. Why you like them

> I like him/her because...

### 6. Activities

> We usually...

### 7. Communication

> We usually keep in touch by...

### 8. Opinion

> I think a good friend should...

### 9. Conclusion

> That's why I value our friendship.

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 Full Example

> My best friend's name is Rahim. We met at university several years ago, and we gradually became close friends. I have known him for about five years.
>
> He is a friendly, honest, hardworking, and supportive person. I especially like him because he is reliable and always tries to help me when I have a problem. We can talk openly about our studies, careers, and personal matters.
>
> We usually spend time together when we are free. Sometimes we go out for food, watch movies, or simply sit somewhere and talk. When we cannot meet, we keep in touch through phone calls and messaging apps.
>
> In my opinion, trust, honesty, respect, and support are the most important qualities in a friendship. I don't think having many friends is necessary. I would rather have a few close friends whom I can truly trust.

**⚠️ এটাও মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের friend-এর information দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# 📝 Lesson 7 Homework

### Task 1

উপরের **20টি question-এর answer** মুখে বলবে।

### Task 2

**"My Best Friend"** নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট record করবে।

### Task 3

একজন friend-এর personality describe করতে **10টি adjective** ব্যবহার করবে।

Example:

> He is friendly.
> He is honest.
> He is hardworking.
> He is helpful.

### Task 4

নিজে **5টি নতুন friendship question** তৈরি করবে।

### Task 5 — 🔥 Conversation Chain

নিজের সাথে এই conversation practice করবে:

> Who is your best friend?

→ How did you meet?

→ How long have you known him/her?

→ What is he/she like?

→ Why do you like him/her?

→ What do you usually do together?

→ How often do you meet?

→ How do you keep in touch?

→ What makes a good friend?

---

# ⭐ Lesson 7 Golden Formula

বন্ধু সম্পর্কে কথা বলার সময়:

> **Who → How met → How long → Personality → Why → Activities → Communication → Opinion**

আর একটা গুরুত্বপূর্ণ rule:

**শুধু "My friend is good" বলবে না।**

বরং:

> My friend is very supportive **because** he always helps me when I have a problem. **Although** we are sometimes busy, we try to stay in touch. **That's why** I really value our friendship.

### 🎯 Today's Target

**"Who is your best friend?"** প্রশ্নের উত্তর যেন **30 seconds–1 minute** naturally বলতে পারো এবং answer-এর পরে অন্তত **5টি follow-up question** চালিয়ে যেতে পারো।

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 8

## Topic: Daily Routine & Everyday Activities ⏰

আজকের lesson-এর মূল লক্ষ্য হলো **তোমার প্রতিদিনের কাজ English-এ ধারাবাহিকভাবে বলা**। এই topic speaking-এর জন্য খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ, কারণ everyday conversation-এ daily routine-এর vocabulary এবং **Present Simple** অনেক বেশি ব্যবহার হয়।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 8 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Daily routine নিয়ে 25টি question
* **Present Simple** দিয়ে দৈনন্দিন কাজ বলা
* **always / usually / often / sometimes / rarely / never** ব্যবহার
* Time বলার practice
* Morning → Afternoon → Evening → Night sequence
* **How often / What time / When / Why** দিয়ে question তৈরি
* 2–3 মিনিট নিজের daily routine বলা
* Follow-up conversation করা

---

# Part 1 — ⏰ 25 Daily Routine Questions

## 🌅 Morning

1. **What time do you usually wake up?**
   তুমি সাধারণত কয়টায় ঘুম থেকে ওঠো?

2. **What do you do after waking up?**
   ঘুম থেকে ওঠার পর কী করো?

3. **Do you exercise in the morning?**
   সকালে কি exercise করো?

4. **What time do you have breakfast?**
   কয়টায় breakfast করো?

5. **What do you usually have for breakfast?**
   breakfast-এ সাধারণত কী খাও?

6. **Do you take a shower in the morning?**
   সকালে কি গোসল করো?

---

## ☀️ Afternoon

7. **What do you usually do in the morning?**

8. **What time do you start studying or working?**

9. **Where do you usually study or work?**

10. **What time do you have lunch?**

11. **What do you usually do after lunch?**

12. **Do you take a break in the afternoon?**

---

## 🌆 Evening

13. **What do you usually do in the evening?**

14. **Do you go outside in the evening?**

15. **Do you spend time with your family?**

16. **Do you exercise in the evening?**

17. **How do you relax after a busy day?**

---

## 🌙 Night

18. **What time do you have dinner?**

19. **What do you usually do after dinner?**

20. **Do you use your phone before going to bed?**

21. **Do you read before sleeping?**

22. **What time do you usually go to bed?**

---

## 🔥 General

23. **How busy are you during the day?**

24. **What is the busiest part of your day?**

25. **What would you like to change about your daily routine?**

---

# Part 2 — ⭐ Present Simple

Daily routine-এর জন্য **Present Simple** খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

### I / You / We / They

> I wake up at 7 AM.

> I have breakfast at 8 AM.

> I study English every day.

> I go to bed at 11 PM.

### He / She

এখানে verb-এ সাধারণত **s/es** যোগ হয়।

> He wakes up at 7 AM.

> She studies English every day.

> He goes to work at 9 AM.

---

# Part 3 — ❌ Common Mistakes

### ভুল:

> I wakes up at 7 AM.

### সঠিক:

> I wake up at 7 AM.

---

### ভুল:

> I goes to university.

### সঠিক:

> I go to university.

---

### ভুল:

> He wake up early.

### সঠিক:

> He wakes up early.

---

### ভুল:

> She go to work.

### সঠিক:

> She goes to work.

---

# Part 4 — 🔄 Daily Routine Vocabulary

| English          | বাংলা                  |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| Wake up          | ঘুম থেকে ওঠা           |
| Get up           | বিছানা থেকে ওঠা        |
| Brush my teeth   | দাঁত ব্রাশ করা         |
| Wash my face     | মুখ ধোয়া              |
| Take a shower    | গোসল করা               |
| Get dressed      | পোশাক পরা              |
| Have breakfast   | নাশতা করা              |
| Go to university | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ে যাওয়া |
| Go to work       | কাজে যাওয়া            |
| Study            | পড়াশোনা করা           |
| Attend class     | ক্লাস করা              |
| Have lunch       | দুপুরের খাবার খাওয়া   |
| Take a break     | বিরতি নেওয়া           |
| Come home        | বাড়িতে আসা            |
| Relax            | বিশ্রাম নেওয়া         |
| Exercise         | ব্যায়াম করা           |
| Have dinner      | রাতের খাবার খাওয়া     |
| Read             | পড়া                   |
| Practice         | অনুশীলন করা            |
| Go to bed        | ঘুমাতে যাওয়া          |
| Fall asleep      | ঘুমিয়ে পড়া           |

---

# Part 5 — ⏰ Time Expressions

### Exact time

> I wake up **at 7 AM**.

> I have lunch **at 1 PM**.

### Morning

> in the morning

### Afternoon

> in the afternoon

### Evening

> in the evening

### Night

> at night

### Days

> on Monday

> on weekends

### General time

> every day

> every morning

> every evening

---

# Part 6 — 📊 Frequency Adverbs

এগুলো speaking-এ খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

| Word         | Meaning   |
| ------------ | --------- |
| Always       | সবসময়    |
| Usually      | সাধারণত   |
| Often        | প্রায়ই   |
| Sometimes    | মাঝে মাঝে |
| Occasionally | মাঝে মাঝে |
| Rarely       | খুব কম    |
| Never        | কখনো না   |

### Examples:

> I **always** brush my teeth in the morning.

> I **usually** have breakfast at 8 AM.

> I **often** exercise in the evening.

> I **sometimes** watch movies at night.

> I **rarely** eat outside.

> I **never** skip breakfast.

---

# Part 7 — 🔥 Answer Expansion

### Q:

**What time do you usually wake up?**

### Beginner:

> I wake up at 7 AM.

### Intermediate:

> I usually wake up at 7 AM. Then I brush my teeth and wash my face.

### Advanced:

> I usually wake up at around 7 AM. After getting out of bed, I brush my teeth, wash my face, and take a shower. Then I have breakfast and prepare for the rest of the day.

---

# Part 8 — 🌅 Morning Routine

### Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 AM. First, I get out of bed and brush my teeth. Then I wash my face and take a shower. After that, I have breakfast. Finally, I get ready for my work or studies.

### Sequence Words

আজ থেকে এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

> **First** → প্রথমে
> **Then** → তারপর
> **After that** → এরপর
> **Next** → পরবর্তী ধাপে
> **Finally** → সবশেষে

Example:

> First, I wake up.
> Then, I brush my teeth.
> After that, I have breakfast.
> Next, I start studying.
> Finally, I go to bed at night.

---

# Part 9 — ☀️ Afternoon Routine

### Question:

**What do you usually do in the afternoon?**

### Answer:

> In the afternoon, I usually have lunch around 1 PM. After lunch, I take a short break. Then I continue studying or working. If I have some free time, I check my phone or talk to my friends.

---

# Part 10 — 🌆 Evening Routine

### Question:

**What do you usually do in the evening?**

### Answer:

> In the evening, I usually finish my work or studies. Then I take a short break. Sometimes I go outside for a walk or exercise. After that, I come home and spend some time with my family.

---

# Part 11 — 🌙 Night Routine

### Question:

**What do you usually do before going to bed?**

### Answer:

> After dinner, I usually spend some time on my phone or computer. I sometimes read or practice English. Before going to bed, I prepare my plans for the next day. I usually go to bed around 11 PM.

---

# Part 12 — 🔥 Follow-up Conversation

### Q1:

**What time do you wake up?**

> I usually wake up at 7 AM.

### Q2:

**What do you do after waking up?**

> I brush my teeth, wash my face, and take a shower.

### Q3:

**What time do you have breakfast?**

> I usually have breakfast at around 8 AM.

### Q4:

**What do you do after breakfast?**

> I start studying or working.

### Q5:

**What do you do in the evening?**

> I usually relax, exercise, or spend time with my family.

### Q6:

**What time do you go to bed?**

> I usually go to bed around 11 PM.

### Q7:

**Are you satisfied with your daily routine?**

> It is mostly good, but I would like to manage my time better.

---

# Part 13 — 🧠 Question Making Practice

একই topic থেকে different question তৈরি করা শিখো।

### What time

> What time do you wake up?

### What

> What do you do after breakfast?

### When

> When do you study English?

### Where

> Where do you usually study?

### How often

> How often do you exercise?

### How long

> How long do you study every day?

### Why

> Why do you wake up early?

### Who

> Who do you usually have dinner with?

### How

> How do you spend your evening?

---

# Part 14 — ⭐ How Long vs How Often

এখানে অনেকেই ভুল করে।

### How often = কত ঘন ঘন

> How often do you exercise?

> I exercise five days a week.

### How long = কতক্ষণ

> How long do you exercise?

> I exercise for 30 minutes.

### Difference:

**How often?**

> Five days a week.

**How long?**

> For 30 minutes.

---

# Part 15 — 🔥 Good Routine vs Bad Routine

### Question:

**What would you like to change about your daily routine?**

### Answer:

> I would like to improve my daily routine. I sometimes spend too much time on my phone, so I want to reduce my screen time. I also want to wake up earlier and exercise regularly. In addition, I want to spend more time practicing English.

---

# Part 16 — 🧠 Opinion Practice

### Question:

**Why is having a daily routine important?**

### Answer:

> I think having a daily routine is important because it helps us manage our time effectively. It also helps us stay organized and focused. When we have a clear routine, we can balance work, study, exercise, and relaxation more easily.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন 4–6 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**What is the most productive part of your day? Why?**

### 2.

**What is the biggest problem with your current routine?**

### 3.

**How can people manage their time more effectively?**

### 4.

**Do you think waking up early makes people more productive?**

### 5.

**How has technology changed your daily routine?**

### 6.

**What would your ideal daily routine look like?**

---

# Part 18 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Daily Routine"**

এই sequence follow করবে:

### Morning

> I usually wake up at...

> First, I...

> Then, I...

### Afternoon

> In the afternoon, I...

### Evening

> In the evening, I...

### Night

> At night, I...

### Opinion

> I like my routine because...

### Improvement

> However, I would like to...

---

# Part 19 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I usually wake up at around 7 AM. First, I get out of bed, brush my teeth, and wash my face. Then I take a shower and have breakfast. After breakfast, I start studying or working.
>
> In the afternoon, I usually have lunch around 1 PM. After lunch, I take a short break and then continue my work. If I have some free time, I check my phone or talk to my friends.
>
> In the evening, I usually finish my work and relax for a while. Sometimes I go outside for a walk or do some exercise. I also spend some time with my family.
>
> At night, I have dinner and then practice English or work on my projects. Before going to bed, I usually plan what I need to do the next day. I normally go to bed around 11 PM.
>
> Overall, I think my routine is quite good, but I want to manage my time better. I especially want to reduce my screen time and spend more time learning English.

**⚠️ এটা মুখস্থ করার দরকার নেই। নিজের actual routine দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# 📝 Lesson 8 Homework

### Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer **মুখে বলবে**:

1. What time do you wake up?
2. What do you do after waking up?
3. What do you have for breakfast?
4. When do you start studying or working?
5. What do you do after lunch?
6. What do you do in the evening?
7. How often do you exercise?
8. What do you do after dinner?
9. What time do you go to bed?
10. What would you like to change about your routine?

### Task 2

**"My Daily Routine"** নিয়ে **2–3 মিনিট voice recording** করবে।

### Task 3

নিজের daily routine নিয়ে **10টি sentence** লিখবে।

### Task 4 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি Daily Routine questions** তৈরি করবে।

Example:

> What time do you wake up?

> Where do you study?

> How often do you exercise?

> How long do you study?

---

# ⭐ Lesson 8 Golden Formula

Daily routine বলার সময়:

> **Time → Action → Sequence → Frequency → Reason → Opinion → Improvement**

Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 AM. First, I brush my teeth and take a shower. Then I have breakfast and start studying. I usually study for several hours during the day. In the evening, I relax and spend time with my family. I like my routine, but I want to manage my time better.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে তুমি যেন **না থেমে 2–3 মিনিট নিজের Daily Routine English-এ বলতে পারো।**

**সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ:** প্রতিটি answer এক sentence-এ শেষ করবে না।
**Answer → Why → Example → More information** দিয়ে answer বড় করবে।
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 9

## Topic: Study, Education & Learning 📚🎓

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **school, college, university, subjects, studying, learning methods, exams, future education** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের বিশেষ লক্ষ্য: **কোনো প্রশ্নের শুধু short answer না দিয়ে 4–6 sentence-এ explain করা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 9 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Education নিয়ে 25টি practical question
* নিজের study সম্পর্কে কথা বলা
* **Present Simple + Present Continuous** ব্যবহার
* **study / learn / practice**-এর difference
* **good at / interested in / difficult for me** ব্যবহার
* কারণ ও example দিয়ে answer expand করা
* Study-related conversation চালানো
* 2–3 মিনিট **"My Education & Study Habits"** বলা

---

# Part 1 — 🎓 25 Important Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **What are you studying?**
   তুমি কী পড়াশোনা করছ?

2. **What did you study at university?**
   University-তে কী পড়েছিলে?

3. **Where did you study?**
   কোথায় পড়াশোনা করেছিলে?

4. **What is your favorite subject?**
   তোমার প্রিয় subject কোনটি?

5. **Why do you like that subject?**
   ওই subject কেন পছন্দ?

6. **Which subject is difficult for you?**
   কোন subject তোমার জন্য কঠিন?

7. **What subjects are you good at?**
   কোন subject-এ তুমি ভালো?

8. **How many hours do you study every day?**
   প্রতিদিন কত ঘণ্টা পড়াশোনা করো?

9. **When do you usually study?**
   সাধারণত কখন পড়াশোনা করো?

10. **Where do you usually study?**
    সাধারণত কোথায় পড়াশোনা করো?

---

## Learning Habits

11. **How do you prepare for an exam?**

12. **Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**

13. **Do you study better in the morning or at night?**

14. **How do you improve your English?**

15. **How often do you practice English speaking?**

16. **What do you do when you don't understand something?**

17. **Do you take notes while studying?**

18. **Do you use the internet for learning?**

19. **What is your biggest problem when studying?**

20. **How do you stay focused while studying?**

---

## Future & Opinion

21. **What skills do you want to learn?**

22. **What do you want to study in the future?**

23. **Why is education important?**

24. **Do you prefer online learning or classroom learning?**

25. **What makes someone a good student?**

---

# Part 2 — ⭐ Study vs Learn vs Practice

এই তিনটি word খুব important।

## Study

কোনো subject নিয়ে পড়াশোনা করা।

> I study English.

> I study computer science.

> I study mathematics.

---

## Learn

নতুন কিছু শেখা বা knowledge অর্জন করা।

> I am learning English.

> I learned Python last year.

> I want to learn Spanish.

---

## Practice

যা শিখেছো সেটা বারবার করে skill improve করা।

> I practice English every day.

> I practice speaking English.

> I practice programming regularly.

### সহজভাবে:

**Study → Learn → Practice**

> I study English to learn the language, and I practice speaking to improve my communication skills.

---

# Part 3 — 🧠 Present Simple vs Present Continuous

### Present Simple

Regular activity:

> I study English every day.

> I practice programming regularly.

### Present Continuous

এখন/বর্তমানে চলছে:

> I am studying English now.

> I am learning React these days.

### Difference:

> I study English every day.

= প্রতিদিন English পড়ি।

> I am studying English now.

= এখন English পড়ছি।

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ Useful Study Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা          |
| ----------- | -------------- |
| Subject     | বিষয়          |
| Course      | কোর্স          |
| Class       | ক্লাস          |
| Lecture     | বক্তৃতা/লেকচার |
| Assignment  | অ্যাসাইনমেন্ট  |
| Exam        | পরীক্ষা        |
| Test        | পরীক্ষা        |
| Grade       | গ্রেড          |
| Result      | ফলাফল          |
| Degree      | ডিগ্রি         |
| University  | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয় |
| Teacher     | শিক্ষক         |
| Student     | শিক্ষার্থী     |
| Notes       | নোট            |
| Book        | বই             |
| Research    | গবেষণা         |
| Skill       | দক্ষতা         |
| Knowledge   | জ্ঞান          |
| Homework    | বাড়ির কাজ     |
| Scholarship | বৃত্তি         |

---

# Part 5 — 🎯 Talking About Your Education

### Question:

**What did you study at university?**

### Beginner:

> I studied computer science.

### Intermediate:

> I studied computer science at university. I learned programming, databases, networking, and software development.

### Advanced:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. During my studies, I learned subjects such as programming, databases, computer networks, operating systems, and software engineering. I was particularly interested in web development and backend development, so I spent additional time working on practical projects.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Answer Expansion

### Q:

**What is your favorite subject?**

### Level 1:

> My favorite subject is programming.

### Level 2:

> My favorite subject is programming because I enjoy solving problems.

### Level 3:

> My favorite subject is programming because I enjoy solving problems and building applications.

### Level 4:

> My favorite subject is programming because I enjoy solving problems and building applications. It also helps me improve my logical thinking and creativity.

### Level 5:

> My favorite subject is programming because I enjoy solving problems and building practical applications. I like the fact that programming requires both logical thinking and creativity. Whenever I build something successfully, I feel motivated to learn more.

---

# Part 7 — 📖 How Do You Study?

### Question:

**How do you prepare for an exam?**

### Answer:

> First, I review the syllabus and organize the topics. Then I study the difficult topics first. I take notes and practice questions regularly. Before the exam, I review the important topics and solve previous questions.

### Useful sequence:

> First → Then → After that → Finally

---

# Part 8 — 🧑‍💻 How Do You Learn English?

### Question:

**How do you improve your English?**

### Answer:

> I try to improve my English by practicing every day. I learn new vocabulary, read English articles, listen to English conversations, and practice speaking. I also try to make questions and answer them in English. My main goal is to become more confident when speaking.

---

# Part 9 — 🔥 Follow-up Conversation

### Q1:

**What are you studying?**

> I am studying computer science.

### Q2:

**Why did you choose it?**

> Because I have always been interested in technology and problem-solving.

### Q3:

**What is your favorite subject?**

> Programming is one of my favorite subjects.

### Q4:

**Why do you like programming?**

> I enjoy building things and solving problems.

### Q5:

**How do you practice?**

> I practice by building small projects and solving programming problems.

### Q6:

**What skill do you want to improve?**

> I want to improve my English communication and technical skills.

### Q7:

**What do you want to do in the future?**

> I want to build a career in software development.

👉 একটি topic থেকে **7-question natural conversation**।

---

# Part 10 — 👤 Studying Alone vs With Friends

### Question:

**Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**

### Answer:

> I prefer studying alone because I can concentrate better. When I study alone, I can choose my own pace and spend more time on difficult topics. However, studying with friends can also be useful when I need to discuss a difficult problem.

### Useful structure:

> **I prefer X because... However, Y can also be useful because...**

---

# Part 11 — 💻 Online Learning vs Classroom Learning

### Question:

**Do you prefer online learning or classroom learning?**

### Answer:

> I prefer online learning for many subjects because it is flexible and convenient. I can learn at my own pace and review lessons whenever I want. However, classroom learning has an advantage because students can interact directly with teachers and classmates. So, I think both methods are useful depending on the situation.

---

# Part 12 — ❗ When You Don't Understand

এই expressionsগুলো real conversation-এ খুব useful।

### Teacher-এর কাছে:

> Could you explain that again, please?

> Could you explain it in a simpler way?

> I didn't understand this part.

> Could you give me an example?

> What does this word mean?

> Could you please repeat that?

> Could you speak a little more slowly?

---

# Part 13 — 🧠 Good Student

### Question:

**What makes someone a good student?**

### Answer:

> I think a good student should be disciplined, curious, and consistent. A good student doesn't only memorize information but also tries to understand the concepts. They ask questions when they don't understand something. Most importantly, they practice regularly and learn from their mistakes.

---

# Part 14 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন একটু challenging questions:

### 1.

**Is education more important than practical skills? Why?**

### 2.

**Can someone become successful without a university degree?**

### 3.

**What is more important: knowledge or experience?**

### 4.

**How has technology changed education?**

### 5.

**What are the advantages and disadvantages of online education?**

### 6.

**Should students focus more on exams or practical skills?**

---

# Part 15 — ⭐ Opinion Structure

যখন কোনো education-related opinion question আসবে:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Opposite side → Conclusion**

Example:

> In my opinion, practical skills are extremely important. They help students apply their theoretical knowledge in real situations. For example, a programming student can learn much more by building a real application than by only reading textbooks. However, theoretical knowledge is also necessary because it provides the foundation. Therefore, I think students should develop both theoretical knowledge and practical skills.

---

# Part 16 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

Topic:

## **"My Education and Study Habits"**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Education

> I studied...

### 2. University

> I studied at...

### 3. Favorite subject

> My favorite subject is...

### 4. Reason

> I like it because...

### 5. Study habit

> I usually study...

### 6. Learning method

> I learn by...

### 7. Difficulties

> One challenge I face is...

### 8. Improvement

> I am trying to improve...

### 9. Future

> In the future, I want to...

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. During my studies, I learned programming, databases, computer networks, operating systems, and software engineering. Among these subjects, I particularly enjoyed programming because I like solving problems and building practical applications.
>
> I usually study in a quiet place where I can concentrate. When I learn something new, I first try to understand the basic concept. Then I practice it by solving problems or building small projects. If I don't understand something, I search for explanations and try to practice again.
>
> I also spend time improving my English because communication is important for both education and career development. I practice vocabulary, reading, listening, and especially speaking.
>
> In the future, I want to continue learning new technologies and improve my practical skills. I believe that education gives us knowledge, but continuous practice and real-world experience help us become better professionals.

**⚠️ এটাও মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের education অনুযায়ী বলবে।**

---

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What are you studying?
2. What is your favorite subject?
3. Why do you like it?
4. What subject is difficult for you?
5. How many hours do you study?
6. How do you prepare for exams?

9. What skill do you want to learn?

### Task 2 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি education-related question** তৈরি করবে।

Example:

Try to use:

* although

* therefore
* in my opinion

---

# ⭐ Lesson 9 Golden Formula

Education topic-এ answer দেওয়ার সময়:

> **What → Where → Why → How → Difficulty → Improvement → Future**

Example:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. I chose this field because I was interested in technology. I especially enjoy programming because I like solving problems. I improve my skills by practicing regularly and working on projects. Although some topics are difficult, I try to understand them through practice. In the future, I want to continue learning and build a successful career in software development.

## 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন এই question-এর উত্তর **কমপক্ষে 1–2 মিনিট** বলতে পারো:

> **"Tell me about your education and how you study."**

**Rule:** মুখস্থ নয় → **নিজের information + নিজের words + continuous speaking.**

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 10

## Topic: Work, Job & Career 💼

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **job, work, career, workplace, skills, responsibilities, salary, future career goals** নিয়ে speaking practice করব।

এটা খুব important topic, কারণ **job interview, professional conversation, self-introduction, networking**—সব জায়গায় এই vocabulary কাজে লাগবে।

---

# 🎯 Lesson 10 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Work & Career নিয়ে 25টি important question
* নিজের job/career সম্পর্কে বলা
* **work / job / career**-এর difference
* নিজের skills describe করা
* **I work as / I work in / I am responsible for** ব্যবহার
* Interview-style questions answer করা
* Career goals নিয়ে কথা বলা

# Part 1 — 💼 Work & Career: 25 Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **What do you do?**
   তুমি কী করো?

2. **Where do you work?**
   তুমি কোথায় কাজ করো?

3. **What is your job?**
   তোমার চাকরি কী?

4. **What do you do for a living?**
   জীবিকার জন্য কী করো?

5. **Do you work or study?**
   তুমি কাজ করো নাকি পড়াশোনা করো?

6. **What kind of work do you do?**
   কী ধরনের কাজ করো?

7. **How long have you been working in this field?**
   এই field-এ কতদিন ধরে কাজ করছ?

8. **What do you like about your work?**
   তোমার কাজের কোন বিষয়টি ভালো লাগে?

9. **What is the most difficult part of your job?**
   তোমার কাজের সবচেয়ে কঠিন অংশ কী?

10. **What are your main responsibilities?**
    তোমার প্রধান দায়িত্বগুলো কী?

---

# Part 2 — 🧑‍💻 Skills & Workplace

11. **What skills do you have?**

12. **What are your strongest skills?**

13. **What skill would you like to improve?**

14. **Do you enjoy working with a team?**

15. **Do you prefer working alone or with a team?**

16. **What kind of work environment do you prefer?**

17. **What makes someone a good employee?**

18. **What makes someone a good manager?**

19. **How do you handle pressure at work?**

20. **How do you manage your time at work?**

# Part 3 — 🚀 Career & Future

21. **What are your career goals?**

22. **What kind of job would you like in the future?**

23. **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

24. **What skills do you need to achieve your career goals?**

25. **Why did you choose your career?**

# Part 4 — ⭐ Work vs Job vs Career

এগুলো খুব important।

## Work

**Work = কাজ**

> I have a lot of work today.

> I work from home.

---

## Job

**Job = নির্দিষ্ট চাকরি/position**

> I have a job as a software developer.

> I am looking for a new job.

---

## Career

**Career = দীর্ঘমেয়াদি পেশাগত পথ**

> I want to build a career in software development.

### সহজভাবে:

> **Work = কাজ**

> **Job = চাকরি**

> **Career = দীর্ঘমেয়াদি পেশা**

---

# Part 5 — 🗣️ "What do you do?"

এটা খুব common question।

### Q:

> **What do you do?**

### Answer 1:

> I am a software developer.

### Answer 2:

> I work as a backend developer.

### Answer 3:

> I work as a software developer, mainly focusing on backend development and APIs.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Important Professional Structures

## 1. I work as...

> I work as a backend developer.

> I work as a software engineer.

---

## 2. I work in...

Field/industry বোঝাতে:

> I work in software development.

> I work in the technology industry.

---

## 3. I am responsible for...

Responsibility বলতে:

> I am responsible for developing APIs.

> I am responsible for maintaining the backend.

> I am responsible for fixing bugs.

---

## 4. I specialize in...

কোনো particular area-তে expertise বোঝাতে:

> I specialize in backend development.

> I specialize in Django and REST APIs.

---

# Part 7 — 🧑‍💻 Describe Your Work

### Question:

**What do you do at work?**

### Beginner:

> I work as a developer.

### Intermediate:

> I work as a backend developer. I develop APIs and work with databases.

### Advanced:

> I work as a backend developer, where I mainly develop REST APIs and work with databases. I also fix bugs, test APIs, review code, and collaborate with frontend developers to integrate the backend with the frontend.

---

# Part 8 — 🛠️ Skills Vocabulary

### Technical Skills

* Programming
* Problem-solving
* Database management
* API development
* Web development
* Debugging
* Testing
* Git
* Communication
* Teamwork

### Soft Skills

* Communication
* Leadership
* Time management
* Problem-solving
* Adaptability
* Teamwork
* Creativity
* Critical thinking
* Decision-making
* Responsibility

---

# Part 9 — ⭐ Describe Your Strengths

### Question:

**What are your strongest skills?**

### Beginner:

> My strongest skills are programming and problem-solving.

### Intermediate:

> My strongest skills are programming, problem-solving, and teamwork. I am also good at learning new technologies.

### Advanced:

> My strongest skills are problem-solving, backend development, and learning new technologies quickly. I am comfortable working with Python and Django, and I enjoy solving technical problems. I also try to communicate clearly and work effectively with team members.

---

# Part 10 — ❗ Talk About Weakness

Interview-এ common question:

> **What is your weakness?**

Avoid:

❌ "I don't have any weaknesses."

Better:

> One area I am working on is public speaking. Sometimes I feel nervous when speaking in front of a large group, so I am practicing regularly to become more confident.

### Formula:

> **Weakness → What you're doing → Improvement**

Example:

> I sometimes spend too much time trying to perfect my work. However, I am learning to manage my time better and focus on priorities.

---

# Part 11 — 👥 Teamwork

### Question:

**Do you prefer working alone or in a team?**

### Answer:

> I can work both independently and as part of a team. However, I enjoy teamwork because different people can bring different ideas and skills. When working in a team, I try to communicate clearly, complete my responsibilities on time, and help others when necessary.

---

# Part 12 — 🔥 Handling Pressure

### Question:

**How do you handle pressure at work?**

### Answer:

> When I am under pressure, I first try to stay calm and identify the most important tasks. Then I break large tasks into smaller steps and prioritize them. If I face a problem that I cannot solve alone, I ask for help instead of wasting too much time.

### Useful words:

* stay calm
* prioritize
* deadline
* manage
* solve
* focus
* break into smaller tasks

---

# Part 13 — ⏰ Time Management

### Question:

**How do you manage your time?**

### Answer:

> I usually make a list of my tasks and prioritize them based on importance and deadlines. I try to complete difficult tasks when I have the most energy. I also avoid unnecessary distractions and take short breaks when needed.

---

# Part 14 — 🚀 Career Goals

### Question:

**What are your career goals?**

### Beginner:

> I want to become a successful software developer.

### Intermediate:

> I want to build a career as a full-stack developer. I want to improve my backend and frontend skills.

### Advanced:

> My long-term goal is to build a strong career as a full-stack developer. I want to become highly skilled in backend development while also improving my frontend and system design knowledge. In the future, I would like to work on large-scale applications and take on more challenging technical responsibilities.

---

# Part 15 — 🔥 Where Do You See Yourself in 5 Years?

### Question:

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

### Answer:

> In five years, I hope to be an experienced software developer with strong technical and problem-solving skills. I want to work on challenging projects, take more responsibility, and continue learning new technologies. I also hope to contribute to my team and eventually take on a leadership role.

---

# Part 16 — 🧠 Follow-up Conversation

### Q1:

**What do you do?**

> I work as a software developer.

### Q2:

**What do you specialize in?**

> I mainly specialize in backend development.

### Q3:

**What technologies do you use?**

> I mainly use Python, Django, REST APIs, databases, and React.

### Q4:

**What do you enjoy about your work?**

> I enjoy solving technical problems and building useful applications.

### Q5:

**What is the most difficult part?**

> Sometimes debugging complex problems can be challenging.

### Q6:

**How do you handle difficult problems?**

> I break the problem into smaller parts and solve them step by step.

### Q7:

**What are your career goals?**

> I want to become a highly skilled full-stack developer.

### Q8:

**What are you doing to achieve that goal?**

> I am continuously learning, building projects, and practicing my technical and communication skills.

---

# Part 17 — 🎤 Interview Practice

এখন interview-এর মতো practice করবে।

### Interviewer:

**Tell me about yourself.**

### Answer:

> My name is Atiar. I have a background in Computer Science and Engineering, and I am interested in software development. I mainly work with Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and databases. I enjoy building web applications and APIs and solving technical problems.
>
> I have worked on several practical projects, which helped me improve my backend and frontend development skills. Currently, I am focusing on improving my technical skills as well as my English communication skills. My goal is to build a strong career as a full-stack developer.

---

# Part 18 — ⭐ Professional Connectors

আজকের speaking-এ এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

### Currently

> Currently, I am focusing on backend development.

### Mainly

> I mainly work with Python and Django.

### In addition

> In addition, I have experience with React.

### However

> I enjoy working independently. However, I also enjoy teamwork.

### For example

> For example, I have built several web applications.

### In the future

> In the future, I want to work on larger projects.

### My goal is to...

> My goal is to become a highly skilled developer.

---

# Part 19 — 🔥 Advanced Career Questions

এগুলোতে 5–7 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**Why did you choose your career?**

### 2.

**What motivates you to work hard?**

### 3.

**What kind of company would you like to work for?**

### 4.

**What is more important: salary or career growth? Why?**

### 5.

**How do you deal with failure at work?**

### 6.

**What would you do if you disagreed with your manager?**

### 7.

**What makes someone successful in their career?**

### 8.

**What skills will be important for your career in the future?**

---

# Part 20 — 🎤 3-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Career & Future Goals**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Education

> I studied...

### 2. Current situation

> Currently, I am...

### 3. Skills

> My main skills are...

### 4. Work

> I work on...

### 5. What you enjoy

> I enjoy...

### 6. Challenges

> One challenge is...

### 7. Improvement

> I am working on...

### 8. Short-term goal

> My short-term goal is...

### 9. Long-term goal

> My long-term goal is...

---

# 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I have a background in Computer Science and Engineering, and I am interested in software development. My main technical skills include Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and database technologies.
>
> I particularly enjoy backend development because I like designing APIs, working with databases, and solving technical problems. I have worked on several practical projects, which have helped me understand how real-world applications are developed.
>
> One of the challenges I face is keeping up with new technologies because the technology industry changes very quickly. To deal with this, I try to learn continuously and build practical projects instead of only studying theory.
>
> My short-term goal is to become a strong software developer and gain professional experience. My long-term goal is to become a highly skilled full-stack developer and work on large-scale applications. I also want to improve my communication and leadership skills so that I can take on greater responsibilities in the future.

---

# 📝 Lesson 10 Homework

### Task 1 — 10 Questions

এই প্রশ্নগুলোর answer **মুখে বলবে**:

1. What do you do?
2. What are your main skills?
3. What do you enjoy about your work?
4. What is difficult about your work?
5. Do you prefer working alone or in a team?
6. How do you handle pressure?
7. How do you manage your time?
8. What are your strengths?
9. What is one weakness you are improving?
10. What are your career goals?

---

### Task 2 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি Work/Career questions** তৈরি করবে।

উদাহরণ:

> What kind of work do you do?

> Why did you choose this career?

> How do you improve your skills?

> What motivates you?

> Where do you see yourself in five years?

---

### Task 3 — Interview Challenge 🎤

এই question-এর উত্তর **2 মিনিট** বলবে:

> **"Tell me about yourself and your career goals."**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 10 Golden Formula

Career topic-এ:

> **Education → Current Work → Skills → Responsibilities → Strength → Challenge → Improvement → Career Goal**

আর interview answer-এ শুধু:

> ❌ "I am a developer."

এখানে থামবে না।

বরং:

> ✅ **"I am a software developer. I mainly work with Python and Django, and I enjoy developing APIs and solving technical problems. I am continuously improving my skills by building practical projects. My long-term goal is to become a highly skilled full-stack developer."**

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি **"Tell me about yourself"** প্রশ্নের উত্তর **2–3 মিনিট confidently** দিতে পারো।

**Lesson 1–10 পর্যন্ত একটা গুরুত্বপূর্ণ pattern তৈরি হয়েছে:**

**Question → Short Answer → Why → Example → Extra Information → Follow-up Question**

এই pattern-টাই পরের lessons-এ ধীরে ধীরে **Intermediate → Advanced speaking**-এর দিকে নিয়ে যাবে।
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 11

## Topic: Food, Cooking & Eating Habits 🍚🍳

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **food, favorite খাবার, cooking, restaurant, eating habits, healthy food এবং food preferences** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য: **খাবার নিয়ে 2–3 মিনিট naturally কথা বলা + follow-up questions করা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 11 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Food নিয়ে 25টি practical question
* খাবারের taste describe করা
* **like / dislike / prefer / would like** ব্যবহার
* Restaurant-এ useful English
* Healthy vs unhealthy food নিয়ে opinion
* **too / very / enough** ব্যবহার
* Question → Answer → Reason → Example দিয়ে speaking expand করা

---

# Part 1 — 🍚 25 Questions About Food

## Basic Questions

1. **What is your favorite food?**
   তোমার প্রিয় খাবার কী?

2. **What food do you like most?**

3. **What food don't you like?**

4. **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**

5. **What do you usually eat for lunch?**

6. **What do you usually eat for dinner?**

7. **Do you like spicy food?**

8. **Do you prefer rice or bread?**

9. **What is your favorite fruit?**

10. **What is your favorite drink?**

---

## Cooking & Eating

11. **Can you cook?**

12. **What can you cook?**

13. **Who usually cooks in your family?**

14. **How often do you cook?**

15. **Do you enjoy cooking?**

16. **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**

17. **How often do you eat outside?**

18. **What kind of restaurants do you like?**

19. **What is your favorite restaurant dish?**

20. **Do you like trying new foods?**

---

## Opinion Questions

21. **What is a healthy diet?**

22. **Do you think fast food is unhealthy?**

23. **Why is healthy food important?**

24. **Has your eating habit changed over time?**

25. **What food would you like to try in the future?**

---

# Part 2 — 🍕 Food Vocabulary

| English    | বাংলা                 |
| ---------- | --------------------- |
| Food       | খাবার                 |
| Meal       | খাবারের সময়/meal     |
| Breakfast  | সকালের নাশতা          |
| Lunch      | দুপুরের খাবার         |
| Dinner     | রাতের খাবার           |
| Snack      | হালকা খাবার           |
| Dish       | রান্না করা খাবারের পদ |
| Ingredient | উপকরণ                 |
| Recipe     | রান্নার প্রণালি       |
| Spicy      | ঝাল                   |
| Sweet      | মিষ্টি                |
| Sour       | টক                    |
| Salty      | লবণাক্ত               |
| Bitter     | তিতা                  |
| Delicious  | সুস্বাদু              |
| Fresh      | তাজা                  |
| Healthy    | স্বাস্থ্যকর           |
| Unhealthy  | অস্বাস্থ্যকর          |
| Homemade   | বাড়িতে তৈরি          |
| Fast food  | দ্রুত প্রস্তুত খাবার  |

---

# Part 3 — 😋 Taste Describe করা

কোনো খাবার সম্পর্কে বলতে:

### Delicious

> The food is delicious.

### Spicy

> I like spicy food.

### Sweet

> This cake is very sweet.

### Salty

> The soup is a little salty.

### Sour

> I like sour fruits.

### Fresh

> The vegetables are fresh.

### Crispy

> The chicken is crispy.

### Soft

> The bread is soft.

### Healthy

> This meal is healthy and nutritious.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ Like / Dislike

### Like

> I like rice.

> I like eating rice.

### Love

> I love biryani.

> I love eating spicy food.

### Enjoy

> I enjoy cooking.

> I enjoy trying new foods.

### Don't like

> I don't like very spicy food.

### Hate

> I hate wasting food.

---

# Part 5 — 🔥 Prefer

দুটি জিনিস compare করতে:

> I prefer rice to bread.

> I prefer homemade food to fast food.

> I prefer tea to coffee.

### Question:

**Do you prefer tea or coffee?**

### Answer:

> I prefer tea to coffee because I like its taste. I usually drink tea in the evening.

---

# Part 6 — 🧠 Prefer vs Would Like

### Prefer = সাধারণ পছন্দ

> I prefer tea to coffee.

### Would like = এখন/ভবিষ্যতে কিছু চাই

> I would like a cup of tea.

> I would like to try Japanese food.

### Difference:

> I prefer rice.

= আমার সাধারণ পছন্দ rice।

> I would like some rice.

= আমি কিছু rice চাই।

---

# Part 7 — 🍳 Can You Cook?

### Question:

**Can you cook?**

### Beginner:

> Yes, I can cook.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes. I can make rice, eggs, vegetables, and some basic chicken dishes.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I can cook several simple dishes. I usually prepare meals when I have enough time. I enjoy cooking because I can control the ingredients and adjust the taste according to my preference.

---

# Part 8 — 🔥 Describe Your Favorite Food

### Question:

**What is your favorite food?**

### Formula:

> **Food → Taste → Ingredients → When → Why**

### Example:

> My favorite food is biryani. I love it because it is flavorful and spicy. It is usually made with rice, meat, spices, and different ingredients. I usually eat it on special occasions or when I go out with friends. I like it because it is delicious and filling.

---

# Part 9 — 🍚 Talk About Your Eating Habits

### Question:

**What do you usually eat in a day?**

### Example:

> I usually have a simple breakfast in the morning. For lunch, I normally eat rice with vegetables, fish, or meat. In the evening, I sometimes have tea and a light snack. For dinner, I usually eat rice or another simple meal. I try to avoid eating too much fast food.

---

# Part 10 — 🥗 Healthy Food

### Question:

**What is a healthy diet?**

### Answer:

> I think a healthy diet should include vegetables, fruits, protein, whole grains, and enough water. We should also avoid eating too much sugar, oily food, and fast food. A balanced diet is important because it helps us stay healthy and active.

---

# Part 11 — 🍔 Fast Food

### Question:

**Do you like fast food?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I like fast food, but I don't eat it very often. I sometimes eat burgers, pizza, or fried chicken when I go out with friends. However, I try not to eat too much fast food because it can be unhealthy if we eat it regularly.

### Useful structure:

> **Yes, but...**

এই structure natural speaking-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# Part 12 — ⭐ Too / Very / Enough

### Very

> The food is **very spicy**.

### Too

> The food is **too spicy**.

**Too = প্রয়োজনের চেয়ে বেশি**

### Enough

> The food is spicy **enough**.

আর:

> I don't have enough time to cook.

> There isn't enough food.

---

# Part 13 — 🍽️ Restaurant English

Restaurant-এ এই expressionsগুলো খুব useful।

### Ordering

> I'd like to order some food.

> Can I have a chicken biryani, please?

> I'd like a glass of water.

> Can I have the menu, please?

### Asking

> What do you recommend?

> Is this dish spicy?

> What are the ingredients?

> Does this contain meat?

### Bill

> Could I have the bill, please?

> Can I pay by card?

---

# Part 14 — 🔥 Restaurant Conversation

### Waiter:

> Hello. What would you like to order?

### You:

> I'd like a chicken biryani, please.

### Waiter:

> Would you like anything to drink?

### You:

> Yes, I'd like a glass of water.

### Waiter:

> Is that all?

### You:

> Yes, that's all. Thank you.

---

# Part 15 — 🧠 Question Expansion

### Basic:

> What is your favorite food?

### Follow-up:

> Why do you like it?

### More:

> When do you usually eat it?

### More:

> Who usually cooks it?

### More:

> How often do you eat it?

### More:

> Is it healthy?

### More:

> Would you recommend it to other people?

👉 একটি question থেকে **7টি conversation question** তৈরি করা যায়।

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 Advanced Opinion

### Question:

**Do you think people eat too much fast food nowadays?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I think many people eat too much fast food nowadays, especially because it is convenient and easily available. People with busy lifestyles may choose fast food because they don't have enough time to cook. However, eating too much fast food can have negative effects on health. I think people should enjoy it occasionally but maintain a balanced diet overall.

---

# Part 17 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Favorite Food and Eating Habits**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Favorite food

> My favorite food is...

### 2. Taste

> It tastes...

### 3. Ingredients

> It is usually made with...

### 4. Frequency

> I usually eat it...

### 5. Cooking

> I can/can't cook...

### 6. Eating habits

> I usually eat...

### 7. Healthy food

> I try to...

### 8. Opinion

> I think healthy eating is important because...

### 9. Future

> I would like to try...

---

# Part 18 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> My favorite food is biryani. I really like it because it is flavorful, spicy, and filling. It is usually made with rice, meat, spices, and other ingredients. I don't eat it every day, but I usually have it on special occasions or when I go out with friends.
>
> I can cook a few simple dishes, such as rice, eggs, vegetables, and chicken. I prefer homemade food because it is usually fresher and I can control the ingredients. However, I sometimes enjoy eating at restaurants, especially when I spend time with my friends.
>
> I also try to maintain healthy eating habits. I eat vegetables and fruits regularly and try not to eat too much fast food or oily food. I think a balanced diet is important because it helps us stay healthy and active.
>
> In the future, I would like to try different types of international food and learn how to cook more dishes.

**⚠️ Example মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের খাবার দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# Part 19 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এগুলোতে 5–7 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে:

1. **Why do people prefer fast food?**
2. **Is homemade food always healthier than restaurant food?**
3. **Why do people's eating habits change as they get older?**
4. **Should children be allowed to eat fast food regularly?**
5. **How does food bring people together?**
6. **Do you think people should learn cooking at school?**
7. **How has social media changed the way people choose food?**

# 📝 Lesson 11 Homework

### Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি প্রশ্নের answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What is your favorite food?
2. Why do you like it?
3. What food don't you like?
4. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
5. Can you cook?
6. What can you cook?
7. Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?
8. How often do you eat fast food?
9. What is a healthy diet?
10. What food would you like to try?

---

### Task 2 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি Food-related question** তৈরি করবে।

Example:

> What food do you like?

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you eat it?

> Who cooks at your home?

> Can you cook?

---

### Task 3 — 🎤 3-Minute Challenge

**"My Favorite Food and Eating Habits"** নিয়ে 3 মিনিট কথা বলবে।

চেষ্টা করবে অন্তত এই words ব্যবহার করতে:

**because → however → usually → sometimes → prefer → for example → in my opinion → in the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 11 Golden Formula

Food topic-এ:

> **What → Taste → Ingredients → Frequency → Preference → Reason → Example → Opinion**

Example:

> My favorite food is biryani. I like it because it is flavorful and spicy. I usually eat it on special occasions. I prefer homemade food because I can control the ingredients. However, I sometimes eat at restaurants with my friends. In my opinion, we should enjoy our favorite foods but maintain a balanced diet.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি:

**“What is your favorite food?”**

এই simple question-এর উত্তর **মাত্র 1 sentence-এ শেষ না করে 1–2 মিনিট naturally বলতে পারো।**

**Lesson 1–11-এর main rule এখন আরও important:**

> **Question → Direct Answer → Why → Example → Extra Information → Follow-up Question**

এভাবে practice করলে শুধু vocabulary নয়, **actual conversation skill** তৈরি হবে।
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 12

## Topic: Home, House & Neighborhood 🏠🌳

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **তোমার বাড়ি, room, family living, neighborhood, nearby places এবং তোমার ideal home** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের main লক্ষ্য:

> **কোনো জায়গা English-এ clearly describe করা।**

এটা খুব useful, কারণ real conversation-এ মানুষ প্রায়ই জিজ্ঞেস করে:

> Where do you live?
> What is your house like?
> What's your neighborhood like?

---

# 🎯 Lesson 12 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Home & neighborhood নিয়ে 25টি question
* নিজের house/room describe করা
* **There is / There are** ব্যবহার
* **next to / near / opposite / behind / in front of** ব্যবহার
* Place describe করার vocabulary
* **Present Simple** দিয়ে neighborhood describe করা
* Direction-related basic English
* 2–3 মিনিট নিজের home ও neighborhood নিয়ে বলা

---

# Part 1 — 🏠 25 Questions About Home

## Basic Questions

1. **Where do you live?**
   তুমি কোথায় থাকো?

2. **Do you live in a house or an apartment?**

3. **How long have you lived there?**

4. **Who do you live with?**

5. **How many rooms are there in your home?**

6. **What is your favorite room?**

7. **Why do you like that room?**

8. **What is your room like?**

9. **What furniture do you have in your room?**

10. **Do you have a balcony?**

---

## Neighborhood

11. **What is your neighborhood like?**

12. **Is your neighborhood quiet or busy?**

13. **Do you like your neighborhood?**

14. **What places are near your home?**

15. **Is there a market near your home?**

16. **Are there any restaurants nearby?**

17. **How far is your university/workplace from your home?**

18. **How do you usually travel around your area?**

19. **Is public transportation easily available?**

20. **Is your neighborhood safe?**

---

## Opinion & Future

21. **What do you like about your home?**

22. **What don't you like about your home?**

23. **What would you like to change about your home?**

24. **What would your ideal home look like?**
