# Part 2 — 🏡 Home Vocabulary

| English      | বাংলা              |
| ------------ | ------------------ |
| House        | বাড়ি              |
| Apartment    | ফ্ল্যাট            |
| Room         | ঘর                 |
| Bedroom      | শোবার ঘর           |
| Living room  | বসার ঘর            |
| Dining room  | খাবার ঘর           |
| Kitchen      | রান্নাঘর           |
| Bathroom     | বাথরুম             |
| Balcony      | বারান্দা           |
| Roof         | ছাদ                |
| Floor        | তলা/মেঝে           |
| Door         | দরজা               |
| Window       | জানালা             |
| Wall         | দেয়াল             |
| Ceiling      | ছাদ/উপরের অংশ      |
| Garden       | বাগান              |
| Garage       | গাড়ি রাখার জায়গা |
| Furniture    | আসবাবপত্র          |
| Neighborhood | আশেপাশের এলাকা     |
| Street       | রাস্তা             |

---

# Part 3 — ⭐ There Is / There Are

Place describe করার জন্য এটা খুব important।

## Singular

> There is a bed in my room.

> There is a table near the window.

> There is a balcony in my apartment.

## Plural

> There are two chairs in my room.

> There are three bedrooms in my house.

> There are many shops near my home.

---

# Part 4 — ❌ Common Mistakes

### ভুল:

> There have a bed in my room.

### সঠিক:

> There is a bed in my room.

---

### ভুল:

> There is two chairs.

### সঠিক:

> There are two chairs.

---

### মনে রাখবে:

**One thing → There is**

> There is a table.

**More than one → There are**

> There are two tables.

---

# Part 5 — 📍 Position Words

জায়গা describe করতে এগুলো খুব important।

### Next to

> The table is next to the bed.

### Near

> My house is near the market.

### Opposite

> There is a school opposite my house.

### Behind

> There is a garden behind the house.

### In front of

> There is a tree in front of my house.

### Between

> The shop is between the bank and the pharmacy.

### On the left

> The supermarket is on the left.

### On the right

> The restaurant is on the right.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Describe Your Room

### Question:

**What is your room like?**

### Beginner:

> My room is small but comfortable.

### Intermediate:

> My room is small but comfortable. There is a bed, a desk, a chair, and a wardrobe. There is also a window next to my desk.

### Advanced:

> My room is relatively small, but it is comfortable and well-organized. There is a bed near the wall, and I have a study desk next to the window. There is also a wardrobe for my clothes and a bookshelf where I keep my books. I like my room because it is quiet, so I can study and work there comfortably.

---

# Part 7 — 🪑 Furniture Vocabulary

* Bed
* Chair
* Table
* Desk
* Sofa
* Wardrobe
* Bookshelf
* Mirror
* Lamp
* Computer
* Television
* Fan
* Air conditioner
* Refrigerator
* Curtains
* Carpet

### Example:

> There is a desk in front of the window.

> My laptop is on the desk.

> My books are on the bookshelf.

---

# Part 8 — 🌳 Describe Your Neighborhood

### Question:

**What is your neighborhood like?**

### Answer:

> My neighborhood is fairly busy but convenient. There are several shops, restaurants, pharmacies, and grocery stores nearby. There is also a school and a mosque in the area. Public transportation is easily available, so it is easy to travel to other parts of the city.

---

# Part 9 — 🏪 Nearby Places

Useful vocabulary:

> Grocery store
> Supermarket
> Pharmacy
> Hospital
> School
> College
> University
> Restaurant
> Café
> Bank
> ATM
> Park
> Mosque
> Market
> Bus stop
> Railway station
> Shopping mall

### Example:

> There is a grocery store near my home.

> There are several restaurants in my area.

> The nearest pharmacy is about five minutes away.

---

# Part 10 — 📏 Talking About Distance

### How far...?

> How far is your university from your home?

Answer:

> It is about five kilometers from my home.

Or:

> It takes about 30 minutes to get there.

### Useful:

> It's about 2 kilometers away.

> It's a five-minute walk.

> It's about 20 minutes by bus.

> It's quite far from my home.

> It's very close to my home.

---

# Part 11 — 🚶 Transportation

### Question:

**How do you usually travel around your area?**

### Answer:

> I usually walk when I need to go somewhere nearby. For longer distances, I usually take a bus or use a ride-sharing service. Public transportation is easily available in my area.

### Useful verbs:

> walk

> drive

> ride

> take a bus

> take a train

> use a taxi

> use a ride-sharing service

---

# Part 12 — 🔥 Follow-up Conversation

### Q1:

**Where do you live?**

> I live in Dhaka.

### Q2:

**Do you live in a house or an apartment?**

> I live in an apartment.

### Q3:

**Who do you live with?**

> I live with my family.

### Q4:

**What is your home like?**

> It is comfortable and relatively spacious.

### Q5:

**What is your favorite room?**

> My favorite room is my bedroom.

### Q6:

**Why do you like it?**

> Because it is quiet and I can study there.

### Q7:

**What is your neighborhood like?**

> It is busy but convenient.

### Q8:

**What places are nearby?**

> There are shops, restaurants, pharmacies, and a market nearby.

### Q9:

**Do you like your neighborhood?**

> Yes, because most things I need are easily accessible.

# Part 13 — 🧭 Basic Directions

আজ basic direction practice করবে।

### Asking

> Excuse me, where is the nearest bank?

> How can I get to the market?

> Is there a pharmacy nearby?

> How far is the bus station?

### Giving directions

> Go straight.

> Turn left.

> Turn right.

> It's on your left.

> It's on your right.

> It's next to the bank.

> It's opposite the school.

> It's behind the hospital.

> It's about five minutes from here.

---

# Part 14 — 🗣️ Mini Direction Conversation

### Person A:

> Excuse me, how can I get to the pharmacy?

### Person B:

> Go straight and turn left at the next intersection.

### Person A:

> Is it far from here?

### Person B:

> No, it's about a five-minute walk.

### Person A:

> Is it next to the bank?

### Person B:

> Yes, exactly. It's next to the bank.

---

# Part 15 — ⭐ What Do You Like About Your Home?

### Question:

**What do you like about your home?**

### Answer:

> I like my home because it is comfortable and peaceful. My favorite thing is that I have a quiet place where I can study and work. I also like the location because there are many useful places nearby.

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 What Would You Change?

### Question:

**What would you like to change about your home?**

### Answer:

> If I could change something, I would make my room a little bigger. I would also like to have a larger study area and more storage space. It would make the room more comfortable and organized.

---

# Part 17 — 🏡 Ideal Home

### Question:

**What would your ideal home look like?**

### Answer:

> My ideal home would be comfortable, modern, and peaceful. I would like to have a spacious living room, a large kitchen, and a private study room. I would also like to have a small garden or balcony. Ideally, the house would be in a quiet area but close to important places such as shops, hospitals, and public transportation.

---

# Part 18 — 🔥 Opinion Question

### Question:

**What is more important: a beautiful home or a good neighborhood?**

### Answer:

> In my opinion, both are important, but I think a good neighborhood is slightly more important. A beautiful home is comfortable, but the surrounding environment also affects our daily life. If the neighborhood is safe, clean, and convenient, it can make our life much easier. So, I would prefer a comfortable home in a good neighborhood rather than a beautiful home in a bad location.

---

# Part 19 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Home and Neighborhood**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Location

> I live in...

### 2. Home type

> I live in a...

### 3. People

> I live with...

### 4. Rooms

> There are...

### 5. Favorite room

> My favorite room is...

### 6. Description

> There is/There are...

### 7. Neighborhood

> My neighborhood is...

### 8. Nearby places

> There are several...

### 9. Transportation

> I usually travel by...

### 10. Opinion

> I like my area because...

### 11. Future

> In the future, I would like to...

---

# Part 20 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I live in Dhaka with my family. We live in an apartment, and it is relatively comfortable. There are several rooms in our home, including bedrooms, a living room, a kitchen, and bathrooms.
>
> My favorite room is my bedroom because it is quiet and comfortable. There is a bed near the wall, a study desk next to the window, and a bookshelf where I keep my books. I spend a lot of time there studying, working, and using my computer.
>
> My neighborhood is quite busy, but it is convenient. There are many shops, restaurants, pharmacies, and grocery stores nearby. There is also a market and several public transportation options. So, I can easily get most of the things I need.
>
> I like my neighborhood because it is convenient and most places are within a short distance. However, I would prefer a quieter and less crowded area in the future. Ideally, I would like to live in a peaceful place with good transportation and a nice environment.

**⚠️ Example মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের home এবং area দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# Part 21 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন 5–7 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **What makes a house feel like a home?**

2. **What are the advantages of living in a big city?**

3. **What are the disadvantages of living in a crowded city?**

4. **Would you prefer living in a city or a village? Why?**

5. **What makes a neighborhood a good place to live?**

6. **How can people make their neighborhood better?**

7. **Would you like to move to another city in the future? Why?**

---

# 📝 Lesson 12 Homework

### Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. Where do you live?
2. Who do you live with?
3. What is your home like?
4. What is your favorite room?
5. What is in your room?
6. What is your neighborhood like?
7. What places are near your home?
8. How do you travel around your area?
9. What do you like about your neighborhood?
10. What would your ideal home look like?

---

### Task 2 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি Home & Neighborhood questions** তৈরি করবে।

Example:

> How many rooms are there in your house?

> What is your favorite room?

> Is there a market near your home?

> How far is your workplace from your home?

---

### Task 3 — 🎤 3-Minute Challenge

**"My Home and Neighborhood"** নিয়ে 3 মিনিট কথা বলবে।

চেষ্টা করবে এগুলো ব্যবহার করতে:

**there is → there are → next to → near → opposite → behind → usually → because → however → in the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 12 Golden Formula

কোনো **place** describe করার সময়:

> **Location → Type → People → Size → Rooms → Objects → Surroundings → Nearby Places → Opinion → Future**

Example:

> I live in an apartment with my family. There are several rooms in our home. My favorite room is my bedroom because it is quiet. There is a desk next to the window and a bookshelf near the wall. My neighborhood is busy but convenient. There are several shops and restaurants nearby. I like the area because it is convenient, although I would prefer a quieter place in the future.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি এই প্রশ্নের উত্তর **2–3 মিনিট** naturally দিতে পারো:

> **"Describe your home and neighborhood."**

আর Lesson 12-এর সবচেয়ে important speaking skill:

> **কোনো মানুষকে describe করা = Lesson 7**
> **কোনো জায়গাকে describe করা = Lesson 12**

এই দুই skill future conversation-এ বারবার ব্যবহার হবে।


---

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 13

## Topic: Shopping, Clothes & Buying Things 🛍️👕💳

আজকের lesson-এ তুমি **shopping, clothes, prices, sizes, colors, bargaining, online shopping এবং buying decisions** নিয়ে English-এ কথা বলা শিখবে।

আজকের main target:

> **দোকানে বা online shopping-এর situation English-এ handle করতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 13 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Shopping নিয়ে 25টি practical question
* Clothes ও products describe করা
* **How much / How many / What size / Which one** দিয়ে question
* Price জিজ্ঞেস করা
* দোকানে conversation করা
* **cheap / expensive / affordable / worth it** ব্যবহার
* **want / need / would like / looking for** ব্যবহার
* Online vs offline shopping নিয়ে opinion
* 2–3 মিনিট shopping experience বলা

---

# Part 1 — 🛍️ 25 Shopping Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **Do you like shopping?**
   তুমি কি shopping করতে পছন্দ করো?

2. **How often do you go shopping?**

3. **What do you usually buy?**

4. **Where do you usually go shopping?**

5. **Do you prefer shopping online or in stores?**

6. **What is your favorite clothing store?**

7. **What kind of clothes do you usually wear?**

8. **What is your favorite color?**

9. **What size do you usually wear?**

10. **How much do you usually spend on clothes?**

---

## Buying Things

11. **What are you looking for?**

12. **How much does this cost?**

13. **Do you have this in another color?**

14. **Do you have a larger size?**

15. **Do you have a smaller size?**

16. **Can I try this on?**

17. **Does it fit me?**

18. **Can you give me a discount?**

19. **Can I pay by card?**

20. **Can I return this if it doesn't fit?**

---

## Opinion Questions

21. **How do you decide what to buy?**

22. **Do you compare prices before buying something?**

23. **Do you usually buy expensive or affordable products?**

24. **Have you ever bought something you didn't need?**

25. **What is the most important thing when buying a product?**

---

# Part 2 — 👕 Clothing Vocabulary

| English | বাংলা     |
| ------- | --------- |
| Shirt   | শার্ট     |
| T-shirt | টি-শার্ট  |
| Pants   | প্যান্ট   |
| Jeans   | জিন্স     |
| Jacket  | জ্যাকেট   |
| Sweater | সোয়েটার  |
| Shoes   | জুতা      |
| Sandals | স্যান্ডেল |
| Socks   | মোজা      |
| Belt    | বেল্ট     |
| Cap     | টুপি      |
| Watch   | ঘড়ি      |
| Bag     | ব্যাগ     |
| Wallet  | মানিব্যাগ |
| Dress   | পোশাক     |
| Size    | মাপ       |
| Color   | রং        |
| Brand   | ব্র্যান্ড |
| Price   | দাম       |
| Quality | মান       |

---

# Part 3 — ⭐ Describing Clothes

কোনো clothing item describe করার সময়:

### Color

> It's black.

> It's dark blue.

> It's white and gray.

### Size

> It's small.

> It's medium-sized.

> It's large.

### Style

> It's casual.

> It's formal.

> It's comfortable.

> It's modern.

### Quality

> The quality is good.

> It looks well-made.

### Example:

> This is a black cotton T-shirt. It's simple, comfortable, and affordable.

---

# Part 4 — 💰 Price Vocabulary

### Cheap

> This shirt is cheap.

### Expensive

> This jacket is expensive.

### Affordable

**Affordable = সাধ্যের মধ্যে**

> This phone is affordable.

### Reasonable

> The price is reasonable.

### Worth it

**Worth it = দামের তুলনায় ভালো/মূল্যবান**

> It's expensive, but I think it's worth it.

### Too expensive

> That's too expensive for me.

---

# Part 5 — 🔥 Want vs Need vs Would Like

## Want

সাধারণভাবে কিছু চাই:

> I want a new shirt.

## Need

প্রয়োজন:

> I need a new pair of shoes.

## Would like

Polite / natural:

> I'd like to buy a jacket.

দোকানে **I'd like...** খুব useful।

---

# Part 6 — 🛒 "I'm Looking For..."

Shopping-এ খুব natural expression।

### Customer:

> I'm looking for a black shirt.

> I'm looking for a pair of jeans.

> I'm looking for a laptop bag.

### Shopkeeper:

> What size are you looking for?

### Customer:

> I'm looking for a medium.

---

# Part 7 — 🔥 Shopping Conversation

### Customer:

> Excuse me. I'm looking for a black shirt.

### Shopkeeper:

> Sure. What size do you need?

### Customer:

> Medium, please.

### Shopkeeper:

> Here you are.

### Customer:

> Can I try it on?

### Shopkeeper:

> Sure. The fitting room is over there.

### Customer:

> How much is it?

### Shopkeeper:

> It's 1,500 taka.

### Customer:

> That's a little expensive. Can you give me a discount?

### Shopkeeper:

> I can give you a small discount.

### Customer:

> Okay. I'll take it.

---

# Part 8 — 👕 Try It On

**Try on = পোশাক পরে দেখা**

> Can I try this on?

> I'd like to try this shirt on.

### Fit

> It fits me well.

> It's too tight.

> It's too loose.

> It's a little big.

> It's a little small.

---

# Part 9 — 📏 Size Questions

### Asking size:

> What size is this?

> Do you have this in medium?

> Do you have a larger size?

> Do you have a smaller size?

### Answer:

> I usually wear a medium.

> I need a large.

> This size fits me perfectly.

---

# Part 10 — 🎨 Color Questions

> Do you have this in black?

> Do you have another color?

> What colors do you have?

> Do you have a blue one?

### Example:

> I like this shirt, but do you have it in black?

---

# Part 11 — 💳 Payment

Useful expressions:

> How much is it?

> How much does it cost?

> Can I pay by card?

> Can I pay in cash?

> Do you accept mobile payment?

> Can I get a receipt?

### At the end:

> I'll take it.

= আমি এটা নেব।

> I'll think about it.

= আমি ভেবে দেখব।

> I don't think I'll buy it.

= মনে হয় আমি এটা কিনব না।

---

# Part 12 — 🔥 Bargaining

বাংলাদেশের shopping situation-এ useful।

### Customer:

> How much is this?

> It's 2,000 taka.

> That's a little expensive.

> Can you give me a discount?

> What's your best price?

> Can you make it 1,500?

### Shopkeeper:

> I can give you a small discount.

### Customer:

> Okay, I'll take it.

---

# Part 13 — 🧠 Online Shopping

### Questions:

> Do you shop online?

> What do you usually buy online?

> Why do you prefer online shopping?

> Have you ever received a damaged product?

### Answer:

> Yes, I sometimes shop online. I usually buy clothes, electronics, and other useful products online. I like online shopping because it is convenient and saves time. I can compare prices and read customer reviews before making a decision.

---

# Part 14 — ⭐ Online vs Offline Shopping

### Question:

**Do you prefer online shopping or shopping in stores?**

### Answer:

> I prefer online shopping for many products because it is convenient and saves time. I can compare prices, read reviews, and order things from home. However, I prefer shopping in stores when buying clothes because I can see the product and try it on before buying.

🔥 Notice:

> **I prefer X because... However, I prefer Y when...**

এটা speaking answer expand করার excellent structure।

---

# Part 15 — 💡 How Do You Decide What to Buy?

### Question:

**How do you decide what to buy?**

### Answer:

> Before buying something, I usually compare the price and quality. I also check customer reviews, especially when shopping online. I think about whether I really need the product. If it is expensive, I usually take more time to make a decision.

---

# Part 16 — ❗ Unnecessary Shopping

### Question:

**Have you ever bought something you didn't need?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have. Sometimes I buy something because it looks interesting or there is a discount. Later, I realize that I didn't really need it. Now I try to think carefully before buying something.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Important Buying Expressions

### When you like something:

> I really like this one.

> This looks good.

> This looks perfect.

### When you're unsure:

> I'm not sure about this one.

> Let me think about it.

> Can I see another one?

### When you don't like it:

> I'm not really interested in this one.

> I don't think this is right for me.

### When you decide:

> I'll take it.

> I'll buy this one.

> I think I'll go with this one.

---

# Part 18 — 🧠 Question Making Practice

একটা shopping situation থেকে অনেক question তৈরি করতে পারবে।

### Product:

> What are you looking for?

### Color:

> What color do you want?

### Size:

> What size do you need?

### Price:

> How much does it cost?

### Quality:

> Is the quality good?

### Alternative:

> Do you have another one?

### Discount:

> Can you give me a discount?

### Payment:

> Can I pay by card?

### Return:

> Can I return it?

---

# Part 19 — 🎤 2–3 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Shopping Habits**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Frequency

> I usually go shopping...

### 2. Products

> I usually buy...

### 3. Preference

> I prefer online/offline shopping because...

### 4. Decision

> Before buying something, I...

### 5. Price

> I usually look for...

### 6. Quality

> I think quality is...

### 7. Experience

> Once, I...

### 8. Future

> In the future, I want to...

---

# Part 20 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I don't go shopping very often, but I usually shop when I need something. I normally buy clothes, shoes, electronics, and other useful products. For many products, I prefer online shopping because it is convenient and saves time.
>
> Before buying something online, I usually compare prices and check customer reviews. I also look at the product quality and return policy. If the product is expensive, I take more time to make a decision because I don't want to waste money.
>
> When buying clothes, however, I sometimes prefer physical stores because I can see the product and try it on. I usually choose comfortable clothes rather than buying something just because it looks fashionable.
>
> I think price and quality are both important, but quality is more important in the long run. In the future, I want to become a smarter shopper and avoid buying things that I don't really need.

---

# Part 21 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন 5–7 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **Why do people buy things they don't need?**

2. **Is online shopping better than traditional shopping?**

3. **How do advertisements influence people's buying decisions?**

4. **Should people always choose the cheapest product? Why or why not?**

5. **What is more important: price, quality, or brand?**

6. **How has online shopping changed people's lifestyles?**

7. **Do you think people shop too much nowadays?**

---

# 📝 Lesson 13 Homework

### Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. Do you like shopping?
2. What do you usually buy?
3. How often do you shop?
4. Do you prefer online or offline shopping?
5. What kind of clothes do you like?
6. What size do you usually wear?
7. How do you decide what to buy?
8. Do you compare prices?
9. Have you ever bought something you didn't need?
10. What is important when buying a product?

---

### Task 2 — 🛍️ Role Play

নিজে **Customer + Shopkeeper** দুইজনের role play করবে।

Example:

> **Customer:** I'm looking for a T-shirt.
> **Shopkeeper:** What size do you need?
> **Customer:** Medium, please.
> **Shopkeeper:** What color would you like?
> **Customer:** Black, please.
> **Shopkeeper:** Here you are.
> **Customer:** Can I try it on?
> **Shopkeeper:** Sure.
> **Customer:** How much is it?
> **Shopkeeper:** It's 1,200 taka.
> **Customer:** Can you give me a discount?
> **Shopkeeper:** I can give you 100 taka off.
> **Customer:** Okay, I'll take it.

---

### Task 3 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি shopping question** তৈরি করবে।

Try:

> **What + Where + When + How much + How many + Which + Why + How often**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 13 Golden Formula

Shopping conversation:

> **Looking for → Color → Size → Try → Price → Discount → Payment → Decision**

Example:

> I'm looking for a black shirt. Do you have it in medium? Can I try it on? How much does it cost? That's a little expensive. Can you give me a discount? Okay, I'll take it.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি **দোকানে 2–3 মিনিটের একটা complete English conversation** চালাতে পারো।

আর একটা important speaking habit:

❌ **How much? — 1,000. — Okay.**

এরকম short conversation না।

বরং:

> **How much is it? → Is there any discount? → Do you have another color? → Can I try it on? → Does it fit me? → What payment methods do you accept?**

এভাবে **একটা situation থেকে একের পর এক question তৈরি করা**—এটাই তোমার speaking course-এর core skill।

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 14

## Topic: Daily Routine, Time & Habits ⏰

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **daily routine, সকাল থেকে রাত পর্যন্ত কাজ, habits, time management, productive habits এবং bad habits** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য:

> **তোমার পুরো দিনের routine 3–5 মিনিট English-এ explain করতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 14 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Daily routine নিয়ে 25টি important question
* সকাল থেকে রাত পর্যন্ত routine describe করা
* **Present Simple** ভালোভাবে ব্যবহার করা
* **usually / always / sometimes / rarely / never** ব্যবহার
* **before / after / then / while / finally** দিয়ে sequence তৈরি
* Time ও frequency নিয়ে কথা বলা
* Good habits ও bad habits describe করা
* নিজের routine compare করা
* 3 মিনিটের natural speaking

---

# Part 1 — ⏰ 25 Daily Routine Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **What time do you usually wake up?**
2. **What do you do after waking up?**
3. **What time do you have breakfast?**
4. **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**
5. **What time do you start studying or working?**
6. **How do you usually spend your morning?**
7. **What do you do in the afternoon?**
8. **What do you usually do in the evening?**
9. **What time do you have dinner?**
10. **What time do you usually go to bed?**

---

## Habits

11. **How often do you exercise?**
12. **How often do you use your phone?**
13. **How much time do you spend online every day?**
14. **Do you read every day?**
15. **Do you watch TV regularly?**
16. **What do you do in your free time?**
17. **Do you have any bad habits?**
18. **What good habits do you have?**
19. **What habit would you like to change?**
20. **How do you stay productive?**

---

## Time & Productivity

21. **How do you manage your time?**
22. **What is the most productive time of your day?**
23. **Do you follow a fixed routine?**
24. **What do you do when you have a busy day?**
25. **What would you like to change about your daily routine?**

---

# Part 2 — 🌅 Daily Routine Vocabulary

| English          | বাংলা                  |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| Wake up          | ঘুম থেকে ওঠা           |
| Get up           | বিছানা থেকে ওঠা        |
| Brush my teeth   | দাঁত ব্রাশ করা         |
| Wash my face     | মুখ ধোয়া              |
| Take a shower    | গোসল করা               |
| Get dressed      | পোশাক পরা              |
| Have breakfast   | নাশতা করা              |
| Leave home       | বাড়ি থেকে বের হওয়া   |
| Go to work       | কাজে যাওয়া            |
| Go to university | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ে যাওয়া |
| Study            | পড়াশোনা করা           |
| Work             | কাজ করা                |
| Have lunch       | দুপুরের খাবার খাওয়া   |
| Take a break     | বিরতি নেওয়া           |
| Exercise         | ব্যায়াম করা           |
| Relax            | বিশ্রাম নেওয়া         |
| Have dinner      | রাতের খাবার খাওয়া     |
| Read             | পড়া                   |
| Go to bed        | বিছানায় যাওয়া        |
| Fall asleep      | ঘুমিয়ে পড়া           |

---

# Part 3 — ⭐ Present Simple for Routine

Daily routine-এর জন্য সাধারণত **Present Simple** ব্যবহার করি।

### I / You / We / They

> I wake up at 7 a.m.

> I study English every day.

> I go to bed at 11 p.m.

### He / She

> He wakes up at 7 a.m.

> She studies English every day.

⚠️ **He/She হলে verb-এ s/es** আসে।

> He go ❌
> He goes ✅

> She study ❌
> She studies ✅

---

# Part 4 — 🔥 Frequency Words

Routine describe করার সময় এগুলো খুব important।

### Always — 100%

> I always brush my teeth in the morning.

### Usually — বেশিরভাগ সময়

> I usually wake up at 7.

### Often — প্রায়ই

> I often read at night.

### Sometimes — মাঝে মাঝে

> I sometimes watch movies.

### Rarely — খুব কম

> I rarely eat fast food.

### Never — কখনো না

> I never smoke.

### Frequency order:

> **Always → Usually → Often → Sometimes → Rarely → Never**

---

# Part 5 — 🕐 Time Expressions

### Exact time

> I wake up **at 7 a.m.**

### Morning

> I study **in the morning.**

### Afternoon

> I work **in the afternoon.**

### Evening

> I exercise **in the evening.**

### Night

> I read **at night.**

### Days

> I exercise **on Monday.**

### Months/Years

> I started learning English **in 2026.**

---

# Part 6 — 🔗 Sequence Words

Routine বলতে এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

> **First**

> **Then**

> **After that**

> **Next**

> **Later**

> **Finally**

### Example:

> First, I wake up at 7 a.m. Then, I brush my teeth and wash my face. After that, I have breakfast. Next, I start studying. Later, I take a break. Finally, I have dinner and go to bed.

এভাবে বললে তোমার speaking অনেক বেশি organized শোনাবে।

---

# Part 7 — 🌅 Morning Routine

### Question:

**What do you do in the morning?**

### Beginner:

> I wake up at 7 a.m. I brush my teeth and have breakfast.

### Intermediate:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. After waking up, I brush my teeth and wash my face. Then I have breakfast and start studying.

### Advanced:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. After getting up, I freshen up and have breakfast. Then I spend some time reviewing my plans for the day. After that, I usually start studying or working on important tasks.

---

# Part 8 — ☀️ Afternoon Routine

### Question:

**What do you usually do in the afternoon?**

### Answer:

> In the afternoon, I usually have lunch and take a short break. After that, I continue studying or working. If I have some free time, I check my messages or watch something for a while.

---

# Part 9 — 🌙 Evening Routine

### Question:

**What do you do in the evening?**

### Answer:

> In the evening, I usually take a break from work or study. Sometimes I go outside or spend time with my family. I may also practice English or work on a personal project. After dinner, I usually read or watch something before going to bed.

---

# Part 10 — 🛏️ Bedtime

### Question:

**What time do you usually go to bed?**

### Answer:

> I usually go to bed around 11 p.m. Before going to bed, I usually check my schedule for the next day. Sometimes I read for a few minutes. Then I turn off my phone and go to sleep.

---

# Part 11 — 🔥 How Often?

এগুলো খুব useful:

### Question:

> How often do you exercise?

### Answer:

> I exercise every day.

অথবা:

> I exercise three times a week.

> I exercise occasionally.

> I rarely exercise.

### Other examples:

> I read books twice a week.

> I practice English every day.

> I watch movies once or twice a week.

---

# Part 12 — 🧠 How Much Time?

### Question:

**How much time do you spend on your phone?**

### Answer:

> I spend about two or three hours on my phone every day.

### More:

> I spend about one hour practicing English.

> I spend several hours working on my computer.

> I spend less time on social media than I used to.

---

# Part 13 — 📱 Talk About Phone Habits

### Question:

**How often do you use your phone?**

### Answer:

> I use my phone every day. I mainly use it for communication, learning, browsing the internet, and checking messages. However, I sometimes spend more time on it than I should. I am trying to reduce unnecessary screen time.

---

# Part 14 — 💪 Good Habits

### Question:

**What good habits do you have?**

### Answer:

> I try to wake up early and plan my day. I practice English regularly and spend time learning technical skills. I also try to keep my work organized and complete important tasks on time.

### Useful vocabulary:

* wake up early
* exercise regularly
* read books
* drink enough water
* study regularly
* plan the day
* manage time
* sleep properly

---

# Part 15 — ❌ Bad Habits

### Question:

**Do you have any bad habits?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have a few bad habits. Sometimes I spend too much time on my phone, especially when I am supposed to study. I also sometimes postpone difficult tasks. I am trying to improve by setting clear priorities and limiting distractions.

### Important phrase:

> **I am trying to improve...**

এটা খুব useful।

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 "Used to"

Past habit describe করতে:

> **I used to...**

Example:

> I used to wake up late.

= আগে দেরিতে উঠতাম।

> I used to watch TV every evening.

> I used to spend a lot of time on social media.

### Present:

> Now I wake up early.

### Full:

> I used to wake up late, but now I usually wake up early.

---

# Part 17 — 🕐 Busy Day

### Question:

**What do you do when you have a busy day?**

### Answer:

> When I have a busy day, I first identify the most important tasks. Then I make a simple schedule and prioritize my work. I try to avoid unnecessary distractions and focus on one task at a time. If necessary, I take short breaks so that I can stay productive.

---

# Part 18 — 🚀 Productivity

### Question:

**How do you stay productive?**

### Answer:

> I try to plan my day before I start working. I usually focus on the most important task first. I keep my phone away when I need to concentrate and take short breaks between tasks. I also try to avoid multitasking because it can reduce my focus.

---

# Part 19 — 🔥 Follow-up Conversation

### Q1:

**What time do you wake up?**

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m.

### Q2:

**What do you do first?**

> I brush my teeth and wash my face.

### Q3:

**What do you do after breakfast?**

> I usually start studying or working.

### Q4:

**How long do you study?**

> I usually study for several hours.

### Q5:

**Do you take breaks?**

> Yes, I take short breaks between tasks.

### Q6:

**What do you do in the evening?**

> I usually relax, practice English, or work on personal projects.

### Q7:

**What time do you sleep?**

> I usually go to bed around 11 p.m.

### Q8:

**Would you like to change your routine?**

> Yes. I would like to reduce my screen time and exercise more regularly.

---

# Part 20 — ⭐ Compare Two Routines

### Question:

**How is your current routine different from your past routine?**

### Answer:

> My current routine is more organized than my old routine. I used to wake up late and spend a lot of time on my phone. Now I try to wake up earlier and plan my day. I also spend more time studying and learning new skills. However, I still need to improve my sleep schedule and exercise habits.

---

# Part 21 — 🎤 3-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Daily Routine**

এই structure follow করবে:

### Morning

> I usually wake up...

### After waking up

> First, I...

### Breakfast

> Then, I...

### Study/Work

> After that, I...

### Afternoon

> In the afternoon...

### Evening

> In the evening...

### Night

> At night...

### Sleep

> Finally, I...

### Habits

> I usually...

> I sometimes...

> I rarely...

### Improvement

> I would like to...

---

# Part 22 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. After getting up, I brush my teeth, wash my face, and have breakfast. Then I usually spend some time planning my day. I try to start with the most important task because I can concentrate better in the morning.
>
> During the day, I spend several hours studying or working. I usually take short breaks between tasks so that I don't lose concentration. In the afternoon, I have lunch and continue with my work. Sometimes I check my phone or take a short break.
>
> In the evening, I usually practice English, work on personal projects, or spend time with my family. After dinner, I sometimes watch something or read for a while. Before going to bed, I usually think about what I need to do the next day.
>
> I normally go to bed around 11 p.m. My routine is not perfect, though. Sometimes I spend too much time on my phone. I am trying to reduce my screen time and become more productive. In the future, I would also like to exercise more regularly and maintain a healthier routine.

**⚠️ এটা মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের actual routine দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# Part 23 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

এখন 5–7 sentence answer দেওয়ার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **Why is having a daily routine important?**

2. **Is waking up early really better than waking up late?**

3. **How can people become more productive?**

4. **What is the biggest waste of time in your daily life?**

5. **How does social media affect people's daily routines?**

6. **Should people follow a strict daily schedule?**

7. **What habit would you most like to change? Why?**

8. **How has your daily routine changed over the last few years?**

# 📝 Lesson 14 Homework

## Task 1 — Speaking 🎤

এই 10টি question-এর answer **মুখে** বলবে:

1. What time do you wake up?
2. What do you do after waking up?
3. What do you do in the morning?
4. What do you do in the afternoon?
5. What do you do in the evening?
6. What time do you go to bed?
7. How often do you exercise?
8. How much time do you spend on your phone?
9. What good habits do you have?
10. What habit would you like to change?

---

## Task 2 — Question Making 🔥

নিজে **10টি Daily Routine questions** তৈরি করবে।

এই question words ব্যবহার করার চেষ্টা করো:

> **What / When / Where / How / How often / How long / Why**

Example:

> What time do you wake up?

> How long do you study?

> How often do you exercise?

> Why do you wake up early?

---

## Task 3 — 3-Minute Challenge

Topic:

> **"Describe your daily routine from morning to night."**

Minimum **3 minutes** কথা বলবে।

এই words ব্যবহার করার চেষ্টা করবে:

**First → Then → After that → Usually → Sometimes → However → Finally → In the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 14 Golden Formula

Daily routine বলার সময়:

> **Time → Activity → Frequency → Reason → Next Activity**

Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. First, I freshen up and have breakfast. Then I start studying. I usually study for several hours, but I take short breaks between tasks. In the evening, I practice English and work on personal projects. Finally, I have dinner and go to bed around 11 p.m.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি এই question-এর উত্তর **3 মিনিটের বেশি** বলতে পারো:

> **"Tell me about your typical day."**

আর একটা গুরুত্বপূর্ণ rule মনে রাখবে:

**Routine = Present Simple**

> I wake up.
> I study.
> I work.
> I eat.
> I exercise.
> I sleep.

কিন্তু **এখন যা করছি**:

> I am studying now.
> I am working now.

এটাই Lesson 14-এর সবচেয়ে important grammar + speaking connection।

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 15

## Topic: Hobbies, Free Time & Entertainment 🎮🎵📚🎬

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **hobbies, free time, movies, music, games, books, internet এবং weekend activities** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের main goal:

> **নিজের পছন্দ, অপছন্দ এবং free-time activities নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট naturally কথা বলতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 15 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Hobbies নিয়ে 25টি important question
* নিজের hobby describe করা
* **like / love / enjoy / prefer / be interested in** ব্যবহার
* Free time নিয়ে কথা বলা
* Movie, music, book describe করা
* **Present Simple + Present Continuous** ব্যবহার
* Preference explain করা
* Opinion দেওয়া
* Follow-up question করা
* 3–5 মিনিটের speaking practice

---

# Part 1 — 🎮 25 Important Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **What do you do in your free time?**
2. **What are your hobbies?**
3. **What is your favorite hobby?**
4. **How often do you do your hobbies?**
5. **When did you start this hobby?**
6. **Why do you enjoy this hobby?**
7. **Do you have any indoor hobbies?**
8. **Do you have any outdoor hobbies?**
9. **What do you usually do on weekends?**
10. **Do you prefer spending time alone or with friends?**

---

## Movies, Music & Books

11. **What kind of movies do you like?**
12. **What is your favorite movie?**
13. **What kind of music do you listen to?**
14. **Who is your favorite singer or band?**
15. **Do you like watching TV series?**
16. **What is your favorite TV series?**
17. **Do you enjoy reading books?**
18. **What kind of books do you like?**
19. **What was the last movie you watched?**
20. **What was the last book you read?**

---

## Opinion & Lifestyle

21. **Do you think hobbies are important?**
22. **How do you usually relax?**
23. **Do you spend too much time on your phone?**
24. **Would you like to learn a new hobby?**
25. **What hobby would you like to try in the future?**

# Part 2 — 🎯 Hobby Vocabulary

| English            | বাংলা         |
| ------------------ | ------------- |
| Hobby              | শখ            |
| Free time          | অবসর সময়     |
| Entertainment      | বিনোদন        |
| Reading            | পড়া          |
| Writing            | লেখা          |
| Drawing            | ছবি আঁকা      |
| Painting           | ছবি আঁকা      |
| Photography        | ফটোগ্রাফি     |
| Gardening          | বাগান করা     |
| Cooking            | রান্না করা    |
| Traveling          | ভ্রমণ         |
| Gaming             | গেম খেলা      |
| Watching movies    | সিনেমা দেখা   |
| Listening to music | গান শোনা      |
| Playing sports     | খেলাধুলা করা  |
| Cycling            | সাইকেল চালানো |
| Swimming           | সাঁতার        |
| Walking            | হাঁটা         |
| Collecting         | সংগ্রহ করা    |
| Blogging           | ব্লগ লেখা     |
