# Part 3 — ⭐ Like / Love / Enjoy

এই তিনটা speaking-এ খুব common।

### Like

> I like reading books.

### Love

> I love watching movies.

### Enjoy

> I enjoy playing games.

⚠️ **Enjoy-এর পরে verb + ing**

> I enjoy read ❌

> I enjoy reading ✅

> I enjoy play games ❌

> I enjoy playing games ✅

---

# Part 4 — 🔥 "Be Interested In"

কোনো বিষয়ে interest বোঝাতে:

> I am interested in photography.

> I am interested in technology.

> I am interested in learning languages.

⚠️ **in-এর পরে noun / verb-ing**

> I am interested in learn English. ❌

> I am interested in learning English. ✅

---

# Part 5 — 🧠 Prefer

দুটি activity compare করতে:

> I prefer reading to watching TV.

> I prefer playing games to watching movies.

> I prefer outdoor activities to indoor activities.

### Question:

**Do you prefer watching movies or reading books?**

### Answer:

> I prefer watching movies because they are more entertaining for me. However, I also enjoy reading when I have enough time.

---

# Part 6 — 🎮 Talking About Your Hobby

### Question:

**What is your favorite hobby?**

### Beginner:

> My favorite hobby is watching movies.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite hobby is watching movies. I usually watch movies in my free time, especially on weekends.

### Advanced:

> One of my favorite hobbies is watching movies. I usually watch movies when I have free time, especially at the weekend. I enjoy different genres, but I particularly like action and science-fiction movies because they are exciting and imaginative.

---

# Part 7 — 📚 Reading Books

### Question:

**Do you enjoy reading books?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I enjoy reading books when I have enough free time. I particularly like books about technology, personal development, and fiction. Reading helps me learn new things and improve my vocabulary. I don't read every day, but I try to read regularly.

---

# Part 8 — 🎬 Movies

### Question:

**What kind of movies do you like?**

Useful genres:

* Action
* Comedy
* Drama
* Horror
* Romance
* Thriller
* Science fiction
* Documentary
* Animation
* Adventure

### Answer:

> I enjoy action and science-fiction movies. I like them because they are exciting and often have interesting stories. I also sometimes watch comedies when I want to relax.

---

# Part 9 — 🎵 Music

### Question:

**What kind of music do you listen to?**

### Answer:

> I listen to different types of music depending on my mood. I usually listen to music when I am relaxing or working. I prefer songs with meaningful lyrics and a good melody.

### Useful expressions:

> I listen to music every day.

> I usually listen to music while working.

> Music helps me relax.

> My favorite type of music is...

---

# Part 10 — 📱 Phone & Entertainment

### Question:

**How do you spend your free time?**

### Answer:

> I usually spend my free time watching movies, listening to music, browsing the internet, and learning new things. Sometimes I work on personal projects or practice English. On weekends, I may spend more time with friends or family.

---

# Part 11 — 🔥 "What Did You Do Last Weekend?"

এখানে **Past Simple** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Question:

> What did you do last weekend?

### Answer:

> Last weekend, I stayed at home for most of the time. I watched a movie, listened to music, and worked on a personal project. In the evening, I spent some time with my family. It was a relaxing weekend.

Notice:

> **Last weekend → Past Simple**

---

# Part 12 — 🧠 Present vs Past

### Regular hobby:

> I watch movies on weekends.

**Present Simple**

### Yesterday:

> I watched a movie yesterday.

**Past Simple**

### Right now:

> I am watching a movie now.

**Present Continuous**

---

# Part 13 — 🔥 Hobby Conversation

### Q1:

**What is your hobby?**

> I enjoy watching movies and learning new things.

### Q2:

**How often do you do it?**

> I usually do it several times a week.

### Q3:

**When did you start?**

> I started this hobby several years ago.

### Q4:

**Why do you like it?**

> Because it helps me relax and learn new things.

### Q5:

**Do you do it alone?**

> Usually, yes, but sometimes I do it with friends.

### Q6:

**Would you like to try another hobby?**

> Yes, I'd like to learn photography in the future.

---

# Part 14 — 🌳 Indoor vs Outdoor Hobbies

### Question:

**Do you prefer indoor or outdoor activities?**

### Answer:

> I enjoy both, but I probably prefer outdoor activities because they help me stay active. I like walking and traveling when I have enough time. However, I also enjoy indoor activities such as watching movies, reading, and using my computer.

---

# Part 15 — 🧘 How Do You Relax?

### Question:

**How do you usually relax?**

### Answer:

> When I want to relax, I usually listen to music or watch a movie. Sometimes I take a walk or spend time with my family. If I have a lot of stress, I try to stay away from my phone for a while and do something quiet.

---

# Part 16 — ⭐ Why Are Hobbies Important?

### Question:

**Do you think hobbies are important?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I think hobbies are important because they help people relax and reduce stress. They also give us an opportunity to learn new skills and develop our interests. For example, reading can improve knowledge and vocabulary, while sports can improve physical health. So, I think everyone should have at least one activity they enjoy.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 New Hobby

### Question:

**What hobby would you like to learn in the future?**

### Answer:

> I would like to learn photography in the future. I think photography is a creative and useful skill. It would allow me to capture interesting moments when I travel or spend time with friends. I would probably start by learning the basics and practicing with my phone or camera.

---

# Part 18 — 🧠 "Would Like To"

Future desire:

> I would like to learn photography.

> I would like to travel more.

> I would like to learn guitar.

> I would like to start reading more books.

### Difference:

> I like photography.

= photography পছন্দ করি।

> I would like to learn photography.

= photography শিখতে চাই।

---

# Part 19 — 🎤 3–5 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Hobbies and Free Time**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Hobbies

> My main hobbies are...

### 2. Favorite

> My favorite hobby is...

### 3. Frequency

> I usually...

### 4. When

> I normally do it...

### 5. Reason

> I enjoy it because...

### 6. Entertainment

> I also enjoy...

### 7. Weekend

> On weekends...

### 8. Relaxation

> When I want to relax...

### 9. New hobby

> I would like to learn...

### 10. Opinion

> I think hobbies are important because...

---

# Part 20 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I have several hobbies that I enjoy in my free time. My main hobbies are watching movies, listening to music, learning new things, and working on personal projects. Among these activities, I particularly enjoy watching movies because it helps me relax.
>
> I usually watch movies on weekends or whenever I have some free time. I enjoy action, adventure, and science-fiction movies because I like exciting stories and interesting ideas. I also listen to music when I am working or relaxing.
>
> Apart from entertainment, I like learning new things. Sometimes I read articles, watch educational videos, or practice English. I think learning can also be a hobby if you genuinely enjoy it.
>
> On weekends, I usually have more free time, so I may watch a movie, work on a personal project, or spend time with my family. When I feel stressed, I usually listen to music or take a walk.
>
> In the future, I would like to learn photography. I think it would be a creative and useful skill, especially when traveling. Overall, I believe hobbies are important because they help us relax, develop new skills, and enjoy our free time.

**⚠️ এটা মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের hobbies দিয়ে replace করবে।**

---

# Part 21 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

প্রতিটি question-এর answer **5–7 sentences** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

### 1.

**Why do people need hobbies?**

### 2.

**Can hobbies become careers?**

### 3.

**Is watching movies a good way to spend free time?**

### 4.

**Do people spend too much time on entertainment nowadays?**

### 5.

**How has technology changed the way people spend their free time?**

### 6.

**Is reading better than watching videos for learning?**

### 7.

**Should children spend less time playing video games?**

### 8.

**What is the difference between a hobby and a waste of time?**

---

# Part 22 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

Speaking fluency বাড়ানোর জন্য এই technique খুব important।

### Main Question:

> **What do you do in your free time?**

### Answer:

> I usually watch movies and listen to music.

### Follow-up:

> **What kind of movies do you like?**

> I like action and science-fiction movies.

### Follow-up:

> **Why do you like them?**

> Because they are exciting and entertaining.

### Follow-up:

> **When do you usually watch them?**

> Mostly on weekends.

### Follow-up:

> **Do you watch them alone?**

> Usually, yes, but sometimes I watch them with friends.

### Follow-up:

> **Would you like to try another hobby?**

> Yes, I'd like to learn photography.

👉 **একটা question → 5টা follow-up → 2–3 মিনিট conversation।**

এটাই speaking fluency তৈরির সবচেয়ে important practiceগুলোর একটি।

---

# 📝 Lesson 15 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What are your hobbies?
2. What is your favorite hobby?
3. Why do you enjoy it?
4. How often do you do it?
5. What do you do on weekends?
6. What kind of movies do you like?
7. What kind of music do you listen to?
8. How do you usually relax?
9. Do you prefer indoor or outdoor activities?
10. What new hobby would you like to learn?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Hobby & Entertainment questions** তৈরি করবে।

Try to use:

> **What → Why → When → Where → How often → How long → Who → Which**

Example:

> What do you do in your free time?

> Why do you enjoy it?

> How often do you do it?

> Who do you usually do it with?

---

## Task 3 — 🎯 5-Minute Challenge

Topic:

> **"My Hobbies, Free Time and Entertainment"**

কমপক্ষে **5 মিনিট** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

এই connectors ব্যবহার করবে:

**because → also → however → for example → usually → sometimes → although → in my opinion → in the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 15 Golden Formula

Hobby topic-এ:

> **What → When → How often → Why → With whom → Example → Future**

Example:

> I enjoy watching movies in my free time. I usually watch them on weekends. I like action and science-fiction movies because they are exciting. Sometimes I watch movies with my friends. For example, we often watch a movie together when we have free time. In the future, I would like to learn photography as a new hobby.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে যেন তুমি এই question-এর উত্তর **3–5 মিনিট** naturally দিতে পারো:

> **"What do you do in your free time, and what are your hobbies?"**

আর Lesson 15-এর সবচেয়ে important speaking habit:

> **একটি answer দিয়ে থেমে যাবে না।**

**Answer → Reason → Example → Personal experience → Future**

এই pattern ব্যবহার করলে তোমার উত্তর ছোট থেকে ধীরে ধীরে **long, natural এবং conversational** হবে।

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 16

## Topic: Food, Cooking & Eating Habits 🍚🍳🥗

আজকের lesson-এ তুমি **food, favorite খাবার, cooking, restaurant, eating habits, healthy food এবং food preferences** নিয়ে English-এ কথা বলা শিখবে।

আজকের main goal:

> **Food নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট naturally কথা বলতে পারা + restaurant-এ basic English conversation করতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 16 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Food নিয়ে 25টি important speaking questions
* Favorite food describe করা
* Taste describe করা
* Cooking নিয়ে কথা বলা
* Restaurant conversation
* Food order করা
* Healthy vs unhealthy food নিয়ে opinion
* **like / dislike / prefer / would rather**
* **some / any / much / many**
* Food নিয়ে follow-up questions
* 3–5 মিনিট speaking practice

---

# Part 1 — 🍚 25 Food Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **What is your favorite food?**
2. **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**
3. **What do you usually eat for lunch?**
4. **What do you usually eat for dinner?**
5. **What is your favorite fruit?**
6. **What is your favorite vegetable?**
7. **Do you like spicy food?**
8. **What kind of food do you dislike?**
9. **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**
10. **How often do you eat outside?**

## Cooking

11. **Can you cook?**
12. **What can you cook?**
13. **Who usually cooks in your family?**
14. **Do you enjoy cooking?**
15. **What would you like to learn to cook?**

## Restaurant & Eating Habits

16. **How often do you go to restaurants?**
17. **What kind of restaurants do you like?**
18. **What do you usually order at a restaurant?**
19. **What is the most delicious food you have ever eaten?**
20. **Have you ever tried foreign food?**

## Opinion

21. **What makes food delicious?**
22. **Is healthy food always tasty?**
23. **Why do people eat fast food?**
24. **What are your eating habits?**
25. **What food would you like to try in the future?**

---

# Part 2 — 🍎 Food Vocabulary

| English   | বাংলা              |
| --------- | ------------------ |
| Food      | খাবার              |
| Meal      | খাবারের সময়/খাবার |
| Breakfast | সকালের নাশতা       |
| Lunch     | দুপুরের খাবার      |
| Dinner    | রাতের খাবার        |
| Snack     | হালকা খাবার        |
| Rice      | ভাত                |
| Bread     | রুটি/পাউরুটি       |
| Meat      | মাংস               |
| Chicken   | মুরগি              |
| Beef      | গরুর মাংস          |
| Fish      | মাছ                |
| Egg       | ডিম                |
| Vegetable | সবজি               |
| Fruit     | ফল                 |
| Soup      | স্যুপ              |
| Salad     | সালাদ              |
| Noodles   | নুডলস              |
| Dessert   | মিষ্টান্ন          |
| Spice     | মসলা               |

---

# Part 3 — 😋 Taste Vocabulary

খাবার describe করার জন্য taste vocabulary খুব important।

### Sweet

> The cake is sweet.

মিষ্টি

### Salty

> The soup is a little salty.

লবণাক্ত

### Spicy

> This curry is very spicy.

ঝাল

### Sour

> The lemon is sour.

টক

### Bitter

> The medicine tastes bitter.

তিতা

### Delicious

> The food is delicious.

সুস্বাদু

### Tasty

> This chicken is really tasty.

### Fresh

> The vegetables are fresh.

### Crispy

> The chicken is crispy.

### Soft

> The bread is soft.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ Favorite Food

### Question:

> **What is your favorite food?**

### Beginner:

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite food is biryani. I like it because it is delicious and flavorful. I usually eat it on special occasions.

### Advanced:

> Biryani is one of my favorite foods. I enjoy it because it has a rich flavor and a combination of spices, rice, and meat. I especially like having it with salad or a cold drink. I don't eat it every day, but I really enjoy it when I have the opportunity.

---

# Part 5 — 🍳 Can You Cook?

### Question:

> **Can you cook?**

### Beginner:

> Yes, I can cook a few things.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I can cook some simple dishes, such as eggs, noodles, rice, and chicken.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I can prepare a few simple dishes. I'm not an expert cook, but I can make basic meals when necessary. I'd like to improve my cooking skills because I think cooking is an important life skill.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Cooking Vocabulary

| English | বাংলা          |
| ------- | -------------- |
| Cook    | রান্না করা     |
| Boil    | সিদ্ধ করা      |
| Fry     | ভাজা           |
| Bake    | বেক করা        |
| Grill   | গ্রিল করা      |
| Chop    | কাটা           |
| Cut     | কাটা           |
| Mix     | মেশানো         |
| Add     | যোগ করা        |
| Wash    | ধোয়া          |
| Peel    | খোসা ছাড়ানো   |
| Serve   | পরিবেশন করা    |
| Taste   | স্বাদ নেওয়া   |
| Prepare | প্রস্তুত করা   |
| Recipe  | রান্নার রেসিপি |

### Example:

> First, wash the vegetables.

> Then, chop them.

> Add some oil and fry the vegetables.

> Finally, serve the food.

---

# Part 7 — 🥘 Describe a Dish

যেকোনো food describe করার formula:

> **Name → Ingredients → Taste → When → Why**

### Example:

> Biryani is a popular rice dish. It usually contains rice, meat, spices, and sometimes potatoes. It has a rich and spicy flavor. People often eat it during special occasions or family gatherings. I like it because it is delicious and filling.

---

# Part 8 — 🏠 Homemade vs Restaurant Food

### Question:

> **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer homemade food because it is usually healthier and cleaner. I also like the taste of homemade food. However, I enjoy restaurant food occasionally because it gives me a chance to try different dishes.

### Useful structure:

> **I prefer X because... However, I also...**

---

# Part 9 — 🍔 Fast Food

### Question:

> **Why do people eat fast food?**

### Answer:

> I think people eat fast food because it is convenient, quick, and often tasty. People who have busy schedules may not have enough time to cook. However, eating too much fast food can be unhealthy, so I think it is better to eat it occasionally.

---

# Part 10 — 🥗 Healthy Food

### Question:

> **What kind of food is healthy?**

### Answer:

> I think fresh vegetables, fruits, fish, eggs, and other nutritious foods are healthy. Drinking enough water is also important. I try to maintain a balanced diet rather than eating only one type of food.

### Useful words:

* Healthy
* Unhealthy
* Nutritious
* Balanced diet
* Fresh
* Protein
* Vitamins
* Calories
* Sugar
* Fat

---

# Part 11 — 🍟 Healthy vs Unhealthy

### Question:

> **Is healthy food always tasty?**

### Answer:

> Not necessarily. Some healthy foods are very tasty, but some people may find them less exciting than fast food. I think the way food is prepared makes a big difference. Healthy food can be delicious if it is prepared properly.

---

# Part 12 — 🧠 Like / Dislike / Prefer

### Like:

> I like fish.

### Love:

> I love biryani.

### Don't like:

> I don't like bitter food.

### Hate:

> I hate extremely spicy food.

### Prefer:

> I prefer rice to bread.

### Would rather:

> I'd rather eat at home than eat outside.

---

# Part 13 — ⭐ "Would Rather"

দুটি option-এর মধ্যে preference:

> **I'd rather eat at home.**

> **I'd rather drink water than soft drinks.**

> **I'd rather cook than order food.**

### Pattern:

> **I'd rather + verb + than + verb**

Example:

> I'd rather cook at home than eat at a restaurant.

---

# Part 14 — 🍽️ Restaurant Conversation

### Customer:

> Excuse me, can I see the menu, please?

### Waiter:

> Sure. Here you are.

### Customer:

> What do you recommend?

### Waiter:

> I'd recommend the chicken biryani.

### Customer:

> Okay. I'll have the chicken biryani.

### Waiter:

> Would you like anything to drink?

### Customer:

> Yes, I'd like a bottle of water.

### Waiter:

> Anything else?

### Customer:

> No, that's all. Thank you.

---

# Part 15 — 🔥 Restaurant Questions

Restaurant-এ এগুলো practice করবে:

> Can I see the menu, please?

> What do you recommend?

> What is today's special?

> Is this spicy?

> Does this contain meat?

> Can I have some water?

> I'd like to order...

> Can I get the bill, please?

> Can I pay by card?

> Can I have this to go?

---

# Part 16 — 💳 Asking for the Bill

### Waiter:

> Was everything okay?

### Customer:

> Yes, everything was great.

### Customer:

> Could I have the bill, please?

### Waiter:

> Certainly.

### Customer:

> Can I pay by card?

### Waiter:

> Yes, of course.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Food Complaint

যদি food ভালো না হয়:

### Customer:

> Excuse me, I have a problem with my order.

### Waiter:

> What's wrong?

### Customer:

> I ordered chicken, but this is beef.

অথবা:

> This food is too spicy for me.

> My food is cold.

> I think this isn't what I ordered.

এগুলো politeভাবে বলতে হবে।

---

# Part 18 — 🧠 Some / Any

Food-related English-এ খুব useful।

### Some

Positive sentence:

> I have some rice.

> There is some milk in the fridge.

### Any

Question / negative:

> Do you have any juice?

> I don't have any milk.

### Examples:

> Would you like some water?

> Do you have any vegetarian food?

> I don't have any vegetables.

---

# Part 19 — Much / Many

### Much → uncountable

> How much rice do you eat?

> How much water do you drink?

> How much milk do we need?

### Many → countable

> How many eggs do you need?

> How many apples do you eat?

> How many restaurants are nearby?

---

# Part 20 — 🔥 Food Experience

### Question:

> **What is the most delicious food you have ever eaten?**

### Answer:

> One of the most delicious foods I have ever eaten was biryani at a family gathering. It was flavorful, fresh, and perfectly cooked. The meat was very tender, and the rice had a great combination of spices. I really enjoyed it because I was also spending time with my family.

Notice:

> **have ever eaten**

এটা **Present Perfect**।

---

# Part 21 — 🌍 Foreign Food

### Question:

> **Have you ever tried foreign food?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I have tried a few foreign dishes. I have tried pizza, pasta, burgers, and noodles. I enjoy trying new food because it gives me an opportunity to experience different flavors. I'd like to try more international dishes in the future.

---

# Part 22 — 🎤 3–5 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Food Habits**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Favorite food

> My favorite food is...

### 2. Taste

> It tastes...

### 3. Ingredients

> It contains...

### 4. Frequency

> I usually eat it...

### 5. Homemade/restaurant

> I prefer...

### 6. Cooking

> I can cook...

### 7. Healthy habits

> I try to...

### 8. Unhealthy habits

> Sometimes I...

### 9. Restaurant

> When I go to a restaurant...

### 10. Future

> I'd like to try...

---

# Part 23 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> Food is an important part of my daily life. I enjoy different kinds of food, but one of my favorite dishes is biryani. I like it because it has a rich flavor and a combination of rice, meat, and spices. I usually eat it on special occasions rather than every day.
>
> For my regular meals, I usually eat rice, vegetables, fish, eggs, or chicken. I generally prefer homemade food because it is healthier and I can control the ingredients. However, I sometimes eat at restaurants when I am busy or when I want to try something different.
>
> I can cook a few simple dishes, such as eggs, noodles, rice, and chicken. I am not an expert cook, but I would like to improve my cooking skills because cooking is a useful life skill.
>
> I also try to maintain healthy eating habits. I try to eat fresh food and drink enough water. However, I sometimes eat fast food because it is convenient and tasty. I think the key is to maintain a balance rather than completely avoiding the food we enjoy.
>
> In the future, I'd like to try more international dishes and learn how to cook different types of food.

---

# Part 24 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–7 sentences** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **Why is food important in people's culture?**

2. **How have people's eating habits changed over time?**

3. **Why do people prefer fast food despite knowing it may be unhealthy?**

4. **Is cooking an important life skill? Why?**

5. **Should schools teach children about healthy eating?**

6. **Is homemade food always healthier than restaurant food?**

7. **How does social media influence people's food choices?**

8. **Why do people enjoy trying food from different cultures?**

---

# Part 25 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

Speaking fluency-এর জন্য আজকের সবচেয়ে important practice।

### Main Question:

> **What is your favorite food?**

### Answer:

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Follow-up 1:

> **Why do you like it?**

> Because it is flavorful and delicious.

### Follow-up 2:

> **How often do you eat it?**

> I usually eat it once or twice a month.

### Follow-up 3:

> **Can you cook it?**

> No, I can't cook it very well yet.

### Follow-up 4:

> **Would you like to learn?**

> Yes, I'd like to learn how to cook it.

### Follow-up 5:

> **Do you prefer homemade or restaurant biryani?**

> I prefer homemade biryani because I like the taste.

🔥 **একটা question থেকে 5–6টা follow-up তৈরি করা = speaking fluency practice.**

---

# 📝 Lesson 16 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What is your favorite food?
2. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
3. What do you usually eat for lunch?
4. Do you like spicy food?
5. Can you cook?
6. What can you cook?
7. Do you prefer homemade or restaurant food?
8. How often do you eat fast food?
9. What is the healthiest food you eat?
10. What food would you like to try?

---

## Task 2 — 🧠 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Food-related questions** তৈরি করবে।

এই words ব্যবহার করো:

> **What / Why / When / Where / How / How often / How much / How many / Which / Have**

Example:

> What is your favorite food?

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you eat it?

> How much water do you drink every day?

> Have you ever tried foreign food?

---

# 🎯 Task 3 — Restaurant Role Play

তুমি **Customer**, আমি **Waiter**—এই situation practice করবে।

তোমার conversation শুরু হবে:

> **Excuse me, can I see the menu, please?**

তারপর conversation চালিয়ে যাবে:

**Menu → Recommendation → Order → Drink → Problem → Bill → Payment**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 16 Golden Formula

Food topic-এ answer expand করার formula:

> **Food → Taste → Ingredients → Frequency → Reason → Experience → Opinion → Future**

Example:

> My favorite food is biryani. It is spicy and flavorful. It contains rice, meat, and different spices. I usually eat it on special occasions. I like it because it is delicious. I once had a very good biryani at a family gathering. In my opinion, homemade food is generally healthier. In the future, I'd like to learn how to cook it myself.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে তুমি যেন এই দুইটা situation confidently handle করতে পারো:

**1.**

> **"Tell me about your favorite food and eating habits."**

**2.**

> **"Imagine you are in a restaurant. Order a meal in English."**

**Lesson 16-এর core skill:**

> **Question → Answer → Reason → Example → Follow-up Question**

এই pattern এখন থেকে প্রতিটি lesson-এ ব্যবহার করবে।

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 16

## Topic: Travel, Places & Transportation ✈️🚌🚆

তুমি আবার **Lesson 16** চেয়েছ, তাই এবার আমরা course-এর পরবর্তী topic হিসেবে **Travel, Places & Transportation** নিচ্ছি।

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য:

> **কোথাও যাওয়া, travel experience বলা, transportation নিয়ে কথা বলা এবং কোনো place describe করা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 16 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Travel নিয়ে 25টি important question
* নিজের travel experience বলা
* কোনো place describe করা
* Transportation নিয়ে কথা বলা
* Direction জিজ্ঞেস করা
* Hotel/transport situation handle করা
* **Past Simple** দিয়ে previous trip বলা
* **Future** দিয়ে future travel plan বলা
* Comparison ও opinion দেওয়া
* 3–5 মিনিট travel speaking

---

# Part 1 — ✈️ 25 Travel Questions

## Basic

1. **Do you like traveling?**
2. **How often do you travel?**
3. **Where do you usually go?**
4. **What is your favorite place?**
5. **What is the most beautiful place you have visited?**
6. **Who do you usually travel with?**
7. **Do you prefer traveling alone or with others?**
8. **What kind of places do you like visiting?**
9. **How do you usually travel?**
10. **What is your favorite means of transportation?**

## Travel Experience

11. **Where did you go on your last trip?**
12. **When did you go there?**
13. **Who did you go with?**
14. **How did you get there?**
15. **What did you do there?**
16. **What did you like about the place?**
17. **What was the most interesting thing about the trip?**
18. **Did you face any problems during the trip?**

## Future & Opinion

19. **Where would you like to travel in the future?**
20. **Why would you like to go there?**
21. **Would you like to travel abroad?**
22. **What country would you like to visit?**
23. **Is traveling expensive?**
24. **What are the benefits of traveling?**
25. **What makes a trip memorable?**

---

# Part 2 — 🗺️ Travel Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা      |
| ----------- | ---------- |
| Travel      | ভ্রমণ করা  |
| Trip        | ভ্রমণ      |
| Journey     | যাত্রা     |
| Destination | গন্তব্য    |
| Tourist     | পর্যটক     |
| Tourism     | পর্যটন     |
| Vacation    | ছুটি/অবকাশ |
| Holiday     | ছুটি       |
| Beach       | সমুদ্রসৈকত |
| Mountain    | পাহাড়     |
| Forest      | বন         |
| River       | নদী        |
| Island      | দ্বীপ      |
| Museum      | জাদুঘর     |
| Hotel       | হোটেল      |
| Airport     | বিমানবন্দর |
| Station     | স্টেশন     |
| Ticket      | টিকিট      |
| Luggage     | লাগেজ      |
| Passport    | পাসপোর্ট   |

---

# Part 3 — 🚌 Transportation Vocabulary

> Bus
> Train
> Car
> Taxi
> Motorcycle
> Bicycle
> Rickshaw
> Boat
> Ship
> Airplane

### Useful verbs:

> take a bus

> catch a train

> ride a bicycle

> drive a car

> fly by plane

> walk

### Examples:

> I usually take the bus.

> I sometimes travel by train.

> I prefer traveling by train.

> I usually go there by car.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ How Do You Usually Travel?

### Question:

**How do you usually travel?**

### Beginner:

> I usually travel by bus.

### Intermediate:

> I usually travel by bus because it is affordable and convenient.

### Advanced:

> I usually travel by bus for short or medium-distance trips because it is relatively affordable and easily available. However, when I travel long distances, I prefer trains or airplanes because they are generally more comfortable and faster.

---

# Part 5 — 🏞️ Describe a Place

কোনো place describe করার formula:

> **Location → Size → Environment → Attractions → Activities → Opinion**

### Example:

> Cox's Bazar is a popular tourist destination in Bangladesh. It is famous for its long natural sea beach. The area has many hotels, restaurants, and tourist attractions. Visitors can enjoy walking on the beach, watching the sunset, and trying local food. I think it is a beautiful and relaxing place to visit.

---

# Part 6 — 🔥 Past Travel Experience

এখানে **Past Simple** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Question:

> **Where did you go on your last trip?**

### Answer:

> I went to Cox's Bazar last year.

Expand:

> I went to Cox's Bazar last year with some friends. We traveled by bus and stayed at a hotel near the beach. During the trip, we visited several places, walked along the beach, and enjoyed local food. We also watched the sunset. Overall, it was a very enjoyable experience.

---

# Part 7 — 🕐 Past Simple Practice

Travel experience বলার সময়:

> I **went** there.

> We **stayed** at a hotel.

> We **visited** several places.

> We **ate** local food.

> We **took** many photos.

> We **returned** home after three days.

### Important irregular verbs:

| Present | Past   |
| ------- | ------ |
| go      | went   |
| see     | saw    |
| eat     | ate    |
| take    | took   |
| come    | came   |
| buy     | bought |
| have    | had    |
| get     | got    |
| make    | made   |
| meet    | met    |

---

# Part 8 — 📸 What Did You Do There?

### Question:

**What did you do during your trip?**

### Answer:

> During the trip, I visited several interesting places. I walked around the area, took many photos, tried local food, and spent time with my friends. We also visited the beach and watched the sunset.

---

# Part 9 — 😍 What Did You Like?

### Question:

**What did you like about the place?**

### Answer:

> I liked the natural beauty of the place the most. The environment was peaceful and the scenery was beautiful. I also enjoyed the local food and spending time with my friends.

Useful:

> **What I liked most was...**

Example:

> What I liked most was the peaceful environment.

---

# Part 10 — 😕 Problems During Travel

### Question:

**Did you face any problems during the trip?**

### Answer:

> Yes, we had a small problem during the trip. Our bus was delayed for a few hours, so we arrived later than expected. However, it didn't affect the overall experience too much.

Useful expressions:

> The bus was delayed.

> My flight was canceled.

> I lost my luggage.

> The hotel was difficult to find.

> We got lost.

> The weather was bad.

---

# Part 11 — 🧭 Asking for Directions

### Useful questions:

> Excuse me, how can I get to the airport?

> Where is the nearest bus station?

> How far is the hotel from here?

> Is there a train station nearby?

> Which bus should I take?

> How long does it take to get there?

---

# Part 12 — 🚶 Giving Directions

> Go straight.

> Turn left.

> Turn right.

> Take the first left.

> Take the second right.

> It's next to the bank.

> It's opposite the hotel.

> It's behind the station.

> It's about ten minutes from here.

---

# Part 13 — 🏨 Hotel Conversation

### Guest:

> Hello. I have a reservation.

### Receptionist:

> Certainly. What's your name?

### Guest:

> My name is Atiar Rahman.

### Receptionist:

> Let me check. Yes, I have your reservation.

### Guest:

> What time is check-in?

### Receptionist:

> Check-in starts at 2 p.m.

### Guest:

> Is breakfast included?

### Receptionist:

> Yes, breakfast is included.

### Guest:

> Great. Thank you.

---

# Part 14 — ✈️ Airport Conversation

### Passenger:

> Excuse me, where is the check-in counter?

### Staff:

> It's over there, next to counter 12.

### Passenger:

> Thank you. Where is Gate 5?

### Staff:

> Go straight and turn right.

### Passenger:

> How long does it take to get there?

### Staff:

> About five minutes.

---

# Part 15 — 🧳 Luggage Vocabulary

> suitcase

> backpack

> luggage

> carry-on bag

> passport

> boarding pass

> ticket

> travel bag

### Examples:

> I have one suitcase and one backpack.

> Where can I collect my luggage?

> I forgot my passport.

> I need to check in my luggage.

---

# Part 16 — 🌍 Future Travel Plans

### Question:

**Where would you like to travel in the future?**

### Beginner:

> I'd like to visit Japan.

### Intermediate:

> I'd like to visit Japan because I am interested in its culture and technology.

### Advanced:

> I'd love to visit Japan in the future because I'm interested in its culture, technology, food, and beautiful landscapes. I'd like to visit Tokyo and Kyoto, try traditional Japanese food, and experience the local lifestyle. I think it would be a memorable trip.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 "Would Like To"

Future desire:

> I'd like to visit Japan.

> I'd like to travel around Europe.

> I'd like to visit the mountains.

> I'd like to travel with my family.

### More natural:

> **I'd love to...**

> I'd love to visit Japan.

> I'd love to travel around the world.

---

# Part 18 — 🏖️ Travel Preferences

### Question:

**Do you prefer mountains or beaches?**

### Answer:

> I prefer beaches because I enjoy the peaceful environment and beautiful scenery. I like walking along the beach and watching the sunset. However, I would also like to visit the mountains because I haven't explored many mountainous areas.

---

# Part 19 — 👤 Alone vs With Others

### Question:

**Do you prefer traveling alone or with others?**

### Answer:

> I prefer traveling with friends or family. Traveling with other people is more enjoyable because we can share experiences and help each other. However, traveling alone can also be a good experience because it gives you more freedom and independence.

---

# Part 20 — 💰 Is Traveling Expensive?

### Question:

**Is traveling expensive?**

### Answer:

> It depends on the destination and the type of trip. Traveling internationally can be expensive because of flights, hotels, food, and transportation. However, local travel can be much more affordable. I think careful planning can help reduce travel costs.

---

# Part 21 — ⭐ Benefits of Traveling

### Question:

**What are the benefits of traveling?**

### Answer:

> Traveling has many benefits. It allows people to explore new places, experience different cultures, and meet new people. It can also help people relax and reduce stress. In addition, traveling can make us more independent and open-minded.

---

# Part 22 — 🔥 Memorable Trip

### Question:

**What makes a trip memorable?**

### Answer:

> I think a trip becomes memorable because of the experiences we have rather than the place itself. Spending time with good people, seeing beautiful places, trying new food, and dealing with unexpected situations can all create lasting memories.

---

# Part 23 — 🎤 3–5 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Travel Experience**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Destination

> My last trip was to...

### 2. Time

> I went there...

### 3. People

> I went with...

### 4. Transportation

> We traveled by...

### 5. Hotel

> We stayed at...

### 6. Activities

> We visited...

### 7. Food

> We tried...

### 8. Experience

> What I liked most was...

### 9. Problem

> We had a small problem...

### 10. Opinion

> Overall, it was...

### 11. Future

> In the future, I'd like to visit...

---

# Part 24 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I really enjoy traveling because it gives me an opportunity to explore new places and take a break from my normal routine. One of my memorable trips was to Cox's Bazar. I went there with some friends, and we traveled by bus.
>
> When we arrived, we checked into a hotel near the beach. During the trip, we visited several interesting places and spent a lot of time near the sea. We walked along the beach, took many photos, tried local food, and watched the sunset.
>
> What I liked most about the trip was the natural beauty and the time I spent with my friends. We also had a small problem because our bus was delayed, but it didn't affect our overall experience.
>
> I prefer traveling with friends because we can share our experiences and have fun together. However, I think traveling alone can also be useful because it helps people become more independent.
>
> In the future, I'd love to travel abroad. I'd especially like to visit Japan because I am interested in its culture, technology, food, and beautiful places. I think traveling can help me learn many new things and create unforgettable memories.

**⚠️ Example মুখস্থ করবে না। নিজের actual experience দিয়ে বলবে।**

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

প্রতিটি question-এর answer **5–7 sentences** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **Why do people enjoy traveling?**

2. **What are the advantages of traveling alone?**

3. **What are the disadvantages of traveling alone?**

4. **Is international travel better than local travel?**

5. **How does tourism affect local communities?**

6. **Why should people experience different cultures?**

7. **Can traveling change a person's personality?**

8. **What is more important on a trip: comfort or adventure?**

---

# Part 26 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

এটা আজকের সবচেয়ে important speaking practice।

### Main Question:

> **Do you like traveling?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling.

### Follow-up:

> **Where have you traveled?**

> I've traveled to several places in Bangladesh.

### Follow-up:

> **Which place did you like most?**

> I liked Cox's Bazar the most.

### Follow-up:

> **Why?**

> Because of its beautiful beach and peaceful environment.

### Follow-up:

> **Who did you go with?**

> I went with some friends.

### Follow-up:

> **Where would you like to go next?**

> I'd like to visit Japan.

🔥 একটা simple question থেকে **6টি question-answer** তৈরি করা হলো।

এটাই speaking fluency build করার core technique।

---

# 📝 Lesson 16 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. Do you like traveling?
2. Where did you go on your last trip?
3. Who did you go with?
4. How did you travel?
5. Where did you stay?
6. What did you do there?
7. What did you like most?
8. Did you face any problems?
9. Where would you like to travel in the future?
10. Why would you like to go there?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Travel questions** তৈরি করবে।

এই words ব্যবহার করো:

> **Where → When → Who → How → What → Why → How long → How far → Which → Would**

Example:

> Where did you go?

> Who did you go with?

> How did you get there?

> How long did you stay?

> What did you do there?

> Why did you like the place?

---

# 🎯 Task 3 — 5-Minute Challenge

Topic:

> **"Describe your last trip and your future travel plans."**

কমপক্ষে **5 মিনিট** বলবে।

এই connectors ব্যবহার করবে:

**First → Then → After that → During the trip → However → Because → In my opinion → Overall → In the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 16 Golden Formula

Travel experience বলার সময়:

> **Where → When → Who → How → Where stayed → What did → What liked → Problem → Opinion → Future**

Example:

> I went to Cox's Bazar last year with my friends. We traveled by bus and stayed at a hotel near the beach. We visited several places, tried local food, and watched the sunset. We had a small problem because our bus was delayed. However, we really enjoyed the trip. In the future, I'd love to travel abroad.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে তুমি যেন confidently বলতে পারো:

> **"Tell me about your last trip."**

এবং:

> **"Where would you like to travel in the future and why?"**

**Lesson 16-এর core speaking skill:**

> **Past experience + Description + Opinion + Future plan**

এখন থেকে তোমার speaking answer শুধু **1–2 sentence** হবে না।
প্রতিটি answer-কে:

**Answer → Reason → Example → Experience → Future**

এইভাবে expand করবে।

---

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 17

## Topic: Education, School, University & Learning 🎓📚

আজকের lesson-এ আমরা **school, university, subjects, teachers, studying, exams, learning methods এবং future education goals** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য:

> **নিজের education, study life এবং learning experience নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট confidently কথা বলতে পারা।**

---

# 🎯 Lesson 17 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Education নিয়ে 25টি important question
* নিজের school/university describe করা
* Subject নিয়ে কথা বলা
* Teacher সম্পর্কে বলা
* Study habits explain করা
* Exam ও preparation নিয়ে কথা বলা
* **because / so / but / however / although** দিয়ে answer বড় করা
* Past education experience বলা
* Future learning goals বলা
* Opinion-based answer দেওয়া

---

# Part 1 — 🎓 25 Education Questions

## Basic Questions

1. **Where did you go to school?**
2. **Where do you study?**
3. **What do you study?**
4. **What is your favorite subject?**
5. **Why do you like that subject?**
6. **Who is your favorite teacher?**
7. **What is your university like?**
8. **How many subjects are you studying?**
9. **Which subject do you find difficult?**
10. **What do you usually do at university?**

## Study Habits

11. **How many hours do you study every day?**
12. **Where do you usually study?**
13. **Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**
14. **How do you prepare for exams?**
15. **What do you do when you don't understand something?**
16. **Do you take notes while studying?**
17. **How do you remember what you learn?**
18. **Do you study better in the morning or at night?**

## Opinion & Future

19. **What makes a good teacher?**
20. **What makes a good student?**
21. **Is education important for success?**
22. **Is practical knowledge more important than academic knowledge?**
23. **What would you like to learn in the future?**
24. **Would you like to study abroad?**
25. **How can education be improved?**

---

# Part 2 — 📚 Education Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা          |
| ----------- | -------------- |
| Education   | শিক্ষা         |
| School      | স্কুল          |
| College     | কলেজ           |
| University  | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয় |
| Student     | শিক্ষার্থী     |
| Teacher     | শিক্ষক         |
| Professor   | অধ্যাপক        |
| Subject     | বিষয়          |
| Course      | কোর্স          |
| Class       | ক্লাস          |
| Lecture     | বক্তৃতা/লেকচার |
| Assignment  | অ্যাসাইনমেন্ট  |
| Exam        | পরীক্ষা        |
| Test        | পরীক্ষা        |
| Result      | ফলাফল          |
| Grade       | গ্রেড          |
| Degree      | ডিগ্রি         |
| Scholarship | বৃত্তি         |
| Knowledge   | জ্ঞান          |
| Skill       | দক্ষতা         |

---

# Part 3 — 🎯 Subject নিয়ে কথা বলা

### Question:

> **What is your favorite subject?**

### Beginner:

> My favorite subject is computer science.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite subject is computer science because I enjoy programming and solving problems.

### Advanced:

> Computer science is one of my favorite subjects because it combines logical thinking with creativity. I particularly enjoy programming because it allows me to solve practical problems and build useful applications.

---

# Part 4 — ❤️ Why Do You Like a Subject?

এই formula ব্যবহার করো:

> **Subject → Reason → Example → Future**

### Example:

> I like programming because I enjoy solving problems. For example, I like building applications and working with databases. It can also help me develop useful skills for my future career.

---

# Part 5 — 😕 Difficult Subject

### Question:

> **Which subject do you find difficult?**

### Answer:

> I find mathematics somewhat difficult. Some topics require a lot of practice and concentration. However, I try to improve by practicing regularly and asking questions when I don't understand something.

### Useful expressions:

> I find it difficult.

> I'm not very good at it yet.

> It takes me more time to understand.

> I need more practice.

> I'm trying to improve.

---

# Part 6 — 👨‍🏫 Talking About Teachers

### Question:

> **What makes a good teacher?**

### Answer:

> In my opinion, a good teacher should be knowledgeable, patient, and supportive. A good teacher should explain difficult topics clearly and encourage students to ask questions. They should also understand that students learn at different speeds.

### Useful adjectives:

> knowledgeable

> patient

> friendly

> helpful

> supportive

> organized

> encouraging

> experienced

---

# Part 7 — 👨‍🎓 What Makes a Good Student?

### Answer:

> I think a good student should be responsible, curious, and consistent. A good student doesn't only memorize information but also tries to understand it. They should ask questions, practice regularly, and learn from their mistakes.

🔥 Very important sentence:

> **A good student doesn't only memorize information; they try to understand it.**

---

# Part 8 — ⏰ Study Routine

### Question:

**How many hours do you study every day?**

### Answer:

> I usually study for several hours a day, depending on my schedule. I try to divide my study time into smaller sessions and take short breaks. When I have an exam, I usually spend more time studying.

---

# Part 9 — 🧠 Study Alone or With Others?

### Question:

**Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer studying alone because I can concentrate better and study at my own pace. However, studying with others can be useful when I need to discuss difficult topics or solve problems together.

### Useful structure:

> **I prefer X because... However, Y is useful when...**

---

# Part 10 — 📝 Exam Preparation

### Question:

**How do you prepare for exams?**

### Answer:

> First, I review the topics that I need to study. Then I make a simple study plan. I read my notes, practice questions, and review difficult topics several times. Before the exam, I try to get enough sleep so that I can concentrate better.

### Sequence:

> First → Then → After that → Finally

---

# Part 11 — ❓ When You Don't Understand

### Question:

**What do you do when you don't understand something?**

### Answer:

> First, I try to study the topic again. If I still don't understand it, I search for another explanation or watch a tutorial. I may also ask a teacher or a friend for help. I think asking questions is better than pretending to understand something.

---

# Part 12 — 🗒️ Note-Taking

### Question:

**Do you take notes while studying?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I usually take notes, especially when I'm learning something difficult. Writing important points in my own words helps me understand and remember them better.

Useful:

> take notes

> write down

> highlight

> review

> summarize

---

# Part 13 — 🧠 How Do You Remember What You Learn?

### Answer:

> I try to understand the topic instead of simply memorizing it. I also review important information regularly and practice what I learn. For technical subjects, I prefer learning through practical examples because they help me remember concepts better.

---

# Part 14 — 💻 Online Learning

### Question:

**Do you like online learning?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I think online learning is very useful. It gives students access to courses, tutorials, books, and educational resources from anywhere. It is also flexible because students can learn at their own pace. However, students need discipline because there can be many distractions online.

---

# Part 15 — 🏫 Traditional vs Online Education

### Question:

**Which is better: online or traditional education?**

### Answer:

> I don't think one is always better than the other. Traditional education provides direct interaction with teachers and classmates, while online education offers more flexibility. I think a combination of both can be very effective.

🔥 Strong opinion pattern:

> **I don't think X is always better than Y.**

---

# Part 16 — 🎓 University Life

### Question:

**What is your university life like?**

### Answer:

> My university life has been a combination of studying, projects, assignments, and social activities. I have learned not only academic subjects but also practical skills. Working on projects has helped me understand how theoretical knowledge can be applied to real-world problems.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Past Education Experience

### Question:

**What was your school life like?**

এখানে Past Simple ব্যবহার করবে।

### Answer:

> My school life was an important part of my education. I studied several subjects and spent a lot of time with my classmates. I enjoyed some subjects more than others, and I also participated in different activities. Looking back, I think my school years taught me many important lessons.

---

# Part 18 — 🎯 Practical vs Academic Knowledge

### Question:

**Is practical knowledge more important than academic knowledge?**

### Answer:

> I think both are important. Academic knowledge gives us a strong foundation, while practical knowledge teaches us how to apply that knowledge in real situations. For example, understanding programming concepts is important, but building real projects helps us become better programmers. So, I believe students should develop both.

---

# Part 19 — 🌍 Study Abroad

### Question:

**Would you like to study abroad?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I would like to study abroad if I get the opportunity. Studying in another country could expose me to a different education system, culture, and learning environment. It could also help me meet people from different backgrounds and develop new skills.

---

# Part 20 — 🚀 Future Learning

### Question:

**What would you like to learn in the future?**

### Answer:

> I would like to continue improving my English communication skills and develop my technical knowledge. I also want to learn more about advanced software development and artificial intelligence. My goal is to keep learning practical skills that can help me build a successful career.

---

# Part 21 — ⭐ Education & Success

### Question:

**Is education important for success?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I believe education is important, but education alone does not guarantee success. Education gives us knowledge, skills, and opportunities, while hard work, experience, discipline, and adaptability are also important. In my opinion, successful people continue learning even after completing their formal education.

---

# Part 22 — 🔥 How Can Education Be Improved?

### Question:

**How can education be improved?**

### Answer:

> I think education should focus more on practical skills and critical thinking. Students should have more opportunities to work on real-world projects instead of only memorizing information for exams. Teachers should also encourage students to ask questions and solve problems creatively.

---

# Part 23 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Reason → Example

আজ থেকে এই pattern খুব strongly practice করবে।

### Question:

> **Why do you like programming?**

### Answer:

> I like programming.

### Reason:

> Because I enjoy solving problems.

### Example:

> For example, I like building applications and creating solutions to practical problems.

### Future:

> I think this skill will be useful for my career.

➡️ Final answer:

> **I like programming because I enjoy solving problems. For example, I like building applications and creating solutions to practical problems. I think this skill will be very useful for my future career.**

---

# Part 24 — 🎤 3–5 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Education and Learning Journey**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. School

> I studied at...

### 2. University

> Later, I studied...

### 3. Subject

> My favorite subject is...

### 4. Reason

> I like it because...

### 5. Difficult subject

> I find ... difficult because...

### 6. Study habits

> I usually study...

### 7. Exams

> Before exams, I...

### 8. Learning method

> I learn best by...

### 9. Practical knowledge

> I believe...

### 10. Future

> In the future, I would like to...

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> Education has always been an important part of my life. I studied different subjects at school, and later I continued my education at university. At university, I became more interested in technical subjects and practical learning.
>
> One of my favorite subjects is computer science because I enjoy solving problems and creating useful applications. I particularly like programming because it combines logical thinking and creativity. However, some subjects can be difficult when they require a lot of theory or practice.
>
> My study habits have changed over time. Now I try to understand concepts instead of simply memorizing information. I usually take notes, practice what I learn, and use practical examples. When I don't understand something, I search for another explanation or ask someone for help.
>
> I believe both academic and practical knowledge are important. Academic knowledge gives us a foundation, while practical experience teaches us how to apply that knowledge. That's why I think students should work on real-world projects.
>
> In the future, I want to continue learning new technical skills and improve my English communication. I believe learning should not stop after university. We should continue learning throughout our lives.

---

# Part 26 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

1. **What is the main purpose of education?**

2. **Is memorization an effective way of learning?**

3. **Should students be allowed to use AI for education?**

4. **What are the advantages of online education?**

5. **What are the disadvantages of online education?**

6. **Should universities focus more on practical skills?**

7. **What makes someone a successful learner?**

8. **Can someone be successful without a university degree?**

9. **How has technology changed education?**

10. **What skills should students learn before entering the job market?**

---

# Part 27 — 🧠 Powerful Speaking Connectors

Education topic-এ এগুলো ব্যবহার করবে:

### Adding

> **Moreover...**

> **In addition...**

> **Besides that...**

### Contrast

> **However...**

> **On the other hand...**

> **Although...**

### Example

> **For example...**

> **For instance...**

### Opinion

> **In my opinion...**

> **I believe that...**

> **From my perspective...**

### Conclusion

> **Therefore...**

> **As a result...**

> **Overall...**

# Part 28 — 📝 Lesson 17 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. Where do you study?
2. What do you study?
3. What is your favorite subject?
4. Why do you like it?
5. Which subject is difficult for you?
6. How do you prepare for exams?
7. Do you prefer studying alone or with others?
8. What makes a good teacher?
9. Is education important for success?
10. What would you like to learn in the future?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Education questions** তৈরি করবে।

এই question words ব্যবহার করো:

> **What → Why → Where → When → Who → How → How often → How long → Which → Would**

Example:

> What do you study?

> Why did you choose this subject?

> How do you prepare for exams?

> Which subject do you find difficult?

> What would you like to learn next?

# 🎯 Task 3 — 5-Minute Challenge

Topic:

> **"Tell me about your education and your future learning goals."**

কমপক্ষে **5 মিনিট** বলবে।

এই connectors ব্যবহার করবে:

**First → Then → Because → For example → However → In addition → In my opinion → Therefore → In the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 17 Golden Formula

Education topic-এ answer expand করার formula:

> **Answer → Reason → Example → Experience → Opinion → Future**

Example:

> I like programming because I enjoy solving problems. For example, I like building real-world applications. My university projects have given me practical experience. In my opinion, practical learning is very important. In the future, I want to develop more advanced technical skills.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে তুমি যেন confidently answer দিতে পারো:

> **“Tell me about your education.”**

এবং:

> **“What are your future learning goals?”**

**Lesson 17-এর core skill:**

> **Education describe + experience explain + opinion give + future goal discuss.**

---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 18

## Topic: Work, Career & Future Plans 💼🚀

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **job, career, workplace, skills, salary, dream job, interview এবং future career plans** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

আজকের মূল লক্ষ্য:

> **নিজের career, skills, job preference এবং future goals নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট confidently কথা বলতে পারা।**

# 🎯 Lesson 18 Goals

আজ তুমি শিখবে:

* Workplace describe করা
* Job preference বলা
* Career goals explain করা

# Part 1 — 💼 25 Important Questions

## Basic Questions

2. **What kind of work do you do?**
3. **Where do you work?**
4. **Do you enjoy your work?**

## Career

## Future

# Part 2 — 💼 Work Vocabulary

| English        | বাংলা             |
| -------------- | ----------------- |
| Job            | চাকরি             |
| Career         | পেশাগত জীবন       |
| Company        | কোম্পানি          |
| Employee       | কর্মচারী          |
| Employer       | নিয়োগকর্তা       |
| Manager        | ম্যানেজার         |
| Colleague      | সহকর্মী           |
| Workplace      | কর্মক্ষেত্র       |
| Salary         | বেতন              |
| Income         | আয়               |
| Experience     | অভিজ্ঞতা          |
| Skill          | দক্ষতা            |
| Responsibility | দায়িত্ব          |
| Position       | পদ                |
| Promotion      | পদোন্নতি          |
| Interview      | সাক্ষাৎকার        |
| Resume         | জীবনবৃত্তান্ত     |
| Internship     | ইন্টার্নশিপ       |
| Project        | প্রকল্প           |
| Career goal    | ক্যারিয়ার লক্ষ্য |

---

# Part 3 — ⭐ Job vs Career

এই দুইটার difference বুঝতে হবে।

### Job

একটি নির্দিষ্ট কাজ/চাকরি।

> I have a job at a software company.

### Career

দীর্ঘমেয়াদী professional journey।

> I want to build a career in software development.

### মনে রাখো:

> **Job = বর্তমান কাজ**

> **Career = দীর্ঘমেয়াদী পেশাগত পথ**

---

# Part 4 — 👨‍💻 "What Do You Do?"

### Question:

> **What do you do?**

### Beginner:

> I am a software developer.

### Intermediate:

> I work as a software developer. I mainly work on web applications and APIs.

### Advanced:

> I work as a software developer, mainly focusing on backend development and web applications. My responsibilities include developing APIs, working with databases, fixing bugs, and improving application performance.

---

# Part 5 — 🏢 Describe Your Work

### Question:

> **What are your main responsibilities?**

### Answer:

> My main responsibilities include developing features, fixing bugs, working with databases, and testing applications. I also communicate with other team members to discuss requirements and solve technical problems.

Useful verbs:

> develop

> design

> build

> test

> maintain

> improve

> debug

> manage

> communicate

> analyze

---

# Part 6 — 🧑‍💻 Skills

### Question:

> **What skills do you need for your job?**

### Answer:

> My job requires both technical and communication skills. I need programming skills, problem-solving ability, teamwork, and time management. I also need to keep learning because technology changes quickly.

### Skill Vocabulary:

* Communication
* Teamwork
* Leadership
* Problem-solving
* Time management
* Creativity
* Programming
* Critical thinking
* Adaptability
* Decision-making

---

# Part 7 — 💪 Strengths

### Question:

> **What are your strengths?**

### Answer:

> I think my main strengths are problem-solving, patience, and willingness to learn. When I face a difficult problem, I usually try different approaches instead of giving up quickly. I also enjoy learning new technologies.

### Useful structure:

> **My main strengths are X, Y, and Z.**

---

# Part 8 — 📈 Weakness / Improvement

### Question:

> **What skills would you like to improve?**

### Answer:

> I would like to improve my communication and public speaking skills. Sometimes I need more confidence when speaking in front of a group. That's why I'm practicing English speaking regularly.

⚠️ Better than saying:

> My weakness is I am bad at English. ❌

Say:

> **I'm currently working on improving my English communication skills.** ✅

---

# Part 9 — 🎯 Dream Job

### Question:

> **What is your dream job?**

### Answer:

> My dream job is to become a successful software engineer. I would like to work on challenging projects and build useful products. I also want to work with experienced developers and continuously improve my technical skills.

---

# Part 10 — ❤️ Why Did You Choose Your Career?

### Question:

> **Why did you choose your career?**

### Answer:

> I chose software development because I enjoy technology and problem-solving. I like creating things and finding solutions to technical problems. I also think software development offers many opportunities to learn and grow professionally.

### Formula:

> **Interest → Reason → Experience → Future**

---

# Part 11 — 🏢 Ideal Company

### Question:

> **What kind of company would you like to work for?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to work for a company that has a supportive environment and opportunities for professional growth. I want to work with experienced people and contribute to meaningful projects. A good learning environment is more important to me than simply having a fancy job title.

---

# Part 12 — 💰 Salary vs Work-Life Balance

### Question:

> **Would you prefer a high salary or a good work-life balance?**

### Answer:

> I think both are important, but I would prefer a reasonable salary with a healthy work-life balance. A good salary is important for financial security, but having enough time for family, health, and personal development is also important.

🔥 Useful phrase:

> **Both are important, but...**

---

# Part 13 — 🏠 Remote Work

### Question:

> **Would you like to work from home?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I would like to work from home sometimes because it can save commuting time and provide a comfortable working environment. However, I also think working in an office can be useful for communication and teamwork. So, I would prefer a hybrid work environment.

---

# Part 14 — 🤝 Teamwork

### Question:

> **Do you prefer working alone or with a team?**

### Answer:

> I can work both independently and as part of a team. When I need to focus deeply on a technical problem, I prefer working independently. However, teamwork is useful when solving complex problems because different people can bring different ideas.

---

# Part 15 — 🕘 Typical Workday

### Question:

> **What does your typical workday look like?**

### Answer:

> I usually start my day by checking my tasks and messages. Then I work on my assigned tasks, attend meetings if necessary, and communicate with my teammates. I spend a lot of time writing and testing code. At the end of the day, I review what I've completed and plan my next tasks.

---

# Part 16 — 🚀 Future Plans

আজকের সবচেয়ে important grammar section।

## Want to

> I want to become a senior developer.

## Would like to

> I would like to work for an international company.

## Plan to

> I plan to improve my technical skills.

## Hope to

> I hope to get a good job soon.

## Would love to

> I would love to work on challenging projects.

---

# Part 17 — 🔥 Difference

### Want to

Strong/direct desire:

> I want to become a software engineer.

### Would like to

Polite desire:

> I would like to become a software engineer.

### Plan to

Actual intention:

> I plan to learn more about backend development.

### Hope to

Future expectation/desire:

> I hope to get a good job.

### Would love to

Strong positive desire:

> I would love to work abroad.

---

# Part 18 — 🗓️ Five-Year Question

Interview-এ খুব common:

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

### Answer:

> In five years, I hope to become an experienced software developer. I want to have strong technical skills and experience working on real-world projects. I would also like to take more responsibility and contribute to important projects. Most importantly, I want to keep learning and growing professionally.

---

# Part 19 — 💼 Job Interview Practice

### Interviewer:

> Tell me about yourself.

### Candidate:

> My name is Atiar. I have a background in computer science and I'm interested in software development. I have experience working with Python, Django, REST APIs, and React. I enjoy building web applications and solving technical problems. Currently, I'm focusing on improving my technical and communication skills.

---

# Part 20 — ⭐ Why Should We Hire You?

### Question:

> **Why should we hire you?**

### Answer:

> I believe I can contribute to the team through my technical skills, problem-solving ability, and willingness to learn. I am comfortable working with backend technologies and APIs, and I enjoy learning new technologies. I'm also responsible and willing to improve based on feedback.

---

# Part 21 — 🔥 Tell Me About Your Project

### Question:

> **Can you tell me about a project you worked on?**

### Answer:

> I worked on a web application where I was responsible for backend development. I used Django and Django REST Framework to build APIs and PostgreSQL for database management. I also worked with the frontend and integrated the APIs with the application. Through this project, I learned a lot about API design, authentication, database relationships, and deployment.

### Project explanation formula:

> **Project → Technology → Responsibility → Challenge → Result → Learning**

---

# Part 22 — 🧠 Handling Difficult Questions

### Question:

> **What do you do when you don't know how to solve a problem?**

### Strong answer:

> First, I try to understand the problem clearly. Then I break it into smaller parts and research possible solutions. I usually check official documentation and reliable resources. If I'm still stuck, I ask an experienced colleague for guidance. After solving the problem, I try to understand why the solution worked so that I can handle similar problems in the future.

🔥 This is an excellent interview answer.

---

# Part 23 — 📚 Career Learning Plan

### Question:

> **How do you improve your professional skills?**

### Answer:

> I try to learn continuously by building projects, reading documentation, watching tutorials, and practicing what I learn. I also try to learn from mistakes and feedback. I believe practical experience is one of the best ways to improve technical skills.

---

# Part 24 — 🎤 3–5 Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic: **My Career and Future Plans**

এই structure follow করবে:

### 1. Background

> I studied...

### 2. Interest

> I'm interested in...

### 3. Current skills

> I have experience with...

### 4. Work

> I have worked on...

### 5. Strengths

> My strengths are...

### 6. Improvement

> I'm currently improving...

### 7. Dream job

> My dream job is...

### 8. Company

> I'd like to work for...

### 9. Five-year goal

> In five years...

### 10. Long-term goal

> In the long term...

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

> I'm interested in building a career in software development. I studied computer science, and during my studies I became particularly interested in web development and backend programming.
>
> I have experience working with technologies such as Python, Django, Django REST Framework, React, and databases. I enjoy building applications, designing APIs, solving technical problems, and learning new technologies.
>
> One of my strengths is that I enjoy solving problems and learning from mistakes. When I face a difficult technical issue, I usually break it down into smaller problems and research possible solutions. However, I'm also working on improving my communication and English speaking skills.
>
> My dream job is to become a strong and experienced software developer. I'd like to work in a company where I can learn from experienced developers and contribute to meaningful projects.
>
> In the next few years, I hope to improve my technical skills, gain professional experience, and take more responsibility. In the long term, I'd like to become a highly skilled developer and possibly work on my own software products.
>
> Overall, my main career goal is continuous learning and professional growth.

---

# Part 26 — 🔥 Advanced Career Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **What does career success mean to you?**

2. **Is salary the most important factor when choosing a job?**

3. **What makes a workplace healthy?**

4. **What are the advantages of remote work?**

5. **What are the disadvantages of remote work?**

6. **Should people change jobs frequently?**

7. **How important is communication in the workplace?**

8. **Is experience more important than a university degree?**

9. **How can someone build a successful career?**

10. **Will AI change the future of jobs?**

---

# Part 27 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main Question:

> **What do you do?**

### Answer:

> I'm a software developer.

### Follow-up:

> **What technologies do you use?**

> I mainly work with Python, Django, REST APIs, and React.

### Follow-up:

> **Why did you choose software development?**

> Because I enjoy technology and problem-solving.

### Follow-up:

> **What are your strengths?**

> My main strengths are problem-solving and willingness to learn.

### Follow-up:

> **What are you currently improving?**

> I'm currently improving my communication and English speaking skills.

### Follow-up:

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

> I hope to become an experienced software developer.

# Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Career questions** তৈরি করবে।

এই words ব্যবহার করো:

> **What → Why → Where → When → How → Which → Would → Do → Are → Will**

Example:

> What is your dream job?

> Why did you choose this career?

> What skills do you need?

> Where would you like to work?

> What will you do to achieve your career goals?

# Task 3 — 🎤 Interview Challenge

নিজেকে interview candidate হিসেবে imagine করো।

এই 5টি প্রশ্নের উত্তর **প্রতিটিতে 1–2 মিনিট** বলবে:

### Q1

> **Tell me about yourself.**

### Q2

> **Why did you choose your career?**

### Q3

> **What are your strengths and weaknesses?**

### Q4

> **Why should we hire you?**

### Q5

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 18 Golden Formula

Career question-এর answer বড় করার formula:

> **Current → Experience → Skills → Strength → Improvement → Goal → Future**

Example:

> I'm interested in software development. I have experience working with Python and Django. My main strengths are problem-solving and willingness to learn. I'm currently improving my communication skills. In the future, I want to gain more professional experience and become a strong software developer.

---

# 🎯 Today's Final Challenge

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **5 মিনিট** এই topic-এ বলবে:

> **“Tell me about yourself, your skills, your career goals, and where you see yourself in five years.”**

আর একটা important rule:

### ❌ শুধু short answer:

> I want a good job.

### ✅ Expanded answer:

> I want to build a successful career in software development. I enjoy solving technical problems and creating useful applications. I also want to work with experienced developers so that I can improve my skills. In the next few years, I hope to gain professional experience and become a strong software developer.

**Lesson 18-এর core skill:**

> **নিজের বর্তমান → skills → experience → career goal → future plan** একসাথে connected English-এ বলা।

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 19

## Topic: Daily Routine, Time Management & Productivity ⏰📅

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **daily routine, সকাল থেকে রাতের activities, time management, productivity, habits এবং daily schedule** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

### 🎯 আজকের Main Goal

> **নিজের একটি পুরো দিন 3–5 মিনিট English-এ describe করতে পারা।**

এখানে আমরা শুধু vocabulary মুখস্থ করব না।
**Question → Answer → Reason → Example → Follow-up** pattern-এ speaking practice করব।

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🌅 Daily Routine

1. **What time do you usually wake up?**
2. **What do you do after waking up?**
3. **What time do you usually have breakfast?**
4. **What do you usually do in the morning?**
5. **When do you start studying or working?**
6. **How do you spend your afternoon?**
7. **What do you usually do in the evening?**
8. **What time do you go to bed?**
9. **How many hours do you sleep?**
10. **What is your favorite part of the day?**

## ⏰ Time Management

11. **How do you manage your time?**
12. **Do you make a daily schedule?**
13. **Do you use a to-do list?**
14. **How do you prioritize your tasks?**
15. **What do you do when you have too many things to do?**
16. **Do you usually finish your tasks on time?**
17. **How do you avoid wasting time?**
18. **What usually distracts you?**
19. **Do you take breaks while working or studying?**
20. **How do you stay focused?**

## 🚀 Productivity & Habits

21. **What makes you productive?**
22. **What is your most productive time of day?**
23. **What bad habit would you like to change?**
24. **What good habit would you like to develop?**
25. **Do you use your phone too much?**
26. **How much time do you spend online?**
27. **How do you relax after a busy day?**
28. **What do you usually do on weekends?**
29. **How has your daily routine changed over time?**
30. **What would you like to change about your routine?**

# Part 2 — ⏰ Daily Routine Vocabulary

| English          | বাংলা                  |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- |
| Wake up          | ঘুম থেকে ওঠা           |
| Get up           | বিছানা থেকে ওঠা        |
| Brush my teeth   | দাঁত ব্রাশ করা         |
| Take a shower    | গোসল করা               |
| Get dressed      | পোশাক পরা              |
| Have breakfast   | নাশতা করা              |
| Go to work       | কাজে যাওয়া            |
| Go to university | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ে যাওয়া |
| Study            | পড়াশোনা করা           |
| Work             | কাজ করা                |
| Have lunch       | দুপুরের খাবার খাওয়া   |
| Take a break     | বিরতি নেওয়া           |
| Come back home   | বাড়ি ফিরে আসা         |
| Have dinner      | রাতের খাবার খাওয়া     |
| Relax            | আরাম করা               |
| Go to bed        | ঘুমাতে যাওয়া          |
| Fall asleep      | ঘুমিয়ে পড়া           |
| Exercise         | ব্যায়াম করা           |
| Read             | পড়া                   |
| Sleep            | ঘুমানো                 |
