# Part 3 — 🌅 Morning Routine

### Question:

> **What do you usually do in the morning?**

### Beginner:

> I wake up at 7 a.m. I brush my teeth and have breakfast.

### Intermediate:

> I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. After getting up, I brush my teeth, take a shower, and have breakfast. Then I start studying or working.

### Advanced:

> I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. After getting up, I freshen up and have breakfast. I then spend a few minutes planning my day before starting my main work or study. I prefer starting my important tasks in the morning because I can concentrate better.

---

# Part 4 — 🧠 "After + Verb-ing"

Daily routine describe করতে এটা খুব useful।

### Examples:

> After waking up, I drink water.

> After having breakfast, I start working.

> After finishing my work, I take a break.

> After coming home, I usually relax.

⚠️ ভুল:

> After wake up, I drink water. ❌

✅

> After waking up, I drink water.

---

# Part 5 — 🕘 Time Expressions

### Every day

> I exercise every day.

### Usually

> I usually wake up at 7.

### Normally

> I normally study in the evening.

### Sometimes

> I sometimes watch movies.

### Often

> I often read books.

### Rarely

> I rarely eat outside.

### Never

> I never smoke.

---

# Part 6 — 📊 Frequency Scale

মনে রাখো:

> **Always → Usually → Often → Sometimes → Rarely → Never**

Example:

> I always brush my teeth in the morning.

> I usually have breakfast at home.

> I often use my computer.

> I sometimes watch movies.

> I rarely eat fast food.

> I never skip breakfast.

---

# Part 7 — 🍳 Breakfast Routine

### Question:

> **What do you usually have for breakfast?**

### Answer:

> I usually have breakfast at home. I often eat eggs, bread, and fruit. Sometimes I have rice or other traditional food. I also drink water or tea.

Expand:

> I prefer having a simple breakfast because I don't like eating too much in the morning.

---

# Part 8 — 💻 Work/Study Routine

### Question:

> **When do you usually start studying or working?**

### Answer:

> I usually start studying or working in the morning. First, I check my tasks and decide what I need to do. Then I start with the most important task. I usually work for a while and then take a short break.

---

# Part 9 — ⏱️ Time Management

### Question:

> **How do you manage your time?**

### Intermediate:

> I manage my time by making a simple daily plan.

### Advanced:

> I usually manage my time by making a daily to-do list. I prioritize important tasks and try to complete them first. I also divide large tasks into smaller steps because they become easier to manage. I try to avoid unnecessary distractions while working.

---

# Part 10 — 📝 To-Do List

### Useful sentences:

> I make a to-do list every morning.

> I write down my important tasks.

> I prioritize my tasks.

> I check my progress throughout the day.

> I mark tasks as completed.

> I review my schedule at night.

### Example:

> Every morning, I write down three or four important tasks that I want to complete during the day.

---

# Part 11 — 🔥 Prioritize

**Prioritize = কোন কাজ আগে করতে হবে তা ঠিক করা**

### Example:

> I prioritize important tasks.

> I always prioritize urgent work.

> I try to finish difficult tasks first.

### Question:

> **How do you prioritize your tasks?**

### Answer:

> I usually prioritize tasks based on their importance and deadline. I try to complete urgent and important tasks first. Less important tasks can usually wait until later.

---

# Part 12 — 📱 Distractions

### Question:

> **What usually distracts you?**

### Answer:

> My phone is one of my biggest distractions. Social media, notifications, and unnecessary browsing can waste a lot of time. That's why I sometimes put my phone away when I need to concentrate.

Useful words:

> distraction

> notification

> social media

> unnecessary browsing

> waste time

> lose focus

---

# Part 13 — 🎯 How Do You Stay Focused?

### Answer:

> I try to work in a quiet environment. I keep my phone away and focus on one task at a time. I also take short breaks when I feel tired. Breaking a large task into smaller steps helps me stay focused.

### Powerful phrase:

> **One task at a time.**

---

# Part 14 — ☕ Taking Breaks

### Question:

> **Do you take breaks while studying or working?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I take short breaks while working or studying. If I work continuously for a long time, I become less focused. During a break, I usually walk around, drink water, or simply relax for a few minutes.

---

# Part 15 — 🌙 Evening Routine

### Question:

> **What do you usually do in the evening?**

### Answer:

> In the evening, I usually finish my work or study. After that, I have dinner and spend some time with my family. Sometimes I watch a movie, listen to music, or practice English. Before going to bed, I usually review what I did during the day.

---

# Part 16 — 😴 Sleep Routine

### Question:

> **What time do you usually go to bed?**

### Answer:

> I usually go to bed around 11 p.m. I try to get enough sleep because it helps me stay focused and productive the next day. However, sometimes I stay up late when I have important work to finish.

Useful:

> go to bed

> fall asleep

> sleep well

> stay up late

> get enough sleep

---

# Part 17 — 🧘 How Do You Relax?

### Question:

> **How do you relax after a busy day?**

### Answer:

> After a busy day, I usually listen to music or watch something entertaining. Sometimes I take a walk or talk with my family. If I'm very tired, I simply rest and try to get enough sleep.

---

# Part 18 — 🚀 Productivity

### Question:

> **What makes you productive?**

### Answer:

> Planning my day makes me more productive. When I know exactly what I need to do, I can focus better. A quiet environment, enough sleep, and regular breaks also help me work more effectively.

---

# Part 19 — ⭐ Most Productive Time

### Question:

> **What is your most productive time of day?**

### Answer:

> I'm usually most productive in the morning. My mind feels fresher, and I can concentrate better. That's why I prefer doing difficult or important tasks earlier in the day.

### Useful:

> **I'm most productive in the morning.**

> **I tend to be more productive at night.**

---

# Part 20 — 🔄 Good & Bad Habits

### Question:

> **What bad habit would you like to change?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to reduce the amount of time I spend on my phone. Sometimes I spend more time browsing the internet than I planned. I want to become more disciplined and use that time for learning or other productive activities.

### Question:

> **What good habit would you like to develop?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to develop a regular reading habit. I think reading every day can improve my knowledge and vocabulary. Even reading for 20 or 30 minutes a day could make a difference over time.

---

# Part 21 — 📱 Phone Usage

### Question:

> **Do you use your phone too much?**

### Answer:

> Sometimes I think I do. I use my phone for communication, learning, entertainment, and work, so it's useful. However, social media and unnecessary browsing can consume a lot of time. I'm trying to use my phone more intentionally.

🔥 Great phrase:

> **use something intentionally**

---

# Part 22 — 📅 Weekend Routine

### Question:

> **What do you usually do on weekends?**

### Answer:

> My weekends are usually more flexible than my weekdays. I spend some time relaxing and catching up with family or friends. I also work on personal projects, practice English, and sometimes watch movies. If I have enough time, I go outside or visit somewhere interesting.

---

# Part 23 — 🔄 How Has Your Routine Changed?

### Question:

> **How has your daily routine changed over time?**

এখানে **Present Perfect** খুব useful।

### Answer:

> My daily routine has changed quite a lot over time. I used to spend more time on entertainment, but now I try to spend more time learning and working on my goals. I've also become more organized and started planning my tasks. However, I'm still trying to improve my time management.

### Key structure:

> **I used to...**

> **I've become...**

> **I've started...**

---

# Part 24 — 🧠 Used To

Past habit:

> I used to wake up late.

> I used to spend a lot of time playing games.

> I used to study only before exams.

Now:

> Now I wake up earlier.

> Now I study regularly.

### Formula:

> **I used to + base verb**

---

# Part 25 — 🎤 Full Daily Routine Example

> My daily routine is fairly organized. I usually wake up at around 7 a.m. After getting up, I brush my teeth, take a shower, and have breakfast. Then I check my schedule and start my most important tasks.
>
> I prefer doing difficult tasks in the morning because I can concentrate better at that time. I usually work or study for a few hours and take short breaks in between. I try to avoid unnecessary phone use because it can distract me.
>
> In the afternoon, I continue working or studying and take some time for lunch and rest. In the evening, I usually finish my main tasks and spend some time with my family. Sometimes I practice English, work on personal projects, watch movies, or listen to music.
>
> Before going to bed, I usually review what I accomplished during the day and think about what I need to do the next day. I normally go to bed around 11 p.m.
>
> I'm still trying to improve my daily routine. In particular, I want to manage my time better, reduce distractions, and develop better learning habits.

---

# Part 26 — 🔥 Advanced Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Why is time management important?**

2. **Why do people waste time?**

3. **How can someone become more productive?**

4. **Is waking up early really important for success?**

5. **How does social media affect productivity?**

6. **Why is sleep important for productivity?**

7. **Is multitasking a good way to work?**

8. **How can people develop good habits?**

9. **Why is consistency more important than motivation?**

10. **How can technology help us manage our time?**

---

# Part 27 — 🔥 Opinion Practice

### Question:

> **Is waking up early important for success?**

### Weak answer:

> Yes, it is important.

### Better answer:

> Yes, I think waking up early can be helpful because it gives people more time to start their day.

### Strong answer:

> I think waking up early can be helpful, but it isn't the only factor that determines success. Some people are naturally more productive in the morning, while others work better at night. What matters more is having a consistent routine and using your time effectively. So, I don't think everyone has to wake up extremely early, but everyone should have a schedule that works for them.

🔥 Notice:

> **Opinion → Reason → Contrast → Conclusion**

---

# Part 28 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main Question:

> **What time do you usually wake up?**

### Answer:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m.

### Follow-up 1:

> **What do you do after waking up?**

> I brush my teeth, take a shower, and have breakfast.

### Follow-up 2:

> **When do you start working?**

> I usually start around 9 a.m.

### Follow-up 3:

> **What is your most productive time?**

> I'm most productive in the morning.

### Follow-up 4:

> **What distracts you?**

> My phone sometimes distracts me.

### Follow-up 5:

> **What do you do in the evening?**

> I usually relax, practice English, or work on personal projects.

### Follow-up 6:

> **What would you like to change?**

> I'd like to reduce my phone usage and manage my time better.

---

# Part 29 — 📝 Question Making Practice

আজ নিজে এই structures দিয়ে questions বানাবে:

### What

> What do you do in the morning?

### When

> When do you usually study?

### How often

> How often do you exercise?

### How long

> How long do you study every day?

### Why

> Why do you prefer studying in the morning?

### How

> How do you manage your time?

### What would

> What would you like to change about your routine?

### How has

> How has your routine changed over time?

---

# Part 30 — 🎯 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **"Describe your daily routine from morning to night."**

এই order follow করবে:

**Wake up → Morning → Breakfast → Work/Study → Lunch → Afternoon → Evening → Dinner → Relax → Night → Sleep**

তারপর নিজের answer expand করবে:

> **Routine → Reason → Habit → Problem → Improvement**

---

# 📝 Lesson 19 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What time do you usually wake up?
2. What do you do after waking up?
3. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
4. When do you start studying or working?
5. How do you manage your time?
6. What usually distracts you?
7. How do you stay focused?
8. What do you do in the evening?
9. What time do you go to bed?
10. What would you like to change about your routine?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 10 Question Making

নিজে daily routine নিয়ে **10টি question** তৈরি করবে।

Must use:

> **What + When + Where + Why + How + How often + How long + Which + Would + How has**

---

# 🎤 Task 3 — 5-Minute Challenge

এই topic-এ 5 মিনিট বলবে:

> **"My Daily Routine and How I Manage My Time"**

এই connectors ব্যবহার করবে:

**First → Then → After that → Usually → However → Sometimes → Because → For example → In addition → Finally → In the future**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 19 Golden Formula

Daily routine-এর answer expand করবে:

> **Time → Activity → Frequency → Reason → Problem → Improvement**

Example:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m. I have breakfast and start working around 9. I'm most productive in the morning because I can concentrate better. However, my phone sometimes distracts me. I'm trying to reduce unnecessary phone use so that I can manage my time more effectively.

### 🎯 Today's Target

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে এই প্রশ্নের উত্তর **3–5 মিনিট** দিতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about your daily routine and how you manage your time.”**

আর মনে রাখবে:

> **Short answer ❌**

> **Answer + Reason + Example + Experience + Future ✅**

এটাই তোমার speaking course-এর পরের ধাপ—**sentence বলা থেকে connected speech-এ যাওয়া।**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 20

## Topic: Food, Cooking, Restaurants & Eating Habits 🍚🍳🍽️

আজকের lesson-এ আমরা **food, favorite খাবার, cooking, restaurant, eating habits, healthy food, junk food এবং food preferences** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি যেন **খাবার সম্পর্কে 3–5 মিনিট naturally English-এ কথা বলতে পারো।**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🍚 Food Basics

1. **What is your favorite food?**
2. **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**
3. **What do you usually eat for lunch?**
4. **What do you usually eat for dinner?**
5. **What is your favorite fruit?**
6. **What is your favorite drink?**
7. **Do you like spicy food?**
8. **Do you prefer sweet or salty food?**
9. **What food do you dislike?**
10. **What is a popular food in your country?**

## 🍳 Cooking

11. **Can you cook?**
12. **What can you cook?**
13. **Who usually cooks in your family?**
14. **How often do you cook?**
15. **What is your favorite dish to cook?**
16. **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**

## 🍽️ Restaurant

17. **How often do you eat at restaurants?**
18. **What kind of restaurants do you like?**
19. **What do you usually order at a restaurant?**
20. **What makes a good restaurant?**
21. **Have you ever had a bad experience at a restaurant?**
22. **Do you prefer eating alone or with others?**

## 🥗 Health & Opinion

23. **What is healthy food?**
24. **Do you think fast food is unhealthy?**
25. **How can people eat more healthily?**
26. **How often do you drink soft drinks?**
27. **Do you think people eat too much junk food?**
28. **Has your eating habit changed over time?**
29. **What food would you like to try?**
30. **If you could eat only one food for a week, what would it be?**

---

# Part 2 — 🍽️ Important Food Vocabulary

| English    | বাংলা                    |
| ---------- | ------------------------ |
| Food       | খাবার                    |
| Meal       | খাবারের সময়/বেলার খাবার |
| Breakfast  | সকালের নাশতা             |
| Lunch      | দুপুরের খাবার            |
| Dinner     | রাতের খাবার              |
| Snack      | হালকা খাবার              |
| Dish       | খাবারের পদ               |
| Ingredient | উপকরণ                    |
| Recipe     | রান্নার প্রণালী          |
| Taste      | স্বাদ                    |
| Flavor     | স্বাদ/ফ্লেভার            |
| Spicy      | ঝাল                      |
| Sweet      | মিষ্টি                   |
| Salty      | লবণাক্ত                  |
| Sour       | টক                       |
| Bitter     | তিতা                     |
| Delicious  | সুস্বাদু                 |
| Healthy    | স্বাস্থ্যকর              |
| Unhealthy  | অস্বাস্থ্যকর             |
| Homemade   | ঘরে তৈরি                 |

---

# Part 3 — ❤️ Favorite Food

### Question:

> **What is your favorite food?**

### Beginner:

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Intermediate:

> My favorite food is biryani because it is delicious and flavorful. I especially like chicken biryani.

### Advanced:

> Biryani is one of my favorite dishes because I really enjoy its rich flavor and combination of rice, meat, spices, and other ingredients. I don't eat it every day, but I especially enjoy having it on special occasions or when I'm eating with friends or family.

---

# Part 4 — 🍳 Breakfast

### Question:

> **What do you usually eat for breakfast?**

### Answer:

> I usually have a simple breakfast. I often eat eggs, bread, and fruit, and sometimes I have traditional food. I also drink tea or water. I prefer a light breakfast because I don't like feeling too full in the morning.

---

# Part 5 — 🍚 Lunch & Dinner

### Question:

> **What do you usually eat for lunch?**

> I usually eat rice with vegetables, fish, chicken, or lentils. I prefer homemade food because it is usually fresh and healthier.

### Question:

> **What do you usually eat for dinner?**

> I usually have a lighter dinner than lunch. Depending on the day, I might eat rice, vegetables, eggs, or other homemade food.

---

# Part 6 — 🌶️ Spicy Food

### Question:

> **Do you like spicy food?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I like spicy food, but I don't like it to be extremely spicy. I enjoy food with a good balance of spices because it makes the food more flavorful.

### Useful:

> **I can handle spicy food.**

> **It's too spicy for me.**

> **I prefer mildly spicy food.**

> **I don't like extremely spicy food.**

---

# Part 7 — 🍰 Sweet or Salty?

### Question:

> **Do you prefer sweet or salty food?**

### Answer:

> I prefer salty food to sweet food. I enjoy snacks and savory dishes more than desserts. However, I sometimes enjoy sweets, especially after a meal.

---

# Part 8 — 😖 Food You Dislike

### Question:

> **What food do you dislike?**

### Answer:

> I don't particularly like very bitter food. I'm also not a big fan of extremely oily dishes. However, I think taste is subjective, so something I dislike might be someone else's favorite food.

🔥 Useful phrase:

> **I'm not a big fan of...**

Example:

> I'm not a big fan of very spicy food.

---

# Part 9 — 👨‍🍳 Can You Cook?

### Question:

> **Can you cook?**

### Beginner:

> Yes, I can cook a few simple dishes.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I can cook some basic dishes, such as rice, eggs, vegetables, and chicken.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I can prepare several simple dishes. I'm not an expert cook, but I can follow a recipe and prepare a decent meal. I'd like to improve my cooking skills because cooking at home can be healthier and more affordable.

---

# Part 10 — 🔪 Cooking Vocabulary

### Actions:

> cut vegetables

> chop onions

> wash rice

> boil water

> fry an egg

> cook rice

> add spices

> mix ingredients

> serve food

> wash dishes

### Examples:

> First, I chop the onions.

> Then I add the spices.

> After that, I cook the ingredients.

> Finally, I serve the food.

---

# Part 11 — 🍳 Describe a Recipe

এই formula ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Ingredients → First → Then → After that → Finally**

### Example:

> To make an omelet, I need eggs, onions, green chili, salt, and a little oil. First, I beat the eggs and mix them with the ingredients. Then I heat some oil in a pan. After that, I pour the mixture into the pan and cook it on both sides. Finally, I serve the omelet.

---

# Part 12 — 🏠 Homemade vs Restaurant Food

### Question:

> **Do you prefer homemade food or restaurant food?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer homemade food because it is usually healthier, fresher, and more affordable. However, I also enjoy eating at restaurants sometimes because it gives me a chance to try different dishes and spend time with friends or family.

### Golden structure:

> **Preference → Reason → Contrast**

---

# Part 13 — 🍽️ Restaurant Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা          |
| ----------- | -------------- |
| Menu        | খাবারের তালিকা |
| Order       | অর্ডার         |
| Waiter      | পরিবেশনকারী    |
| Customer    | ক্রেতা         |
| Table       | টেবিল          |
| Bill        | বিল            |
| Reservation | বুকিং          |
| Dish        | খাবারের পদ     |
| Main course | প্রধান খাবার   |
| Dessert     | মিষ্টান্ন      |
| Service     | সেবা           |
| Tip         | বকশিশ          |

---

# Part 14 — 🗣️ Restaurant Conversation

### Waiter:

> Good evening. Here's the menu.

### Customer:

> Thank you. What do you recommend?

### Waiter:

> Our chicken biryani is very popular.

### Customer:

> Sounds good. I'll have the chicken biryani.

### Waiter:

> Would you like anything to drink?

### Customer:

> Yes, I'll have a bottle of water, please.

### Waiter:

> Certainly.

---

# Part 15 — ⭐ Useful Restaurant Expressions

### Ordering:

> **I'd like to order...**

> **I'll have...**

> **Can I get...?**

> **Could I have...?**

Example:

> I'd like to order a chicken burger.

---

### Asking for recommendation:

> **What do you recommend?**

> **What's popular here?**

> **What is today's special?**

---

### Asking about ingredients:

> **What ingredients are in this dish?**

> **Does this contain eggs?**

> **Is this spicy?**

---

# Part 16 — 💵 Asking for the Bill

### Customer:

> Excuse me, could I have the bill, please?

### Waiter:

> Certainly.

### Customer:

> Can I pay by card?

### Waiter:

> Yes, you can.

---

# Part 17 — 😕 Bad Restaurant Experience

### Question:

> **Have you ever had a bad experience at a restaurant?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I once had a bad experience at a restaurant. The food took a long time to arrive, and when it finally came, it wasn't very fresh. The service was also slow. I didn't complain aggressively, but I told the waiter about the problem.

---

# Part 18 — 🥗 Healthy Food

### Question:

> **What is healthy food?**

### Answer:

> Healthy food usually includes vegetables, fruits, whole grains, protein, and other nutritious ingredients. A healthy diet should also have a good balance of different nutrients. In addition, people should drink enough water and avoid eating too much processed food.

---

# Part 19 — 🍔 Fast Food

### Question:

> **Do you think fast food is unhealthy?**

### Strong Answer:

> I think eating too much fast food can be unhealthy because many fast foods contain high amounts of salt, sugar, and unhealthy fats. However, eating fast food occasionally is not necessarily a problem. The important thing is to maintain a balanced diet and avoid making it a regular habit.

🔥 Notice:

> **I think... However... The important thing is...**

এভাবে opinion answer naturally বড় করা যায়।

---

# Part 20 — 📱 Eating Habits

### Question:

> **Has your eating habit changed over time?**

### Answer:

> Yes, my eating habits have changed over time. I used to eat more snacks and fast food, but now I try to eat more homemade food. I've also become more conscious about what I eat. However, I still enjoy fast food occasionally.

### Grammar:

> **I used to eat...**

> **Now I try to...**

> **I've become more...**

---

# Part 21 — 🌍 Food Culture

### Question:

> **What is a popular food in your country?**

### Answer:

> Bangladesh has a rich food culture. Rice is a major part of the traditional diet, and people enjoy dishes made with fish, meat, vegetables, lentils, and different spices. Biryani is also very popular, especially in cities. Different regions have their own special dishes and cooking styles.

---

# Part 22 — 🍴 Food You Want to Try

### Question:

> **What food would you like to try?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to try authentic Japanese food. I've seen different Japanese dishes online, and I'm curious about their flavors and preparation methods. I'd especially like to try sushi and ramen if I get the opportunity to visit Japan.

---

# Part 23 — 🔥 One Food for a Week

### Question:

> **If you could eat only one food for a week, what would it be?**

### Answer:

> If I had to choose only one food for a week, I would probably choose biryani. I enjoy its flavor and variety. However, eating only one food for a whole week wouldn't be healthy, so I wouldn't actually recommend doing it.

### Important grammar:

> **If I had to choose..., I would...**

---

# Part 24 — 🧠 Useful Food Adjectives

Instead of always saying **good**, use:

> delicious

> tasty

> flavorful

> fresh

> spicy

> sweet

> salty

> crispy

> juicy

> soft

> crunchy

> oily

> healthy

> nutritious

### Examples:

> The chicken is very juicy.

> The vegetables are fresh.

> The food is too oily.

> The soup is flavorful.

> The chips are crispy.

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main Question:

> **What is your favorite food?**

> My favorite food is biryani.

### Follow-up:

> **Why do you like it?**

> I like it because it is flavorful and delicious.

### Follow-up:

> **How often do you eat it?**

> I usually eat it occasionally, especially on special occasions.

### Follow-up:

> **Can you cook it?**

> I can prepare a simple version, but I'm still improving my cooking skills.

### Follow-up:

> **Do you prefer homemade or restaurant biryani?**

> I usually prefer homemade food, but I enjoy restaurant biryani sometimes.

### Follow-up:

> **Who usually cooks in your family?**

> Usually, someone in my family prepares the main meals.

🔥 One topic থেকে **6-level conversation** তৈরি হলো।

---

# Part 26 — 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Eating Habits

> Food is an important part of my daily life. I enjoy trying different dishes, but I generally prefer homemade food because it is fresh, affordable, and usually healthier.
>
> For breakfast, I usually have something simple, such as eggs, bread, or fruit. For lunch, I often eat rice with vegetables, fish, chicken, or lentils. I usually prefer a lighter dinner.
>
> One of my favorite foods is biryani because I really enjoy its rich flavor and combination of spices and ingredients. I don't eat it every day, but I especially enjoy it when I'm with friends or family.
>
> I can cook some simple dishes, although I'm not an expert. I can prepare things like eggs, rice, vegetables, and chicken. I'd like to improve my cooking skills because I think cooking is an important life skill.
>
> I also try to maintain healthy eating habits. I think people should eat a balanced diet and avoid too much junk food. In the future, I'd like to become more consistent about eating healthy food and drinking enough water.

---

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

2. **Why do people eat fast food even when they know it can be unhealthy?**

3. **Is homemade food always healthier than restaurant food?**

4. **Should schools teach children how to cook?**

5. **How have social media and food delivery apps changed eating habits?**

6. **Why do people enjoy trying food from other cultures?**

7. **Is vegetarian food healthier than meat-based food?**

8. **How can people develop healthier eating habits?**

9. **Should governments regulate unhealthy food advertising?**

10. **How important is food in social relationships?**

> **"My Favorite Food, Eating Habits and Food Preferences"**

এই structure follow করবে:

# Part 29 — 📝 Lesson 20 Homework

## Task 1 — 🎤 Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What is your favorite food?
2. Why do you like it?
3. What do you usually eat for breakfast?
4. What do you usually eat for lunch?
5. Can you cook?
6. What can you cook?
7. Do you prefer homemade or restaurant food?
8. Do you like spicy food?
9. What food would you like to try?
10. What would you like to change about your eating habits?

# Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Food questions** তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **What → Why → Where → When → Who → How → How often → Which → Would → If**

Examples:

> What is your favorite food?

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you eat outside?

> Which restaurant do you prefer?

> What would you like to try?

> If you could eat one food for a week, what would it be?

---

# Task 3 — 🍳 Cooking Challenge

একটি খাবার কীভাবে রান্না করতে হয় সেটা English-এ explain করবে।

Use:

> **First → Then → Next → After that → Finally**

Example:

> First, wash the rice. Then add water and put it on the stove. Next, let it cook for some time. After that, check whether the rice is ready. Finally, serve it.

---

# ⭐ Lesson 20 Golden Formula

Food topic-এর answer expand করবে:

> **Preference → Reason → Example → Experience → Contrast → Future**

### Example:

> I prefer homemade food because it is usually healthier and more affordable. For example, I can control the ingredients when I cook at home. I also enjoy eating at restaurants occasionally because I can try different dishes. However, I don't want to eat outside too frequently. In the future, I'd like to improve my cooking skills and develop healthier eating habits.

---

> **“Tell me about your favorite food, eating habits, cooking skills, and restaurant preferences.”**

**Food describe → Preference explain → Reason give → Experience tell → Opinion give → Future habit discuss.**

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 21

## Topic: Hobbies, Free Time, Entertainment & Interests 🎮🎵📚⚽

আজকের lesson-এ আমরা **hobbies, free time, movies, music, games, books, sports, internet এবং personal interests** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি যেন:

> **নিজের hobbies ও free-time activities নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট naturally English-এ কথা বলতে পারো।**

এখানে শুধু vocabulary নয়—**question তৈরি + answer expand + follow-up conversation** practice করব।

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🎯 Hobbies

2. **What do you like to do in your free time?**

19. **What kind of books do you like?**

## ⚽ Interests & Free Time

21. **Do you play any sports?**
22. **What is your favorite sport?**
23. **Do you like spending time with friends?**
24. **What do you usually do on weekends?**
25. **Do you prefer spending free time alone or with others?**
26. **How often do you use social media?**
27. **What activity helps you relax?**
28. **What new hobby would you like to learn?**
29. **How have your hobbies changed over time?**
30. **Why are hobbies important?**

# Part 2 — 🎯 Hobby Vocabulary

# Part 3 — ❤️ What Are Your Hobbies?

### Question:

> **What are your hobbies?**

### Beginner:

> My hobbies are reading and watching movies.

### Intermediate:

> My main hobbies are reading, watching movies, and learning new things. I usually do these activities in my free time.

### Advanced:

> I have several interests, but I particularly enjoy reading, watching movies, and learning new things. I also enjoy working on personal projects because they allow me to develop my skills while doing something interesting. When I have more free time, I like traveling or spending time with friends.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ Favorite Hobby

### Question:

> **What is your favorite hobby?**

### Answer:

> My favorite hobby is reading. I enjoy reading because it helps me learn new things and expand my knowledge. I usually read when I have free time, especially in the evening.

### Expand:

> **My favorite hobby is...**

> **I enjoy it because...**

> **I usually do it...**

> **It helps me...**

---

# Part 5 — ⏰ How Often?

### Question:

> **How often do you do your hobby?**

### Answer:

> I usually read several times a week. I don't always have enough free time every day, but I try to read whenever I can.

### Frequency:

> every day

> every week

> several times a week

> once a week

> occasionally

> sometimes

> rarely

---

# Part 6 — 🕐 When Did You Start?

### Question:

> **When did you start this hobby?**

### Answer:

> I started this hobby a few years ago. At first, I wasn't very interested in it, but gradually I started enjoying it more. Now it has become a regular part of my free time.

🔥 Useful structure:

> **I started... → At first... → Gradually... → Now...**

---

# Part 7 — 🤔 Why Do You Enjoy It?

### Question:

> **Why do you enjoy your hobby?**

### Answer:

> I enjoy it because it helps me relax and learn something new at the same time. It also gives me a break from my regular routine. Whenever I feel stressed, spending some time on my hobby helps me feel better.

---

# Part 8 — 🏠 Indoor vs Outdoor Hobbies

### Question:

> **Do you prefer indoor or outdoor hobbies?**

### Answer:

> I prefer indoor hobbies because they are convenient and I can do them at home. For example, I enjoy reading, watching movies, and working on personal projects. However, I also like outdoor activities such as walking and traveling when I have enough time.

🔥 Pattern:

> **Preference → Reason → Example → Contrast**

---

# Part 9 — 🎬 Movies

### Question:

> **What kind of movies do you like?**

### Answer:

> I enjoy action, comedy, and science-fiction movies. I like movies that have interesting stories and strong characters. Sometimes I also watch movies based on real events because they can be informative as well as entertaining.

### Movie vocabulary:

> actor

> actress

> character

> story

> plot

> scene

> director

> ending

> action

> comedy

> thriller

> horror

> science fiction

> documentary

---

# Part 10 — 🎥 Movie Opinion

### Question:

> **What makes a good movie?**

### Answer:

> I think a good movie needs an interesting story, strong characters, good acting, and effective direction. A good ending is also important. However, different people have different preferences, so what makes a movie good depends on the viewer.

---

# Part 11 — 📺 Movies vs TV Series

### Question:

> **Do you prefer movies or TV series?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer movies because they tell a complete story in a relatively short time. However, TV series can develop characters and stories in much more detail. So, when I have more free time, I sometimes prefer watching a good series.

---

# Part 12 — 🎵 Music

### Question:

> **What kind of music do you listen to?**

### Answer:

> I listen to different types of music depending on my mood. I usually enjoy relaxing music when I'm working or studying. When I want to feel energetic, I prefer more upbeat music.

### Useful expressions:

> I listen to music.

> I'm really into...

> I'm a fan of...

> I don't listen to ... very often.

Example:

> I'm really into relaxing music.

---

# Part 13 — 🎧 Why Do You Listen to Music?

### Question:

> **Why do you enjoy listening to music?**

### Answer:

> I enjoy listening to music because it helps me relax and improve my mood. Sometimes I listen to music while working or traveling. It can also make boring activities more enjoyable.

---

# Part 14 — 📚 Reading

### Question:

> **Do you enjoy reading books?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I enjoy reading, especially when I want to learn something new. I usually prefer books related to technology, personal development, and general knowledge. I think reading regularly can improve both knowledge and vocabulary.

---

# Part 15 — 📖 What Kind of Books?

### Question:

> **What kind of books do you like?**

### Answer:

> I mainly enjoy non-fiction books, especially books about technology, personal development, and history. I also sometimes read novels because they are entertaining and can improve my imagination.

---

# Part 16 — 🎮 Gaming

### Question:

> **Do you play video games?**

### Beginner:

> Yes, sometimes I play video games.

### Intermediate:

> I sometimes play video games when I have free time. I mainly play for entertainment and relaxation.

### Advanced:

> I occasionally play video games, but I try not to spend too much time on them. I think gaming can be entertaining and can sometimes improve problem-solving and teamwork skills, but excessive gaming can become a distraction.

---

# Part 17 — ⚽ Sports

### Question:

> **Do you play any sports?**

### Answer:

> I don't play sports regularly, but I enjoy watching them. I particularly like football because it is exciting and unpredictable. I'd also like to become more physically active in the future.

---

# Part 18 — 🧑‍🤝‍🧑 Alone or With Others?

### Question:

> **Do you prefer spending free time alone or with others?**

### Answer:

> It depends on my mood. Sometimes I prefer being alone because I can read, watch something, or work on personal projects without distractions. At other times, I enjoy spending time with friends because we can talk, eat together, or go somewhere interesting.

🔥 Excellent phrase:

> **It depends on my mood.**

---

# Part 19 — 📱 Social Media

### Question:

> **How often do you use social media?**

### Answer:

> I use social media almost every day, mainly for communication, news, entertainment, and learning. However, I try to control my usage because social media can easily become a distraction.

---

# Part 20 — 🧠 New Hobby

### Question:

> **What new hobby would you like to learn?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to learn photography. I think photography is a creative hobby that can help me notice and appreciate interesting places and moments. I'd like to learn how to use a camera properly and take better photographs.

---

# Part 21 — 🔄 Hobbies Changed Over Time

### Question:

> **How have your hobbies changed over time?**

### Answer:

> My hobbies have changed quite a lot over time. When I was younger, I spent more time playing games and watching cartoons. Now I prefer activities that are both enjoyable and useful, such as reading, learning new skills, and working on personal projects.

### Important grammar:

> **When I was younger, I used to...**

> **Now I prefer...**

> **My interests have changed...**

---

# Part 22 — 🎯 Hobbies & Productivity

### Question:

> **Can hobbies improve productivity?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I think hobbies can improve productivity if we manage our time properly. Hobbies give our minds a break and can reduce stress. After relaxing, people may return to their work with more energy and concentration. However, hobbies should not become a reason to avoid important responsibilities.

---

# Part 23 — 🌱 Why Are Hobbies Important?

### Strong Answer:

> I think hobbies are important because they give people an opportunity to relax and enjoy their free time. They can also help us develop new skills and discover new interests. Some hobbies improve creativity, while others improve physical health or social skills. Most importantly, hobbies can make life more enjoyable and balanced.

---

# Part 24 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main Question:

> **What are your hobbies?**

> My main hobbies are reading, watching movies, and learning new things.

### Follow-up 1:

> **Which one is your favorite?**

> Reading is probably my favorite.

### Follow-up 2:

> **Why do you like reading?**

> Because it helps me learn new things and improve my vocabulary.

### Follow-up 3:

> **When do you usually read?**

> I usually read in the evening.

### Follow-up 4:

> **What kind of books do you read?**

> I mainly read books about technology and personal development.

### Follow-up 5:

> **Would you like to develop another hobby?**

> Yes, I'd like to learn photography.

### Follow-up 6:

> **Why photography?**

> Because I think it would be a creative and enjoyable skill to learn.

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Hobbies and Free Time

> I have several hobbies and interests that I enjoy in my free time. My main hobbies are reading, watching movies, listening to music, and learning new things. I also enjoy working on personal projects because they allow me to improve my skills while doing something interesting.
>
> Reading is probably my favorite hobby. I enjoy reading because it helps me learn new things and expand my knowledge. I mainly read books and articles related to technology, personal development, and general knowledge. I usually read in the evening when I have finished my main tasks.
>
> I also enjoy watching movies. I particularly like action, comedy, and science-fiction movies. I prefer movies with interesting stories and strong characters. Sometimes I watch movies simply to relax after a busy day.
>
> I listen to music depending on my mood. I prefer relaxing music when I'm working or studying, while I sometimes listen to energetic music when I want to feel more active.
>
> In the future, I'd like to learn photography because I think it would be a creative and useful hobby. Overall, I believe hobbies are important because they help us relax, learn new things, and maintain a healthy balance in life.

---

2. **Can hobbies become careers?**

3. **Should people spend more time on hobbies?**

4. **How has technology changed entertainment?**

5. **Is social media a good form of entertainment?**

6. **Are video games harmful or beneficial?**

7. **Why do people enjoy watching movies?**

8. **Can reading change someone's life?**

10. **How can people find a hobby they truly enjoy?**

# Part 27 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

আজ থেকে opinion questions-এর জন্য এই formula ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Contrast → Conclusion**

### Question:

> **Are video games harmful?**

### Answer:

> I don't think video games are always harmful. They can be entertaining and sometimes help develop problem-solving or teamwork skills. For example, some games require players to think strategically and work together. However, excessive gaming can waste time and affect other responsibilities. So, I think moderation is the key.

---

আজ নিজে hobbies নিয়ে questions বানাবে।

Use:

> **What**

> **Why**

> **When**

> **Where**

> **Who**

> **How often**

> **How long**

> **Which**

> **Would**

> **How has**

Examples:

> What do you do in your free time?

> Why do you enjoy reading?

> When did you start this hobby?

> How often do you watch movies?

> Which type of music do you prefer?

> What hobby would you like to learn?

> How have your hobbies changed over time?

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

# Part 2 — 🌍 Travel Vocabulary

| ----------- | ------------------- |
| Travel      | ভ্রমণ               |
| Trip        | ভ্রমণ/সফর           |
| Journey     | যাত্রা              |
| Vacation    | ছুটি/অবকাশ          |

# Part 3 — ✈️ Travel vs Trip vs Journey

এই তিনটা ভালোভাবে বুঝবে।

### Travel

সাধারণভাবে ভ্রমণের activity।

> I love traveling.

### Trip

নির্দিষ্ট একটি ভ্রমণ।

> I went on a trip to Cox's Bazar.

### Journey

এক জায়গা থেকে অন্য জায়গায় যাওয়ার process।

> The journey took five hours.

### সহজভাবে:

> **Travel = activity**

> **Trip = specific visit**

> **Journey = the process of going**

---

# Part 4 — ❤️ Do You Like Traveling?

### Question:

> **Do you like traveling?**

### Beginner:

> Yes, I like traveling.

### Intermediate:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling because I can visit new places and experience different environments.

### Advanced:

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling. It gives me an opportunity to take a break from my normal routine and experience new places, cultures, and food. I also think traveling helps people become more independent and open-minded.

---

# Part 5 — 📍 Favorite Place

### Question:

> **What is your favorite place?**

### Answer:

> One of my favorite places is Cox's Bazar. I like it because of the long beach, natural scenery, and relaxing atmosphere. I especially enjoy visiting the beach in the evening because the environment is peaceful and beautiful.

---

# Part 6 — 🏖️ Beach or Mountains?

### Question:

> **Do you prefer the beach or the mountains?**

### Answer:

> I prefer the beach because I enjoy the peaceful atmosphere and the sound of the waves. I also like walking along the beach and watching the sunset. However, I'd like to spend more time in the mountains in the future because I enjoy nature and beautiful scenery.

### Formula:

> **Preference → Reason → Example → Future**

---

# Part 7 — 🗺️ Describe a Place

যেকোনো place describe করতে এই formula ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Location → Appearance → Activities → Atmosphere → Opinion**

### Example:

> Cox's Bazar is a popular tourist destination in Bangladesh. It is famous for its long sandy beach and beautiful natural scenery. Visitors can walk along the beach, enjoy seafood, and watch the sunset. The atmosphere can be lively, especially during holidays. Personally, I find it relaxing and enjoyable.

---

# Part 8 — 🚌 Transportation

### Question:

> **How do you usually travel?**

### Answer:

> I usually travel by bus or train when I'm traveling within the country. For short distances, I prefer using a car or public transportation. If the destination is very far away, traveling by plane is more convenient.

---

# Part 9 — 🚆 Bus vs Train

### Question:

> **Do you prefer traveling by bus or train?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer traveling by train because it is usually more comfortable and gives me a chance to enjoy the scenery. However, buses can be more convenient because they often have more routes and schedules.

🔥 Useful:

> **I generally prefer...**

> **However...**

> **On the other hand...**

---

# Part 10 — ✈️ Traveling by Plane

### Question:

> **Have you ever traveled by plane?**

### If YES:

> Yes, I have traveled by plane before. It was an interesting experience because it was my first time flying. I was a little nervous at first, but I became comfortable after takeoff.

### If NO:

> No, I haven't traveled by plane yet. However, I'd like to experience it in the future, especially if I get the opportunity to travel abroad.

### Grammar:

> **I have traveled...**

> **I haven't traveled... yet.**

---

# Part 11 — 🧳 What Do You Pack?

### Question:

> **What do you take with you when you travel?**

### Answer:

> I usually take clothes, toiletries, a phone charger, my wallet, identification documents, and some basic medicines. I also carry a power bank because my phone is important for communication and navigation.

Useful vocabulary:

> clothes

> charger

> power bank

> wallet

> passport

> identification

> toiletries

> backpack

> luggage

---

# Part 12 — 🏨 Hotel

### Question:

> **What makes a good hotel?**

### Answer:

> I think a good hotel should be clean, safe, comfortable, and reasonably priced. Good customer service is also important. A convenient location and reliable internet connection are additional advantages, especially for people who travel for work.

---

# Part 13 — 🏨 Hotel Conversation

### Receptionist:

> Good evening. How can I help you?

### Guest:

> I'd like to check in.

### Receptionist:

> Do you have a reservation?

### Guest:

> Yes, I have a reservation under the name Atiar Rahman.

### Receptionist:

> Could I see your ID, please?

### Guest:

> Sure. Here you are.

### Receptionist:

> Thank you. Here's your room key.

### Guest:

> Thank you. What time is breakfast?

### Receptionist:

> Breakfast is served from 7 to 10 a.m.

### Guest:

> Great. Thank you.

---

# Part 14 — 📅 Last Trip

### Question:

> **Where did you go on your last trip?**

এখানে **Past Simple** ব্যবহার করবে।

### Answer:

> I went to Cox's Bazar on my last trip. I traveled there with some friends. We stayed at a hotel near the beach. During the trip, we visited several places, tried local food, and spent time at the beach. Overall, it was a memorable experience.

---

# Part 15 — 🕐 Past Trip Structure

Trip story বলার formula:

> **When → Where → Who → Transportation → Hotel → Activities → Experience → Ending**

Example:

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with my friends. We traveled by bus and stayed at a hotel near the beach. We visited several tourist attractions and tried different foods. We also spent time walking along the beach. The weather was pleasant, and we had a great time. Overall, it was one of my most memorable trips.

---

# Part 16 — 😍 Memorable Experience

### Question:

> **What was the most memorable part of your last trip?**

### Answer:

> The most memorable part was watching the sunset at the beach. The view was beautiful, and the atmosphere was peaceful. I also enjoyed spending that time with my friends because we don't usually get many opportunities to travel together.

---

# Part 17 — 😕 Bad Travel Experience

### Question:

> **Have you ever had a bad travel experience?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I once had a difficult travel experience because of heavy traffic. The journey took much longer than expected, and we arrived very late. At first, I was frustrated, but eventually everything worked out. That experience taught me to leave earlier and plan my trips more carefully.

🔥 Useful:

> **The journey took longer than expected.**

> **Everything worked out.**

> **It taught me to...**

---

# Part 18 — 🗺️ Problems While Traveling

Common problems:

> traffic jam

> delayed flight

> lost luggage

> bad weather

> getting lost

> expensive hotel

> language barrier

> missed train

> lack of information

### Example:

> One common problem while traveling is getting lost in an unfamiliar place. Another problem can be transportation delays. That's why it's useful to plan ahead and keep important information on your phone.

---

# Part 19 — 🌎 Travel Abroad

### Question:

> **Would you like to travel abroad?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I'd definitely like to travel abroad. I'd like to experience different cultures, try new food, and visit famous places. I also think traveling to another country can improve a person's understanding of different lifestyles and perspectives.

---

# Part 20 — 🇯🇵 Dream Country

### Question:

> **Which country would you like to visit first?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to visit Japan first. I'm interested in its technology, culture, food, and natural scenery. I'd like to visit Tokyo and some traditional places as well. I think it would be a very interesting cultural experience.

---

# Part 21 — 🎫 Free Ticket

### Question:

> **What would you do if you had a free ticket to any country?**

### Answer:

> If I had a free ticket to any country, I'd probably choose Japan. I'd spend a few weeks exploring different cities and trying local food. I'd also visit historical and cultural places and take lots of photographs. I'd try to experience both modern and traditional Japan.

### Grammar:

> **If I had..., I would...**

---

# Part 22 — 🧠 Travel Opinion

### Question:

> **Why is traveling important?**

### Strong Answer:

> I think traveling is important because it allows people to experience new places and cultures. It can also help people become more independent and adaptable. Traveling gives us a break from our normal routine and creates memorable experiences. In addition, it can teach us things that we may not learn from books or classrooms.

---

# Part 23 — 🔥 Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main:

> **Do you like traveling?**

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling.

### Follow-up:

> **Where have you been?**

> I've visited several places within Bangladesh.

### Follow-up:

> **Which place did you like most?**

> I particularly enjoyed Cox's Bazar.

### Follow-up:

> **Who did you go with?**

> I went with some friends.

### Follow-up:

> **How did you travel?**

> We traveled by bus.

### Follow-up:

> **What did you do there?**

> We visited tourist attractions, tried local food, and spent time at the beach.

### Follow-up:

> **Where would you like to go next?**

> I'd like to visit Japan in the future.

---

# Part 24 — 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Travel Experience

> I really enjoy traveling because it gives me a chance to take a break from my normal routine and experience new places. I usually travel with friends or family, although I would also like to try traveling alone someday.
>
> One of the places I particularly enjoyed visiting was Cox's Bazar. I traveled there with some friends, and we stayed at a hotel near the beach. We visited several tourist attractions, tried local food, and spent a lot of time walking along the beach.
>
> The most memorable part of the trip was watching the sunset. The view was beautiful, and the atmosphere was very peaceful. We also took many photographs and spent a lot of time talking and enjoying ourselves.
>
> I usually prefer traveling by train for long journeys because I find it comfortable and enjoyable. However, planes are more convenient when the destination is very far away.
>
> In the future, I'd like to travel abroad. Japan is one of the countries I'd particularly like to visit because I'm interested in its culture, technology, food, and natural scenery. Overall, I think traveling is a great way to learn, relax, and create memorable experiences.

---

# Part 25 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Why do people enjoy traveling?**

2. **Is traveling a good way to learn about other cultures?**

3. **What are the advantages of traveling alone?**

4. **What are the disadvantages of traveling alone?**

5. **Is international travel better than domestic travel?**

6. **How can tourism benefit a country?**

7. **Can tourism have negative effects on local communities?**

8. **Why do some people prefer luxury travel?**

9. **How has technology changed the way people travel?**

10. **Is traveling more valuable than buying expensive things?**

---

# Part 26 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

Travel-related opinion question-এর জন্য:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Contrast → Conclusion**

### Question:

> **Is traveling alone better than traveling with friends?**

### Answer:

> I think both have advantages. Traveling alone gives you more freedom because you can choose your own schedule and activities. For example, you don't have to wait for other people when deciding where to go. On the other hand, traveling with friends can be more enjoyable because you can share experiences and memories. So, I think the best choice depends on the person's personality and purpose.

---

# Part 27 — 📝 Question Making

আজ travel নিয়ে নিজে **10টি question** তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **What**

> **Where**

> **When**

> **Who**

> **Why**

> **How**

> **How often**

> **Which**

> **Would**

> **If**

Examples:

> Where did you go on your last trip?

> Why do you like traveling?

> Who do you usually travel with?

> How do you usually travel?

> Which country would you like to visit?

> What would you do if you had a free ticket?

---

# Part 28 — 🎤 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **“My Last Trip and My Future Travel Plans”**

এই sequence follow করবে:

**Where → When → Who → Transportation → Hotel → Activities → Food → Memorable moment → Problem → Opinion → Future destination**

---

# 📝 Lesson 22 Homework

### Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি question-এর answer মুখে বলবে:

1. Do you like traveling?
2. Where did you go on your last trip?
3. Who did you go with?
4. How did you travel?
5. Where did you stay?
6. What did you do there?
7. What was the most memorable part?
8. Did you face any problems?
9. Where would you like to travel in the future?
10. Why would you like to go there?

---

### Task 2 — 🔥 Past Trip Story

নিজের একটি real বা imaginary trip নিয়ে **2–3 মিনিট** বলবে।

Use:

> **Last year...**

> **First...**

> **Then...**

> **After that...**

> **Unfortunately...**

> **Fortunately...**

> **Finally...**

> **Overall...**

---

### Task 3 — 🚀 Future Travel

এই question-এর answer **2 মিনিট** বলবে:

> **“If you could travel to any country, where would you go and what would you do there?”**

Must use:

> **I would...**

> **I'd like to...**

> **I would probably...**

> **I hope to...**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 22 Golden Formula

Travel story বলার সময়:

> **When → Where → Who → How → What → Experience → Problem → Opinion → Future**

Example:

> Last year, I went to Cox's Bazar with my friends. We traveled by bus and stayed at a hotel near the beach. We visited several places and tried local food. The most memorable part was watching the sunset. Although we faced some traffic problems, everything worked out in the end. I really enjoyed the trip, and I'd like to visit another beautiful place in the future.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 22 Final Target

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about your last trip, your favorite destination, and where you would like to travel in the future.”**

### 🔥 Lesson 22 Core Skill

**Place describe → Past experience tell → Travel problem explain → Opinion give → Future plan discuss.**
