# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 23

## Topic: Education, School, University & Learning 🎓📚

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **school, university, subjects, teachers, exams, study habits, online learning, skills, education system এবং future learning goals** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি যেন **নিজের education, study experience এবং learning goals নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট confidently English-এ কথা বলতে পারো।**

আজকের বিশেষ focus:

> **Past education → Present study → Learning methods → Opinion → Future goals**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🎓 Education Basics

1. **What did you study?**
2. **Where did you study?**
3. **What was your favorite subject?**
4. **Which subject did you dislike?**
5. **Why did you choose your subject?**
6. **Did you enjoy school?**
7. **Did you enjoy university?**
8. **Who was your favorite teacher?**
9. **What did you like most about your school or university?**
10. **What was difficult about your studies?**

## 📚 Study Habits

11. **How do you usually study?**
12. **How many hours do you study every day?**
13. **Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**
14. **Where do you usually study?**
15. **How do you prepare for exams?**
16. **Do you take notes while studying?**
17. **How do you remember what you learn?**
18. **What distracts you while studying?**
19. **Do you prefer books or online resources?**
20. **What helps you learn better?**

## 💻 Modern Education

21. **Do you prefer online or offline classes?**
22. **What are the advantages of online learning?**
23. **What are the disadvantages of online learning?**
24. **Can AI improve education?**
25. **Is a university degree necessary for success?**

## 🚀 Future

26. **What skill would you like to learn?**
27. **Would you like to study abroad?**
28. **What course would you like to take?**
29. **How do you plan to improve yourself?**
30. **What does a good education mean to you?**

---

# Part 2 — 📚 Important Education Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা          |
| ----------- | -------------- |
| Education   | শিক্ষা         |
| School      | বিদ্যালয়      |
| College     | কলেজ           |
| University  | বিশ্ববিদ্যালয় |
| Student     | শিক্ষার্থী     |
| Teacher     | শিক্ষক         |
| Lecturer    | প্রভাষক        |
| Professor   | অধ্যাপক        |
| Subject     | বিষয়          |
| Course      | কোর্স          |
| Class       | ক্লাস          |
| Lecture     | লেকচার         |
| Assignment  | অ্যাসাইনমেন্ট  |
| Exam        | পরীক্ষা        |
| Quiz        | কুইজ           |
| Grade       | গ্রেড          |
| Degree      | ডিগ্রি         |
| Scholarship | বৃত্তি         |
| Knowledge   | জ্ঞান          |
| Skill       | দক্ষতা         |

---

# Part 3 — 🎓 What Did You Study?

### Question:

> **What did you study?**

### Beginner:

> I studied computer science.

### Intermediate:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. I learned programming, databases, software development, networking, and other computer-related subjects.

### Advanced:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. During my studies, I learned about programming, databases, algorithms, software engineering, networking, and web development. I became particularly interested in software development because I enjoy solving problems and building practical applications.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ Why Did You Choose Your Subject?

### Question:

> **Why did you choose Computer Science?**

### Answer:

> I chose Computer Science because I have always been interested in technology. I enjoy solving problems and creating things using computers. I also believe that technology offers many opportunities for learning and career development.

### Stronger:

> Another reason is that the field is constantly changing, so there is always something new to learn.

---

# Part 5 — ❤️ Favorite Subject

### Question:

> **What was your favorite subject?**

### Answer:

> My favorite subject was programming because I enjoyed writing code and solving problems. I found it interesting to turn an idea into a working program. It was sometimes challenging, but solving a difficult problem gave me a sense of achievement.

---

# Part 6 — 😖 Difficult Subject

### Question:

> **Which subject did you find difficult?**

### Answer:

> I found some mathematical and theoretical subjects difficult. At first, I struggled to understand some of the concepts. However, after practicing regularly and reviewing the basics, I gradually became more comfortable with them.

🔥 Useful pattern:

> **At first → However → After → Gradually**

---

# Part 7 — 🏫 School/University Experience

### Question:

> **Did you enjoy university?**

### Answer:

> Yes, overall I enjoyed my university experience. I learned many technical skills and had opportunities to work on projects. I also met different people and learned how to work in teams. Of course, there were stressful periods, especially during exams and project deadlines, but they helped me become more disciplined.

---

# Part 8 — 👨‍🏫 Favorite Teacher

### Question:

> **Who was your favorite teacher?**

### Answer:

> One of my favorite teachers was a programming teacher. I liked the way the teacher explained difficult concepts using practical examples. The teacher was also patient and encouraged students to ask questions. That made the subject more interesting for me.

---

# Part 9 — 📝 How Do You Study?

### Question:

> **How do you usually study?**

### Answer:

> I usually start by understanding the basic concepts. Then I take notes and practice what I've learned. For technical subjects, I prefer learning through practical examples and projects rather than only reading theory. Finally, I review the material and try to explain it in my own words.

🔥 Excellent learning formula:

> **Understand → Note → Practice → Review → Explain**

---

# Part 10 — ⏰ How Many Hours?

### Question:

> **How many hours do you study every day?**

### Answer:

> It depends on my schedule. On a normal day, I try to study for a few focused hours rather than studying continuously for a very long time. I believe the quality of study is more important than simply counting hours.

---

# Part 11 — 👤 Alone or Group?

### Question:

> **Do you prefer studying alone or with others?**

### Answer:

> I generally prefer studying alone when I need deep concentration. However, group study can be useful when I need to discuss difficult topics or solve problems together. So, I use both methods depending on the situation.

---

# Part 12 — 🧠 How Do You Remember?

### Question:

> **How do you remember what you learn?**

### Answer:

> I try not to memorize everything directly. First, I make sure I understand the concept. Then I practice it and try to explain it without looking at my notes. I also review important topics regularly because repetition helps me remember them for longer.

🔥 Important:

> **Understand first, memorize later.**

---

# Part 13 — 📖 Books vs Online Resources

### Question:

> **Do you prefer books or online resources?**

### Answer:

> I use both, but I prefer online resources for technical learning because they are easy to update and provide practical examples. However, books can provide deeper and more structured knowledge. I think the best approach is to use both depending on the topic.

---

# Part 14 — 💻 Online vs Offline Learning

### Question:

> **Do you prefer online or offline classes?**

### Answer:

> I prefer a combination of both. Online learning is flexible because I can learn at my own pace and access courses from anywhere. On the other hand, offline classes provide direct interaction with teachers and classmates. So, I think a blended approach can be very effective.

---

# Part 15 — 💻 Advantages of Online Learning

### Question:

> **What are the advantages of online learning?**

### Answer:

> Online learning is flexible and convenient. Students can learn from almost anywhere and often choose their own schedule. There are also many courses and educational resources available online. Another advantage is that students can replay lectures and learn difficult topics at their own pace.

---

# Part 16 — ⚠️ Disadvantages of Online Learning

### Question:

> **What are the disadvantages of online learning?**

### Answer:

> One major disadvantage is the lack of direct interaction. Some students may also find it difficult to stay motivated and disciplined when studying alone. Technical problems and internet issues can also interrupt classes. In addition, online learning may not be ideal for subjects that require hands-on practice.

---

# Part 17 — 🤖 AI & Education

### Question:

> **Can AI improve education?**

### Strong Answer:

> Yes, I think AI can improve education if it is used properly. It can provide personalized explanations, generate practice questions, give feedback, and help students understand difficult concepts. However, students should not become completely dependent on AI. Teachers, critical thinking, and independent learning are still very important.

🔥 Useful structure:

> **Yes → Benefits → Example → However → Conclusion**

---

# Part 18 — 🎓 Is a Degree Necessary?

### Question:

> **Is a university degree necessary for success?**

### Answer:

> I don't think a university degree is the only path to success. A degree can provide knowledge, structure, and opportunities, especially in certain professions. However, practical skills, experience, communication, and continuous learning are also extremely important. In some fields, people can become successful through skills and experience without a traditional degree.

---

# Part 19 — 📅 Exam Preparation

### Question:

> **How do you prepare for exams?**

### Answer:

> I usually start by reviewing the syllabus and identifying the important topics. Then I create a study plan and divide the material into smaller sections. I review my notes, practice previous questions, and test myself regularly. Before the exam, I focus more on revision rather than trying to learn everything from the beginning.

---

# Part 20 — 📱 Study Distractions

### Question:

> **What distracts you while studying?**

### Answer:

> My phone and social media can sometimes distract me. Notifications make it difficult to concentrate, especially when I'm trying to study for a long period. That's why I sometimes put my phone on silent mode or keep it away while studying.

---

# Part 21 — 🔥 Learning a New Skill

### Question:

> **What skill would you like to learn?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to improve my English communication skills. I can understand English reasonably well, but I want to become more confident when speaking. I also want to improve my ability to explain ideas clearly and participate in professional conversations.

---

# Part 22 — 🌍 Study Abroad

### Question:

> **Would you like to study abroad?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I'd like to study abroad if I get the opportunity. Studying in another country could expose me to a different education system, culture, and professional environment. It would also give me an opportunity to meet people from different backgrounds and become more independent.

---

# Part 23 — 🚀 Future Learning Plan

### Question:

> **How do you plan to improve yourself?**

### Answer:

> I plan to improve myself through continuous learning and practical experience. I want to strengthen my technical skills, improve my English communication, and work on real-world projects. I also want to develop better time management and problem-solving skills. My goal is to keep improving gradually rather than trying to learn everything at once.

---

# Part 24 — 🧠 Education Opinion

### Question:

> **What does a good education mean to you?**

### Strong Answer:

> To me, good education is not just about getting high grades or memorizing information. It should teach people how to think, solve problems, communicate, and make good decisions. Practical skills are also important because students need to apply what they learn in real life. Most importantly, good education should encourage curiosity and lifelong learning.

---

# Part 25 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main:

> **What did you study?**

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering.

### Follow-up:

> **Why did you choose it?**

> I chose it because I'm interested in technology and problem-solving.

### Follow-up:

> **What was your favorite subject?**

> Programming was one of my favorite subjects.

### Follow-up:

> **Why did you like programming?**

> I enjoyed creating programs and solving technical problems.

### Follow-up:

> **What skill are you currently improving?**

> I'm currently improving my English communication skills.

### Follow-up:

> **What would you like to learn next?**

> I'd like to improve my advanced programming and communication skills.

---

# Part 26 — 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Education and Learning Journey

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering at university. During my studies, I learned about programming, databases, algorithms, software engineering, networking, and web development.
>
> I chose Computer Science because I have always been interested in technology and problem-solving. I particularly enjoyed programming because it allowed me to turn ideas into working applications. Although some subjects were difficult, I gradually improved through regular practice.
>
> My learning style has changed over time. I used to focus more on memorizing information, especially before exams. Now I try to understand concepts first and then apply them through practical work. For technical subjects, I prefer building projects because practical experience helps me understand things more deeply.
>
> I use both books and online resources. Online resources are especially useful for technology because they provide updated information and practical examples. However, I also believe books are valuable for learning fundamental concepts.
>
> Currently, I'm focusing on improving my technical skills and English communication skills. In the future, I want to continue learning, work on real-world projects, and become a highly skilled professional.
>
> Overall, I believe education should not end after university. Learning should be a lifelong process.

---

# Part 27 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Is the current education system effective?**
2. **Should students get more practical education?**
3. **Are exams the best way to measure students' knowledge?**
4. **Is online learning better than traditional classroom learning?**
5. **Should students be allowed to use AI for assignments?**
6. **What skills should schools teach students?**
7. **Why is critical thinking important?**
8. **Is memorization useful in education?**
9. **Should university education be free?**
10. **What will education look like in the future?**

---

# Part 28 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

### Question:

> **Should students use AI for learning?**

### Strong Answer:

> I think students can use AI as a learning tool, but they should use it responsibly. AI can explain difficult concepts, provide examples, and generate practice questions. For example, students can ask AI to explain a difficult topic in simpler language. However, students should not simply copy AI-generated answers without understanding them. They should use AI to support their learning rather than replace their own thinking.

---

# Part 29 — 📝 Question Making Practice

আজ education নিয়ে নিজে **10টি question** তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **What**

> **Why**

> **Where**

> **When**

> **Who**

> **How**

> **Which**

> **Would**

> **Should**

> **Can**

Examples:

> What did you study?

> Why did you choose your subject?

> How do you study?

> Which subject do you like most?

> Would you like to study abroad?

> Should students use AI?

---

# Part 30 — 🎤 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **“My Education, Learning Style and Future Learning Goals”**

এই order follow করবে:

**Education → University → Subject → Favorite subject → Difficult subject → Study method → Exam preparation → Online learning → AI → Current skills → Future learning**

Connectors:

> **First**

> **During my studies**

> **At first**

> **However**

> **For example**

> **Nowadays**

> **In addition**

> **In my opinion**

> **In the future**

> **Overall**

---

# 📝 Lesson 23 Homework

## Task 1 — Speaking

এই 10টি প্রশ্নের answer মুখে বলবে:

1. What did you study?
2. Why did you choose your subject?
3. What was your favorite subject?
4. Which subject was difficult for you?
5. How do you usually study?
6. Do you prefer studying alone or with others?
7. Do you prefer online or offline learning?
8. How do you prepare for exams?
9. What skill would you like to learn?
10. What does good education mean to you?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Education questions** তৈরি করবে।

Must use:

> **What + Why + Where + When + Who + How + Which + Would + Should + Can**

---

# 🎯 Task 3 — Compare

এই topic-এ **2–3 মিনিট** বলবে:

> **“Online Learning vs Traditional Classroom Learning”**

Structure:

> **Online advantage → Online disadvantage → Classroom advantage → Classroom disadvantage → Your preference → Reason**

---

# 🎤 Task 4 — University Interview

নিজেকে একজন interviewer এবং candidate হিসেবে imagine করো।

### Q1

> Tell me about your educational background.

### Q2

> Why did you choose your field?

### Q3

> What was your favorite subject?

### Q4

> What was the biggest challenge during your studies?

### Q5

> What skills did you develop at university?

### Q6

> What are you currently learning?

### Q7

> What would you like to learn in the future?

প্রতিটি answer **45–60 seconds** বলার চেষ্টা করবে।

---

# ⭐ Lesson 23 Golden Formula

Education topic-এর answer expand করবে:

> **Background → Reason → Experience → Challenge → Learning Method → Current Skill → Future Goal**

Example:

> I studied Computer Science and Engineering because I was interested in technology. During university, I learned programming and software development. Some subjects were challenging, but regular practice helped me improve. Now I prefer understanding concepts and applying them through projects rather than memorizing information. I'm currently improving my technical and communication skills. In the future, I want to continue learning and gain more practical experience.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 23 Final Target

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about your educational background, what you learned, how you study, and what you want to learn in the future.”**

### 🔥 Lesson 23 Core Skill

**Education describe → Past experience explain → Learning method explain → Compare → Opinion give → Future learning plan discuss.**


---
# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 24

## Topic: Daily Routine, Time Management & Productivity ⏰📅💼

আজকের Lesson-এ আমরা **daily routine, morning routine, study/work schedule, time management, productivity, procrastination, habits এবং future routine** নিয়ে English speaking practice করব।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি যেন **নিজের পুরো দিনের routine + কাজের অভ্যাস + time management নিয়ে 3–5 মিনিট naturally English-এ বলতে পারো।**

আজকের focus:

> **Routine → Time → Habit → Problem → Solution → Opinion → Future Plan**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🌅 Daily Routine

1. **What time do you usually wake up?**
2. **What do you do after waking up?**
3. **What is your morning routine?**
4. **What time do you usually go to bed?**
5. **How do you usually spend your day?**
6. **What do you usually do in the evening?**
7. **What do you do before going to bed?**
8. **Do you have a fixed daily routine?**
9. **Has your daily routine changed over time?**
10. **What is your favorite part of the day?**

## ⏰ Time Management

11. **How do you manage your time?**
12. **Do you make a daily schedule?**
13. **How do you decide what to do first?**
14. **Do you use a to-do list?**
15. **Are you good at managing time?**
16. **What usually wastes your time?**
17. **How do you deal with distractions?**
18. **Do you often procrastinate?**
19. **How do you stay focused?**
20. **How do you balance work and free time?**

## 🚀 Productivity

21. **When are you most productive?**
22. **What helps you stay productive?**
23. **What makes you lose motivation?**
24. **How do you take breaks?**
25. **Do you prefer working in the morning or at night?**
26. **How many hours do you work or study?**
27. **How do you handle a busy day?**
28. **What habit would you like to change?**
29. **What would you like to accomplish every day?**
30. **What does a productive day mean to you?**

---

# Part 2 — ⏰ Important Vocabulary

| English         | বাংলা              |
| --------------- | ------------------ |
| Routine         | দৈনন্দিন রুটিন     |
| Schedule        | সময়সূচি           |
| Habit           | অভ্যাস             |
| Task            | কাজ                |
| Goal            | লক্ষ্য             |
| Priority        | অগ্রাধিকার         |
| Deadline        | শেষ সময়সীমা       |
| Productivity    | উৎপাদনশীলতা        |
| Focus           | মনোযোগ             |
| Distraction     | মনোযোগের বিঘ্ন     |
| Procrastination | কাজ পিছিয়ে দেওয়া |
| Break           | বিরতি              |
| Rest            | বিশ্রাম            |
| Plan            | পরিকল্পনা          |
| Organize        | গুছানো             |
| Manage          | পরিচালনা করা       |
| Complete        | সম্পন্ন করা        |
| Improve         | উন্নত করা          |
| Routine         | নিয়মিত কাজের ধারা |
| Balance         | ভারসাম্য           |

---

# Part 3 — 🌅 What Time Do You Wake Up?

### Question:

> **What time do you usually wake up?**

### Beginner:

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m.

### Intermediate:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. After waking up, I wash my face, drink some water, and prepare for the day.

### Advanced:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. I try not to check my phone immediately after waking up. Instead, I freshen up, drink some water, and think about the main tasks I need to complete during the day.

---

# Part 4 — 🌅 Morning Routine

### Question:

> **What is your morning routine?**

### Answer:

> My morning routine is fairly simple. I usually wake up, freshen up, and have breakfast. After that, I check my schedule and decide what I need to focus on first. Then I start studying or working.

### Useful sequence:

> **First → Then → After that → Finally**

---

# Part 5 — 📅 Describe Your Whole Day

### Question:

> **How do you usually spend your day?**

### Answer:

> I usually start my day in the morning by having breakfast and planning my tasks. Then I spend several hours studying or working. I take short breaks in between so that I can stay focused. In the afternoon, I continue with my main tasks and sometimes take some time to relax. In the evening, I usually spend time learning something, working on personal projects, or talking with friends and family. Before going to bed, I review what I accomplished during the day and think about the next day's tasks.

---

# Part 6 — 🌙 Evening Routine

### Question:

> **What do you usually do in the evening?**

### Answer:

> In the evening, I usually finish my important tasks and take some time to relax. I may watch something, listen to music, read, or work on a personal project. If I have unfinished work, I try to complete the most important task before going to bed.

---

# Part 7 — 😴 Before Bed

### Question:

> **What do you do before going to bed?**

### Answer:

> Before going to bed, I usually finish my remaining tasks and prepare for the next day. Sometimes I read or listen to something relaxing. I also try to avoid using my phone too much before sleeping.

---

# Part 8 — 🔄 Fixed Routine

### Question:

> **Do you have a fixed daily routine?**

### Answer:

> My routine is partly fixed. I usually wake up and sleep at roughly the same time, but the rest of my schedule changes depending on my work and responsibilities. I prefer having a flexible routine because unexpected tasks can come up.

🔥 Important phrase:

> **My routine is partly fixed.**

---

# Part 9 — 📈 Routine Changed Over Time

### Question:

> **Has your daily routine changed over time?**

### Answer:

> Yes, my daily routine has changed quite a lot over time. In the past, I didn't plan my day very carefully. I often spent too much time on unimportant activities. Now I try to organize my tasks and focus on my priorities. I'm still improving my time-management skills.

### Grammar:

> **In the past...**

> **I used to...**

> **Now I...**

> **I've started...**

---

# Part 10 — ⏰ Time Management

### Question:

> **How do you manage your time?**

### Answer:

> I try to manage my time by setting priorities. First, I identify the most important tasks. Then I estimate how much time each task will take and create a simple schedule. I also try to avoid unnecessary distractions while working.

---

# Part 11 — 📝 To-Do List

### Question:

> **Do you use a to-do list?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I often use a to-do list. It helps me remember important tasks and gives me a clear idea of what I need to do. I usually put the most important tasks at the top of the list.

### Useful phrases:

> **make a to-do list**

> **complete a task**

> **cross something off the list**

> **set priorities**

Example:

> I completed three tasks and crossed them off my list.

---

# Part 12 — 🥇 Priorities

### Question:

> **How do you decide what to do first?**

### Answer:

> I usually consider both importance and urgency. If something is important and has a close deadline, I do it first. Less important tasks can wait until I finish the main priorities.

🔥 Vocabulary:

> **important**

> **urgent**

> **deadline**

> **priority**

---

# Part 13 — 📱 Time Wasters

### Question:

> **What usually wastes your time?**

### Answer:

> Social media and unnecessary phone usage can waste a lot of my time. Sometimes I also spend too much time watching random videos. I'm trying to become more aware of these habits and reduce unnecessary screen time.

---

# Part 14 — 😴 Procrastination

### Question:

> **Do you often procrastinate?**

### Answer:

> Sometimes I do procrastinate, especially when a task seems difficult or boring. I may keep thinking about the task instead of starting it. One thing that helps me is breaking a large task into smaller steps and starting with the easiest one.

🔥 Important phrase:

> **I keep putting it off.**

Meaning:

> আমি কাজটা বারবার পিছিয়ে দিই।

Example:

> I keep putting off difficult tasks.

---

# Part 15 — 🎯 How to Stay Focused

### Question:

> **How do you stay focused?**

### Answer:

> I try to focus on one task at a time. I keep my phone away or turn off unnecessary notifications. I also work in short focused sessions and take small breaks between them. This helps me maintain concentration.

---

# Part 16 — 🔥 One Task at a Time

### Useful Expressions

❌ Don't say:

> I do many tasks together.

Better:

> **I focus on one task at a time.**

> **I try to avoid multitasking.**

> **I give my full attention to one task.**

Example:

> I work better when I focus on one task at a time instead of trying to do everything simultaneously.

---

# Part 17 — ☕ Taking Breaks

### Question:

> **How do you take breaks?**

### Answer:

> I usually take short breaks after working for a while. During a break, I might walk around, drink water, stretch, or simply rest for a few minutes. I try not to spend the entire break scrolling through social media because that can make it difficult to return to work.

---

# Part 18 — 🌅 Morning vs Night

### Question:

> **Do you prefer working in the morning or at night?**

### Answer:

> I prefer working in the morning because I usually feel fresh and focused at that time. I find it easier to concentrate on difficult tasks early in the day. However, I sometimes work at night when I have a deadline or need extra time.

---

# Part 19 — ⚖️ Work-Life Balance

### Question:

> **How do you balance work and free time?**

### Answer:

> I try to finish my important tasks first and then spend some time relaxing. I think working continuously without taking breaks can reduce productivity. That's why I try to maintain a balance between work, learning, rest, and personal activities.

🔥 Key phrase:

> **maintain a healthy balance**

---

# Part 20 — 🚀 Productivity

### Question:

> **What helps you stay productive?**

### Answer:

> Planning, a quiet environment, and clear goals help me stay productive. I also find it easier to work when I know exactly what I need to accomplish. Breaking large tasks into smaller steps makes them feel more manageable.

---

# Part 21 — 😕 What Makes You Lose Motivation?

### Question:

> **What makes you lose motivation?**

### Answer:

> I sometimes lose motivation when I don't see progress quickly. Repeating the same task for a long time can also make me tired. When this happens, I usually take a short break, review my goals, and start again with a smaller task.

---

# Part 22 — 📚 Study/Work Hours

### Question:

> **How many hours do you work or study?**

### Answer:

> It depends on the day. On a normal day, I try to spend several focused hours on work or study. I don't think working for a very long time automatically means being productive. I prefer shorter periods of focused work with proper breaks.

---

# Part 23 — 🚨 Busy Day

### Question:

> **How do you handle a busy day?**

### Answer:

> When I have a busy day, I don't try to do everything at once. First, I identify the most urgent and important tasks. Then I complete them one by one. If necessary, I postpone less important tasks. This helps me stay calm and organized.

---

# Part 24 — 🔄 Habit You Want to Change

### Question:

> **What habit would you like to change?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to reduce unnecessary phone usage. Sometimes I spend more time on social media than I originally planned. I want to replace some of that time with reading, exercise, or learning useful skills.

---

# Part 25 — 🎯 Productive Day

### Question:

> **What does a productive day mean to you?**

### Strong Answer:

> To me, a productive day doesn't mean being busy every minute. It means making meaningful progress on the things that matter. If I complete my most important tasks, learn something new, and still have enough time to rest, I consider the day productive.

🔥 This is a very natural advanced answer.

---

# Part 26 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main:

> **What time do you usually wake up?**

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you do after waking up?**

> I freshen up, drink some water, and have breakfast.

### Follow-up:

> **When do you start working?**

> I usually start working after breakfast.

### Follow-up:

> **How do you manage your tasks?**

> I make a simple to-do list and prioritize the important tasks.

### Follow-up:

> **What distracts you?**

> My phone and social media can sometimes distract me.

### Follow-up:

> **How do you stay focused?**

> I keep my phone away and focus on one task at a time.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you do in the evening?**

> I usually relax, learn something, or work on personal projects.

### Follow-up:

> **What time do you sleep?**

> I usually go to bed around midnight.

---

# Part 27 — 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Daily Routine

> My daily routine is fairly simple, although it can change depending on my responsibilities. I usually wake up around 7 a.m. After waking up, I freshen up, drink some water, and have breakfast. Then I check my schedule and decide which tasks I need to focus on first.
>
> During the day, I spend several focused hours studying or working. I usually make a to-do list and prioritize the most important tasks. I try to focus on one task at a time because multitasking can reduce my concentration.
>
> I sometimes get distracted by my phone or social media. When that happens, I try to put my phone away and return to the task. I also take short breaks between focused work sessions so that I don't become too tired.
>
> In the evening, I usually finish my important tasks and spend some time relaxing. I might read, watch something, listen to music, or work on a personal project. Before going to bed, I review what I accomplished during the day and think about what I need to do the next day.
>
> My routine isn't perfect, and I sometimes procrastinate. However, I'm trying to improve my time management and reduce unnecessary distractions. For me, a productive day means making meaningful progress while also maintaining a healthy balance between work, learning, and rest.

---

# Part 28 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Why do people procrastinate?**
2. **Is being busy the same as being productive?**
3. **Why is time management important?**
4. **How can people stop wasting time?**
5. **Is waking up early really important for success?**
6. **Should people follow a strict daily routine?**
7. **How does social media affect productivity?**
8. **Is multitasking good or bad?**
9. **How can students manage study and entertainment?**
10. **What is the difference between being busy and being productive?**

---

# Part 29 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

### Question:

> **Is waking up early important for success?**

### Answer:

> I don't think waking up early automatically makes someone successful. However, waking up early can give people more quiet time to plan and complete important tasks. For example, some people find it easier to concentrate in the morning. On the other hand, some people are more productive at night. So, I think the most important thing is having a routine that matches your lifestyle and allows you to work effectively.

🔥 Notice:

> **I don't think...**

> **However...**

> **For example...**

> **On the other hand...**

> **So, I think...**

---

# Part 30 — 📝 Question Making Practice

আজ **Daily Routine & Productivity** নিয়ে নিজে 10টি question তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **What**

> **When**

> **Where**

> **Why**

> **Who**

> **How**

> **How often**

> **How long**

> **Would**

> **Should**

Examples:

> What time do you wake up?

> How do you manage your time?

> How often do you exercise?

> Why do you procrastinate?

> How do you stay focused?

> What would you change about your routine?

> Should people follow a strict routine?

---

## Topic:

> **“My Daily Routine and How I Manage My Time”**

এই order follow করবে:

**Wake up → Morning → Study/Work → Lunch → Afternoon → Evening → Free time → Night → Time management → Distraction → Future improvement**

Must use:

> **Usually**

> **After that**

> **Then**

> **Sometimes**

> **However**

> **Because**

> **For example**

> **In the future**

> **Overall**

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Daily Routine questions** তৈরি করবে।

Must use:

> **What + When + Where + Why + Who + How + How often + How long + Would + Should**

---

## Task 3 — ⚡ Compare

এই topic-এ **2–3 মিনিট** বলবে:

> **“Morning Person vs Night Person”**

Structure:

> **Morning advantage → Morning disadvantage → Night advantage → Night disadvantage → Your preference → Reason**

---

## Task 4 — 🎯 Daily Schedule

নিজের একটি imaginary English schedule বলবে:

> **7:00 — Wake up**

> **8:00 — Breakfast**

> **9:00 — Study/Work**

> **1:00 — Lunch**

> **2:00 — Work**

> **6:00 — Break**

> **8:00 — Personal learning**

> **10:00 — Relax**

> **12:00 — Sleep**

প্রতিটি সময়ের সাথে **একটি complete English sentence** বলবে।

Example:

> **At 9 a.m., I start my work and focus on my most important task of the day.**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 24 Golden Formula

Daily routine-এর answer expand করবে:

> **Time → Activity → Frequency → Reason → Problem → Solution → Future**

Example:

> I usually wake up around 7 a.m. and start my day with a simple morning routine. I spend several hours studying or working during the day. I sometimes get distracted by my phone, so I try to keep it away while working. I also make a to-do list to prioritize important tasks. In the future, I'd like to improve my sleep schedule and become more consistent with my daily routine.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 24 Final Target

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about your daily routine, how you manage your time, what distracts you, and how you want to improve your productivity.”**

### 🔥 Lesson 24 Core Skill

**Routine describe → Time explain → Habit describe → Problem identify → Solution explain → Opinion give → Future improvement discuss.**

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 25

## Topic: **Work, Career, Jobs & Professional Life** 💼👨‍💻

> **Continuity:** Lesson 24 ছিল **Daily Routine → Time Management → Productivity**।
> Lesson 25-এ আমরা সেই একই বিষয়কে এগিয়ে নিয়ে যাব:
>
> **Daily Routine → Work/Study → Productivity → Career → Future Goal**
>
> অর্থাৎ আগের lesson-এর vocabulary ও sentence pattern আবার ব্যবহার হবে, কিন্তু নতুন **Work & Career** topic শেখানো হবে।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি যেন English-এ বলতে পারো:

> **“What do you do, what kind of job you want, what skills you have, and what your future career goals are.”**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 💼 Work & Job

1. **What do you do?**
2. **Are you working or studying?**
3. **What kind of work do you do?**
4. **What does a typical workday look like for you?**
5. **What do you like about your work?**
6. **What do you dislike about your work?**
7. **What is the most difficult part of your work?**
8. **What is the most enjoyable part of your work?**
9. **Do you prefer working alone or with a team?**
10. **How do you manage your workload?**

## 🎓 Career

11. **What career do you want to pursue?**
12. **Why did you choose this career?**
13. **What skills are important for your career?**
14. **What skills are you currently improving?**
15. **What kind of company would you like to work for?**
16. **What is your dream job?**
17. **What makes a good employee?**
18. **What makes a good manager?**
19. **Would you prefer a high salary or a good work-life balance?**
20. **What is more important: skills or experience?**

## 🚀 Future

21. **Where do you see yourself in five years?**
22. **What are your short-term career goals?**
23. **What are your long-term career goals?**
24. **Would you like to work abroad?**
25. **Would you like to start your own business?**
26. **What professional skill would you like to learn?**
27. **How can you improve your career?**
28. **What kind of workplace do you prefer?**
29. **What would you do if you lost your job?**
30. **What does career success mean to you?**

---

# Part 3 — 💼 What Do You Do?

### Question:

> **What do you do?**

### Beginner:

> I am a student.

### Intermediate:

> I have a background in Computer Science and Engineering. I am interested in software development.

### Advanced:

> I have a background in Computer Science and Engineering, and I'm interested in building a career in software development. I'm particularly interested in backend and full-stack development because I enjoy solving problems and building practical applications.

---

# Part 4 — 🎓 Are You Working or Studying?

### Question:

> **Are you working or studying?**

### Answer:

> At the moment, I'm focusing on developing my professional skills. I spend part of my day learning and practicing technical skills, and I also work on personal projects. My goal is to prepare myself for a professional software development career.

### Notice the connection with Lesson 24:

> **I spend part of my day...**

> **My goal is to...**

> **I try to...**

> **I focus on...**

---

# Part 5 — ⏰ Typical Workday

### Question:

> **What does a typical workday look like for you?**

### Answer:

> A typical day starts with planning my tasks. I usually identify my priorities and decide what I need to complete first. Then I spend several focused hours working or learning. I take short breaks between tasks to maintain my concentration. In the evening, I review what I've accomplished and plan the next day's work.

🔥 This directly connects with Lesson 24.

### Formula:

> **Start → Plan → Work → Break → Finish → Review**

---

# Part 6 — ❤️ What Do You Like About Your Work?

### Question:

> **What do you like about your work?**

### Answer:

> I enjoy solving problems and learning new things. I especially like tasks that require me to think creatively and find practical solutions. I also enjoy seeing the final result of something I've built.

---

# Part 7 — 😕 What Don't You Like?

### Question:

> **What do you dislike about your work?**

### Answer:

> I don't particularly like repetitive tasks. Doing the same thing again and again can become boring. However, I understand that some repetitive tasks are necessary, so I try to complete them efficiently and focus more on the important parts of the work.

🔥 Natural phrase:

> **I don't particularly like...**

---

# Part 8 — 🧩 Difficult Part of Work

### Question:

> **What is the most difficult part of your work?**

### Answer:

> The most difficult part is sometimes managing several tasks at the same time. When I have multiple deadlines, I need to prioritize carefully. I try to break large tasks into smaller steps and complete them one by one.

### Connection:

Lesson 24:

> **Prioritize → Break tasks → Focus**

Lesson 25:

> **Workload → Deadline → Productivity**

---

# Part 9 — 👤 Alone or Team?

### Question:

> **Do you prefer working alone or with a team?**

### Answer:

> I prefer a combination of both. I like working alone when I need deep concentration, especially for programming or problem-solving. However, teamwork is important when a project requires different skills. Working with others also helps me learn different approaches to solving problems.

---

# Part 10 — 📋 Workload

### Question:

> **How do you manage your workload?**

### Answer:

> I manage my workload by setting priorities and creating a simple schedule. I first focus on urgent and important tasks. Then I work on less urgent tasks. If the workload is too large, I divide it into smaller tasks and complete them step by step.

### Golden structure:

> **Prioritize → Schedule → Divide → Complete**

---

# Part 11 — 🚀 Career Choice

### Question:

> **What career do you want to pursue?**

### Answer:

> I want to pursue a career in software development. I'm particularly interested in backend and full-stack development. I enjoy programming, problem-solving, and building applications. I also like the fact that technology is constantly changing, which means there are always new things to learn.

---

# Part 12 — 🤔 Why This Career?

### Question:

> **Why did you choose this career?**

### Answer:

> I chose this career because I enjoy technology and problem-solving. I like building things from scratch and seeing how different parts of a system work together. I also believe software development offers opportunities for continuous learning and professional growth.

---

# Part 13 — 🧠 Important Skills

### Question:

> **What skills are important for your career?**

### Answer:

> I think technical knowledge, problem-solving, communication, teamwork, and time management are all important. Technical skills help you do the job, while communication and teamwork help you work effectively with others. Time management is also important because professionals often have multiple responsibilities and deadlines.

---

# Part 14 — 📈 Skills You Are Improving

### Question:

> **What skills are you currently improving?**

### Answer:

> I'm currently improving my technical and communication skills. I'm working on programming and software development, while also trying to become more confident in English speaking. I believe both technical ability and communication skills are important for building a successful career.

---

# Part 15 — 🏢 Dream Company

### Question:

> **What kind of company would you like to work for?**

### Answer:

> I'd like to work for a company that provides a supportive and professional environment. I want to work with experienced people, contribute to real-world projects, and continue learning. A good company should also value teamwork, innovation, and employee development.

---

# Part 16 — ⭐ Dream Job

### Question:

> **What is your dream job?**

### Answer:

> My dream job is to become a skilled software engineer. I'd like to work on challenging projects that solve real-world problems. I also want to work with a strong team where I can learn from experienced developers and contribute my own ideas.

---

# Part 17 — 👨‍💼 What Makes a Good Employee?

### Question:

> **What makes a good employee?**

### Answer:

> A good employee should be responsible, reliable, hardworking, and willing to learn. Good communication and teamwork are also important. In addition, a good employee should take responsibility for mistakes and try to improve rather than blaming others.

---

# Part 18 — 👨‍💼 What Makes a Good Manager?

### Question:

> **What makes a good manager?**

### Answer:

> A good manager should communicate clearly, listen to employees, and make fair decisions. They should understand their team's strengths and weaknesses and help employees improve. A good manager should also know how to motivate people without creating unnecessary pressure.

---

# Part 19 — 💰 Salary vs Work-Life Balance

### Question:

> **Would you prefer a high salary or a good work-life balance?**

### Strong Answer:

> I think both are important, but I would prefer a reasonable salary with a healthy work-life balance. A high salary is valuable, but if someone is constantly stressed and has no time for family, health, or personal development, it may not be worth it. Ideally, I'd like to have a career that provides both financial stability and personal satisfaction.

🔥 Excellent structure:

> **Both → Preference → Reason → Example → Ideal situation**

---

# Part 20 — 🧠 Skills vs Experience

### Question:

> **What is more important: skills or experience?**

### Answer:

> I think both are important, and they support each other. Skills allow someone to perform a job effectively, while experience teaches them how to handle real-world situations. A person with strong skills but no experience may need time to adapt, while an experienced person may struggle if their skills are outdated. So, continuous learning and practical experience are both necessary.

---

# Part 21 — 🔮 Where Do You See Yourself in Five Years?

### Question:

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

### Answer:

> In five years, I hope to be an experienced software developer with strong technical and communication skills. I'd like to have worked on several real-world projects and taken on more responsibility. I also hope to continue learning new technologies and become someone who can contribute valuable solutions to a development team.

---

# Part 22 — 🎯 Short-Term Goal

### Question:

> **What are your short-term career goals?**

### Answer:

> My short-term goal is to strengthen my technical skills and gain practical professional experience. I want to improve my programming, backend development, database knowledge, and communication skills. I also want to work on real-world projects and learn from experienced developers.

---

# Part 23 — 🚀 Long-Term Goal

### Question:

> **What are your long-term career goals?**

### Answer:

> My long-term goal is to become a highly skilled software engineer. I want to take responsibility for complex projects and eventually become capable of designing and building complete systems. I also want to continue learning and possibly take on leadership responsibilities in the future.

---

# Part 24 — 🌍 Working Abroad

### Question:

> **Would you like to work abroad?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I would consider working abroad if I got a good opportunity. Working in another country could expose me to different technologies, professional cultures, and ways of working. However, my main priority would be finding an environment where I can grow professionally and contribute meaningfully.

---

# Part 25 — 💡 Starting a Business

### Question:

> **Would you like to start your own business?**

### Answer:

> Yes, possibly in the future. I think starting a business can be challenging but also rewarding. First, I'd like to gain enough professional experience and understand the industry better. Once I have a strong idea and enough experience, I might consider building my own technology-based business.

---

# Part 26 — 🧩 What If You Lost Your Job?

### Question:

> **What would you do if you lost your job?**

### Answer:

> If I lost my job, I would first try to understand what went wrong and learn from the experience. Then I would update my CV, improve any weak skills, and start applying for suitable positions. I would also use the time to learn something new and build practical projects.

### Grammar:

> **If + Past Simple → would + verb**

> If I lost my job, I would...

---

# Part 27 — 🏆 Career Success

### Question:

> **What does career success mean to you?**

### Strong Answer:

> To me, career success is not only about earning a high salary. It means becoming highly skilled, doing meaningful work, continuing to learn, and being respected as a professional. Financial stability is important, but personal satisfaction and professional growth are also important. Ultimately, I want to build a career that I'm proud of.

---

# Part 28 — 🗣️ Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main:

> **What career do you want to pursue?**

> I want to pursue a career in software development.

### Follow-up:

> **Why did you choose it?**

> Because I enjoy technology and problem-solving.

### Follow-up:

> **What skills do you need?**

> I need strong programming, communication, teamwork, and problem-solving skills.

### Follow-up:

> **What are you currently improving?**

> I'm improving my technical skills and English communication.

### Follow-up:

> **What kind of company do you want to work for?**

> I'd like to work for a company with a supportive and professional environment.

### Follow-up:

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

> I hope to be an experienced software developer working on challenging real-world projects.

---

# Part 29 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Career & Professional Goals

> I want to build a career in software development because I'm interested in technology, programming, and problem-solving. I enjoy creating applications and finding solutions to technical problems.
>
> A typical productive day for me starts with planning my tasks. I usually identify my priorities and then spend several focused hours studying, practicing, or working on projects. I try to avoid distractions and focus on one task at a time.
>
> At the moment, I'm working on improving both my technical and communication skills. I believe that being a good developer requires more than just programming knowledge. Communication, teamwork, problem-solving, and time management are also very important.
>
> In the short term, I want to gain practical professional experience and improve my technical abilities. I want to work on real-world projects and learn from experienced developers.
>
> In the long term, I want to become a highly skilled software engineer. I'd like to work on challenging projects, take more responsibility, and eventually become someone who can design and build complete systems.
>
> For me, career success isn't just about earning a high salary. I want to have a meaningful career where I can continuously learn, solve real problems, grow professionally, and maintain a healthy work-life balance.

---

# Part 30 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **What makes someone successful in their career?**
2. **Is a high salary more important than job satisfaction?**
3. **Why is work-life balance important?**
4. **Should people choose a career based on salary?**
5. **Is experience more important than education?**
6. **Why do people change careers?**
7. **Should employees work from home?**
8. **What makes a workplace productive?**
9. **How will AI change jobs in the future?**
10. **What will the workplace look like in ten years?**

---

# Part 31 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

### Question:

> **Is a high salary more important than job satisfaction?**

### Answer:

> I don't think salary is the only factor that matters when choosing a career. A good salary provides financial security, which is important. However, if someone dislikes their work and feels stressed every day, money alone may not make them happy. Job satisfaction, career growth, workplace culture, and work-life balance are also important. Ideally, people should look for a career that provides both financial stability and personal satisfaction.

---

# Part 32 — 📝 Question Making Practice

আজ **Work & Career** নিয়ে নিজে 10টি questions তৈরি করবে।

Must use:

> **What**

> **Why**

> **Where**

> **When**

> **Who**

> **How**

> **Which**

> **Would**

> **Should**

> **If**

Examples:

> What kind of job do you want?

> Why did you choose this career?

> What skills are important?

> How do you manage your workload?

> Which company would you like to work for?

> Would you like to work abroad?

> What would you do if you lost your job?

---

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

### Lesson 25:

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

অর্থাৎ তুমি আলাদা আলাদা topic মুখস্থ করবে না।

**এক topic থেকে পরের topic naturally connect করবে।**

---

# Part 34 — 🎤 5-Minute Speaking Challenge

## Topic:

> **“My Daily Work/Study Routine and My Future Career”**

এই order follow করবে:

**Daily routine → Work/Study → Tasks → Productivity → Skills → Career choice → Job preference → Short-term goal → Long-term goal → Five-year plan**

Connectors:

> **Currently**

> **Usually**

> **Because**

> **For example**

> **However**

> **In addition**

> **At the moment**

> **In the short term**

> **In the long term**

> **In the future**

> **Ultimately**

2. What career do you want to pursue?

4. What skills are important for your career?

6. What kind of company would you like to work for?
7. Do you prefer working alone or with a team?

---

## Task 2 — 🔥 Question Making

নিজে **10টি Work & Career questions** তৈরি করবে।

Use:

> **What + Why + Where + When + Who + How + Which + Would + Should + If**

---

## Task 3 — ⚡ Interview Practice

নিজেকে job interview-এ imagine করো।

### Interviewer:

> **Tell me about yourself.**

### You:

> I have a background in...

তারপর:

> **Why do you want this job?**

> **What are your strengths?**

> **What is one weakness you're working on?**

> **Why should we hire you?**

> **Where do you see yourself in five years?**

প্রতিটি answer **45–60 seconds** বলবে।

---

# ⭐ Lesson 25 Golden Formula

Career answer expand করবে:

> **Current Situation → Interest → Reason → Skills → Experience → Short-term Goal → Long-term Goal**

### Example:

> Currently, I'm focusing on developing my professional skills. I'm interested in software development because I enjoy technology and problem-solving. I have been developing my programming and communication skills through learning and practical projects. In the short term, I want to gain professional experience and work on real-world projects. In the long term, I want to become a highly skilled software engineer and take responsibility for complex projects.

---

# 🎯 Lesson 25 Final Target

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about what you do, your career interests, your skills, your work routine, and your future professional goals.”**

### 🔥 Lesson 25 Core Skill

**Daily routine → Work routine → Productivity → Skills → Career choice → Job preference → Career goals → Future plan**

**Lesson 24-এর flow বজায় রেখেই Lesson 25 শেষ হলো।**

# 🟢 English Speaking Complete Course — Lesson 26

## Topic: **Workplace, Colleagues, Communication & Teamwork** 🤝💼

### 🔗 Previous Lesson-এর সাথে Connection

**Lesson 24:** Daily Routine → Time Management → Productivity
**Lesson 25:** Work → Career → Skills → Career Goals
**Lesson 26:** **Workplace → Colleagues → Communication → Teamwork → Problem Solving**

অর্থাৎ এখন আমরা **career নিয়ে কথা বলা থেকে actual workplace communication**-এ যাব।

### 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ বলতে পারবে:

> **Who you work with → How you communicate → How you work in a team → How you handle problems → How you deal with colleagues**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 🏢 Workplace

1. **What kind of workplace do you prefer?**
2. **What makes a good workplace?**
3. **What does a typical workplace look like?**
4. **Do you prefer working from home or from an office?**
5. **What do you like about your workplace?**
6. **What would you change about your workplace?**
7. **How important is a friendly workplace?**
8. **What makes a workplace productive?**
9. **How do you organize your work?**
10. **How do you handle deadlines?**

## 🤝 Colleagues & Teamwork

11. **Do you enjoy working with colleagues?**
12. **What makes a good colleague?**
13. **Do you prefer working alone or in a team?**
14. **What makes a good team?**
15. **How do you communicate with your teammates?**
16. **How do you divide work in a team?**
17. **What do you do when a teammate makes a mistake?**
18. **How do you handle disagreements?**
19. **Have you ever worked on a team project?**
20. **What have you learned from teamwork?**

## 🗣️ Communication & Problems

21. **Why is communication important at work?**
22. **How do you explain a difficult idea?**
23. **What do you do when you don't understand something?**
24. **How do you ask for help?**
25. **How do you give feedback?**
26. **How do you receive criticism?**
27. **What would you do if you disagreed with your manager?**
28. **How do you solve problems at work?**
29. **How do you handle pressure?**
30. **What is more important: communication or technical skills?**

---

# Part 2 — 🧠 Important Vocabulary

| English        | বাংলা          |
| -------------- | -------------- |
| Workplace      | কর্মস্থল       |
| Colleague      | সহকর্মী        |
| Coworker       | সহকর্মী        |
| Teammate       | দলের সদস্য     |
| Manager        | ম্যানেজার      |
| Team leader    | দলনেতা         |
| Meeting        | মিটিং          |
| Communication  | যোগাযোগ        |
| Discussion     | আলোচনা         |
| Feedback       | মতামত/ফিডব্যাক |
| Criticism      | সমালোচনা       |
| Agreement      | সম্মতি         |
| Disagreement   | মতবিরোধ        |
| Cooperation    | সহযোগিতা       |
| Responsibility | দায়িত্ব       |
| Task           | কাজ            |
| Deadline       | শেষ সময়সীমা   |
| Problem        | সমস্যা         |
| Solution       | সমাধান         |
| Pressure       | চাপ            |

---

# Part 3 — 🏢 What Kind of Workplace Do You Prefer?

### Question:

> **What kind of workplace do you prefer?**

### Beginner:

> I prefer a friendly workplace.

### Intermediate:

> I prefer a professional and friendly workplace where people communicate clearly and respect each other.

### Advanced:

> I prefer a professional but supportive workplace where employees can communicate openly, share ideas, and learn from each other. I also value an environment where people are treated with respect and are encouraged to improve their skills.

---

# Part 4 — ⭐ What Makes a Good Workplace?

### Question:

> **What makes a good workplace?**

### Answer:

> I think a good workplace should have clear communication, mutual respect, teamwork, and opportunities for growth. Employees should understand their responsibilities and feel comfortable asking questions. A supportive environment can also improve motivation and productivity.

### 🔥 Formula:

> **Communication + Respect + Teamwork + Growth = Good Workplace**

---

# Part 5 — 🏠 Work From Home vs Office

### Question:

> **Do you prefer working from home or from an office?**

### Answer:

> I think both have advantages. Working from home can save time and provide flexibility, while working from an office makes communication and teamwork easier. Personally, I prefer a combination of both, depending on the type of work.

### Advanced:

> For tasks that require deep concentration, working from home can be very effective. However, meetings, brainstorming, and collaborative tasks can sometimes work better in an office.

---

# Part 6 — 👥 Working With Colleagues

### Question:

> **Do you enjoy working with colleagues?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I enjoy working with colleagues because teamwork allows people to share ideas and solve problems together. Different people have different strengths, so working together can produce better results.

---

# Part 7 — 🤝 What Makes a Good Colleague?

### Question:

> **What makes a good colleague?**

### Answer:

> A good colleague should be respectful, responsible, cooperative, and reliable. They should communicate clearly and be willing to help others when necessary. They should also take responsibility for their own work instead of blaming others.

### Useful words:

> **Respectful**

> **Responsible**

> **Reliable**

> **Helpful**

> **Cooperative**

---

# Part 8 — 👥 Teamwork

### Question:

> **What makes a good team?**

### Answer:

> A good team needs clear communication, trust, cooperation, and shared goals. Each member should understand their responsibilities. Team members should also support each other and discuss problems openly instead of hiding them.

---

# Part 9 — 📋 Dividing Work

### Question:

> **How do you divide work in a team?**

### Answer:

> First, we identify all the tasks that need to be completed. Then we divide them according to each person's skills and responsibilities. We also set deadlines and regularly communicate about our progress.

### 🔥 Natural sequence:

> **Identify → Divide → Assign → Set deadlines → Track progress**

---

# Part 10 — 🗣️ Communication

### Question:

> **Why is communication important at work?**

### Answer:

> Communication is important because it helps people understand their responsibilities and avoid misunderstandings. Good communication also makes teamwork easier. If people communicate clearly, they can identify problems earlier and find solutions faster.

---

# Part 11 — 🧠 Explaining a Difficult Idea

### Question:

> **How do you explain a difficult idea?**

### Answer:

> I first try to understand the idea clearly myself. Then I explain it using simple language and practical examples. If necessary, I break the idea into smaller parts and explain each part step by step. Finally, I ask the other person if everything is clear.

🔥 Great professional pattern:

> **Understand → Simplify → Example → Break down → Confirm**

---

# Part 12 — ❓ When You Don't Understand

### Question:

> **What do you do when you don't understand something?**

### Answer:

> I don't pretend that I understand it. I politely ask the person to explain it again. If necessary, I ask a specific question about the part that I find confusing.

### Real-life English:

> **Could you explain that again, please?**

> **Could you give me an example?**

> **I'm not sure I understand this part.**

> **Could you clarify what you mean?**

---

# Part 13 — 🙋 Asking for Help

### Question:

> **How do you ask for help?**

### Natural English:

> **Could you help me with this task?**

> **Could you give me some guidance?**

> **I'm having some difficulty with this. Could you take a look?**

> **Could you explain how this works?**

### Full answer:

> If I have tried to solve a problem myself but still can't figure it out, I ask a colleague or senior teammate for help. Before asking, I try to understand the problem so that I can explain exactly where I'm stuck.

🔥 Very professional answer.

---

# Part 14 — 📝 Giving Feedback

### Question:

> **How do you give feedback?**

### Answer:

> I try to give feedback respectfully and focus on the work rather than the person. I first mention what was done well and then explain what could be improved. I also try to provide a practical suggestion instead of simply pointing out a problem.

### Example:

❌ Bad:

> Your work is bad.

✅ Better:

> **The overall approach is good, but I think we could improve this part by simplifying the process.**

---

# Part 15 — 📈 Receiving Criticism

### Question:

> **How do you receive criticism?**

### Answer:

> I try to take constructive criticism positively. Instead of becoming defensive, I listen carefully and try to understand what I can improve. If the feedback is useful, I apply it to my work.

🔥 Key phrase:

> **I try not to take it personally.**

---

# Part 16 — 😤 Handling Disagreements

### Question:

> **How do you handle disagreements?**

### Answer:

> I try to remain calm and understand the other person's point of view. Then I explain my own reasoning and compare the different options. If possible, I focus on facts and evidence rather than personal opinions. The goal should be to find the best solution, not simply to prove that I'm right.

🔥 Advanced sentence:

> **The goal should be to find the best solution, not to prove who is right.**

---

# Part 17 — 👨‍💼 Disagreement With Manager

### Question:

> **What would you do if you disagreed with your manager?**

### Answer:

> I would respectfully explain my concerns and provide reasons for my opinion. I would also listen to my manager's perspective because they may have information that I don't know about. If the final decision is different from my suggestion, I would respect the decision and do my best to support the team.

---

# Part 18 — ❌ Teammate Makes a Mistake

### Question:

> **What do you do when a teammate makes a mistake?**

### Answer:

> First, I would try to understand the mistake and its impact. If I can help fix it, I would offer my assistance. I wouldn't immediately blame the person because mistakes can happen to anyone. After solving the problem, the team should discuss how to prevent the same mistake in the future.

---

# Part 19 — ⏰ Deadlines

### Question:

> **How do you handle deadlines?**

### Answer:

> I try to start important tasks early instead of waiting until the last moment. I break large tasks into smaller steps and set mini-deadlines for myself. If I realize that I might miss a deadline, I communicate the problem as early as possible rather than waiting until the last minute.

🔥 Important:

> **Don't wait until the last minute.**

---

# Part 20 — 😰 Work Pressure

### Question:

> **How do you handle pressure?**

### Answer:

> When I'm under pressure, I try to stay calm and focus on the most important task. I break the workload into smaller parts and complete them one by one. I also avoid wasting time worrying about things that I cannot control.

---

# Part 21 — 🧩 Problem Solving

### Question:

> **How do you solve problems at work?**

### Answer:

> First, I try to understand the problem clearly. Then I identify the possible causes and consider different solutions. After choosing the most appropriate solution, I test it and check whether it actually solves the problem. If necessary, I ask someone with more experience for advice.

### 🔥 Problem-solving formula:

> **Understand → Analyze → Find solutions → Choose → Test → Improve**

---

# Part 22 — 💻 Technical vs Communication Skills

### Question:

> **What is more important: communication or technical skills?**

### Strong Answer:

> I don't think one is more important than the other. Technical skills are necessary to perform the actual job, while communication skills help people work effectively with others. For example, a developer may have excellent programming skills, but if they cannot explain their ideas clearly, teamwork can become difficult. So, I believe professionals need a combination of both.

---

# Part 23 — 🎤 Real Workplace Conversation

## Situation: You don't understand a task.

### Manager:

> Can you finish this task by tomorrow?

### You:

> Yes, I'll try my best.

### Manager:

> Great. Please update the API and add the required validation.

### You:

> Sure. Just to clarify, should I also update the existing tests?

### Manager:

> Yes, please update them as well.

### You:

> Got it. I'll work on it and let you know if I run into any problems.

🔥 Notice these professional phrases:

> **Just to clarify...**

> **Got it.**

> **I'll work on it.**

> **I'll let you know.**

> **If I run into any problems...**

---

# Part 24 — 📞 Asking for Clarification

এই phrases মুখস্থ না করে **situation অনুযায়ী ব্যবহার** করবে:

### যখন বুঝতে পারোনি:

> **Could you explain that again?**

### যখন detail দরকার:

> **Could you give me a little more detail?**

### যখন নিশ্চিত হতে চাও:

> **Just to clarify, do you mean...?**

### যখন example দরকার:

> **Could you give me an example?**

### যখন deadline জানতে চাও:

> **When do you need this by?**

### যখন priority জানতে চাও:

> **Is this the top priority?**

---

# Part 25 — 🤝 Team Meeting English

### Meeting শুরু:

> **Shall we get started?**

### নিজের update:

> **I've completed the first part of the task.**

> **I'm currently working on the second part.**

### Problem:

> **I've run into a small issue.**

### Help:

> **I could use some help with this part.**

### Suggestion:

> **I have a suggestion.**

### Agreement:

> **I agree with that approach.**

### Partial agreement:

> **I agree to some extent, but I think we should also consider...**

### Ending:

> **So, shall we go with this approach?**

---

# Part 26 — 🧠 Full Workplace Conversation

### Manager:

> How is the project going?

### You:

> It's going well. I've completed the authentication module, and I'm currently working on the product API.

### Manager:

> Are you facing any problems?

### You:

> Yes, I've run into a small issue with the database query. I've tried a few approaches, but I haven't found the best solution yet.

### Manager:

> What do you think the problem is?

### You:

> I think the query is retrieving more data than necessary. I'm going to optimize it and test the performance again.

### Manager:

> Sounds good. Let me know if you need help.

### You:

> Sure. I'll keep you updated. Thank you.

🔥 এই ধরনের conversation future job interview এবং workplace English-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# Part 27 — 🎯 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: Teamwork and Workplace Communication

> I believe communication and teamwork are extremely important in a professional environment. Even if someone has strong technical skills, they need to communicate effectively with colleagues and managers.
>
> When I work on a team, I prefer to clearly understand my responsibilities and deadlines. I usually break my tasks into smaller parts and track my progress. If I don't understand something, I ask questions rather than making assumptions.
>
> I also believe disagreements are normal in a workplace. When I disagree with someone, I try to understand their point of view and explain my own reasoning calmly. I prefer focusing on facts and possible solutions rather than making the discussion personal.
>
> If a teammate makes a mistake, I would first try to help solve the problem. After that, I think the team should discuss what caused the mistake and how to prevent it in the future.
>
> Overall, I think a successful team needs trust, communication, responsibility, cooperation, and a shared goal.

---

# Part 28 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Why do teams sometimes fail?**
2. **What makes someone a good team leader?**
3. **How can workplace conflicts be solved?**
4. **Should managers listen to employees' opinions?**
5. **Why do misunderstandings happen at work?**
6. **Is working from home better than working in an office?**
7. **How can employees improve communication?**
8. **Should employees always agree with their managers?**
9. **How should a manager handle an underperforming employee?**
10. **Can a team succeed without good communication?**

---

# Part 29 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

### Question:

> **Can a team succeed without good communication?**

### Strong Answer:

> I think it would be very difficult for a team to succeed without good communication. Team members need to understand their responsibilities, deadlines, and expectations. Without clear communication, misunderstandings can easily happen. For example, two people might unknowingly work on the same task while another important task is ignored. Therefore, good communication is essential for coordination, productivity, and teamwork.

### Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Result → Conclusion**

---

> **Why**

> **Where**

> **When**

> **Who**

> **How**

> **Which**

> **Would**

> **Should**

> **If**

Example:

> What makes a good colleague?

> Why is communication important?

> How do you handle disagreements?

> Which workplace do you prefer?

> Would you work from home?

> What would you do if your teammate made a mistake?

# ⭐ Lesson 26 Golden Formula

Workplace answer expand করবে:

> **Situation → Responsibility → Communication → Problem → Solution → Result → Learning**

Example:

> In a team project, I was responsible for a particular part of the system. I communicated regularly with my teammates and kept them updated on my progress. At one point, we faced a technical problem. Instead of blaming anyone, we discussed the issue and looked for possible solutions. We eventually solved the problem and completed the task. From that experience, I learned that clear communication and teamwork are essential for solving problems effectively.

# 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ বলতে পারবে:

> **What phone you use → How you use it → Who you call → What apps you use → How often you use your phone → Advantages/Disadvantages → Your opinion**

---

# Part 1 — 🗣️ 30 Important Questions

## 📱 About Your Phone

1. **Do you have a smartphone?**
2. **What kind of phone do you use?**
3. **When did you get your phone?**
4. **How long have you been using your current phone?**
5. **Are you happy with your phone?**
6. **What do you like about your phone?**
7. **What don't you like about your phone?**
8. **How often do you use your phone?**
9. **How many hours do you use your phone every day?**
10. **What do you mainly use your phone for?**

## 📞 Calls & Messages

11. **Who do you usually call?**
12. **Who do you text most often?**
13. **Do you prefer calling or texting?**
14. **How often do you make phone calls?**
15. **Do you answer unknown calls?**
16. **Do you prefer voice calls or video calls?**
17. **How often do you send text messages?**
18. **Do you use voice messages?**
19. **Do you like long phone conversations?**
20. **How do you communicate with your family by phone?**

## 📲 Apps & Phone Habits

21. **What apps do you use most?**
22. **What is your favorite phone app?**
23. **Do you use your phone for studying?**
24. **Do you use your phone for work?**
25. **Do you use your phone for entertainment?**
26. **What do you do when your phone battery is low?**
27. **How often do you check your phone?**
28. **Could you live without your phone?**
29. **What are the advantages of smartphones?**
30. **What are the disadvantages of smartphones?**
