# Part 2 — 📚 Important Vocabulary

| English      | বাংলা                   |
| ------------ | ----------------------- |
| Smartphone   | স্মার্টফোন              |
| Mobile phone | মোবাইল ফোন              |
| Screen       | পর্দা                   |
| Battery      | ব্যাটারি                |
| Charger      | চার্জার                 |
| Camera       | ক্যামেরা                |
| Speaker      | স্পিকার                 |
| Headphone    | হেডফোন                  |
| Call         | ফোন করা                 |
| Text         | মেসেজ করা               |
| Message      | বার্তা                  |
| Contact      | যোগাযোগের ব্যক্তি/নম্বর |
| App          | অ্যাপ                   |
| Notification | বিজ্ঞপ্তি               |
| Internet     | ইন্টারনেট               |
| Password     | পাসওয়ার্ড              |
| Download     | ডাউনলোড করা             |
| Upload       | আপলোড করা               |
| Screenshot   | স্ক্রিনশট               |
| Video call   | ভিডিও কল                |

---

# Part 3 — 📱 Do You Have a Smartphone?

### Question:

> **Do you have a smartphone?**

### 🟢 Beginner

> Yes, I have a smartphone.

### 🟡 Intermediate

> Yes, I have a smartphone. I use it every day for communication, studying, entertainment, and browsing the internet.

### 🔴 Advanced

> Yes, I use a smartphone every day. It has become an important part of my daily life because I use it for communication, learning, entertainment, online services, and many other activities.

---

# Part 4 — 📱 What Kind of Phone Do You Use?

### Question:

> **What kind of phone do you use?**

### Answer:

> I use an Android smartphone. It has a good camera, a large screen, and enough storage for my daily needs.

### Advanced:

> I currently use an Android smartphone. I mainly care about performance, battery life, camera quality, and storage when choosing a phone.

---

# Part 5 — 🕐 How Long Have You Used It?

### Question:

> **How long have you been using your current phone?**

### Answer:

> I've been using my current phone for about two years.

### More examples:

> I've been using it for six months.

> I've been using it since 2024.

### Grammar:

**for + period**

> for two years

**since + starting point**

> since 2024

---

# Part 6 — ❤️ What Do You Like About Your Phone?

### Question:

> **What do you like about your phone?**

### Answer:

> I like its performance and camera. It also has a good battery life, so I don't need to charge it frequently. The screen is also comfortable for watching videos and reading.

---

# Part 7 — 😕 What Don't You Like?

### Question:

> **What don't you like about your phone?**

### Answer:

> One thing I don't like is that the battery doesn't last as long when I use it heavily. Sometimes it also gets warm when I use demanding apps for a long time.

---

# Part 8 — ⏰ How Often Do You Use Your Phone?

### Question:

> **How often do you use your phone?**

### Answer:

> I use my phone several times throughout the day. I use it for calls, messages, learning, entertainment, and checking information online.

### Advanced:

> I use my phone frequently throughout the day, but I try to avoid checking it unnecessarily when I'm studying or working.

---

# Part 9 — ⏱️ How Many Hours?

### Question:

> **How many hours do you use your phone every day?**

### Answer:

> I use my phone for several hours a day. It depends on my schedule. On busy days, I use it less, while on free days, I may use it more.

---

# Part 10 — 🎯 What Do You Mainly Use It For?

### Question:

> **What do you mainly use your phone for?**

### Answer:

> I mainly use my phone for communication, learning, browsing the internet, and entertainment. I also use it to check emails, watch educational videos, and stay connected with friends and family.

### Useful pattern:

> **I mainly use it for...**

---

# Part 11 — 📞 Who Do You Usually Call?

### Question:

> **Who do you usually call?**

### Answer:

> I usually call my family and close friends. Sometimes I also make phone calls for work, study, or other important matters.

---

# Part 12 — 💬 Who Do You Text Most Often?

### Question:

> **Who do you text most often?**

### Answer:

> I usually text my friends and family members. We often send short messages to share information or make plans.

---

# Part 13 — 📞 Calling vs Texting

### Question:

> **Do you prefer calling or texting?**

### Answer:

> It depends on the situation. I prefer texting for short and simple conversations because it's convenient. However, I prefer calling when something needs a quick explanation or when the conversation is important.

🔥 Useful phrase:

> **It depends on the situation.**

---

# Part 14 — 📞 How Often Do You Make Calls?

### Question:

> **How often do you make phone calls?**

### Answer:

> I make phone calls several times a week. Most of my calls are with family, friends, or people I need to contact for important matters.

---

# Part 15 — ☎️ Unknown Calls

### Question:

> **Do you answer unknown calls?**

### Answer:

> Not usually. If I don't recognize the number, I normally don't answer immediately. If the call is important, the person can usually send a message or call again.

---

# Part 16 — 🎥 Voice Call vs Video Call

### Question:

> **Do you prefer voice calls or video calls?**

### Answer:

> I usually prefer voice calls because they are quick and convenient. However, video calls are better when I want to have a more personal conversation or communicate with someone who lives far away.

---

# Part 17 — 🎤 Voice Messages

### Question:

> **Do you use voice messages?**

### Answer:

> Yes, sometimes I use voice messages when typing would take too long. They are especially useful when I want to explain something quickly.

---

# Part 18 — 📱 Favorite App

### Question:

> **What is your favorite phone app?**

### Answer:

> My favorite apps are the ones I use for learning and communication. I like apps that help me learn something useful or stay connected with other people.

---

# Part 19 — 📚 Phone for Studying

### Question:

> **Do you use your phone for studying?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I use my phone for studying quite often. I watch educational videos, read articles, search for information, use dictionaries, and practice English.

### Advanced:

> My phone is useful as a learning tool because I can access educational resources almost anywhere.

---

# Part 20 — 💼 Phone for Work

### Question:

> **Do you use your phone for work?**

### Answer:

> Yes. I sometimes use my phone to check emails, communicate with others, join meetings, and manage work-related information.

---

# Part 21 — 🎬 Phone for Entertainment

### Question:

> **Do you use your phone for entertainment?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I sometimes use my phone for entertainment. I watch videos, listen to music, browse social media, and occasionally play games.

---

# Part 22 — 🔋 Low Battery

### Question:

> **What do you do when your phone battery is low?**

### Answer:

> If my battery is low, I usually reduce my phone usage and charge it as soon as possible. If I don't have access to a charger, I turn on battery-saving mode.

### Natural expressions:

> **My battery is running low.**

> **I need to charge my phone.**

> **My phone is about to die.**

⚠️ এখানে **die** মানে ফোন নষ্ট হয়ে যাওয়া নয়; battery শেষ হয়ে যাওয়া।

---

# Part 23 — 🔔 Notifications

### Question:

> **How often do you check your phone?**

### Answer:

> I probably check my phone several times a day. I usually check messages and notifications, but I'm trying not to check it unnecessarily because it can distract me.

---

# Part 24 — 📵 Could You Live Without Your Phone?

### Question:

> **Could you live without your phone?**

### Answer:

> It would be difficult, but I think I could manage for a short period. Smartphones are useful for communication, information, navigation, learning, and many other things. However, I don't think people should become completely dependent on them.

---

# Part 25 — 👍 Advantages of Smartphones

### Question:

> **What are the advantages of smartphones?**

### Answer:

> Smartphones make communication fast and convenient. We can call people, send messages, access information, study online, use maps, make payments, and use many different services. They can save a lot of time and make everyday tasks easier.

---

# Part 26 — 👎 Disadvantages of Smartphones

### Question:

> **What are the disadvantages of smartphones?**

### Answer:

> One major disadvantage is that smartphones can be addictive. People may spend too much time on social media or watching videos. Excessive phone use can also reduce concentration and affect sleep. Privacy and security can also be concerns.

---

# Part 27 — 🧠 Phone Problem-Solving

### Situation:

Your phone is not working properly.

### Question:

> **What would you do if your phone suddenly stopped working?**

### Answer:

> First, I would restart the phone and check whether the problem is temporary. Then I would check the battery, storage, and software updates. If I couldn't solve the problem myself, I would contact a technician or customer support.

---

# Part 28 — 🗣️ Real-Life Phone Conversation

### Situation: Calling a friend

**You:**

> Hi, are you free right now?

**Friend:**

> Yes, what's up?

**You:**

> I wanted to ask you about tomorrow's plan.

**Friend:**

> Sure. What do you want to know?

**You:**

> What time should we meet?

**Friend:**

> How about 5 p.m.?

**You:**

> That works for me. Where should we meet?

**Friend:**

> Let's meet near the main gate.

**You:**

> Sounds good. See you tomorrow.

**Friend:**

> See you!

---

# Part 29 — 📞 Useful Phone Expressions

### Answering:

> **Hello, who's speaking?**

### Asking:

> **Can I speak to Rahim, please?**

### Someone isn't available:

> **He's not available right now.**

### Ask to call later:

> **Can I call you back later?**

### Bad connection:

> **The connection is breaking up.**

### Can't hear:

> **I can't hear you clearly.**

### Call ended:

> **The call got disconnected.**

### Busy:

> **I'm a little busy right now.**

### Call later:

> **I'll call you back in a few minutes.**

---

# Part 30 — 🧠 Question → Answer → Follow-up

### Main:

> **Do you have a smartphone?**

> Yes, I do.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you mainly use it for?**

> I mainly use it for communication, learning, and entertainment.

### Follow-up:

> **How often do you use it?**

> I use it several times throughout the day.

### Follow-up:

> **Do you prefer calling or texting?**

> I prefer texting for short conversations, but I prefer calling when something is important.

### Follow-up:

> **What apps do you use most?**

> I mostly use communication, learning, and entertainment apps.

### Follow-up:

> **Could you live without your phone?**

> It would be difficult, but I could manage for a short period.

---

# Part 31 — 🔥 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Phone and How I Use It

> I use a smartphone every day, and it has become an important part of my daily life. I mainly use it for communication, learning, browsing the internet, and entertainment.
>
> I usually make phone calls to my family and close friends. For short conversations, I prefer texting because it is quick and convenient. However, when something is important or needs a detailed explanation, I prefer making a phone call.
>
> I also use my phone as a learning tool. I watch educational videos, search for information, read articles, and use different apps to improve my skills. Sometimes I also use it for work-related communication.
>
> Of course, smartphones have some disadvantages. They can be distracting, especially when I receive many notifications. If I spend too much time on my phone, I may lose concentration. So, I try to reduce unnecessary phone usage when I need to study or work.
>
> Overall, I think smartphones are very useful when we use them properly. They can save time and make communication and learning much easier.

---

# Part 32 — 🔥 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Have smartphones made our lives better?**
2. **Are people too dependent on smartphones?**
3. **Should children have smartphones?**
4. **How do smartphones affect communication?**
5. **Can smartphones improve education?**
6. **Why do people spend so much time on their phones?**
7. **Are smartphones making people less social?**
8. **Should people stop using phones before bedtime?**
9. **What would life be like without smartphones?**
10. **Will smartphones become more important in the future?**

---

# Part 33 — 🧠 Opinion Formula

### Question:

> **Are people too dependent on smartphones?**

### Strong Answer:

> Yes, I think many people have become too dependent on smartphones. We use them for communication, entertainment, learning, shopping, navigation, and many other activities. As a result, some people find it difficult to spend even a short amount of time without their phones. However, smartphones themselves are not necessarily bad. The problem is how we use them. I think people should use smartphones as useful tools rather than becoming completely dependent on them.

### Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Contrast → Solution → Conclusion**

---

> **Sounds good.**

> **See you tomorrow.**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 21 Golden Formula

Phone topic-এর answer expand করবে:

> **Device → Usage → Communication → Apps → Benefit → Problem → Solution → Opinion**

### Example:

> I use a smartphone every day. I mainly use it for communication, learning, and entertainment. I usually text my friends and call my family. I also use several apps for studying and browsing the internet. Smartphones are very convenient, but they can also be distracting. That's why I try to control my screen time when I'm studying or working.

---

আজকের শেষে बिना notes দেখে **3–5 মিনিট** বলতে পারবে:

> **“Tell me about your phone, how you use it, who you communicate with, what you use it for, and the advantages and disadvantages of smartphones.”**

**Phone describe → Daily usage → Call → Text → Apps → Study → Entertainment → Advantages → Disadvantages → Opinion**

# 1. 🎯 Lesson Goal

Lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* নিজের phone সম্পর্কে বলতে
* phone কী কাজে ব্যবহার করো বলতে
* call ও message নিয়ে কথা বলতে
* phone-এর সুবিধা ও অসুবিধা বলতে

* follow-up question-এর উত্তর দিতে

# 3. 🟢 Beginner Questions

প্রথমে খুব সহজ প্রশ্ন।

### Q1. Do you have a phone?

**Answer:**

> Yes, I have a phone.

### Q2. Do you have a smartphone?

> Yes, I have a smartphone.

### Q3. What phone do you use?

> I use an Android phone.

### Q4. Do you like your phone?

> Yes, I like my phone.

### Q5. Is your phone new?

> Yes, it is relatively new.

### Q6. Is your phone expensive?

> No, it is not very expensive.

### Q7. Do you use your phone every day?

> Yes, I use it every day.

### Q8. Do you use your phone at night?

> Yes, sometimes I use it at night.

### Q9. Do you use your phone for studying?

> Yes, I use it for studying.

### Q10. Do you use your phone for entertainment?

> Yes, I use it for entertainment.

---

# 4. 🟡 Intermediate Questions

এখন question একটু বড় হবে।

### Q11. What do you mainly use your phone for?

> I mainly use my phone for communication, studying, browsing the internet, and entertainment.

### Q12. How often do you use your phone?

> I use my phone several times throughout the day.

### Q13. How many hours do you use your phone every day?

> I use my phone for several hours a day, depending on my schedule.

### Q14. Who do you usually call?

> I usually call my family and close friends.

### Q15. Who do you text most often?

> I usually text my friends and family members.

### Q16. Do you prefer calling or texting?

> I prefer texting for short conversations, but I prefer calling when something is important.

### Q17. What apps do you use most?

> I mostly use communication, learning, and entertainment apps.

### Q18. How often do you check your phone?

> I check my phone several times a day, especially when I receive notifications.

### Q19. What do you do when your battery is low?

> I usually charge my phone or turn on battery-saving mode.

### Q20. Do you use your phone for work?

> Yes, I sometimes use my phone for work-related communication and checking information.

---

# 5. 🔴 Advanced Questions

এখন **reason + example + opinion** দিতে হবে।

### Q21. What are the main advantages of smartphones?

> Smartphones make communication faster and more convenient. We can call people, send messages, access information, study online, use maps, and complete many everyday tasks. They are especially useful because we can carry them almost everywhere.

---

### Q22. What are the disadvantages of smartphones?

> One major disadvantage is that smartphones can be distracting. People may spend too much time watching videos or checking social media. Excessive phone use can reduce concentration and may also affect sleep. Therefore, people should try to control their screen time.

---

### Q23. Do you think people are too dependent on smartphones?

> Yes, I think many people have become too dependent on smartphones. We use them for communication, entertainment, education, navigation, shopping, and many other activities. As a result, some people find it difficult to spend even a short time without their phones. I think smartphones are useful tools, but we shouldn't become completely dependent on them.

---

### Q24. Could you live without your phone?

> I could probably live without my phone for a short period, but it would be difficult. I use my phone for communication, learning, accessing information, and many daily activities. However, I think taking occasional breaks from smartphones can actually be beneficial.

---

### Q25. How has the smartphone changed your daily life?

> Smartphones have made many parts of my daily life easier. I can communicate with people instantly, access educational resources, search for information, and manage different tasks from one device. However, they can also be distracting, so I need to use them responsibly.

---

# 6. 🧠 Question-Making Mastery

এটা তোমার course-এর **সবচেয়ে important অংশগুলোর একটি**।

একটা topic নাও:

> **Phone**

এখন WH words ব্যবহার করে question তৈরি করো।

### WHAT

> What do you use your phone for?

### WHY

> Why do you use your phone every day?

### WHEN

> When do you usually use your phone?

### WHERE

> Where do you usually use your phone?

### WHO

> Who do you usually call?

### HOW

> How often do you use your phone?

### WHICH

> Which app do you use most?

### HOW MANY

> How many hours do you use your phone?

### HOW LONG

> How long have you been using your current phone?

### WOULD

> Would you like to buy a new phone?

### COULD

> Could you live without your phone?

### SHOULD

> Should children have smartphones?

---

# 7. 🔥 Question Expansion

একটি simple question থেকে কীভাবে বড় conversation তৈরি করতে হয় দেখো।

### Main Question:

> **Do you have a smartphone?**

### Follow-up 1:

> What phone do you use?

### Follow-up 2:

> How long have you been using it?

### Follow-up 3:

> What do you mainly use it for?

### Follow-up 4:

> How many hours do you use it every day?

### Follow-up 5:

> Which apps do you use most?

### Follow-up 6:

> Do you use it for studying?

### Follow-up 7:

> Do you use it for entertainment?

### Follow-up 8:

> What do you like about your phone?

### Follow-up 9:

> What don't you like about it?

### Follow-up 10:

> Would you like to buy a new phone?

👉 এভাবেই **একটি question → 10টি question → 3–5 মিনিট speaking** তৈরি হয়।

---

# 8. 📞 Phone Conversation

## Situation: Calling a Friend

**You:**

> Hello! Are you free right now?

**Friend:**

> Yes, what's up?

**You:**

> I wanted to ask you about tomorrow.

**Friend:**

> Sure. What do you want to know?

**You:**

> What time should we meet?

**Friend:**

> How about 5 p.m.?

**You:**

> That works for me.

**Friend:**

> Where should we meet?

**You:**

> Let's meet near the main gate.

**Friend:**

> Sounds good.

**You:**

> Great. See you tomorrow.

---

# 9. 📱 Useful Real-Life Phone English

### ফোন ধরার সময়

> **Hello, who's speaking?**

### কাউকে চাইলে

> **Can I speak to Rahim, please?**

### সে available না

> **He's not available right now.**

### পরে call

> **Can I call you back later?**

### শুনতে পাচ্ছি না

> **I can't hear you clearly.**

### Network problem

> **The connection is breaking up.**

### Call কেটে গেছে

> **The call got disconnected.**

### ব্যস্ত

> **I'm a little busy right now.**

### পরে call করবে

> **I'll call you back later.**

---

# 10. 💬 Situation Practice

### Situation 1 — Friend is calling

Friend:

> Hey, are you busy?

You:

> No, I'm free right now. What's up?

---

### Situation 2 — You can't hear

> Sorry, I can't hear you clearly. Could you speak a little louder?

---

### Situation 3 — You are busy

> I'm a little busy right now. Can I call you back later?

---

### Situation 4 — Asking about a plan

> Are we still meeting tomorrow?

> What time should we meet?

> Where should we meet?

---

# 11. 🧩 Problem-Solving Questions

### Q:

> What would you do if your phone stopped working?

### Answer:

> First, I would restart the phone and check whether the problem is temporary. Then I would check the battery and software. If I couldn't solve the problem myself, I would contact customer support or take it to a technician.

---

### Q:

> What would you do if your phone battery died while you were outside?

> I would try to find a charger or a power bank. If neither was available, I would use another phone if I needed to make an urgent call.

---

# 12. 🧠 Grammar Patterns

## Pattern 1 — Present Simple

> I use my phone every day.

> I call my family regularly.

> I check my messages frequently.

---

## Pattern 2 — Present Continuous

> I am using my phone right now.

> I am talking to my friend.

---

## Pattern 3 — Present Perfect

> I have used smartphones for many years.

> I have been using my current phone for two years.

---

## Pattern 4 — Would

> I would buy a new phone if my current phone stopped working.

---

## Pattern 5 — Could

> I could live without my phone for a few days.

---

# 13. 🔥 Full Speaking Answer

## Topic: **My Phone**

> I use a smartphone every day, and it is an important part of my daily life. I mainly use it for communication, studying, browsing the internet, and entertainment.
>
> I usually call my family and close friends. For short conversations, I prefer texting because it is quick and convenient. However, I prefer calling when something is important or needs a detailed explanation.
>
> I also use my phone as a learning tool. I watch educational videos, search for information, read articles, and use different apps for learning. Sometimes I also use my phone for work-related communication.
>
> There are many advantages to using a smartphone. It makes communication easier and gives us quick access to information. However, smartphones can also be distracting. If we spend too much time on them, they can affect our concentration.
>
> Overall, I think smartphones are very useful, but we should use them responsibly and avoid unnecessary screen time.

---

# 14. 🔥 Advanced Discussion

এখন প্রতিটি question-এর answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

### 1.

**Are smartphones making our lives better?**

### 2.

**Are people becoming too dependent on smartphones?**

### 3.

**Should children have smartphones?**

### 4.

**How do smartphones affect communication?**

### 5.

**Can smartphones improve education?**

### 6.

**Why do people spend so much time on their phones?**

### 7.

**Are smartphones making people less social?**

### 8.

**Should people avoid using phones before sleeping?**

### 9.

**What would life be like without smartphones?**

### 10.

**How will smartphones change in the future?**

---

# 15. 🎯 Opinion Answer Formula

যেকোনো opinion question-এ এই structure ব্যবহার করো:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Opposite Side → Conclusion**

### Example:

**Are smartphones useful?**

> In my opinion, smartphones are extremely useful. They make communication faster and give us easy access to information. For example, we can study online, use maps, and communicate with people from anywhere. However, excessive phone use can be harmful and distracting. Therefore, I think smartphones are useful as long as we use them responsibly.

---

> **“My Phone and How I Use It”**

### ① Your phone

> What do you use it for?

> Who do you call?

> Who do you text?

> How do you entertain yourself with it?

### ⑧ Advantages

> What are the benefits?

### ⑨ Disadvantages

> What are the problems?

> What do you think about smartphones?

### Example:

# 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ বলতে পারবে:

> **What computer you use → Why you use it → What you do with it → Hardware → Software → Study → Work → Problems → Advantages → Disadvantages**

এবং সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ:

> **Computer topic থেকে নিজে question তৈরি করে 3–5 মিনিট conversation চালাতে পারবে।**

# 2. 🟢 Beginner Questions

প্রথমে simple questions।

### Q1. Do you have a computer?

> Yes, I have a computer.

### Q2. Do you use a computer every day?

> Yes, I use a computer every day.

### Q3. Do you have a laptop or desktop?

> I have a laptop.

### Q4. Do you like using computers?

> Yes, I like using computers.

### Q5. Is your computer new?

> No, it is a few years old.

### Q6. Do you use a keyboard?

> Yes, I use a keyboard.

### Q7. Do you use a mouse?

> Yes, I do.

### Q8. Do you use your computer for studying?

> Yes, I use it for studying.

### Q9. Do you use your computer for work?

> Yes, I use it for work.

### Q10. Do you watch videos on your computer?

> Yes, sometimes I watch videos on my computer.

---

# 3. 🟡 Intermediate Questions

এখন একটু বিস্তারিত answer।

### Q11. What kind of computer do you use?

> I use a laptop. I mainly use it for studying, programming, browsing the internet, and entertainment.

### Q12. How often do you use your computer?

> I use my computer almost every day, usually for several hours.

### Q13. What do you mainly use your computer for?

> I mainly use it for studying, programming, working on projects, browsing the internet, and watching videos.

### Q14. Which operating system do you use?

> I mainly use Linux, although I have also used Windows.

### Q15. What software do you use most?

> I frequently use a code editor, web browser, terminal, and other development tools.

### Q16. Do you prefer a laptop or desktop?

> I prefer a laptop because it is portable and convenient.

### Q17. What do you like about your computer?

> I like its performance and portability. It is powerful enough for my daily tasks.

### Q18. What don't you like about your computer?

> Sometimes it becomes slow when I run many applications at the same time.

### Q19. How long do you use your computer every day?

> I usually use it for several hours a day, depending on my workload.

### Q20. What do you do when your computer becomes slow?

> I close unnecessary applications, check the storage, restart the computer, and look for possible software problems.

---

# 4. 🔴 Advanced Questions

এখন answer-এ **reason + example + opinion** যোগ করবে।

### Q21. Why is a computer important in your daily life?

> A computer is important in my daily life because I use it for learning, programming, communication, and professional work. It allows me to work on complex tasks more efficiently than a phone. I can also store files, write documents, develop software, and access a wide range of resources.

---

### Q22. What is the difference between a phone and a computer?

> Both phones and computers are useful digital devices, but they are designed for different purposes. A phone is more portable and convenient for communication and quick tasks. A computer is generally better for complex tasks such as programming, video editing, data analysis, and professional work. Therefore, I think they complement each other rather than completely replacing one another.

---

### Q23. What are the most important parts of a computer?

> Some of the most important parts are the processor, RAM, storage, motherboard, monitor, keyboard, and mouse. The processor handles instructions, RAM provides temporary working memory, and storage keeps data for long-term use. Each component has a different role, and they work together to make the computer function properly.

---

### Q24. What makes a computer good?

> A good computer should have a powerful enough processor, sufficient RAM, fast storage, and good reliability. The right specifications depend on the user's needs. For example, a programmer may need different hardware from someone who mainly uses a computer for browsing and writing documents.

---

### Q25. How has the computer changed your life?

> Computers have made many tasks easier and more efficient for me. I can learn new skills, write documents, develop software, communicate with others, and access information quickly. They have also made it possible to work on projects that would be difficult to complete manually.

---

# 5. 💻 Laptop vs Desktop

### Question:

> **Do you prefer a laptop or a desktop computer?**

### Answer:

> I prefer a laptop because it is portable and convenient. I can use it in different places and easily carry it with me. However, desktops can provide better performance and upgrade options for certain types of work. So, the better choice depends on the user's needs.

### Useful comparison:

> **Laptop → Portable → Convenient**

> **Desktop → Powerful → Upgradeable**

---

# 6. 🧠 Hardware vs Software

### Question:

> **What is the difference between hardware and software?**

### Answer:

> Hardware refers to the physical parts of a computer that we can touch, such as the keyboard, mouse, monitor, RAM, and processor. Software refers to the programs and instructions that run on the computer. For example, Windows, Linux, web browsers, and code editors are software.

### Easy formula:

> **Hardware = Physical**

> **Software = Programs**

---

# 7. ⚙️ Computer Parts Speaking Practice

### Question:

> What does a keyboard do?

> A keyboard is used to enter text, numbers, and commands into a computer.

### Question:

> What does a mouse do?

> A mouse is used to move the pointer and interact with items on the screen.

### Question:

> What does a monitor do?

> A monitor displays information, images, videos, and other visual content.

### Question:

> What does RAM do?

> RAM temporarily stores data that the computer is actively using.

### Question:

> What does storage do?

> Storage keeps files, applications, and other data for long-term use.

### Question:

> What does a processor do?

> The processor executes instructions and performs calculations needed by the computer.

---

# 8. 📚 Computer for Studying

### Question:

> **Do you use your computer for studying?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I use my computer regularly for studying. I watch educational videos, read documents, search for information, practice programming, and take notes. A computer allows me to access many learning resources in one place.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you usually study on your computer?**

> I usually study programming, English, technical subjects, and other topics related to my interests.

---

# 9. 💼 Computer for Work

### Question:

> **Do you use your computer for work?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I use my computer for various work-related tasks. I write code, work on projects, communicate with others, manage files, and search for technical information.

---

# 10. 👨‍💻 Computer for Programming

### Question:

> **Why do you need a computer for programming?**

### Answer:

> A computer is essential for programming because programming requires development tools, code editors, terminals, databases, and testing environments. A phone can be useful for learning basic concepts, but a computer is much more practical for building and testing real applications.

---

# 11. 📁 Files & Folders

### Question:

> **How do you organize your files?**

### Answer:

> I organize my files into different folders based on their purpose. For example, I keep study materials, projects, documents, and personal files in separate folders. I also try to use clear file names so that I can find things easily.

### Useful phrases:

> **Create a folder**

> **Rename a file**

> **Move a file**

> **Copy a file**

> **Delete a file**

> **Back up a file**

---

# 12. 🌐 Computer & Internet

### Question:

> **What do you use your computer and the internet for?**

### Answer:

> I use my computer and the internet for studying, programming, communication, research, and entertainment. I search for information, watch educational videos, download resources, communicate with others, and work on online projects.

👉 এই answer naturally **Lesson 23 — Internet**-এর দিকে নিয়ে যাবে।

---

# 13. 🛠️ Computer Problems

### Question:

> **What do you do when your computer becomes slow?**

### Answer:

> First, I close unnecessary applications and check whether too many programs are running. Then I check the available storage and restart the computer if necessary. If the problem continues, I try to identify whether it is caused by software or hardware.

---

### Question:

> **What would you do if your computer stopped working?**

> First, I would check the power connection and restart the computer. If that didn't work, I would try to identify the problem by checking the hardware and software. If I couldn't solve it myself, I would ask a technician for help.

---

# 14. 🧩 Real-Life Computer Conversation

### Situation: Asking a friend for help

**You:**

> Hey, can you help me with my computer?

**Friend:**

> Sure. What's wrong?

**You:**

> It's running very slowly.

**Friend:**

> Did you restart it?

**You:**

> Yes, but the problem is still there.

**Friend:**

> How many programs are running?

**You:**

> Quite a few. Maybe that's the problem.

**Friend:**

> Try closing the unnecessary ones.

**You:**

> Okay, I'll try that. Thanks.

**Friend:**

> No problem.

---

# 15. 🗣️ Useful Computer English

### Problem বলতে:

> **My computer is running slowly.**

> **My computer has stopped responding.**

> **The program has crashed.**

> **The screen is frozen.**

> **I can't open the file.**

> **The application isn't working.**

### Help চাইতে:

> **Could you help me fix this?**

> **Could you take a look at my computer?**

> **Do you know how to fix this problem?**

### Solution বলতে:

> **Try restarting the computer.**

> **Check the settings.**

> **Update the software.**

> **Close the unnecessary programs.**

---

# 16. 🧠 Question-Making Mastery

এখন **Computer** topic থেকে question তৈরি করি।

### WHAT

> What do you use your computer for?

### WHY

> Why do you use a computer?

### WHEN

> When do you usually use your computer?

### WHERE

> Where do you usually use your computer?

### WHO

> Who taught you how to use a computer?

### HOW

> How often do you use your computer?

### WHICH

> Which operating system do you use?

### HOW MANY

> How many hours do you use your computer every day?

### HOW LONG

> How long have you been using computers?

### WOULD

> Would you like to buy a new computer?

### COULD

> Could you work without a computer?

### SHOULD

> Should children learn computer skills?

---

একটি question থেকে conversation তৈরি করো।

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

⬇️

> Do you use it for work?

⬇️

⬇️

# 18. 🔥 Phone vs Computer

### Question:

> **Which do you use more, your phone or your computer?**

### Answer:

> I use both, but I use my phone more for quick communication and everyday tasks. I use my computer more for serious work, programming, studying, and larger projects. The phone is more convenient for quick tasks, while the computer is better for complex work.

### Compare:

| Phone               | Computer           |
| ------------------- | ------------------ |
| Portable            | Less portable      |
| Quick communication | Complex work       |
| Small screen        | Large screen       |
| Easy to carry       | More powerful      |
| Quick tasks         | Professional tasks |

---

# 19. 🔴 Advanced Discussion

প্রতিটি question-এর answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

1. **Are computers still important in the age of smartphones?**
2. **Can smartphones replace computers?**
3. **Should every student learn computer skills?**
4. **How have computers changed education?**
5. **How have computers changed jobs?**
6. **What are the disadvantages of using computers too much?**
7. **Will computers become more powerful in the future?**
8. **Should children spend more time learning computers?**
9. **How important are computer skills for getting a job?**
10. **Could modern society function without computers?**

---

# 20. 🧠 Opinion Answer Formula

### Question:

> **Can smartphones replace computers?**

### Strong Answer:

> In my opinion, smartphones cannot completely replace computers. Smartphones are excellent for communication, browsing, and quick everyday tasks. However, computers are much better for complex activities such as programming, video editing, data analysis, and professional work. For example, writing and testing a large software project would be very difficult on a smartphone. Therefore, I think smartphones and computers will continue to serve different purposes.

### Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Comparison → Conclusion**

---

# 21. 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: My Computer

> I use a computer regularly for studying, programming, working on projects, browsing the internet, and entertainment. I prefer using a computer for serious tasks because it has a larger screen and is more comfortable for long periods of work.
>
> I use different software depending on what I am doing. For programming, I use a code editor, terminal, browser, and other development tools. I also use my computer to organize files and access online learning resources.
>
> One of the biggest advantages of a computer is that it allows me to perform complex tasks efficiently. I can write code, manage large files, create documents, and work on different projects.
>
> However, computers can sometimes become slow or have software problems. When that happens, I usually check the running applications, storage, and system settings before asking for help.
>
> Overall, computers are very important in my daily life because they help me learn, work, communicate, and develop new skills.

---

> **However**

> **In addition**

> **Therefore**

> **In my opinion**

> **Overall**

# ⭐ Lesson 22 Golden Formula

Computer topic-এর যেকোনো answer expand করবে:

> **Computer → Type → Usage → Study → Work → Software → Problem → Solution → Advantage → Opinion**

### Example:

> I use a laptop every day. I mainly use it for studying, programming, and work. I use different software depending on my tasks. Sometimes my computer becomes slow when I run many programs at once. In that situation, I close unnecessary applications and check the system. Overall, I think computers are extremely useful for learning and professional work.

# 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ বলতে পারবে:

> **What the Internet is → How you use it → What you search for → Websites → Online learning → Communication → Entertainment → Online services → Benefits → Problems → Internet safety**

# 1. 📚 Important Vocabulary

| English        | বাংলা              |
| -------------- | ------------------ |
| Internet       | ইন্টারনেট          |
| Website        | ওয়েবসাইট          |
| Web browser    | ওয়েব ব্রাউজার     |
| Search engine  | সার্চ ইঞ্জিন       |
| Search         | অনুসন্ধান করা      |
| Online         | অনলাইন             |
| Offline        | অফলাইন             |
| Connection     | সংযোগ              |
| Network        | নেটওয়ার্ক         |
| Wi-Fi          | ওয়াই-ফাই          |
| Mobile data    | মোবাইল ডাটা        |
| Download       | ডাউনলোড            |
| Upload         | আপলোড              |
| Browse         | ব্রাউজ করা         |
| Search         | খোঁজা              |
| Information    | তথ্য               |
| Account        | অ্যাকাউন্ট         |
| Username       | ব্যবহারকারীর নাম   |
| Password       | পাসওয়ার্ড         |
| Link           | লিংক               |
| Website        | ওয়েবসাইট          |
| Email          | ই-মেইল             |
| Online class   | অনলাইন ক্লাস       |
| Online meeting | অনলাইন মিটিং       |
| Streaming      | অনলাইনে দেখা/শোনা  |
| Privacy        | গোপনীয়তা          |

| Scam           | প্রতারণা           |

---

# 2. 🟢 Beginner Questions

প্রথমে খুব সহজ question।

### Q1. Do you use the Internet?

> Yes, I use the Internet every day.

### Q2. Do you use the Internet on your phone?

> Yes, I do.

### Q3. Do you use the Internet on your computer?

> Yes, I do.

### Q4. Do you have an Internet connection at home?

> Yes, I have an Internet connection at home.

### Q5. Do you use Wi-Fi?

> Yes, I usually use Wi-Fi.

### Q6. Do you use mobile data?

> Yes, sometimes I use mobile data.

### Q7. Do you browse the Internet every day?

> Yes, I browse the Internet every day.

### Q8. Do you search for information online?

> Yes, I often search for information online.

### Q9. Do you watch videos online?

> Yes, I watch videos online.

### Q10. Do you study online?

> Yes, I sometimes study online.

---

# 3. 🟡 Intermediate Questions

এখন answer একটু বিস্তারিত হবে।

### Q11. How often do you use the Internet?

> I use the Internet several times throughout the day. I use it for studying, communication, work, and entertainment.

---

### Q12. What do you mainly use the Internet for?

> I mainly use the Internet for learning, searching for information, communicating with others, and entertainment.

---

### Q13. What do you usually search for online?

> I usually search for educational materials, programming tutorials, news, technical information, and answers to questions I don't know.

---

### Q14. Which websites do you visit most often?

> I frequently visit educational websites, search engines, video platforms, documentation sites, and other websites related to my interests.

---

### Q15. Do you prefer using Wi-Fi or mobile data?

> I prefer Wi-Fi when I am at home because it is usually more convenient. I use mobile data when I am outside.

---

### Q16. How much time do you spend online every day?

> I spend several hours online every day, mainly because I use the Internet for studying and work.

---

### Q17. Do you use the Internet for communication?

> Yes. I use it for email, messaging, video calls, and communicating with friends, family, and colleagues.

---

### Q18. Do you use the Internet for entertainment?

> Yes. I watch videos, listen to music, read articles, and sometimes browse entertainment websites.

---

### Q19. Do you buy things online?

> Yes, sometimes I buy things online because it is convenient and allows me to compare different products.

---

### Q20. What would you do if the Internet stopped working?

> First, I would check the Wi-Fi connection and restart the router. If the problem continued, I would contact my Internet service provider.

---

# 4. 🔴 Advanced Questions

এখন answer হবে:

> **Opinion + Reason + Example + Conclusion**

---

### Q21. Why is the Internet important in modern life?

> The Internet is extremely important in modern life because it provides quick access to information and communication. People use it for education, work, business, entertainment, banking, shopping, and many other activities. It has made many tasks faster and more convenient. However, people also need to use it responsibly because there are risks such as scams, misinformation, and privacy problems.

---

### Q22. How has the Internet changed education?

> The Internet has changed education significantly. Students can now access online courses, tutorials, digital books, research papers, and educational videos from almost anywhere. It also allows students to communicate with teachers and classmates online. However, students need to evaluate online information carefully because not everything available on the Internet is accurate.

---

### Q23. What are the advantages of the Internet?

> One major advantage of the Internet is easy access to information. It also makes communication faster and provides opportunities for online education, remote work, online shopping, and digital services. People can learn new skills without necessarily attending a physical institution. Overall, the Internet has made many aspects of life more convenient.

---

### Q24. What are the disadvantages of the Internet?

> The Internet has several disadvantages. People can become addicted to online content and waste a lot of time. There are also risks related to scams, misinformation, cybercrime, and privacy. In addition, spending too much time online can reduce face-to-face communication. Therefore, people need to use the Internet carefully.

---

### Q25. Can we live without the Internet?

> Technically, we can live without the Internet, but modern life would become much more difficult. Many educational, professional, financial, and communication services now depend on Internet access. Businesses and organizations also rely heavily on online systems. So, while living without the Internet is possible, it would be extremely inconvenient for most people today.

---

# 5. 🔎 Searching for Information

এটি খুব important speaking topic।

### Question:

> **How do you search for information on the Internet?**

### Answer:

> When I need information, I usually use a search engine. First, I think about the main keywords related to my question. Then I search for those keywords and compare information from several reliable sources. If the topic is important, I don't depend on just one website.

### Useful expressions:

> **I search for...**

> **I look for information about...**

> **I compare different sources.**

> **I check whether the source is reliable.**

> **I don't rely on a single source.**

---

# 6. 🎓 Internet for Learning

### Question:

> **How do you use the Internet for learning?**

### Answer:

> I use the Internet regularly for learning. I watch educational videos, read articles, search for documentation, take online courses, and practice different skills. When I don't understand a topic, I search for explanations from different sources.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you usually learn online?**

> I usually learn programming, English, technical subjects, and other topics that are useful for my career.

---

# 7. 💼 Internet for Work

### Question:

> **How does the Internet help you with work?**

### Answer:

> The Internet helps me communicate with others, find information, access online tools, manage projects, and share files. It also allows people to work remotely and collaborate even when they are in different locations.

---

# 8. 📧 Internet for Communication

### Question:

> **How do you communicate with people online?**

### Answer:

> I communicate with people through email, messaging apps, video calls, and other online platforms. The Internet makes communication fast because I can contact someone almost instantly, even if they are far away.

---

# 9. 🎬 Internet for Entertainment

### Question:

> **How do you use the Internet for entertainment?**

### Answer:

> I use the Internet to watch videos, listen to music, read interesting articles, and sometimes watch movies or other online content. I try to balance entertainment with more productive activities.

---

# 10. 🛒 Online Shopping

### Question:

> **Do you prefer online shopping or traditional shopping?**

### Answer:

> I think both have advantages. Online shopping is convenient because I can compare prices and products from home. Traditional shopping allows me to see and check a product physically before buying it. So, my choice depends on the type of product.

---

# 11. 🔐 Internet Safety

### Question:

> **How can people stay safe on the Internet?**

### Answer:

> People should use strong and unique passwords and avoid sharing sensitive information with strangers. They should also be careful when clicking unknown links or downloading files from unreliable sources. It's important to check whether a website is trustworthy before entering personal or financial information.

### Useful phrases:

> **Use a strong password.**

> **Don't share sensitive information.**

> **Avoid suspicious links.**

> **Check the website carefully.**

> **Don't download unknown files.**

> **Enable two-factor authentication.**

---

# 12. ⚠️ Scam & Fake Information

### Question:

> **How can you identify suspicious information online?**

### Answer:

> I look at the source first. I check whether the website or person providing the information is trustworthy. I also compare the information with other reliable sources. If something seems too good to be true, I become more careful before believing or sharing it.

### Useful phrase:

> **If something seems too good to be true, be careful.**

---

# 13. 🌐 Internet Problems

### Situation:

Your Internet is not working.

### Question:

> **What would you do if your Internet connection stopped working?**

### Answer:

> First, I would check whether the problem is with my device or the network. Then I would restart the router and check the cables or Wi-Fi connection. If other devices also had the same problem, I would contact my Internet service provider.

---

# 14. 🗣️ Real-Life Conversation

## Situation: Internet Problem

**You:**

> Excuse me, is the Internet working?

**Friend:**

> No, I think there's a problem with the connection.

**You:**

> I can't access any websites either.

**Friend:**

> Did you restart the router?

**You:**

> Not yet. I'll restart it now.

**Friend:**

> Okay. Let's see if that fixes the problem.

**You:**

> It seems to be working now.

**Friend:**

> Great!

**You:**

> Thanks for your help.

---

# 15. 💬 Useful Internet English

### Internet slow:

> **The Internet is very slow.**

### No connection:

> **I don't have an Internet connection.**

### Website doesn't open:

> **The website isn't loading.**

### Connection lost:

> **The connection has dropped.**

### Search:

> **I'll search for it online.**

### Information:

> **I'll look it up.**

### Download:

> **I'm downloading the file.**

### Upload:

> **I'm uploading the document.**

### Link:

> **Can you send me the link?**

### Internet problem:

> **I think there's a problem with the network.**

> **How do you access the Internet?**

### Answer:

**How has the Internet changed education?**

### 5.

**How has the Internet changed the workplace?**

### 6.

**Does the Internet make people more connected?**

### 7.

**What are the biggest dangers of the Internet?**

### 8.

**Should children have unlimited Internet access?**

### 9.

**How can we identify fake information online?**

### 10.

**What will the Internet look like in the future?**

---

# 20. 🧠 Opinion Answer Formula

### Question:

> **Does the Internet make life better?**

### Strong Answer:

> In my opinion, the Internet has made life much easier in many ways. It gives us quick access to information, education, communication, entertainment, and online services. For example, people can study from home and communicate with someone on the other side of the world. However, the Internet also creates problems such as misinformation, scams, and excessive screen time. Therefore, I think the Internet is extremely useful when it is used responsibly.

### Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Opposite Side → Conclusion**

---

# 21. 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: The Internet in My Daily Life

> I use the Internet every day for many different purposes. I mainly use it for studying, programming, communication, research, and entertainment.
>
> When I need information, I usually search online. I use different websites and compare information from reliable sources. The Internet is especially useful for learning because I can access educational videos, articles, courses, and documentation from almost anywhere.
>
> I also use the Internet for communication. I can send messages, emails, and make video calls. It helps me stay connected with friends, family, and other people.
>
> However, the Internet also has some disadvantages. People can waste a lot of time online, and there are risks such as scams, misinformation, and privacy problems. That's why I think we should use the Internet carefully.
>
> Overall, the Internet is one of the most useful technologies in modern life. It makes learning, communication, and many everyday activities much easier.

---

> **“How I Use the Internet in My Daily Life”**

# ⭐ Lesson 23 Golden Formula

Internet topic-এর যেকোনো answer expand করবে:

> **Internet → Access → Usage → Search → Study → Work → Communication → Entertainment → Benefits → Problems → Safety → Opinion**

### Example:

> I use the Internet every day through my phone and computer. I mainly use it for studying, work, communication, and entertainment. I often search for educational and technical information. The Internet makes learning and communication much easier. However, it can also be distracting and unsafe if we don't use it carefully. Therefore, I try to use reliable sources and avoid suspicious websites.

# 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ বলতে পারবে:

> **Which social media you use → Why you use it → How often → What you post → Who you follow → Messaging → Comments → Benefits → Problems → Privacy → Your opinion**

সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ:

> **Social Media topic থেকে নিজে question তৈরি করে 3–5 মিনিট conversation চালাতে পারবে।**

---

# 1. 📚 Important Vocabulary

| English               | বাংলা                         |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Social media          | সামাজিক যোগাযোগমাধ্যম         |
| Social media platform | সামাজিক যোগাযোগের প্ল্যাটফর্ম |
| Account               | অ্যাকাউন্ট                    |
| Profile               | প্রোফাইল                      |
| Username              | ব্যবহারকারীর নাম              |
| Post                  | পোস্ট                         |
| Upload                | আপলোড করা                     |
| Share                 | শেয়ার করা                    |
| Like                  | পছন্দ করা                     |
| Comment               | মন্তব্য                       |
| Reply                 | উত্তর দেওয়া                  |
| Follow                | অনুসরণ করা                    |
| Unfollow              | অনুসরণ বন্ধ করা               |
| Follower              | অনুসারী                       |
| Following             | যাদের অনুসরণ করি              |
| Friend request        | বন্ধুত্বের অনুরোধ             |
| Message               | বার্তা                        |
| Direct message        | ব্যক্তিগত বার্তা              |
| Story                 | স্টোরি                        |
| Video                 | ভিডিও                         |
| Photo                 | ছবি                           |
| Content               | কনটেন্ট                       |
| Creator               | কনটেন্ট নির্মাতা              |
| Notification          | বিজ্ঞপ্তি                     |
| Trending              | জনপ্রিয়/আলোচিত               |
| Viral                 | দ্রুত জনপ্রিয় হওয়া          |
| Privacy               | গোপনীয়তা                     |
| Privacy settings      | গোপনীয়তা সেটিংস              |
| Fake account          | ভুয়া অ্যাকাউন্ট              |
| Cyberbullying         | অনলাইনে হয়রানি               |
| Addiction             | আসক্তি                        |
| Block                 | ব্লক করা                      |
| Report                | রিপোর্ট করা                   |

---

# 2. 🟢 Beginner Questions

প্রথমে simple questions।

### Q1. Do you use social media?

> Yes, I use social media.

### Q2. Which social media platforms do you use?

> I use several social media platforms.

### Q3. Do you have a social media account?

> Yes, I do.

### Q4. Do you use social media every day?

> Yes, I use it every day.

### Q5. Do you like social media?

> Yes, I like it.

### Q6. Do you post photos?

> Yes, sometimes I post photos.

### Q7. Do you watch videos on social media?

> Yes, I often watch videos.

### Q8. Do you read comments?

> Yes, I sometimes read comments.

### Q9. Do you send messages on social media?

> Yes, I send messages to my friends.

### Q10. Do you follow other people?

> Yes, I follow many people.

---

# 3. 🟡 Intermediate Questions

এখন একটু বিস্তারিত answer।

### Q11. Which social media platform do you use most?

> I use one or two platforms more than others, mainly for communication, entertainment, and learning.

### Q12. How often do you use social media?

> I use social media every day, usually for a limited amount of time.

### Q13. What do you usually do on social media?

> I usually watch videos, read posts, communicate with friends, follow interesting pages, and sometimes share useful content.

### Q14. What kind of content do you like?

> I like educational, technology-related, entertaining, and informative content.

### Q15. Who do you usually follow?

> I usually follow friends, educational pages, content creators, and people who share useful information.

### Q16. Do you post regularly?

> No, I don't post regularly. I usually post when I have something interesting or useful to share.

### Q17. Do you comment on posts?

> Sometimes I comment when I have something meaningful to say.

### Q18. Do you send direct messages?

> Yes, I use direct messages to communicate privately with friends and other people.

### Q19. How much time do you spend on social media?

> I usually spend a limited amount of time on social media, although it can increase when I have free time.

### Q20. Do you check social media before sleeping?

> Sometimes I do, but I try to avoid using social media too much before sleeping.

---

# 4. 🔴 Advanced Questions

এখন answer হবে:

> **Opinion + Reason + Example + Conclusion**

---

### Q21. Why do people use social media?

> People use social media for many reasons. They use it to communicate with friends and family, follow news, share experiences, learn new things, promote businesses, and entertain themselves. Social media also allows people to connect with communities that share similar interests. However, people should use it carefully because excessive use can become distracting.

---

### Q22. What are the advantages of social media?

> Social media makes communication fast and convenient. It allows people to stay connected with friends and family, discover useful information, promote businesses, and share creative content. It can also provide educational opportunities and help people build professional networks. Therefore, social media can be very useful when used properly.

---

### Q23. What are the disadvantages of social media?

> One major disadvantage is that social media can be addictive. People may spend too much time scrolling through content instead of focusing on important tasks. It can also spread misinformation, create privacy problems, and negatively affect people's mental well-being. That's why people need to control their usage.

---

### Q24. Is social media good for students?

> Social media can be useful for students if they use it for educational purposes. Students can follow educational pages, watch tutorials, join learning communities, and communicate with classmates. However, social media can also be a major distraction. So, students should balance educational use with responsible screen time.

---

### Q25. Is social media making people less social?

> In some ways, yes. Social media allows people to communicate easily, but online communication cannot always replace face-to-face interaction. Some people spend so much time online that they have less time for real-world relationships. However, social media can also help people maintain relationships when they live far apart. So, the effect depends on how people use it.

---

# 5. 📱 Your Social Media Usage

### Question:

> **What do you usually do on social media?**

### Answer:

> I usually watch videos, read posts, communicate with friends, and follow pages related to my interests. Sometimes I also share useful information with other people.

### Follow-up:

> **What kind of content do you watch?**

> I mainly watch educational, technology-related, informative, and entertaining content.

---

# 6. 👥 Followers & Following

### Question:

> **Who do you usually follow on social media?**

### Answer:

> I usually follow friends, educational pages, technology-related accounts, content creators, and people who share useful information.

### Follow-up:

> **Why do you follow them?**

> I follow them because I find their content useful, interesting, or informative.

### Useful pattern:

> **I follow ___ because ___.**

Examples:

> I follow educational pages because they help me learn.

> I follow technology channels because I'm interested in technology.

---

# 7. 📝 Posting

### Question:

> **Do you post regularly on social media?**

### Answer:

> No, I don't post regularly. I usually post when I have something interesting, useful, or important to share.

### Follow-up:

> **What do you usually post?**

> I sometimes share photos, useful information, or updates about things I'm interested in.

---

# 8. ❤️ Likes & Comments

### Question:

> **Do you usually like and comment on posts?**

### Answer:

> I sometimes like posts that I find interesting or useful. I don't comment on everything, but I usually comment when I have something meaningful to add.

### Useful expressions:

> **I really like this post.**

> **That's interesting.**

> **I agree with you.**

> **That's a good point.**

> **Thanks for sharing.**

> **I have a different opinion.**

---

# 9. 💬 Messaging

### Question:

> **How do you communicate with your friends on social media?**

### Answer:

> I usually communicate with my friends through direct messages. We send text messages, photos, videos, and sometimes voice messages.

### Follow-up:

> **Do you prefer messaging or calling?**

> I prefer messaging for short conversations, but I prefer calling when something needs a quick or detailed discussion.

---

# 10. 🎥 Video Content

### Question:

> **Do you watch videos on social media?**

### Answer:

> Yes, I watch videos on social media. I usually watch educational videos, technology content, news, and entertainment. I try to avoid spending too much time watching short videos because they can be very addictive.

---

# 11. 🔥 Social Media Addiction

### Question:

> **Why do people spend so much time on social media?**

### Answer:

> I think social media is designed to keep people engaged. There is always new content to watch, and notifications encourage people to check their accounts repeatedly. Short videos and personalized recommendations can also make people continue scrolling. As a result, people may spend much more time online than they originally planned.

### Useful expression:

> **I spend more time than I planned.**

---

# 12. 🔐 Privacy

### Question:

> **Why is privacy important on social media?**

### Answer:

> Privacy is important because people share a lot of personal information online. If that information is not protected properly, it could be misused. People should be careful about what they post and who can see their information. They should also use strong passwords and appropriate privacy settings.

---

# 13. 🚨 Fake Accounts

### Question:

> **What would you do if you found a fake account using your name?**

### Answer:

> First, I would report the account to the social media platform. I would also inform my friends so they don't believe any false information posted by that account. If necessary, I would change my passwords and improve my account security.

---

# 14. 🚫 Blocking & Reporting

### Question:

> **When should you block or report someone on social media?**

### Answer:

> I would block or report someone if they repeatedly harass me, send inappropriate messages, spread harmful content, or try to scam me. I think people should not tolerate serious online harassment or threats.

### Useful phrases:

> **I'll block the account.**

> **I'll report the post.**

> **This account looks suspicious.**

> **I don't want to interact with them.**

---

# 15. 🧠 Question-Making Mastery

এখন **Social Media** topic থেকে question তৈরি করি।

### WHAT

> What do you usually do on social media?

### WHY

> Why do you use social media?

### WHEN

> When do you usually check social media?

### WHERE

> Where do you usually use social media?

### WHO

> Who do you follow on social media?

### HOW

> How often do you use social media?

### WHICH

> Which social media platform do you use most?

### HOW MANY

> How many hours do you spend on social media?

### HOW LONG

> How long have you been using social media?

### WOULD

> Would you stop using social media for a month?

### COULD

> Could you live without social media?

### SHOULD

> Should children use social media?

---

# 17. 🔗 Internet → Social Media Connection

এটা previous lesson-এর সাথে connect করবে।

### Question:

> **How is social media related to the Internet?**

### Answer:

> Social media is one of the many services that depend on the Internet. We use an Internet connection to access social media platforms, send messages, upload content, and communicate with other people. Without the Internet, most modern social media platforms would not work.

---

# 18. 📱 Phone → Internet → Social Media

### Question:

> **How do you access social media?**

### Answer:

> I usually access social media through my smartphone and computer. I use Wi-Fi when I'm at home and mobile data when I'm outside. I mainly use social media for communication, learning, and entertainment.

🔥 এখানে **Lesson 21 + 22 + 23 + 24** একসাথে practice হচ্ছে।

---

# 19. 🔴 Advanced Discussion Questions

প্রতিটি answer **5–8 sentences** বলবে।

### 1.

**Has social media made communication better?**

### 2.

**Is social media good or bad for society?**

### 3.

**Should children be allowed to use social media?**

### 4.

**Why is social media addictive?**

### 5.

**Does social media waste people's time?**

### 6.

**Can social media help students learn?**

### 7.

**How does social media affect relationships?**

### 8.

**Is online friendship as valuable as real-life friendship?**

### 9.

**How can people protect their privacy on social media?**

### 10.

**Will social media become more important in the future?**

---

# 20. 🧠 Opinion Answer Formula

### Question:

> **Is social media good or bad?**

### Strong Answer:

> In my opinion, social media can be both useful and harmful. It allows people to communicate, learn new things, share information, and build communities. For example, students can follow educational pages and learn from useful content. However, excessive use can waste time and cause problems such as addiction, misinformation, and privacy concerns. Therefore, I think social media is beneficial when people use it responsibly and maintain a healthy balance.

### Formula:

> **Opinion → Reason → Example → Opposite Side → Solution → Conclusion**

---

# 21. 🎤 Full Speaking Example

## Topic: Social Media in My Daily Life

> I use social media regularly, mainly for communication, learning, and entertainment. I usually check social media to see new posts, watch videos, communicate with friends, and follow pages related to my interests.
>
> I don't post very frequently. I usually post when I have something useful or interesting to share. I also sometimes like and comment on other people's posts.
>
> Social media has several advantages. It helps people communicate quickly and allows them to discover useful information and educational content. It can also be useful for businesses and professional networking.
>
> However, social media has some disadvantages as well. People can spend too much time scrolling through content, and some online information may be false. Privacy can also become a problem if people share too much personal information.
>
> Overall, I think social media is a useful tool, but we should control our usage and be careful about the information we share online.

---

# 🎯 Lesson Goal

এই lesson শেষে তুমি পারবে:

* Internet নিয়ে basic প্রশ্নের উত্তর দিতে
