# **Lesson 30 — Revision 🔄**

---

# 🎯 Today's Main Goal

আজ তুমি English-এ শুধু—

> **I like...**
> **I don't like...**

বলবে না।

বরং বলতে পারবে:

> **I really like...**
> **I enjoy...**
> **I'm interested in...**
> **I'm fond of...**
> **I prefer...**
> **I'd rather...**
> **I'm not a big fan of...**
> **I don't really like...**
> **I can't stand...**

এবং **কেন পছন্দ/অপছন্দ করো** সেটা explain করতে পারবে।

---

# 1. 📚 Important Vocabulary

| English     | বাংলা              |
| ----------- | ------------------ |
| Like        | পছন্দ করা          |
| Love        | খুব পছন্দ/ভালোবাসা |
| Enjoy       | উপভোগ করা          |
| Prefer      | বেশি পছন্দ করা     |
| Choose      | বেছে নেওয়া        |
| Favorite    | প্রিয়             |
| Interest    | আগ্রহ              |
| Interested  | আগ্রহী             |
| Dislike     | অপছন্দ করা         |
| Hate        | ঘৃণা করা           |
| Avoid       | এড়িয়ে চলা        |
| Fan         | ভক্ত               |
| Preference  | পছন্দ              |
| Choice      | পছন্দ/নির্বাচন     |
| Comfortable | আরামদায়ক          |
| Interesting | আকর্ষণীয়          |
| Boring      | বিরক্তিকর          |
| Exciting    | উত্তেজনাপূর্ণ      |
| Useful      | উপকারী             |
| Difficult   | কঠিন               |
| Easy        | সহজ                |
| Relaxing    | আরামদায়ক          |
| Delicious   | সুস্বাদু           |
| Healthy     | স্বাস্থ্যকর        |
| Unhealthy   | অস্বাস্থ্যকর       |
| Convenient  | সুবিধাজনক          |
| Expensive   | ব্যয়বহুল          |
| Cheap       | সস্তা              |
| Beautiful   | সুন্দর             |
| Annoying    | বিরক্তিকর          |
| Fun         | মজার               |

---

# 2. 🟢 Beginner — Basic Sentences

### Positive ❤️

> I like tea.

> I like cricket.

> I like music.

> I like watching movies.

> I enjoy reading books.

> I love traveling.

### Negative 👎

> I don't like coffee.

> I don't like horror movies.

> I don't like crowded places.

> I don't enjoy cooking.

> I dislike noisy places.

---

# 3. ⭐ Different Ways to Say “I Like”

একই কথা বারবার **I like** দিয়ে বলবে না।

### 1. I like...

> I like cricket.

### 2. I really like...

> I really like cricket.

### 3. I love...

> I love listening to music.

### 4. I enjoy...

> I enjoy watching movies.

### 5. I'm interested in...

> I'm interested in technology.

### 6. I'm fond of...

> I'm fond of classical music.

### 7. I'm a big fan of...

> I'm a big fan of football.

### 8. I prefer...

> I prefer tea to coffee.

### 9. I'd rather...

> I'd rather stay home tonight.

---

# 4. ⭐ Different Ways to Say “I Don't Like”

### 1. I don't like...

> I don't like crowded places.

### 2. I don't really like...

> I don't really like horror movies.

### 3. I'm not a big fan of...

> I'm not a big fan of spicy food.

### 4. I'm not really into...

> I'm not really into gaming.

### 5. I dislike...

> I dislike unnecessary arguments.

### 6. I tend to avoid...

> I tend to avoid junk food.

### 7. I can't stand...

> I can't stand very loud music.

⚠️ **“I can't stand” = খুব strong dislike**

---

# 5. 🟢 Beginner Questions

### Q1. What do you like?

> I like music, movies, and sports.

### Q2. What is your favorite food?

> My favorite food is ________.

### Q3. Do you like tea?

> Yes, I do.

### Q4. Do you like coffee?

> No, I don't.

### Q5. Do you like music?

> Yes, I love music.

### Q6. What kind of music do you like?

> I like pop and romantic music.

### Q7. Do you like sports?

> Yes, I do.

### Q8. What sport do you like?

> I like cricket.

### Q9. Do you like watching movies?

> Yes, I enjoy watching movies.

### Q10. What don't you like?

> I don't like very noisy places.

---

# 6. 🟡 Question + Reason

এখান থেকেই speaking improve হবে।

### Q:

> Do you like coffee?

### ❌ Weak:

> Yes.

### ✅ Better:

> Yes, I like coffee.

### 🔥 Strong:

> Yes, I really like coffee because it helps me feel refreshed in the morning.

---

### Q:

> Do you like sports?

### Strong:

> Yes, I enjoy sports because they help me stay active and reduce stress.

---

### Q:

> Do you like cooking?

### Answer:

> I don't really enjoy cooking because it takes a lot of time. However, I like trying new food.

---

# 7. 🧠 Golden Formula

যেকোনো **Like/Dislike** answer:

> **Preference + Reason + Example**

### Example:

> I really like listening to music because it helps me relax. For example, I usually listen to music when I'm traveling or taking a break.

---

# 8. ❤️ Likes — Food

### Q:

> What food do you like?

### Answer:

> I really like rice, fish, chicken, and vegetables. I especially enjoy homemade food because it is tasty and usually healthier than fast food.

### Follow-up:

> Why do you like it?

> How often do you eat it?

> Can you cook it?

> Who usually prepares it?

---

# 9. 👎 Dislikes — Food

### Q:

> What food don't you like?

### Answer:

> I'm not a big fan of very spicy food. I can eat mildly spicy food, but extremely spicy food is uncomfortable for me.

### Follow-up:

> Why don't you like it?

> Did you dislike it as a child?

> What food do you prefer?

---

# 10. 🎵 Likes — Music

### Q:

> What kind of music do you like?

### Answer:

> I mainly like pop, romantic, and instrumental music. I enjoy music that has a good melody and meaningful lyrics.

### Follow-up:

> Who is your favorite singer?

> When do you listen to music?

> Does music affect your mood?

---

# 11. 🎬 Likes — Movies

### Q:

> What kind of movies do you like?

### Answer:

> I enjoy action, thriller, and science-fiction movies because they are exciting and keep me interested. I especially like movies with strong stories and interesting characters.

### Follow-up:

> What's your favorite movie?

> Who is your favorite actor?

> Do you prefer cinema or home?

---

# 12. 🏏 Likes — Sports

### Q:

> What sport do you like?

### Answer:

> I really enjoy cricket because it is exciting and requires both individual skills and teamwork. I also enjoy watching important matches with friends.

### Follow-up:

> Who is your favorite player?

> Do you play cricket?

> Which team do you support?

---

# 13. 🏃 Likes — Exercise

### Q:

> What type of exercise do you like?

### Answer:

> I prefer walking and simple exercises because they are easy to include in my daily routine. They also help me stay active without requiring expensive equipment.

---

# 14. 🔥 Prefer — Very Important

**Prefer = একটি জিনিস অন্যটির চেয়ে বেশি পছন্দ করা।**

### Structure 1:

> **I prefer A to B.**

Example:

> I prefer tea to coffee.

> I prefer movies to TV shows.

> I prefer walking to running.

---

### Structure 2:

> **I prefer + verb-ing**

> I prefer watching movies at home.

> I prefer listening to music alone.

---

### Structure 3:

> **I prefer to + verb**

> I prefer to stay at home.

> I prefer to study in the morning.

---

# 15. 🔥 Would Rather

**Would rather = এখন/নির্দিষ্ট পরিস্থিতিতে কোনটা করতে বেশি চাই**

### Structure:

> **I'd rather + verb**

Examples:

> I'd rather stay home.

> I'd rather watch a movie.

> I'd rather walk than take a bus.

> I'd rather study than play games.

### Compare:

> I prefer tea to coffee.

**General preference**

vs.

> I'd rather drink tea right now.

**Current choice**

---

# 16. 🟡 Comparison

### Question:

> Do you prefer tea or coffee?

### Weak:

> Tea.

### Better:

> I prefer tea.

### Strong:

> I prefer tea to coffee because I like its taste and I usually find it more relaxing.

---

### Q:

> Do you prefer movies or music?

### Answer:

> I enjoy both, but I prefer movies when I want entertainment and music when I want to relax.

🔥 এই ধরনের answer conversation-এ অনেক natural।

---

# 17. 🔥 Strong Likes & Dislikes

### Weak:

> I like music.

### Stronger:

> I really like music.

### Very Natural:

> I'm a big fan of music.

---

### Weak:

> I don't like noisy places.

### Natural:

> I'm not a big fan of noisy places.

### Strong:

> I can't stand extremely noisy places.

---

# 18. 🧠 Question-Making Mastery

Likes & Dislikes topic থেকে questions:

### WHAT

> What do you like?

### WHY

> Why do you like it?

### WHICH

> Which food do you prefer?

### WHO

> Who do you like spending time with?

### WHEN

> When do you enjoy listening to music?

### WHERE

> Where do you prefer studying?

### HOW

> How do you decide what you like?

### WOULD

> Would you rather stay home or go outside?

### COULD

> Could you live without social media?

### SHOULD

> Should people choose healthy food even if they don't like it?

---

# 19. 🔥 One Question → Many Follow-ups

### Main Question:

> **What do you like doing in your free time?**

Then:

> Why do you enjoy it?

↓

> How often do you do it?

↓

> Where do you usually do it?

↓

> Who do you do it with?

↓

> When did you start doing it?

↓

> What do you like most about it?

↓

> Is there anything you dislike about it?

↓

> Would you recommend it to others?

↓

> What would you rather do if you had more free time?

🔥 **একটা question → 8+ questions**

---

# 20. 🟡 Likes & Dislikes — Daily Life

### Q:

> Do you like waking up early?

### Answer:

> Yes, I generally like waking up early because I feel more productive in the morning. However, it can be difficult when I don't get enough sleep.

---

### Q:

> Do you like studying?

> I enjoy learning new things, but I don't always enjoy studying for long hours. I prefer short, focused study sessions.

---

### Q:

> Do you like traveling?

> Yes, I really enjoy traveling because I can see new places, experience different cultures, and take a break from my normal routine.

---

# 21. 🔴 Advanced Questions

## Q21. Can our likes and dislikes change over time?

> Yes, I think our preferences can change as we grow older. For example, someone may dislike a particular type of food as a child but enjoy it later. Our experiences, environment, friends, and interests can all influence our preferences.

---

## Q22. Should people always follow their preferences?

> Not necessarily. Personal preferences are important, but sometimes we need to make choices based on responsibility or long-term benefits. For example, someone may prefer junk food, but choosing healthier food may be better for their health.

---

## Q23. Is it important to respect other people's preferences?

> Yes, I think it is very important. People have different backgrounds, experiences, and personalities, so naturally they will have different likes and dislikes. We don't have to agree with everyone, but we should respect their choices as long as they don't harm others.

---

## Q24. Can social media influence what people like?

> Yes, social media can strongly influence people's preferences. People are constantly exposed to products, music, movies, fashion, and opinions online. Trends can make certain things popular very quickly. However, people should still think independently instead of following every trend.

---

## Q25. Is popularity a good reason to like something?

> Popularity can be one reason to discover something, but it shouldn't be the only reason to like it. Something can be extremely popular but still not match my personal interests. I think people should try different things and decide what they genuinely enjoy.

---

# 22. 🗣️ Polite Disagreement

Likes & Dislikes-এর ক্ষেত্রে অন্যের opinion-এর সাথে disagree করতে হবে।

### ❌ Too direct:

> You are wrong.

### ✅ Better:

> I understand your point, but I see it differently.

### আরও:

> I respect your opinion, but I prefer something else.

> That's interesting, but I don't completely agree.

> I can understand why you like it, but it's not really my thing.

> We have different tastes.

🔥 এগুলো real conversation-এর জন্য খুব useful।

---

# 23. 🎤 Full Conversation

### A:

> What kind of movies do you like?

### B:

> I really like action and thriller movies.

### A:

> Why do you like them?

### B:

> Because they are exciting and usually keep me interested.

### A:

> Do you prefer watching them at home or in the cinema?

### B:

> I prefer watching them at home because it's more comfortable.

### A:

> What about music? What kind of music do you like?

### B:

> I mainly listen to pop and instrumental music.

### A:

> Do you listen to music while studying?

### B:

> Sometimes, but I prefer instrumental music when I'm studying.

### A:

> What about sports?

### B:

> I enjoy cricket, but I'm not a big fan of football.

### A:

> Why not?

### B:

> I don't dislike football, but I find cricket more interesting.

🔥 এখানে **Movies → Music → Sports → Likes/Dislikes** natural conversation হয়েছে।

---

# 24. 🧠 Full Speaking Structure

যেকোনো preference question-এর জন্য:

> **What → Like/Dislike → Reason → Example → Comparison → Alternative → Opinion**

### Example:

> I really like watching movies because they help me relax. For example, I often watch movies at the weekend. I prefer watching movies at home to going to the cinema because it is more comfortable. However, I enjoy going to the cinema when I want a more exciting experience. Overall, movies are one of my favorite forms of entertainment.

---

# 25. 🎤 Full Speaking Practice

## Topic: **My Likes and Dislikes**

> There are many things that I like and dislike in my daily life. I enjoy listening to music, watching movies, playing sports, and learning new things. I especially like activities that help me relax or improve myself.
>
> When it comes to food, I prefer healthy homemade food, although I sometimes enjoy fast food. I also prefer tea to coffee.
>
> I enjoy watching action and thriller movies because they are exciting. When it comes to music, I mainly listen to songs that have good melodies and meaningful lyrics.
>
> I also enjoy sports, especially cricket, because it is exciting and requires teamwork. However, I'm not a big fan of very noisy places because I find them uncomfortable.
>
> My likes and dislikes are not always fixed. They can change as I gain new experiences. I think it's important to know what we like, but it's equally important to respect other people's preferences.
>
> Overall, I believe our likes and dislikes make our personalities unique.

---

# 28. 🔥 Challenge — Natural Conversation

তোমার সাথে কেউ জিজ্ঞেস করল:

> **What do you like doing in your free time?**

তুমি শুধু answer দিয়ে থেমে যাবে না।

তুমি **নিজে তাকে একটা question back করবে।**

### Example:

> I enjoy watching movies and listening to music in my free time. They help me relax after a busy day. **What about you? What do you usually do in your free time?**

এটাই real conversation-এর খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ skill:

> **Answer → Reason → Example → Question Back**

---

# ⭐ Lesson 29 Golden Formula

### Likes:

> **I like → I really like → I enjoy → I'm interested in → I'm a big fan of**

### Dislikes:

> **I don't like → I don't really like → I'm not a big fan of → I dislike → I can't stand**

### Preference:

> **I prefer A to B.**

### Current Choice:

> **I'd rather A than B.**

### Opinion:

> **I think... because... For example... However... Overall...**

---

# 🟢 Part 2 — Basic Question Revision

প্রথমে short answer practice করবে।

### Personal

> What is your name?

> Where are you from?

> What do you do?

> What are your hobbies?

> What do you usually do in your free time?

---

### Family

> How many people are there in your family?

> Who are you closest to in your family?

> What do you usually do with your family?

---

### Daily Life

> What time do you wake up?

> What do you do in the morning?

> What do you usually do in the evening?

> What do you do at the weekend?

---

### Food

> What is your favorite food?

> What do you usually eat for breakfast?

> Do you like cooking?

> Do you prefer homemade food or fast food?

---

### Study / Work

> What do you study?

> What do you do?

> What do you like about your work or studies?

> What is difficult about your work or studies?

---

### Technology

> How often do you use your phone?

> What do you use your computer for?

> How often do you use the internet?

> Which social media platform do you use most?

---

### Entertainment

> What kind of movies do you like?

> What kind of music do you listen to?

> What sport do you like?

---

### Health

> How do you stay healthy?

> How often do you exercise?

> How many hours do you sleep?

---

### Preferences

> What do you like?

> What don't you like?

> What do you prefer?

> Why do you prefer it?

---

# 🧠 Part 3 — The Most Important Skill

## Answer শুধু 1 sentence হবে না।

### ❌ Beginner

> What do you do in your free time?

> I watch movies.

### 🟡 Better

> I watch movies in my free time. I enjoy watching movies because they help me relax.

### 🟢 Intermediate

> I usually watch movies in my free time. I enjoy action and thriller movies because they are exciting and keep me interested. I normally watch them at home, especially at the weekend.

### 🔥 Advanced

> In my free time, I usually watch movies, listen to music, or learn something new. I particularly enjoy thriller and science-fiction movies because they keep me engaged and make me think. I normally watch movies at home because it's more comfortable, although I sometimes enjoy going to the cinema with friends.

### Formula:

> **Answer → Reason → Example → Extra Information**

---

# 🔥 Part 4 — Question Expansion

একটি question থেকে অনেক question তৈরি করতে হবে।

### Main:

> What is your favorite sport?

### Follow-up:

> Why do you like it?

↓

> How often do you play it?

↓

> Where do you play?

↓

> Who do you play with?

↓

> Who is your favorite player?

↓

> Which team do you support?

↓

> Do you watch professional matches?

↓

> What can sports teach us?

↓

> How does sport help your health?

---

## 🎯 Golden Rule

যেকোনো topic-এ এই 8টি question মাথায় রাখবে:

> **What?**

> **Why?**

> **When?**

> **Where?**

> **Who?**

> **How?**

> **Which?**

> **Would/Should?**

---

# 🧩 Part 5 — One Topic → Full Conversation

## Topic: Movies 🎬

### Person A:

> What kind of movies do you like?

### You:

> I really like action and thriller movies because they are exciting.

### A:

> Who is your favorite actor?

### You:

> My favorite actor is ________. I like their acting and screen presence.

### A:

> Do you prefer watching movies at home or in the cinema?

### You:

> I prefer watching movies at home because it's more comfortable. However, I enjoy going to the cinema for big movies.

### A:

> Who do you usually watch movies with?

### You:

> I usually watch movies alone, but sometimes I watch them with my friends.

### A:

> How often do you watch movies?

### You:

> I usually watch one or two movies a week when I have enough free time.

**Music**

> What kind of music do you listen to?

↓

**Sports**

> Do you play any sports?

↓

↓

> What do you usually do in the evening?

↓

> How often do you use your phone?

এভাবে conversation naturally change করতে হবে।

## Topic: Family

> Who do you live with?

> What food do you usually eat?

> What do you like about Bangladesh?

> Which social media platform do you use most?

> What kind of movies do you enjoy?

## Topic: Music

> When do you usually listen to music?

# 🔴 Part 9 — Advanced Question Patterns

এখন **Yes/No question-এর বাইরে** যেতে হবে।

### Opinion

> What do you think about social media?

> Why do you think exercise is important?

### Comparison

> Which is better, studying alone or studying with friends?

### Preference

> Would you rather live in a city or a village?

### Experience

> What did you do last weekend?

> What would you do if you had a free day?

> What should people do to stay healthy?

# 🟢 Part 10 — Tense Revision Through Speaking

Speaking-এর সময় tense automatically use করতে হবে।

## Present Simple

> I usually wake up at 7 a.m.

## Present Continuous

> I am learning English now.

## Past Simple

> I watched a movie yesterday.

## Past Continuous

> I was watching a movie when my friend called me.

## Present Perfect

> I have watched many English movies.

## Future

> I will practice English tomorrow.

## Going to

> I am going to study English tonight.

---

# 🔥 Part 11 — Same Topic, Different Tenses

## Topic: Sports

### Present

> I play cricket with my friends.

### Past

> I played cricket last Friday.

### Present Perfect

> I have played cricket since I was a child.

### Future

> I will play cricket next weekend.

### Continuous

> I am playing cricket this afternoon.

# 🗣️ Part 12 — Real Conversation Test

এখন ধরে নাও তুমি নতুন একজন মানুষের সাথে দেখা করেছ।

### Conversation

**A:** Hi! Nice to meet you.

**You:**

> Nice to meet you too.

**A:** Where are you from?

**You:**

> I'm from Bangladesh.

**A:** Where do you live?

**You:**

> I live in ________.

**A:** What do you do?

**You:**

> I'm a ________.

**A:** What do you do in your free time?

**You:**

> I usually watch movies, listen to music, and play sports.

**A:** What kind of movies do you like?

**You:**

> I enjoy action and thriller movies because they are exciting.

**A:** What kind of music do you listen to?

**You:**

> I mainly listen to ________.

**A:** Do you play sports?

**You:**

> Yes, I sometimes play cricket with my friends.

**A:** Do you exercise?

**You:**

> Yes, I try to exercise regularly because I want to stay healthy.

**A:** What do you usually do at the weekend?

**You:**

> I usually spend time with my family, meet friends, watch movies, or relax at home.

**A:** It was nice talking to you.

**You:**

> It was nice talking to you too.

---

# 🧠 Part 13 — 5 Levels of Answer

একই question-এর answer কীভাবে বড় করবে সেটা খুব গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

### Question:

> **What do you do in your free time?**

### Level 1

> I watch movies.

### Level 2

> I watch movies in my free time.

### Level 3

> I usually watch movies in my free time because they help me relax.

### Level 4

> I usually watch movies in my free time because they help me relax. I also listen to music and play sports with my friends.

### Level 5

> I usually watch movies, listen to music, and play sports in my free time. I particularly enjoy watching movies because they help me relax after a busy day. I also like playing cricket with my friends because it keeps me active and gives me an opportunity to spend time with them. When I have more time, I try to learn something new as well.

🔥 **তোমার target = Level 4 → Level 5**

---

# 🔥 Part 14 — Emergency Speaking Formula

কথা বলার সময় answer মাথায় না এলে এই structure ব্যবহার করবে:

> **Personally, I think...**

> **The main reason is...**

> **For example...**

> **Another reason is...**

> **However...**

> **Overall...**

### Example:

> Personally, I think exercise is very important. The main reason is that it helps us stay healthy. For example, walking or running can improve our fitness. Another reason is that exercise can reduce stress. However, people should also get enough rest. Overall, regular exercise is an important part of a healthy lifestyle.

---

# 🧠 Part 15 — Conversation Connectors

কথা smooth করার জন্য:

### Starting

> Well,...

> Actually,...

> Personally,...

> In my opinion,...

### Adding

> Also,...

> In addition,...

> Besides,...

> Another thing is...

### Example

> For example,...

> For instance,...

### Contrast

> However,...

> But,...

> On the other hand,...

### Result

> Therefore,...

> So,...

> That's why...

### Ending

> Overall,...

> In general,...

> That's basically it.

---

# 🚨 Part 16 — Don't Translate Word by Word

বাংলা থেকে সরাসরি translate করার অভ্যাস কমাতে হবে।

### ❌ বাংলা চিন্তা:

> আমার ক্রিকেট খেলতে ভালো লাগে।

তারপর translate:

> My cricket playing feels good.

### ✅ Natural English:

> I enjoy playing cricket.

---

### ❌

> আমার গান শুনতে ভালো লাগে।

### ✅

> I enjoy listening to music.

---

### ❌

> আমার ঘুরতে ভালো লাগে।

### ✅

> I enjoy traveling.

---

### ❌

> আমার সকালে উঠতে ভালো লাগে না।

### ✅

> I don't like waking up early.

---

# 🗣️ Part 17 — Think in English

Question:

> What do you usually do in the evening?

মনে মনে বাংলা:

> "আমি সন্ধ্যায় সাধারণত..." ❌

Instead directly:

> **I usually...**

তারপর:

> **In the evening, I usually...**

তারপর:

> **In the evening, I usually finish my work, have dinner, and spend some time relaxing.**

---

# 🏆 Part 18 — 10-Minute Speaking Test

এখন **notes না দেখে** প্রতিটি question-এর answer বলবে।

### Round 1 — Personal

1. Tell me about yourself.
2. Where are you from?
3. What do you do?
4. What are your hobbies?

### Round 2 — Daily Life

5. Tell me about your daily routine.
6. What do you usually do in the morning?
7. What do you usually do in the evening?
8. What do you do at weekends?

### Round 3 — Interests

9. What kind of movies do you like?
10. What kind of music do you like?
11. What sport do you like?
12. What do you like doing in your free time?

### Round 4 — Technology

13. How often do you use your phone?
14. What do you use your computer for?
15. How do you use the internet?
16. What do you think about social media?

### Round 5 — Health

17. How do you stay healthy?
18. How often do you exercise?
19. What healthy food do you like?
20. How do you deal with stress?

# 🔥 Part 19 — Random Question Challenge

এগুলো **randomly** answer করবে।

1. What is your favorite color?
2. Why do you like it?
3. What is your favorite food?
4. What did you do yesterday?
5. What are you going to do tomorrow?
6. What did you do last weekend?
7. What are you doing now?
8. What kind of music do you like?
9. Do you prefer tea or coffee?
10. Would you rather stay home or go outside?
11. Which transport do you use most?
12. What do you like about your city?
13. What do you like about Bangladesh?
14. What is your favorite sport?
15. Who is your favorite player?
16. What do you do to stay healthy?
17. What don't you like?
18. What do you enjoy doing with your friends?
19. What are you interested in?
20. What are your future goals?

# 🧠 Part 20 — Follow-up Challenge

তুমি একটি answer দেবে।

তারপর **নিজেকেই follow-up question করবে।**

### Example:

> I like cricket.

নিজেকে:

> Why do I like cricket?

Answer:

> Because it is exciting.

তারপর:

> How often do I play cricket?

Answer:

> I play it once or twice a week.

তারপর:

> Who do I play with?

Answer:

> I usually play with my friends.

এভাবে:

> **Answer → Question → Answer → Question → Answer**

এটি fluency তৈরির জন্য অত্যন্ত গুরুত্বপূর্ণ।

---

নিজের সাথে কথা বলবে:

> **Hello. Let me tell you about myself.**

তারপর:

**Introduction**

↓

**Family**

↓

**Daily routine**

↓

**Study/work**

↓

**Hobbies**

↓

**Friends**

↓

**Food**

↓

**Movies**

↓

**Music**

↓

**Sports**

↓

**Health**

↓

**Technology**

↓

**Social Media**

↓

**Likes & Dislikes**

↓

**Future**

# 🔥 Part 22 — Final 5-Minute Model Answer

## Topic: **Tell Me About Yourself**

> Hello, my name is ________. I am from Bangladesh and I live in ________. Currently, I am studying/working as ________.
>
> There are several things I enjoy doing in my free time. I like watching movies, listening to music, playing sports, and learning new things. Among these activities, I particularly enjoy ________ because ________.
>
> I also like spending time with my family and friends. At the weekend, I usually relax, meet friends, or work on my personal activities.
>
> I try to maintain a healthy lifestyle by exercising, eating reasonably healthy food, drinking enough water, and getting enough sleep.
>
> I use technology every day. I use my phone and computer for communication, learning, entertainment, and work. I also use the internet and social media, but I try to use them responsibly.
>
> In terms of my likes and dislikes, I enjoy ________, but I'm not a big fan of ________. I prefer ________ to ________ because ________.
>
> In the future, I want to improve my English communication skills and become more confident when speaking with other people.
>
> Overall, I am interested in learning, improving myself, and developing useful skills for my future.

---

# 🚨 সবচেয়ে গুরুত্বপূর্ণ Rule

Lesson 1–29-এর সব question **মুখস্থ করার দরকার নেই।**

তোমার লক্ষ্য হবে এই **question-making system** master করা:

> **What?**
> **Why?**
> **Who?**
> **When?**
> **Where?**
> **Which?**
> **How?**
> **How often?**
> **How long?**
> **Would?**
> **Could?**
> **Should?**
> **Have you ever...?**
> **What do you think...?**
> **What would you do if...?**

তারপর:

> **Question → Answer → Reason → Example → Follow-up → Opinion**

এটা পারলে তুমি শুধু Lesson 1–29-এর topic না, **নতুন topic নিয়েও English conversation করতে পারবে।**
